## 1. `CupDiagram` (inherited, 495 lines, sha256 `ba70609166f499630e2dde5f6f4749bce7a65050e5d0b97df904f1531ce333de`)

```lean
import Mathlib.Data.Finset.Sort
import Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Prod
import Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Sigma
import Lean.Elab.Tactic.Omega

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev Rank (a k : ℕ) := a + 4 + k

/-- The ordered endpoints in native (3.2.1), in zero-based site numbering. -/
def Eligible {n : ℕ} (a : ℕ) (p : Fin n × Fin n) : Prop :=
  p.1 < p.2 ∧ (p.2.val = p.1.val + 1 ∨ p.2.val < a + 3)

instance {n a : ℕ} : DecidablePred (@Eligible n a) :=
  fun p => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (p.1 < p.2 ∧ (_ ∨ _)))

abbrev Cup (a n : ℕ) := {p : Fin n × Fin n // Eligible a p}

instance {a n : ℕ} : Fintype (Cup a n) := Subtype.fintype (Eligible a)

def left {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : Fin n := c.val.1
def right {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : Fin n := c.val.2

theorem ends_lt {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : left c < right c := c.property.1

def touches {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (x : Fin n) : Prop :=
  x = left c ∨ x = right c

instance {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : DecidablePred (touches c) :=
  fun x => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (x = left c ∨ x = right c))

theorem eligible_core_or_tail {a n : ℕ} (p : Fin n × Fin n) :
    Eligible a p ↔
      (p.1 < p.2 ∧ p.2.val < a + 3) ∨
      (a + 2 ≤ p.1.val ∧ p.2.val = p.1.val + 1) := by
  simp only [Eligible, Fin.lt_def]
  omega

theorem card_height_five : Fintype.card (Cup 5 (Rank 5 0)) = 29 := by decide


instance {a n : ℕ} : DecidableEq (Cup a n) := inferInstanceAs (DecidableEq (Subtype _))

theorem card_height_five_core :
    ((Finset.univ : Finset (Cup 5 (Rank 5 0))).filter
      (fun c => (right c).val < 8)).card = 28 := by decide

theorem card_height_five_tail :
    ((Finset.univ : Finset (Cup 5 (Rank 5 0))).filter
      (fun c => 8 ≤ (right c).val)).card = 1 := by decide

def free {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : Finset (Fin n) :=
  (Finset.univ.erase (left c)).erase (right c)

theorem mem_free {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (x : Fin n) :
    x ∈ free c ↔ ¬ touches c x := by
  simp [free, touches, and_comm]

theorem free_card {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : (free c).card = n - 2 := by
  have h : right c ∈ (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)).erase (left c) := by
    simp [(ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)).symm]
  rw [free, Finset.card_erase_of_mem h, Finset.card_erase_of_mem (Finset.mem_univ _)]
  simp
  omega


def slotValue {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (i : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  if i < (left c).val then i else
    if i + 1 < (right c).val then i + 1 else i + 2

def siteValue {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (x : ℕ) : ℕ :=
  if x < (left c).val then x else
    if x < (right c).val then x - 1 else x - 2

theorem slot_props {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {i : ℕ} (hi : i < n - 2) :
    slotValue c i < n ∧ slotValue c i ≠ (left c).val ∧
      slotValue c i ≠ (right c).val := by
  have hl := (left c).isLt
  have hr := (right c).isLt
  have h := ends_lt c
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at h
  unfold slotValue
  split <;> (try split) <;> omega

theorem site_lt {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {x : Fin n} (hx : x ∈ free c) :
    siteValue c x.val < n - 2 := by
  have hn := x.isLt
  have hl := (left c).isLt
  have hr := (right c).isLt
  have h := ends_lt c
  have hf := (mem_free c x).mp hx
  simp only [touches, not_or, ← Fin.val_ne_iff] at hf
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at h
  unfold siteValue
  split <;> (try split) <;> omega

/-- Through slots increase in the physical site order. -/
def throughOrder {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : Fin (n - 2) ≃o ↥(free c) where
  toFun i := ⟨⟨slotValue c i.val, (slot_props c i.isLt).1⟩,
    (mem_free c _).mpr (by
      rintro (h | h)
      · exact (slot_props c i.isLt).2.1 (congrArg Fin.val h)
      · exact (slot_props c i.isLt).2.2 (congrArg Fin.val h))⟩
  invFun x := ⟨siteValue c x.val.val, site_lt c x.property⟩
  left_inv i := by
    apply Fin.ext
    change siteValue c (slotValue c i.val) = i.val
    have h := ends_lt c
    simp only [Fin.lt_def] at h
    unfold siteValue slotValue
    split <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> omega
  right_inv x := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    apply Fin.ext
    change slotValue c (siteValue c x.val.val) = x.val.val
    have h := ends_lt c
    have hf := (mem_free c x.val).mp x.property
    simp only [touches, not_or, ← Fin.val_ne_iff] at hf
    simp only [Fin.lt_def] at h
    unfold siteValue slotValue
    split <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> omega
  map_rel_iff' := by
    intro i j
    change slotValue c i.val ≤ slotValue c j.val ↔ i.val ≤ j.val
    unfold slotValue
    split <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> (try split) <;> omega


/-- A point on the middle row either leaves this half diagram or follows the cup. -/
def mate {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) : Equiv.Perm (Fin n) :=
  Equiv.swap (left c) (right c)

theorem mate_eq_self_iff {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (x : Fin n) :
    mate c x = x ↔ ¬ touches c x := by
  have h := ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)
  by_cases hl : x = left c
  · subst x
    simp [mate, touches, h.symm]
  by_cases hr : x = right c
  · subst x
    simp [mate, touches, hl, h]
  simp [mate, touches, hl, hr, Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne]

/-- An external line either emerges on the opposite side or returns to its own side. -/
inductive Exit (n : ℕ) where
  | through (site : Fin n)
  | returning (site : Fin n)
  deriving DecidableEq

/-- Follow a line starting at a free site of c, alternating across the glued cups. -/
def traceSite {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n) (x : Fin n) : Exit n :=
  if ¬ touches d x then .through x
  else
    let y := mate d x
    if ¬ touches c y then .returning y
    else .through (mate c y)

theorem trace_equal {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {x : Fin n} (h : x ∈ free c) :
    traceSite c c x = .through x := by
  simp [traceSite, (mem_free c x).mp h]

/-- The only possible closed middle component has both arcs on the same pair. -/
def loopEdges {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n) : Prop :=
  left c = left d ∧ right c = right d

theorem loopEdges_iff {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n) : loopEdges c d ↔ c = d := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨hl, hr⟩
    exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext hl hr)
  · rintro rfl
    exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩

theorem mate_of_ends {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {x y : Fin n}
    (hxy : x ≠ y) (hx : touches c x) (hy : touches c y) :
    mate c x = y := by
  rcases hx with hx | hx <;> rcases hy with hy | hy
  · exact (hxy (hx.trans hy.symm)).elim
  · rw [hx, hy]; exact Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _
  · rw [hx, hy]; exact Equiv.swap_apply_right _ _
  · exact (hxy (hx.trans hy.symm)).elim

theorem mate_touches {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {x : Fin n} (hx : touches c x) :
    touches c (mate c x) := by
  rcases hx with hx | hx
  · rw [hx]; exact Or.inr (Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _)
  · rw [hx]; exact Or.inl (Equiv.swap_apply_right _ _)

theorem trace_disjoint {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n)
    (h : ∀ z, touches d z → ¬ touches c z) (x : Fin n) :
    traceSite c d x =
      if touches d x then .returning (mate d x) else .through x := by
  by_cases hd : touches d x
  · simp [traceSite, hd, h _ (mate_touches d hd)]
  · simp [traceSite, hd]

/-- b is shared, u is free on the source c, and v is free on the target d. -/
structure Shared {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n) (b u v : Fin n) : Prop where
  distinctBU : b ≠ u
  distinctBV : b ≠ v
  distinctUV : u ≠ v
  source : ∀ z, touches c z ↔ z = b ∨ z = v
  target : ∀ z, touches d z ↔ z = b ∨ z = u

theorem trace_shared {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v x : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (hx : x ∈ free c) :
    traceSite c d x = .through (Equiv.swap u v x) := by
  have hsource := (mem_free c x).mp hx
  have hn : x ≠ v := fun h => hsource ((s.source x).mpr (Or.inr h))
  by_cases hu : x = u
  · subst x
    have hdu := (s.target u).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
    have hdb := (s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
    have hcb := (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
    have hcv := (s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
    have hd := mate_of_ends d s.distinctBU.symm hdu hdb
    have hc := mate_of_ends c s.distinctBV hcb hcv
    simp [traceSite, hdu, hd, hcb, hc]
  · have hb : x ≠ b := fun h => hsource ((s.source x).mpr (Or.inl h))
    have hdt : ¬ touches d x := fun h => ((s.target x).mp h).elim hb hu
    simp [traceSite, hdt, Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hu hn]

theorem shared_free_swap {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v x : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) :
    Equiv.swap u v x ∈ free d ↔ x ∈ free c := by
  have hb : Equiv.swap u v b = b :=
    Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne s.distinctBU s.distinctBV
  have hv : Equiv.swap u v v = u := Equiv.swap_apply_right _ _
  rw [mem_free, mem_free, s.source, s.target]
  have h₁ : Equiv.swap u v x = b ↔ x = b :=
    by simpa only [hb] using ((Equiv.swap u v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := x) (y := b))
  have h₂ : Equiv.swap u v x = u ↔ x = v :=
    by simpa only [hv] using ((Equiv.swap u v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := x) (y := v))
  simp only [h₁, h₂]

/-- The permutation in the through quotient, with both ends numbered increasingly. -/
def throughTransport {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) : Equiv.Perm (Fin (n - 2)) :=
  (throughOrder c).toEquiv.trans
    ((Equiv.subtypeEquiv (Equiv.swap u v) (fun _ => (shared_free_swap s).symm)).trans
      (throughOrder d).symm.toEquiv)

theorem transport_site {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Fin (n - 2)) :
    ((throughOrder d) (throughTransport s i)).val =
      Equiv.swap u v ((throughOrder c) i).val := by
  simp [throughTransport]


theorem touch_other {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {b : Fin n} (h : touches c b) :
    b ≠ mate c b ∧ ∀ z, touches c z ↔ z = b ∨ z = mate c b := by
  have hn := ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)
  rcases h with h | h
  · subst b
    simp [mate, touches, hn]
  · subst b
    simp [mate, touches, hn.symm, or_comm]

theorem overlap_trichotomy {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n) :
    c = d ∨ (∀ z, touches d z → ¬ touches c z) ∨ ∃ b u v, Shared c d b u v := by
  by_cases H : ∃ b, touches c b ∧ touches d b
  · obtain ⟨b, hc, hd⟩ := H
    obtain ⟨hbv, hv⟩ := touch_other c hc
    obtain ⟨hbu, hu⟩ := touch_other d hd
    by_cases he : mate d b = mate c b
    · left
      have Ht : ∀ z, touches c z ↔ touches d z := by
        intro z
        rw [hv, hu, he]
      have Hcl := (Ht _).mp (Or.inl rfl)
      have Hcr := (Ht _).mp (Or.inr rfl)
      have hc := ends_lt c
      have hd := ends_lt d
      rcases Hcl with hl | hl <;> rcases Hcr with hr | hr
      · rw [hl, hr] at hc
        exact (lt_irrefl _ hc).elim
      · exact (loopEdges_iff _ _).mp ⟨hl, hr⟩
      · rw [hl, hr] at hc
        exact (asymm hc hd).elim
      · rw [hl, hr] at hc
        exact (lt_irrefl _ hc).elim
    · exact Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨b, mate d b, mate c b, hbu, hbv, he, hv, hu⟩)
  · right; left
    exact fun z hd hc => H ⟨z, hc, hd⟩

theorem transport_unique {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v b' u' v' : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (t : Shared c d b' u' v') :
    throughTransport s = throughTransport t := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply (throughOrder d).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  rw [transport_site, transport_site]
  obtain h := ((trace_shared s ((throughOrder c) i).property).symm.trans
    (trace_shared t ((throughOrder c) i).property))
  exact Exit.through.inj h

/-- All four endpoints below the cutoff imply identity on later through slots. -/
theorem transport_late {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Fin (n - 2)) (t : ℕ)
    (hc : (right c).val < t + 2) (hd : (right d).val < t + 2)
    (hi : t ≤ i.val) : throughTransport s i = i := by
  have hlc := ends_lt c
  have hld := ends_lt d
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hlc hld
  have hslots (e : Cup a n) (h : (right e).val < t + 2)
      (hl : (left e).val < (right e).val) : slotValue e i.val = i.val + 2 := by
    unfold slotValue
    split <;> (try split) <;> omega
  have hv := (s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  have hu := (s.target u).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  apply (throughOrder d).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  rw [transport_site]
  have hu' : ((throughOrder c) i).val ≠ u := by
    intro h
    have h' := congrArg Fin.val h
    change slotValue c i.val = u.val at h'
    rw [hslots c hc hlc] at h'
    rcases hu with h | h <;> rw [h] at h' <;> omega
  have hv' : ((throughOrder c) i).val ≠ v := by
    intro h
    exact (mem_free c _).mp ((throughOrder c) i).property (h ▸ hv)
  rw [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hu' hv']
  apply Fin.ext
  change slotValue c i.val = slotValue d i.val
  rw [hslots c hc hlc, hslots d hd hld]

theorem transport_adjacent_forward {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v)
    (hcl : left c = v) (hcr : right c = b)
    (hdl : left d = b) (hdr : right d = u)
    (hb : b.val = v.val + 1) (hu : u.val = b.val + 1) :
    throughTransport s = Equiv.refl _ := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply (throughOrder d).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  rw [transport_site]
  by_cases h : ((throughOrder c) i).val = u
  · rw [h, Equiv.swap_apply_left]
    apply Fin.ext
    have h' := congrArg Fin.val h
    change slotValue c i.val = u.val at h'
    change v.val = slotValue d i.val
    simp only [slotValue, hcl, hcr, hdl, hdr] at h' ⊢
    split at h' <;> (try split at h') <;> split <;> (try split) <;> omega
  · have h' : ((throughOrder c) i).val ≠ v := by
      intro he
      exact (mem_free c _).mp ((throughOrder c) i).property (Or.inl (he.trans hcl.symm))
    rw [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne h h']
    apply Fin.ext
    have hh : slotValue c i.val ≠ u.val := fun he => h (Fin.ext he)
    change slotValue c i.val = slotValue d i.val
    simp only [slotValue, hcl, hcr, hdl, hdr] at hh ⊢
    by_cases hv' : i.val < v.val
    · have hb' : i.val < b.val := by omega
      simp [hv', hb']
    · by_cases he : i.val = v.val
      · exfalso
        apply hh
        simp [he, hb, hu]
      · have hb' : ¬ i.val < b.val := by omega
        have h₁ : ¬ i.val + 1 < b.val := by omega
        have h₂ : ¬ i.val + 1 < u.val := by omega
        simp [hv', hb', h₁, h₂]

theorem transport_reverse {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (t : Shared d c b v u) :
    throughTransport t = (throughTransport s).symm := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply (throughOrder c).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  rw [transport_site]
  have h := transport_site s ((throughTransport s).symm i)
  rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply] at h
  rw [h, Equiv.swap_comm v u]
  simpa only [Equiv.symm_swap] using
    (Equiv.swap u v).symm_apply_apply (((throughOrder c) ((throughTransport s).symm i)).val)

/-- Consecutive tail cups induce the identity, even across inactive sites. -/
theorem transport_adjacent {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v)
    (hc : (right c).val = (left c).val + 1)
    (hd : (right d).val = (left d).val + 1) :
    throughTransport s = Equiv.refl _ := by
  have hb := (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hb' := (s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  rcases hb with hlc | hrc <;> rcases hb' with hld | hrd
  · exfalso
    have he : right c = right d := Fin.ext (by
      have h := congrArg Fin.val (hlc.symm.trans hld)
      omega)
    have h1 := (s.source (right c)).mp (Or.inr rfl)
    have h2 := (s.target (right d)).mp (Or.inr rfl)
    rcases h1 with h1 | h1 <;> rcases h2 with h2 | h2
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (hlc.symm.trans h1.symm)
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (hlc.symm.trans h1.symm)
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt d) (hld.symm.trans h2.symm)
    · exact s.distinctUV (h2.symm.trans (he.symm.trans h1))
  · let t : Shared d c b v u :=
      ⟨s.distinctBV, s.distinctBU, s.distinctUV.symm, s.target, s.source⟩
    have H := transport_adjacent_forward t
    have hdl : left d = u := by
      rcases (s.target _).mp (Or.inl rfl) with hz | hz
      · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt d) (hz.trans hrd)).elim
      · exact hz
    have hcr : right c = v := by
      rcases (s.source _).mp (Or.inr rfl) with hz | hz
      · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (hlc.symm.trans hz.symm)).elim
      · exact hz
    have h := H hdl hrd.symm hlc.symm hcr
      (by rw [hrd, ← hdl]; exact hd) (by rw [← hcr, hlc]; exact hc)
    simpa using congrArg Equiv.symm ((transport_reverse s t).symm.trans h)
  · have hcl : left c = v := by
      rcases (s.source _).mp (Or.inl rfl) with hz | hz
      · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (hz.trans hrc)).elim
      · exact hz
    have hdr : right d = u := by
      rcases (s.target _).mp (Or.inr rfl) with hz | hz
      · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt d) (hld.symm.trans hz.symm)).elim
      · exact hz
    exact transport_adjacent_forward s hcl hrc.symm hld.symm hdr
      (by rw [hrc, ← hcl]; exact hc) (by rw [← hdr, hld]; exact hd)
  · exfalso
    have he : left c = left d := Fin.ext (by
      have h := congrArg Fin.val (hrc.symm.trans hrd)
      omega)
    have h1 := (s.source (left c)).mp (Or.inl rfl)
    have h2 := (s.target (left d)).mp (Or.inl rfl)
    rcases h1 with h1 | h1 <;> rcases h2 with h2 | h2
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (h1.trans hrc)
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) (h1.trans hrc)
    · exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt d) (h2.trans hrd)
    · exact s.distinctUV (h2.symm.trans (he.symm.trans h1))

theorem transport_fixes_inactive {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v)
    (i : Fin (Rank a k - 2)) (hi : a + 2 ≤ i.val) :
    throughTransport s i = i := by
  have hc := c.property.2
  have hd := d.property.2
  change (right c).val = (left c).val + 1 ∨ (right c).val < a + 3 at hc
  change (right d).val = (left d).val + 1 ∨ (right d).val < a + 3 at hd
  have hcl := ends_lt c
  have hdl := ends_lt d
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hcl hdl
  have hbc := (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hbd := (s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  rcases hc with hc | hc <;> rcases hd with hd | hd
  · rw [transport_adjacent s hc hd]; rfl
  · refine transport_late s i (a + 2) ?_ (by omega) hi
    rcases hbc with hc' | hc' <;> rcases hbd with hd' | hd' <;>
      have he := congrArg Fin.val (hc'.symm.trans hd') <;> omega
  · refine transport_late s i (a + 2) (by omega) ?_ hi
    rcases hbc with hc' | hc' <;> rcases hbd with hd' | hd' <;>
      have he := congrArg Fin.val (hc'.symm.trans hd') <;> omega
  · exact transport_late s i (a + 2) (by omega) (by omega) hi


/-- With disjoint arcs, the two endpoints of the target cup return to the source.
The other free sites each contribute one through strand. -/
theorem disjoint_through_card {a n : ℕ} (c d : Cup a n)
    (h : ∀ z, touches d z → ¬ touches c z) :
    ((free c).filter (fun z => ¬ touches d z)).card = n - 4 := by
  have hl : left d ∈ free c := (mem_free _ _).mpr (h _ (Or.inl rfl))
  have hr : right d ∈ (free c).erase (left d) := by
    exact Finset.mem_erase.mpr ⟨(ne_of_lt (ends_lt d)).symm,
      (mem_free _ _).mpr (h _ (Or.inr rfl))⟩
  have he : (free c).filter (fun z => ¬ touches d z) =
      ((free c).erase (left d)).erase (right d) := by
    ext z
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_erase, touches, not_or]
    tauto
  rw [he, Finset.card_erase_of_mem hr, Finset.card_erase_of_mem hl, free_card]
  omega

#print axioms disjoint_through_card
#print axioms card_height_five_core
#print axioms card_height_five_tail
#print axioms overlap_trichotomy
#print axioms transport_unique
#print axioms transport_adjacent
#print axioms transport_fixes_inactive

#print axioms eligible_core_or_tail
#print axioms card_height_five
#print axioms throughOrder
#print axioms trace_equal
#print axioms loopEdges_iff
#print axioms trace_disjoint
#print axioms trace_shared
#print axioms transport_site

end BrauerHook
```

## 2. `HookFiber` (inherited, 183 lines, sha256 `78d65da467183ba5f0f454ee1e630c8bd1db94d86ce7433ea97d070b3b26aa95`)

```lean
import Mathlib.Basic.Complex.Basic
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Fin
import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Group.Finset.Basic
import Mathlib.GroupTheory.Perm.Sign
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Dimension.Finite
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Basis
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring

open scoped BigOperators

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev Letters (d : ℕ) := Fin (d + 1)
abbrev TabloidModule (d : ℕ) := Letters d → ℂ

def total {d : ℕ} : TabloidModule d →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ where
  toFun f := ∑ j, f j
  map_add' _ _ := Finset.sum_add_distrib
  map_smul' c f := by
    change (∑ j, c * f j) = c * ∑ j, f j
    exact (Finset.mul_sum _ _ _).symm

abbrev Standard (d : ℕ) := LinearMap.ker (total (d := d))

/-- The unique tabloid for each possible singleton entry in the second row. -/
def tabloid {d : ℕ} (b : Letters d) : TabloidModule d := fun x =>
  if x = b then 1 else 0

/-- The hook column has exactly two boxes. Its alternating column orbit is this difference. -/
def hookPolytabloid {d : ℕ} (top bottom : Letters d) : TabloidModule d :=
  tabloid bottom - tabloid top

theorem total_tabloid {d : ℕ} (i : Letters d) : total (tabloid i) = 1 := by
  simp [total, tabloid]

theorem polytabloid_mem_standard {d : ℕ} (top bottom : Letters d) :
    hookPolytabloid top bottom ∈ Standard d := by
  change total (tabloid bottom - tabloid top) = 0
  simp only [map_sub, total_tabloid, sub_self]

/-- Coordinates in the polytabloids with top-left entry the last letter. -/
noncomputable def expand {d : ℕ} (f : Fin d → ℂ) : TabloidModule d :=
  Fin.lastCases (-∑ j, f j) f

@[simp] theorem expand_last {d : ℕ} (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    expand f (Fin.last d) = -∑ j, f j := by simp [expand]

@[simp] theorem expand_castSucc {d : ℕ} (f : Fin d → ℂ) (i : Fin d) :
    expand f i.castSucc = f i := by simp [expand]

theorem expand_mem {d : ℕ} (f : Fin d → ℂ) : expand f ∈ Standard d := by
  change (∑ j, expand f j) = 0
  rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc]
  simp

noncomputable def standardCoords (d : ℕ) : (Fin d → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] Standard d where
  toFun f := ⟨expand f, expand_mem f⟩
  invFun f i := f.val i.castSucc
  left_inv f := funext (expand_castSucc f)
  right_inv f := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext j
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun i => ?_) j
    · change expand (fun i => f.val i.castSucc) (Fin.last d) = f.val (Fin.last d)
      rw [expand_last]
      have h : ∑ i, f.val i = 0 := f.property
      rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc] at h
      exact neg_eq_of_add_eq_zero_left (by simpa [add_comm] using h)
    · simp
  map_add' f g := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext j
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun i => ?_) j
    · simp [Finset.sum_add_distrib]
      ring
    · simp
  map_smul' c f := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    funext j
    refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun i => ?_) j
    · simp [Finset.mul_sum]
    · simp

/-- A unit coordinate is the ordinary (d,1) polytabloid e_i-e_last. -/
theorem expand_delta {d : ℕ} (i : Fin d) :
    expand (fun j => if j = i then 1 else 0) = hookPolytabloid (Fin.last d) i.castSucc := by
  funext j
  refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun h => ?_) j
  · simp [hookPolytabloid, tabloid, (Fin.castSucc_ne_last i).symm]
  · simp [hookPolytabloid, tabloid, Fin.castSucc_ne_last]

theorem standard_eq_polytabloid_span (d : ℕ) :
    Standard d = Submodule.span ℂ
      {v | ∃ top bottom : Letters d, top ≠ bottom ∧ v = hookPolytabloid top bottom} := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · intro v hv
    have h : v = expand (fun i => v i.castSucc) :=
      (congrArg Subtype.val ((standardCoords d).apply_symm_apply ⟨v, hv⟩)).symm
    rw [h]
    have hv' : (fun i : Fin d => v i.castSucc) =
        ∑ i : Fin d, (v i.castSucc) • (fun j : Fin d => if j = i then (1 : ℂ) else 0) := by
      funext j
      simp [eq_comm]
    rw [congrArg expand hv']
    change ((standardCoords d) (∑ i : Fin d, v i.castSucc •
      (fun j : Fin d => if j = i then (1 : ℂ) else 0))).val ∈ _
    rw [map_sum, Submodule.coe_sum]
    refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun i _ => ?_
    rw [map_smul, Submodule.coe_smul]
    apply Submodule.smul_mem
    change expand _ ∈ _
    rw [expand_delta]
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨_, _, (Fin.castSucc_ne_last i).symm, rfl⟩
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro v ⟨top, bottom, _, rfl⟩
    exact polytabloid_mem_standard top bottom

theorem standard_dim (d : ℕ) : Module.finrank ℂ (Standard d) = d := by
  rw [← (standardCoords d).finrank_eq]
  simp

def permute {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) (f : TabloidModule d) :
    TabloidModule d := fun i => f (p.symm i)

theorem permute_tabloid {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) (b : Letters d) :
    permute p (tabloid b) = tabloid (p b) := by
  funext i
  simp [permute, tabloid, Equiv.symm_apply_eq]

theorem permute_mem {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) (v : Standard d) :
    permute p v.val ∈ Standard d := by
  change (∑ j, v.val (p.symm j)) = 0
  rw [Equiv.sum_comp]
  exact v.property

noncomputable def permSign {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) : ℂ :=
  ((p.sign : ℤ) : ℂ)

theorem sign_mul {d : ℕ} (p q : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) :
    permSign (p * q) = permSign p * permSign q := by
  simp [permSign]

@[simp] theorem sign_one {d : ℕ} : permSign (1 : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) = 1 := by
  simp [permSign]

theorem sign_sq {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) : permSign p * permSign p = 1 := by
  unfold permSign
  rw [← Int.cast_mul]
  have h := Int.units_eq_one_or p.sign
  rcases h with h | h <;> simp [h]

/-- The through permutation acts on hook coordinates, with the transpose twist when selected. -/
noncomputable def hookAct {d : ℕ} (transpose : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d))
    (f : Fin d → ℂ) : Fin d → ℂ := fun i =>
  (if transpose then permSign p else 1) * permute p (expand f) i.castSucc

theorem expand_hookAct {d : ℕ} (transpose : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d))
    (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    expand (hookAct transpose p f) =
      (if transpose then permSign p else 1) • permute p (expand f) := by
  let v : Standard d := ⟨_, permute_mem p ⟨_, expand_mem f⟩⟩
  let w : Standard d := (if transpose then permSign p else 1) • v
  exact congrArg Subtype.val ((standardCoords d).apply_symm_apply w)

noncomputable def pairing {d : ℕ} (f g : TabloidModule d) : ℂ := ∑ t, f t * g t

theorem pairing_expanded {d : ℕ} (f g : Fin d → ℂ) :
    pairing (expand f) (expand g) = (∑ t, f t * g t) + (∑ t, f t) * (∑ t, g t) := by
  rw [pairing, Fin.sum_univ_castSucc]
  simp

theorem pairing_invariant {d : ℕ} (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) (f g : TabloidModule d) :
    pairing (permute p f) (permute p g) = pairing f g := by
  exact Equiv.sum_comp p.symm (fun t => f t * g t)

#print axioms expand_delta
#print axioms standard_eq_polytabloid_span
#print axioms standard_dim
#print axioms expand_hookAct
#print axioms pairing_expanded
#print axioms pairing_invariant

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import CupDiagram
import HookFiber
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Charpoly.Coeff
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

def liftSlot (a k : ℕ) (i : Letters (a + 1)) : Fin (Rank a k - 2) :=
  ⟨i.val, by have := i.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩

theorem liftSlot_injective (a k : ℕ) : Function.Injective (liftSlot a k) :=
  fun _ _ h => Fin.ext (congrArg (fun i : Fin (Rank a k - 2) => i.val) h)

theorem transport_active_bound {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Letters (a + 1)) :
    (throughTransport s (liftSlot a k i)).val < a + 2 := by
  by_contra! h
  have he := transport_fixes_inactive s (throughTransport s (liftSlot a k i)) h
  have hh := (throughTransport s).injective he
  have hi := i.isLt
  change i.val < a + 2 at hi
  have hh' := congrArg Fin.val hh
  change (throughTransport s (liftSlot a k i)).val = i.val at hh'
  omega

/-- Restriction of the physical through permutation to the first a+2 strands. -/
noncomputable def activeTransport {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) : Equiv.Perm (Letters (a + 1)) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (fun i => ⟨_, transport_active_bound s i⟩) (by
    apply Finite.injective_iff_bijective.mp
    intro i j h
    apply liftSlot_injective a k
    apply (throughTransport s).injective
    exact Fin.ext (congrArg (fun i : Letters (a + 1) => i.val) h))

theorem activeTransport_commutes {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Letters (a + 1)) :
    liftSlot a k (activeTransport s i) = throughTransport s (liftSlot a k i) := rfl

theorem activeTransport_unique {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v b' u' v' : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (t : Shared c d b' u' v') :
    activeTransport s = activeTransport t := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply liftSlot_injective a k
  rw [activeTransport_commutes, activeTransport_commutes, transport_unique s t]

theorem activeTransport_adjacent {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v)
    (hc : (right c).val = (left c).val + 1) (hd : (right d).val = (left d).val + 1) :
    activeTransport s = Equiv.refl _ := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply liftSlot_injective a k
  rw [activeTransport_commutes, transport_adjacent s hc hd]
  rfl

noncomputable def mixedTransport {a k : ℕ} (c d : Cup a (Rank a k)) :
    Option (Equiv.Perm (Letters (a + 1))) := by
  classical
  exact if h : ∃ b u v, Shared c d b u v then
    some (activeTransport h.choose_spec.choose_spec.choose_spec) else none

theorem mixedTransport_shared {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) :
    mixedTransport c d = some (activeTransport s) := by
  classical
  unfold mixedTransport
  rw [dite_eq_left (show ∃ b u v, Shared c d b u v from ⟨b, u, v, s⟩)]
  congr 1
  exact activeTransport_unique _ _

theorem mixedTransport_disjoint {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    (h : ∀ z, touches d z → ¬ touches c z) :
    mixedTransport c d = none := by
  classical
  unfold mixedTransport
  rw [dite_eq_right]
  rintro ⟨b, u, v, s⟩
  exact h b ((s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)) ((s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl))

abbrev NativeIndex (a k : ℕ) := Cup a (Rank a k) × Fin (a + 1)

noncomputable def delta {d : ℕ} (i : Fin d) : Fin d → ℂ :=
  fun j => if j = i then 1 else 0

noncomputable def cupMetric (a k : ℕ) : Matrix (NativeIndex a k) (NativeIndex a k) ℂ :=
  fun i j => if i.1 = j.1 then delta j.2 i.2 + 1 else 0

noncomputable def cupMetricInv (a k : ℕ) : Matrix (NativeIndex a k) (NativeIndex a k) ℂ :=
  fun i j => if i.1 = j.1 then delta j.2 i.2 - ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹ else 0

noncomputable def nativeMove (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool)
    (c d : Cup a (Rank a k)) (j : Fin (a + 1)) : Fin (a + 1) → ℂ :=
  if c = d then 0 else
    match mixedTransport (k := k) c d with
    | none => 0
    | some p => hookAct transpose p (delta j)

noncomputable def interaction (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (NativeIndex a k) (NativeIndex a k) ℂ :=
  fun i j => nativeMove a k transpose j.1 i.1 j.2 i.2

/-- Equal cups contribute a loop; otherwise the line trace supplies either zero or the
through action. Rows are target cups and columns are source cups. -/
noncomputable def nativePair (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i j : NativeIndex a k) :
    ℂ[X] :=
  (if j.1 = i.1 then X * C (pairing (expand (delta i.2)) (expand (delta j.2))) else 0) +
    C (pairing (expand (delta i.2)) (expand (nativeMove a k transpose j.1 i.1 j.2)))

noncomputable def nativeGram (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (NativeIndex a k) (NativeIndex a k) ℂ[X] :=
  nativePair a k transpose

noncomputable def nativeDet (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) : ℂ[X] :=
  (nativeGram a k transpose).det

theorem pairing_delta {d : ℕ} (i : Fin d) (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    pairing (expand (delta i)) (expand f) = f i + ∑ t, f t := by
  rw [pairing_expanded]
  simp [delta, ite_mul]

theorem pairing_loop {d : ℕ} (i j : Fin d) :
    pairing (expand (delta i)) (expand (delta j)) = delta j i + 1 := by
  rw [pairing_delta]
  simp [delta]

private theorem sum_weight {d : ℕ} (i : Fin d) (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    (∑ t, (delta t i + 1) * f t) = f i + ∑ t, f t := by
  simp [delta, add_mul, ite_mul, Finset.sum_add_distrib]

private theorem sum_cup_diagonal {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (f : Cup a n → ℂ) :
    (∑ q, if c = q then f q else 0) = f c := by
  classical
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single c]
  · simp
  · intro b _ hb
    simp [Ne.symm hb]
  · simp

theorem gram_eq_metric_char (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    nativeGram a k transpose =
      (cupMetric a k).map C * Matrix.charmatrix (-interaction a k transpose) := by
  classical
  have hact (i j : NativeIndex a k) :
      (cupMetric a k * interaction a k transpose) i j =
        pairing (expand (delta i.2)) (expand (nativeMove a k transpose j.1 i.1 j.2)) := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
    change (∑ c : Cup a (Rank a k), ∑ t : Fin (a + 1),
      (if i.1 = c then delta t i.2 + 1 else 0) *
        nativeMove a k transpose j.1 c j.2 t) = _
    simp_rw [ite_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_irrel, Finset.sum_const_zero]
    rw [sum_cup_diagonal, sum_weight, pairing_delta]
  rw [Matrix.charmatrix]
  change _ = _ * (Matrix.scalar (NativeIndex a k) X - C.mapMatrix (-interaction a k transpose))
  rw [map_neg, sub_neg_eq_add, mul_add]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  change nativePair a k transpose i j = _
  rw [Matrix.add_apply]
  rw [Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.mul_diagonal]
  have hm : (cupMetric a k).map C * C.mapMatrix (interaction a k transpose) =
      (cupMetric a k * interaction a k transpose).map C := by
    exact (Matrix.map_mul (f := C)).symm
  rw [hm]
  simp only [nativePair, Matrix.map_apply, hact, pairing_loop, cupMetric]
  by_cases h : i.1 = j.1
  · simp [h, mul_comm]
  · simp [h, Ne.symm h]

private theorem metric_fiber_inv (a : ℕ) (i j : Fin (a + 1)) :
    (∑ t : Fin (a + 1), (delta t i + 1) *
      (delta j t - ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹)) = delta j i := by
  rw [sum_weight, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
  have hsum : (∑ t : Fin (a + 1), delta j t) = 1 := by simp [delta]
  rw [hsum]
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul,
    Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
  have hn : (a : ℂ) + 2 ≠ 0 := by
    have h : (((a + 2 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (by omega)
    simpa using h
  field_simp
  ring

theorem metric_inverse (a k : ℕ) :
    cupMetric a k * cupMetricInv a k = 1 := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  change (∑ c : Cup a (Rank a k), ∑ t : Fin (a + 1),
    (if i.1 = c then delta t i.2 + 1 else 0) *
    (if c = j.1 then delta j.2 t - ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹ else 0)) = _
  simp_rw [ite_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_irrel, Finset.sum_const_zero]
  rw [sum_cup_diagonal]
  by_cases h : i.1 = j.1
  · rw [h]
    simp only [ite_true, metric_fiber_inv]
    simp [delta, Matrix.one_apply, Prod.ext_iff, h]
  · have hn : i ≠ j := fun he => h (congrArg Prod.fst he)
    simp [h, hn]

theorem metric_det_ne (a k : ℕ) : (cupMetric a k).det ≠ 0 := by
  have h := congrArg Matrix.det (metric_inverse a k)
  rw [Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_one] at h
  intro he
  rw [he, zero_mul] at h
  exact zero_ne_one h

theorem nativeDet_eq (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    nativeDet a k transpose =
      C (cupMetric a k).det * (-interaction a k transpose).charpoly := by
  unfold nativeDet
  rw [gram_eq_metric_char, Matrix.det_mul, ← Matrix.charpoly]
  change (C.mapMatrix (cupMetric a k)).det * _ = _
  rw [← RingHom.map_det]

theorem nativeDet_degree (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    (nativeDet a k transpose).natDegree = Fintype.card (Cup a (Rank a k)) * (a + 1) := by
  rw [nativeDet_eq, Polynomial.natDegree_C_mul (metric_det_ne a k),
    Matrix.charpoly_natDegree_eq_dim, Fintype.card_prod, Fintype.card_fin]

theorem native_height_five_degree :
    (nativeDet 5 0 false).natDegree = 174 := by
  rw [nativeDet_degree, card_height_five]

#print axioms activeTransport_commutes
#print axioms activeTransport_adjacent
#print axioms mixedTransport_shared
#print axioms mixedTransport_disjoint
#print axioms nativeGram
#print axioms gram_eq_metric_char
#print axioms metric_inverse
#print axioms nativeDet_eq
#print axioms nativeDet_degree
#print axioms native_height_five_degree

end BrauerHook
```

## 4. `NativeTail` (inherited, 208 lines, sha256 `5e44838ad2b70e8c1b7196662ed82d043d753eb5d3023acf874e0dbf1ddd7b4f`)

```lean
import NativeGram

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev CoreCup (a : ℕ) := {p : Fin (a+3) × Fin (a+3) // p.1 < p.2}

def embedCore (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) : Cup a (Rank a k) :=
  ⟨(⟨p.val.1.val, by have := p.val.1.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩,
    ⟨p.val.2.val, by have := p.val.2.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩),
   Or.inr p.val.2.isLt |> And.intro p.property⟩

def embedTail (a k : ℕ) (i : Fin (k+1)) : Cup a (Rank a k) :=
  ⟨(⟨a+2+i.val, by have := i.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩,
    ⟨a+3+i.val, by have := i.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩),
   ⟨by change a+2+i.val < a+3+i.val; omega,
    Or.inl (by change a+3+i.val = a+2+i.val+1; omega)⟩⟩

@[simp] theorem left_core_value (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (left (embedCore a k p)).val = p.val.1.val := rfl

@[simp] theorem right_core_value (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (right (embedCore a k p)).val = p.val.2.val := rfl

@[simp] theorem left_tail_value (a k : ℕ) (i : Fin (k+1)) :
    (left (embedTail a k i)).val = a+2+i.val := rfl

@[simp] theorem right_tail_value (a k : ℕ) (i : Fin (k+1)) :
    (right (embedTail a k i)).val = a+3+i.val := rfl

theorem core_injective (a k : ℕ) : Function.Injective (embedCore a k) := by
  intro p q h
  apply Subtype.ext
  apply Prod.ext
  · exact Fin.ext (congrArg (fun c => (left c).val) h)
  · exact Fin.ext (congrArg (fun c => (right c).val) h)

theorem tail_injective (a k : ℕ) : Function.Injective (embedTail a k) := by
  intro p q h
  apply Fin.ext
  have he := congrArg (fun c => (left c).val) h
  simp only [left_tail_value] at he
  omega

theorem core_tail_ne (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (k+1)) :
    embedCore a k p ≠ embedTail a k i := by
  intro h
  have he := congrArg (fun c => (right c).val) h
  have hp := p.val.2.isLt
  simp only [right_core_value, right_tail_value] at he
  omega

theorem core_tail_cover (a k : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a k)) :
    (∃ p, embedCore a k p = c) ∨ ∃ i, embedTail a k i = c := by
  obtain hc | hc := (eligible_core_or_tail c.val).mp c.property
  · left
    refine ⟨⟨(⟨_, lt_trans hc.1 hc.2⟩, ⟨_, hc.2⟩), hc.1⟩, ?_⟩
    apply Subtype.ext
    exact Prod.ext (Fin.ext rfl) (Fin.ext rfl)
  · right
    let i : Fin (k+1) := ⟨c.val.1.val - (a+2), by
      have h := c.val.2.isLt
      change c.val.2.val < a+4+k at h
      omega⟩
    refine ⟨i, Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (Fin.ext ?_) (Fin.ext ?_))⟩ <;>
      simp only [i, embedTail] <;> omega

/-- Native cups reindexed by complete core and by the ordered adjacent tail. -/
noncomputable def coreTailEquiv (a k : ℕ) :
    CoreCup a ⊕ Fin (k+1) ≃ Cup a (Rank a k) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (Sum.elim (embedCore a k) (embedTail a k))
    ⟨by
      intro p q h
      cases p <;> cases q <;> simp only [Sum.elim_inl, Sum.elim_inr] at h
      · rw [core_injective a k h]
      · exact (core_tail_ne a k _ _ h).elim
      · exact (core_tail_ne a k _ _ h.symm).elim
      · rw [tail_injective a k h],
     by
       intro c
       obtain ⟨p,hp⟩ | ⟨i,hi⟩ := core_tail_cover a k c
       · exact ⟨Sum.inl p, hp⟩
       · exact ⟨Sum.inr i, hi⟩⟩

theorem tail_adjacent_shared (a k : ℕ) (i j : Fin (k+1)) (h : i.val+1 = j.val) :
    Shared (embedTail a k i) (embedTail a k j)
      (right (embedTail a k i)) (right (embedTail a k j)) (left (embedTail a k i)) := by
  have he : right (embedTail a k i) = left (embedTail a k j) := Fin.ext (by
    simp only [right_tail_value, left_tail_value]; omega)
  refine ⟨he ▸ ne_of_lt (ends_lt (embedTail a k j)), (ne_of_lt (ends_lt (embedTail a k i))).symm,
    ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro hz
    have ho := congrArg Fin.val hz
    simp only [right_tail_value, left_tail_value] at ho
    omega
  · intro z
    simp only [touches, or_comm]
  · intro z
    rw [he]
    rfl

theorem tail_disjoint (a k : ℕ) (i j : Fin (k+1))
    (h₀ : i ≠ j) (h₁ : i.val+1 ≠ j.val) (h₂ : j.val+1 ≠ i.val) :
    ∀ z, touches (embedTail a k j) z → ¬ touches (embedTail a k i) z := by
  intro z hd hc
  have h₀' : i.val ≠ j.val := fun he => h₀ (Fin.ext he)
  rcases hd with hd | hd <;> rcases hc with hc | hc <;>
    have h := congrArg Fin.val (hd.symm.trans hc) <;>
    simp only [left_tail_value, right_tail_value] at h <;> omega

theorem core_tail_disjoint_after_first (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (k+1))
    (hj : 0 < j.val) :
    ∀ z, touches (embedTail a k j) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a k p) z := by
  intro z hd hc
  have hp := p.val.2.isLt
  have hl := p.property
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hl
  rcases hd with hd | hd <;> rcases hc with hc | hc <;>
    have h := congrArg Fin.val (hd.symm.trans hc) <;>
    simp only [left_core_value, right_core_value, left_tail_value, right_tail_value] at h <;> omega

theorem core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a)
    (hp : p.val.2.val < a+2) :
    ∀ z, touches (embedTail a k 0) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a k p) z := by
  intro z hd hc
  have hl := p.property
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hl
  rcases hd with hd | hd <;> rcases hc with hc | hc <;>
    have h := congrArg Fin.val (hd.symm.trans hc) <;>
    simp only [left_core_value, right_core_value, left_tail_value, right_tail_value, Fin.val_zero] at h <;> omega

theorem core_first_tail_shared_marked (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a)
    (hp : p.val.2.val = a+2) :
    Shared (embedCore a k p) (embedTail a k 0)
      (right (embedCore a k p)) (right (embedTail a k 0)) (left (embedCore a k p)) := by
  have he : right (embedCore a k p) = left (embedTail a k 0) := Fin.ext (by simp [hp])
  have hlu : left (embedCore a k p) ≠ right (embedTail a k 0) := by
    intro hh
    have h := congrArg Fin.val hh
    have hl := p.property
    simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hl
    simp only [left_core_value, right_tail_value, Fin.val_zero] at h
    omega
  refine ⟨?_, (ne_of_lt (ends_lt (embedCore a k p))).symm, hlu.symm, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [he]
    exact ne_of_lt (ends_lt _)
  · intro z
    simp only [touches, or_comm]
  · intro z
    rw [he]
    rfl

theorem tail_mixed_next (a k : ℕ) (i j : Fin (k+1)) (h : i.val+1 = j.val) :
    mixedTransport (k := k) (embedTail a k i) (embedTail a k j) =
      some (Equiv.refl _) := by
  let sh := tail_adjacent_shared a k i j h
  rw [mixedTransport_shared sh, activeTransport_adjacent sh] <;>
    simp only [left_tail_value, right_tail_value] <;> omega

theorem tail_mixed_prev (a k : ℕ) (i j : Fin (k+1)) (h : j.val+1 = i.val) :
    mixedTransport (k := k) (embedTail a k i) (embedTail a k j) =
      some (Equiv.refl _) := by
  let s := tail_adjacent_shared a k j i h
  let t : Shared _ _ _ _ _ :=
    Shared.mk s.distinctBV s.distinctBU s.distinctUV.symm s.target s.source
  rw [mixedTransport_shared t, activeTransport_adjacent t] <;>
    simp only [left_tail_value, right_tail_value] <;> omega

theorem hookAct_refl {d : ℕ} (transpose : Bool) (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    hookAct transpose (Equiv.refl _) f = f := by
  funext i
  change (if transpose then permSign (1 : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) else 1) *
      expand f i.castSucc = f i
  cases transpose <;> simp

theorem nativeMove_tail (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i j : Fin (k+1)) (q : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a k transpose (embedTail a k i) (embedTail a k j) q =
      if i.val+1 = j.val ∨ j.val+1 = i.val then delta q else 0 := by
  by_cases h₀ : i = j
  · subst j
    simp [nativeMove]
  have hne : embedTail a k i ≠ embedTail a k j := fun h => h₀ (tail_injective a k h)
  by_cases h₁ : i.val+1 = j.val
  · simp [nativeMove, hne, tail_mixed_next a k i j h₁, h₁, hookAct_refl]
  by_cases h₂ : j.val+1 = i.val
  · simp [nativeMove, hne, tail_mixed_prev a k i j h₂, h₂, hookAct_refl]
  · simp [nativeMove, hne, h₁, h₂, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := k) (tail_disjoint a k i j h₀ h₁ h₂)]

theorem nativeMove_core_tail_late (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (p : CoreCup a)
    (i : Fin (k+1)) (hi : 0 < i.val) (q : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a k transpose (embedCore a k p) (embedTail a k i) q = 0 := by
  simp [nativeMove, core_tail_ne a k p i, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := k)
    (core_tail_disjoint_after_first a k p i hi)]

theorem nativeMove_tail_core_late (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (p : CoreCup a)
    (i : Fin (k+1)) (hi : 0 < i.val) (q : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a k transpose (embedTail a k i) (embedCore a k p) q = 0 := by
  have hd : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a k p) z → ¬ touches (embedTail a k i) z :=
    fun z hc ht => core_tail_disjoint_after_first a k p i hi z ht hc
  simp [nativeMove, Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a k p i), mixedTransport_disjoint (k := k) hd]

#print axioms coreTailEquiv
#print axioms core_first_tail_shared_marked
#print axioms core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked
#print axioms nativeMove_tail
#print axioms nativeMove_core_tail_late
#print axioms nativeMove_tail_core_late

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import NativeTail

namespace BrauerHook

private theorem envSlotVal {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (i : Fin (n-2)) :
    ((throughOrder c) i).val.val = slotValue c i.val := rfl

private theorem physical_active_transport {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Letters (a+1)) :
    slotValue d (activeTransport s i).val =
      if slotValue c i.val = u.val then v.val
      else if slotValue c i.val = v.val then u.val else slotValue c i.val := by
  let x := ((throughOrder c) (liftSlot a k i)).val
  have h := congrArg Fin.val (transport_site s (liftSlot a k i))
  rw [← activeTransport_commutes] at h
  change slotValue d (activeTransport s i).val = (Equiv.swap u v x).val at h
  change slotValue d (activeTransport s i).val = _
  rw [h, Equiv.swap_apply_def]
  have hvx : x.val = slotValue c i.val := rfl
  rw [← hvx]
  by_cases hxu : x = u
  · subst u
    simp
  · have hxu' : x.val ≠ u.val := fun hh => hxu (Fin.ext hh)
    by_cases hxv : x = v
    · subst v
      simp [hxu, hxu']
    · have hxv' : x.val ≠ v.val := fun hh => hxv (Fin.ext hh)
      simp [hxu, hxu', hxv, hxv']

theorem active_swap_site {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Letters (a+1)) :
    slotValue d (activeTransport s i).val =
      if slotValue c i.val = u.val then v.val else slotValue c i.val := by
  rw [physical_active_transport s i]
  have h := ((slot_props c (liftSlot a k i).isLt).2.1)
  have h' := ((slot_props c (liftSlot a k i).isLt).2.2)
  have hf : slotValue c i.val ≠ v.val := by
    rcases (s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl) with hv | hv
    · exact fun he => h (he.trans (congrArg Fin.val hv))
    · exact fun he => h' (he.trans (congrArg Fin.val hv))
  simp [hf]

/-- A marked core cup (i,m) sends the external through label to i.
The other through labels retain physical sites and are compressed increasingly. -/
theorem marked_to_first_tail (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : p.val.2.val = a+2)
    (i : Letters (a+1)) :
    (activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a k p hp) i).val =
      if i.val < p.val.1.val then i.val else
        if i.val < a+1 then i.val+1 else p.val.1.val := by
  have h := active_swap_site (core_first_tail_shared_marked a k p hp) i
  simp only [slotValue, right_core_value, left_core_value, left_tail_value, right_tail_value,
    hp, Fin.val_zero, add_zero] at h
  have hi := i.isLt
  have hj := (activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a k p hp) i).isLt
  change i.val < a+2 at hi
  change _ < a+2 at hj
  rw [ite_eq_left hj] at h
  have hl := p.property
  simp only [Fin.lt_def, hp] at hl
  by_cases h₁ : i.val < p.val.1.val
  · have hn : i.val ≠ a+3 := by omega
    simp only [h₁, hn, ite_true, ite_false] at h ⊢
    exact h
  · by_cases h₂ : i.val < a+1
    · have hm : i.val+1 < a+2 := by omega
      have hn : i.val+1 ≠ a+3 := by omega
      simp only [h₁, hm, hn, h₂, ite_true, ite_false] at h ⊢
      exact h
    · have hm : ¬ i.val+1 < a+2 := by omega
      have hn : i.val+2 = a+3 := by omega
      simp only [h₁, hm, hn, h₂, ite_true, ite_false] at h ⊢
      exact h

theorem marked_base_transport (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a)
    (hleft : p.val.1.val = a+1) (hright : p.val.2.val = a+2) :
    activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a k p hright) = Equiv.refl _ := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply Fin.ext
  rw [marked_to_first_tail a k p hright, hleft]
  by_cases hi : i.val < a+1
  · simp [hi]
  · have hi' := i.isLt
    change i.val < a+2 at hi'
    simp only [hi, ite_false, Equiv.refl_apply]
    omega

/-- A comparison of active hook actions is independent of added sites once the
cup endpoints and transport endpoints have the same physical coordinates. -/
theorem transport_independent_of_rank
    {a k l : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)} {c' d' : Cup a (Rank a l)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} {b' u' v' : Fin (Rank a l)}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (t : Shared c' d' b' u' v')
    (hc₁ : (left c).val = (left c').val) (hc₂ : (right c).val = (right c').val)
    (hd₁ : (left d).val = (left d').val) (hd₂ : (right d).val = (right d').val)
    (hu : u.val = u'.val) (hv : v.val = v'.val) :
    activeTransport s = activeTransport t := by
  have hc (i : ℕ) : slotValue c i = slotValue c' i := by simp only [slotValue, hc₁, hc₂]
  have hd (i : ℕ) : slotValue d i = slotValue d' i := by simp only [slotValue, hd₁, hd₂]
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  have h₁ := active_swap_site s i
  have h₂ := active_swap_site t i
  rw [hc, hd, hu, hv, ← h₂] at h₁
  apply liftSlot_injective a l
  apply (throughOrder d').injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  apply Fin.ext
  exact h₁

#print axioms active_swap_site
#print axioms marked_to_first_tail
#print axioms marked_base_transport
#print axioms transport_independent_of_rank

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import MarkedTransport

open scoped BigOperators

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev CoreSite (a : ℕ) := Fin (a+3)
abbrev SeedSite (a : ℕ) := Fin (Rank a 0)

def injectCoreSite (a : ℕ) (i : CoreSite a) : SeedSite a :=
  ⟨i.val, by have := i.isLt; simp only [Rank]; omega⟩

theorem injectCoreSite_inj (a : ℕ) : Function.Injective (injectCoreSite a) :=
  fun _ _ h => Fin.ext (congrArg (fun i : SeedSite a => i.val) h)

def sortedCorePair (a : ℕ) (i j : CoreSite a) (h : i ≠ j) : CoreCup a :=
  ⟨(min i j, max i j), by
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne h with hl | hl
    · simpa only [min_eq_left hl.le, max_eq_right hl.le] using hl
    · simpa only [min_eq_right hl.le, max_eq_left hl.le] using hl⟩

theorem sortedCorePair_touches (a : ℕ) (i j : CoreSite a) (h : i ≠ j) (z : SeedSite a) :
    touches (embedCore a 0 (sortedCorePair a i j h)) z ↔
      z = injectCoreSite a i ∨ z = injectCoreSite a j := by
  change (z = injectCoreSite a (min i j) ∨ z = injectCoreSite a (max i j)) ↔ _
  rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne h with hl | hl
  · rw [min_eq_left hl.le, max_eq_right hl.le]
  · rw [min_eq_right hl.le, max_eq_left hl.le, or_comm]

def coreSwapPerm (a : ℕ) (e : CoreCup a) : Equiv.Perm (CoreSite a) :=
  Equiv.swap e.val.1 e.val.2

def siteSwapPerm (a : ℕ) (e : CoreCup a) : Equiv.Perm (SeedSite a) :=
  mate (embedCore a 0 e)

theorem swap_embed (a : ℕ) (e : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) :
    siteSwapPerm a e (injectCoreSite a z) = injectCoreSite a (coreSwapPerm a e z) := by
  change Equiv.swap (injectCoreSite a e.val.1) (injectCoreSite a e.val.2) (injectCoreSite a z) =
    injectCoreSite a (Equiv.swap e.val.1 e.val.2 z)
  simp [Equiv.swap_apply_def, (injectCoreSite_inj a).eq_iff]
  split <;> (try split) <;> rfl

def swapCup (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) : CoreCup a :=
  sortedCorePair a (coreSwapPerm a e p.val.1) (coreSwapPerm a e p.val.2)
    (fun h => ne_of_lt p.property ((coreSwapPerm a e).injective h))

theorem swapCup_touches (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (z : SeedSite a) :
    touches (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p)) z ↔
      touches (embedCore a 0 p) (siteSwapPerm a e z) := by
  rw [swapCup, sortedCorePair_touches, ← swap_embed, ← swap_embed, touches]
  have he (x : SeedSite a) :
      siteSwapPerm a e (siteSwapPerm a e x) = x := Equiv.swap_apply_self _ _ _
  change (z = siteSwapPerm a e (left (embedCore a 0 p)) ∨
      z = siteSwapPerm a e (right (embedCore a 0 p))) ↔
    (siteSwapPerm a e z = left (embedCore a 0 p) ∨ siteSwapPerm a e z = right (embedCore a 0 p))
  have hh (x : SeedSite a) : z = siteSwapPerm a e x ↔ siteSwapPerm a e z = x := by
    constructor
    · intro hz
      rw [hz, he]
    · intro hz
      rw [← hz, he]
  simp only [hh]

theorem coreCup_eq_touches (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a)
    (h : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z ↔ touches (embedCore a 0 q) z) :
    p = q := by
  apply core_injective a 0
  have hl := (h (left (embedCore a 0 p))).mp (Or.inl rfl)
  have hr := (h (right (embedCore a 0 p))).mp (Or.inr rfl)
  have hp := ends_lt (embedCore a 0 p)
  have hq := ends_lt (embedCore a 0 q)
  rcases hl with hl | hl <;> rcases hr with hr | hr
  · rw [hl,hr] at hp; exact (lt_irrefl _ hp).elim
  · exact (loopEdges_iff _ _).mp ⟨hl,hr⟩
  · rw [hl,hr] at hp; exact (asymm hp hq).elim
  · rw [hl,hr] at hp; exact (lt_irrefl _ hp).elim

theorem swapCup_flip (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) : swapCup a p p = p := by
  apply coreCup_eq_touches
  intro z
  rw [swapCup, sortedCorePair_touches]
  change (z = injectCoreSite a ((Equiv.swap p.val.1 p.val.2) p.val.1) ∨
    z = injectCoreSite a ((Equiv.swap p.val.1 p.val.2) p.val.2)) ↔ _
  rw [Equiv.swap_apply_left, Equiv.swap_apply_right]
  exact or_comm

theorem swapCup_off (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a)
    (h : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 e) z) :
    swapCup a e p = p := by
  apply coreCup_eq_touches
  intro z
  rw [swapCup_touches]
  constructor
  · intro ht
    have hh := (mate_eq_self_iff _ _).mpr (h _ ht)
    change siteSwapPerm _ _ (siteSwapPerm _ _ z) = siteSwapPerm _ _ z at hh
    rw [show siteSwapPerm a e (siteSwapPerm a e z) = z from Equiv.swap_apply_self _ _ _] at hh
    rwa [hh]
  · intro ht
    rw [show siteSwapPerm a e z = z from (mate_eq_self_iff _ _).mpr (h _ ht)]
    exact ht

def seedSlot (a : ℕ) : Letters (a+1) ≃ Fin (Rank a 0-2) where
  toFun := liftSlot a 0
  invFun i := ⟨i.val, by have h := i.isLt; simp only [Rank] at h; omega⟩
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl

private theorem swapFree (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (z : SeedSite a) :
    siteSwapPerm a e z ∈ free (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p)) ↔
      z ∈ free (embedCore a 0 p) := by
  rw [mem_free, mem_free, swapCup_touches]
  have h : siteSwapPerm a e (siteSwapPerm a e z) = z := Equiv.swap_apply_self _ _ _
  rw [h]

def coreRelabel (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) : Equiv.Perm (Letters (a+1)) :=
  (seedSlot a).trans ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 p)).toEquiv.trans
    ((Equiv.subtypeEquiv (siteSwapPerm a e) (fun _ => (swapFree a e p _).symm)).trans
      ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p))).symm.toEquiv.trans (seedSlot a).symm)))

theorem coreRelabel_site (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (i : Letters (a+1)) :
    ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p))) (seedSlot a (coreRelabel a e p i))).val =
      siteSwapPerm a e ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 p)) (seedSlot a i)).val := by
  simp [coreRelabel]

theorem coreRelabel_flip (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    coreRelabel a p p = Equiv.refl _ := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  have h := coreRelabel_site a p p i
  rw [swapCup_flip] at h
  apply (seedSlot a).injective
  apply (throughOrder (embedCore a 0 p)).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  rw [h]
  exact (mate_eq_self_iff _ _).mpr
    ((mem_free _ _).mp ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 p)) (seedSlot a i)).property)

theorem coreRelabel_shared (a : ℕ) (e p q : CoreCup a)
    {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (h : swapCup a e p = q)
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) b u v)
    (hswap : siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap u v) :
    coreRelabel a e p = activeTransport s := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  have hr := coreRelabel_site a e p i
  rw [h, hswap] at hr
  have ht := transport_site s (liftSlot a 0 i)
  rw [← activeTransport_commutes] at ht
  apply (seedSlot a).injective
  apply (throughOrder (embedCore a 0 q)).injective
  apply Subtype.ext
  exact hr.trans ht.symm


theorem mate_of_touches {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) {u v : Fin n}
    (hu : touches c u) (hv : touches c v) (hne : u ≠ v) :
    mate c = Equiv.swap u v := by
  rcases hu with hu | hu <;> rcases hv with hv | hv
  · exact (hne (hu.trans hv.symm)).elim
  · rw [hu, hv]
    rfl
  · rw [hu, hv, Equiv.swap_comm]
    rfl
  · exact (hne (hu.trans hv.symm)).elim

theorem shared_ne {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) : c ≠ d := by
  intro h
  have hn : ¬ touches c u := fun ht => ((s.source u).mp ht).elim s.distinctBU.symm s.distinctUV
  exact hn (h ▸ (s.target u).mpr (Or.inr rfl))

private theorem swap_collect {n : ℕ} {b u v z : Fin n} (hbu : b ≠ u) (hvu : v ≠ u) :
    ((Equiv.swap b v) z = b ∨ (Equiv.swap b v) z = u) ↔ z = v ∨ z = u := by
  have h₁ : (Equiv.swap b v) z = b ↔ z = v := by
    simpa only [Equiv.swap_apply_right] using
      ((Equiv.swap b v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := z) (y := v))
  have h₂ : (Equiv.swap b v) z = u ↔ z = u := by
    simpa only [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hbu.symm hvu.symm] using
      ((Equiv.swap b v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := z) (y := u))
  rw [h₁,h₂]

theorem exchange_yields_shared (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 e) (embedCore a 0 p) b u v) :
    Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p)) u v b ∧
      siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap v b := by
  have he : siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap b v :=
    mate_of_touches _ ((s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)) ((s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)) s.distinctBV
  refine ⟨⟨s.distinctUV, s.distinctBU.symm, s.distinctBV.symm,
    fun z => (s.target z).trans or_comm, ?_⟩, he.trans (Equiv.swap_comm _ _)⟩
  intro z
  rw [swapCup_touches, he, s.target]
  exact (swap_collect s.distinctBU s.distinctUV.symm).trans or_comm

theorem swapCup_moved (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (h : swapCup a e p ≠ p) :
    ∃ b u v, Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 (swapCup a e p)) b u v ∧
      siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap u v := by
  obtain he | he | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy (embedCore a 0 e) (embedCore a 0 p)
  · have he' := core_injective a 0 he
    exact (h (he' ▸ swapCup_flip a p)).elim
  · exact (h (swapCup_off a e p he)).elim
  · exact ⟨u,v,b,exchange_yields_shared a e p s⟩

theorem swapCup_fixed_iff (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) :
    swapCup a e p = p ↔
      e = p ∨ ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 e) z := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    obtain he | he | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy (embedCore a 0 e) (embedCore a 0 p)
    · exact Or.inl (core_injective a 0 he)
    · exact Or.inr he
    · exact (shared_ne (exchange_yields_shared a e p s).1
        (congrArg (embedCore a 0) h.symm)).elim
  · rintro (rfl | he)
    · exact swapCup_flip a _
    · exact swapCup_off a e p he

theorem shared_has_swap (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) b u v) :
    ∃ e : CoreCup a, swapCup a e p = q ∧ siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap u v := by
  have hv := (s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  have hu := (s.target u).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  have hbound (p : CoreCup a) (z : SeedSite a) (hz : touches (embedCore a 0 p) z) :
      z.val < a+3 := by
    rcases hz with rfl | rfl
    · exact p.val.1.isLt
    · exact p.val.2.isLt
  let i : CoreSite a := ⟨u.val, hbound q u hu⟩
  let j : CoreSite a := ⟨v.val, hbound p v hv⟩
  have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => s.distinctUV (Fin.ext (congrArg (fun z : CoreSite a => z.val) h))
  let e := sortedCorePair a i j hij
  have ht (z : SeedSite a) : touches (embedCore a 0 e) z ↔ z = u ∨ z = v :=
    sortedCorePair_touches a i j hij z
  have he : siteSwapPerm a e = Equiv.swap u v :=
    mate_of_touches _ ((ht u).mpr (Or.inl rfl)) ((ht v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)) s.distinctUV
  refine ⟨e, ?_, he⟩
  apply coreCup_eq_touches
  intro z
  rw [swapCup_touches, he, s.source, s.target]
  have hc : Equiv.swap u v b = b :=
    Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne s.distinctBU s.distinctBV
  have h₁ : (Equiv.swap u v) z = b ↔ z = b := by
    simpa only [hc] using ((Equiv.swap u v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := z) (y := b))
  have h₂ : (Equiv.swap u v) z = v ↔ z = u := by
    simpa only [Equiv.swap_apply_left] using ((Equiv.swap u v).apply_eq_iff_eq (x := z) (y := u))
  rw [h₁,h₂]

private theorem unique_shared_swap {a n : ℕ} {c d : Cup a n} {b u v b' u' v' : Fin n}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (s' : Shared c d b' u' v') :
    Equiv.swap u v = Equiv.swap u' v' := by
  have hfree : u ∈ free c := by
    apply (mem_free c u).mpr
    intro hu
    exact ((s.source u).mp hu).elim s.distinctBU.symm s.distinctUV
  have h : Equiv.swap u' v' u = v := by
    have he := (trace_shared s hfree).symm.trans (trace_shared s' hfree)
    rw [Equiv.swap_apply_left] at he
    exact (Exit.through.inj he).symm
  have hnv : u ≠ v' := fun he => (mem_free c u).mp hfree ((s'.source u).mpr (Or.inr he))
  have hu : u = u' := by
    by_contra hn
    rw [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hn hnv] at h
    exact s.distinctUV h
  subst u'
  rw [Equiv.swap_apply_left] at h
  rw [h]

theorem core_exchange_unique (a : ℕ) (e e' p q : CoreCup a)
    (h : swapCup a e p = q) (h' : swapCup a e' p = q) (hne : p ≠ q) :
    e = e' := by
  obtain ⟨b,u,v,s,he⟩ := swapCup_moved a e p (by rw [h]; exact hne.symm)
  obtain ⟨b',u',v',s',he'⟩ := swapCup_moved a e' p (by rw [h']; exact hne.symm)
  rw [h] at s
  rw [h'] at s'
  have hs := (he.trans (unique_shared_swap s s')).trans he'.symm
  apply coreCup_eq_touches
  intro z
  rw [← not_iff_not, ← mate_eq_self_iff, ← mate_eq_self_iff]
  change siteSwapPerm a e z = z ↔ siteSwapPerm a e' z = z
  rw [hs]


instance (a : ℕ) : Fintype (CoreCup a) := Subtype.fintype (fun p : CoreSite a × CoreSite a => p.1 < p.2)

noncomputable def transpositionEntry (a : ℕ) (transpose : Bool)
    (p q : CoreCup a) (i j : Fin (a+1)) : ℂ := by
  classical
  exact ∑ e, if swapCup a e p = q then hookAct transpose (coreRelabel a e p) (delta j) i else 0

noncomputable def complementaryEntry (a : ℕ) (transpose : Bool)
    (p : CoreCup a) (i j : Fin (a+1)) : ℂ := by
  classical
  exact ∑ e, if (∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 e) z) then
      hookAct transpose (coreRelabel a e p) (delta j) i else 0

/-- The transposition sum on the core includes the cup flip and all local
through transpositions. Its moving-cup part is the native interaction. -/
theorem transposition_sum_core (a : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (p q : CoreCup a)
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    transpositionEntry a transpose p q i j =
      nativeMove a 0 transpose (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i +
      if p = q then delta j i + complementaryEntry a transpose p i j else 0 := by
  classical
  by_cases h : p = q
  · subst q
    have hcases (e : CoreCup a) :
        (if swapCup a e p = p then hookAct transpose (coreRelabel a e p) (delta j) i else 0) =
          (if e = p then delta j i else 0) +
          (if (∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 e) z) then
            hookAct transpose (coreRelabel a e p) (delta j) i else 0) := by
      by_cases he : e = p
      · subst e
        have hn : ¬ ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 p) z :=
          fun h => h _ (Or.inl rfl) (Or.inl rfl)
        rw [ite_eq_right hn]
        simp [swapCup_flip, coreRelabel_flip, hookAct_refl]
      · simp [swapCup_fixed_iff, he]
    simp only [transpositionEntry, nativeMove, ite_true,
      Pi.zero_apply, zero_add, complementaryEntry]
    simp_rw [hcases]
    rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib]
    simp
  · have hn : embedCore a 0 p ≠ embedCore a 0 q := fun he => h (core_injective a 0 he)
    obtain he | hd | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q)
    · exact (hn he).elim
    · have hm (e : CoreCup a) : swapCup a e p ≠ q := by
        intro he
        obtain ⟨b,u,v,s,_⟩ := swapCup_moved a e p (by rw [he]; exact Ne.symm h)
        rw [he] at s
        exact hd b ((s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)) ((s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl))
      simp [transpositionEntry, hm, nativeMove, h, hn, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd]
    · obtain ⟨e,he,hes⟩ := shared_has_swap a p q s
      have ht := coreRelabel_shared a e p q he s hes
      unfold transpositionEntry
      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single e]
      · simp [he, ht, nativeMove, h, hn, mixedTransport_shared s]
      · intro f _ hf
        have hq : swapCup a f p ≠ q := fun hh => hf (core_exchange_unique a f e p q hh he h)
        simp [hq]
      · simp

#print axioms transposition_sum_core

#print axioms exchange_yields_shared
#print axioms swapCup_moved
#print axioms swapCup_fixed_iff
#print axioms shared_has_swap
#print axioms core_exchange_unique

#print axioms swapCup_touches
#print axioms swapCup_flip
#print axioms swapCup_off
#print axioms coreRelabel_site
#print axioms coreRelabel_flip
#print axioms coreRelabel_shared

end BrauerHook
```

## 7. `PrintedHook` (inherited, 152 lines, sha256 `daaab76bd5c0e8ab81da2f27dea6ea2f8f8a490f94f21c7741540a8651f56760`)

```lean
import Mathlib.RingTheory.Polynomial.Chebyshev
import Mathlib.Basic.Complex.Basic
import Mathlib.Tactic.NormNum
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Lean.Elab.Tactic.Omega

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

/-- Native P^U_j = U_(j-1)(x/2), including negative j. -/
noncomputable def nativeU (j : ℤ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (Polynomial.Chebyshev.U ℂ (j - 1)).comp (C (1/2) * X)

@[simp] theorem nativeU_zero : nativeU 0 = 0 := by
  simp [nativeU]

@[simp] theorem nativeU_one : nativeU 1 = 1 := by
  simp [nativeU]

private theorem undo_half : C (2 : ℂ) * (C (1/2) * X) = X := by
  rw [← mul_assoc, ← C_mul]
  norm_num

@[simp] theorem nativeU_two : nativeU 2 = X := by
  simp only [nativeU, show (2 : ℤ) - 1 = 1 by decide, Polynomial.Chebyshev.U_one,
    mul_comp, ← C_ofNat, C_comp, X_comp, undo_half]

theorem nativeU_step (j : ℤ) :
    nativeU (j + 2) = X * nativeU (j + 1) - nativeU j := by
  unfold nativeU
  have h₂ : j + 2 - 1 = (j - 1) + 2 := by omega
  have h₁ : j + 1 - 1 = (j - 1) + 1 := by omega
  rw [h₂, h₁, Polynomial.Chebyshev.U_add_two]
  simp only [sub_comp, mul_comp, ← C_ofNat, C_comp, X_comp, undo_half]

theorem nativeU_neg (j : ℤ) : nativeU (-j) = -nativeU j := by
  unfold nativeU
  rw [show -j - 1 = -(j - 1) - 2 by omega, Polynomial.Chebyshev.U_neg_sub_two, neg_comp]

noncomputable def ordinaryQ (a : ℂ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X + C (a - 1)) * (X - 2) * (X + C (2 * a)) * (X + C (a - 3))

noncomputable def ordinaryR (a : ℂ) : ℂ[X] :=
  C (a + 2) * (X + C (a - 2)) * (X^2 + C (2*a-3) * X - C (2*a))

noncomputable def transposeQ (a : ℂ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X + 2) * (X - C (a - 1)) * (X - C (a + 1))

noncomputable def transposeR (a : ℂ) : ℂ[X] :=
  C (a + 2) * (X + 1) * (X - C a)

/-- Formula (3.3.9), with k measured from native rank a+4. -/
noncomputable def printedOrdinaryP (a : ℂ) (k : ℤ) : ℂ[X] :=
  nativeU (k + 6) +
    C (2 * (2*a - 3)) * nativeU (k + 5) +
    C (5*a^2 - 21*a + 13) * nativeU (k + 4) +
    C (a-2) *
      (C (2*a^2 - 17*a + 7) * nativeU (k + 3) -
        C (6*a^2 - 19*a + 5) * nativeU (k + 2)) +
    C (4*(a^3 - 6*a^2 + 8*a - 1)) * nativeU (k + 1) -
    C ((a-3)*(2*a^2 - 4*a - 1)) * nativeU k -
    C (3*a^2 - 3*a - 4) * nativeU (k - 1) -
    C (a+1) * nativeU (k - 2)

/-- Formula (3.3.10) with its missing joining plus supplied. -/
noncomputable def correctedTransposeP (a : ℂ) (k : ℤ) : ℂ[X] :=
  nativeU (k + 5) - C (2*(a-1)) * nativeU (k + 4) +
    C (a*(a-5)) * nativeU (k + 3) +
    C (3*a*(a-1)) * nativeU (k + 2) +
    C (2*(a^2 - 2*a - 1)) * nativeU (k + 1) +
    C (a*(a-1)) * nativeU k - C (a+1) * nativeU (k - 1)

private theorem step_shift (k j : ℤ) :
    nativeU (k + (j + 2)) = X * nativeU (k + (j + 1)) - nativeU (k + j) := by
  simpa only [add_assoc] using nativeU_step (k + j)

private theorem step_down_one (k : ℤ) :
    nativeU (k - 1) = X * nativeU k - nativeU (k + 1) := by
  have h := nativeU_step (k - 1)
  rw [show k - 1 + 2 = k + 1 by omega, show k - 1 + 1 = k by omega] at h
  rw [h]
  ring

private theorem step_down_two (k : ℤ) :
    nativeU (k - 2) = X * nativeU (k - 1) - nativeU k := by
  have h := nativeU_step (k - 2)
  rw [show k - 2 + 2 = k by omega, show k - 2 + 1 = k - 1 by omega] at h
  rw [h]
  ring

theorem ordinaryP_seed_identity (a : ℂ) (k : ℤ) :
    printedOrdinaryP a k = ordinaryQ a * nativeU (k + 2) - ordinaryR a * nativeU (k + 1) := by
  have h₆ := step_shift k 4
  have h₅ := step_shift k 3
  have h₄ := step_shift k 2
  have h₃ := step_shift k 1
  have h₂ := nativeU_step k
  norm_num at h₆ h₅ h₄ h₃
  rw [printedOrdinaryP, ordinaryQ, ordinaryR, h₆, h₅, h₄, h₃, h₂,
    step_down_two, step_down_one]
  simp only [map_add, map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_ofNat, map_one]
  ring

theorem transposeP_seed_identity (a : ℂ) (k : ℤ) :
    correctedTransposeP a k =
      transposeQ a * nativeU (k + 2) - transposeR a * nativeU (k + 1) := by
  have h₅ := step_shift k 3
  have h₄ := step_shift k 2
  have h₃ := step_shift k 1
  have h₂ := nativeU_step k
  norm_num at h₅ h₄ h₃
  rw [correctedTransposeP, transposeQ, transposeR, h₅, h₄, h₃, h₂, step_down_one]
  simp only [map_add, map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_ofNat, map_one]
  ring

/-- The seven ordered factors and exponents in rendered (3.3.7). -/
noncomputable def ordinaryCPrinted (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X - 3)^((a-1)*(a+1)*(a+3)/3) *
  (X - 2)^((a-1)*(a+2)/2) *
  X^(a*(a+3)/2) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-3))^(a*(a+1)/2) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-1))^((a+1)*(a+2)/2) *
  (X + C (2*(a : ℂ))) *
  (X + C (2*((a : ℂ)+1)))

noncomputable def ordinaryCCorrected (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X - 3)^((a-1)*(a+1)*(a+3)/3) *
  (X - 2)^((a-1)*(a+2)/2) *
  X^((a+3)*(a+2)*(a-2)/6) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-3))^(a*(a+1)/2) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-1))^((a+1)*(a+2)/2) *
  (X + C (2*(a : ℂ))) *
  (X + C (2*((a : ℂ)+1)))

noncomputable def transposeCPrinted (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X + 4)^(a*(a+1)*(a+2)/6) *
  (X + 2)^(a*(a+1)/2) *
  (X + 1)^((a-1)*(a+1)*(a+3)/3) *
  (X - C ((a : ℂ)-1))^(a*(a+1)/2) *
  (X - C ((a : ℂ)+1))^((a+1)*(a+2)/2)

#print axioms nativeU
#print axioms nativeU_zero
#print axioms nativeU_one
#print axioms nativeU_two
#print axioms nativeU_neg
#print axioms nativeU_step
#print axioms ordinaryP_seed_identity
#print axioms transposeP_seed_identity

end BrauerHook
```
