```lean
import PrintedHook
import Mathlib.Algebra.Polynomial.FieldDivision
import Mathlib.Algebra.Polynomial.Degree.IsMonicOfDegree
import Mathlib.Tactic.ComputeDegree
import Mathlib.Tactic.Positivity
import Mathlib.Tactic.Linarith

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

/-- Cleared partial fraction coefficients for the proposed ordinary core resolvent.
This identity is independent of their spectral identification. -/
theorem ordinary_residues_polynomial (a : ℂ) :
    let m := a + 3; let r := a + 2; let d := a + 1
    let l₁ : ℂ[X] := X + C (a-1)
    let l₂ : ℂ[X] := X - 2
    let l₃ : ℂ[X] := X + C (2*a)
    let l₄ : ℂ[X] := X + C (a-3)
    C (2*m*d^2) * ordinaryR a =
       C (m*r*(a^2-a+2)) * (l₂*l₃*l₄) +
       C (2*m*r*a) * (l₁*l₃*l₄) +
       C (4*a*r^2) * (l₁*l₂*l₄) +
       C (a*r*d^2) * (l₁*l₂*l₃) := by
  simp only [ordinaryR, map_add, map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_one, map_ofNat]
  ring

/-- Cleared partial fraction coefficients for the proposed transpose resolvent. -/
theorem transpose_residues_polynomial (a : ℂ) :
    let m := a + 3; let r := a + 2; let d := a + 1
    let l₁ : ℂ[X] := X + 2
    let l₂ : ℂ[X] := X - C (a-1)
    let l₃ : ℂ[X] := X - C (a+1)
    C (2*m*d) * transposeR a =
       C (2*r^2) * (l₂*l₃) + C (m*a*r) * (l₁*l₃) + C (d*r^2) * (l₁*l₂) := by
  simp only [transposeR, map_add, map_sub, map_mul, map_pow, map_one, map_ofNat]
  ring

theorem ordinaryQ_degree (a : ℂ) : IsMonicOfDegree (ordinaryQ a) 4 := by
  constructor
  · unfold ordinaryQ
    compute_degree!
  · unfold ordinaryQ
    monicity!

theorem transposeQ_degree (a : ℂ) : IsMonicOfDegree (transposeQ a) 3 := by
  constructor
  · unfold transposeQ
    compute_degree!
  · unfold transposeQ
    monicity!

theorem ordinaryR_degree_bound (a : ℂ) : (ordinaryR a).natDegree ≤ 3 := by
  unfold ordinaryR
  compute_degree!

theorem transposeR_degree_bound (a : ℂ) : (transposeR a).natDegree ≤ 2 := by
  unfold transposeR
  compute_degree!

private theorem coprime_linear (z : ℂ) (p : ℂ[X]) (h : p.eval z ≠ 0) :
    IsCoprime (X - C z) p := by
  obtain ⟨q,hq⟩ := (dvd_iff_isRoot).mpr (show (p - C (p.eval z)).IsRoot z by simp [IsRoot])
  refine ⟨-(C (p.eval z)⁻¹ * q), C (p.eval z)⁻¹, ?_⟩
  have he : p = (X - C z) * q + C (p.eval z) := by
    rw [← hq, sub_add_cancel]
  calc
    -(C (p.eval z)⁻¹ * q) * (X - C z) + C (p.eval z)⁻¹ * p =
        C (p.eval z)⁻¹ * (p - (X - C z) * q) := by ring
    _ = C (p.eval z)⁻¹ * C (p.eval z) := by rw [← hq, sub_sub_cancel]
    _ = 1 := by rw [← C_mul, inv_mul_cancel₀ h, C_1]

private theorem real_nat_two (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) : (2 : ℝ) ≤ a := by exact_mod_cast ha
private theorem real_nat_one (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) : (1 : ℝ) ≤ a := by exact_mod_cast ha

private theorem complex_real_ne {x : ℝ} (h : x ≠ 0) : (x : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast h

theorem ordinary_seeds_coprime (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    IsCoprime (ordinaryQ a) (ordinaryR a) := by
  let A := (a : ℂ)
  change IsCoprime (ordinaryQ A) (ordinaryR A)
  have hQ : ordinaryQ A =
      (X - C (1-A)) * (X - C 2) * (X - C (-2*A)) * (X - C (3-A)) := by
    simp only [ordinaryQ, map_sub, map_mul, map_neg, map_ofNat, map_one]
    ring
  have hA := real_nat_two a ha
  have h₁ : (ordinaryR A).eval (1-A) = (A+2)*(A^2-A+2) := by
    simp only [ordinaryR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_pow, eval_X, eval_C]
    ring
  have h₂ : (ordinaryR A).eval 2 = (A+2)*A*(2*(A-1)) := by
    simp only [ordinaryR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_pow, eval_X, eval_C]
    ring
  have h₃ : (ordinaryR A).eval (-2*A) = (A+2)*(-A-2)*(4*A) := by
    simp only [ordinaryR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_pow, eval_X, eval_C]
    ring
  have h₄ : (ordinaryR A).eval (3-A) = (A+2)*(-A)*(A-1) := by
    simp only [ordinaryR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_pow, eval_X, eval_C]
    ring
  have H₁ : ((a : ℂ)+2)*((a : ℂ)^2-(a : ℂ)+2) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show ((a : ℝ)+2)*((a : ℝ)^2-(a : ℝ)+2) ≠ 0 by
      apply mul_ne_zero <;> apply ne_of_gt <;> nlinarith))
  have H₂ : ((a : ℂ)+2)*(a : ℂ)*(2*((a : ℂ)-1)) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show ((a : ℝ)+2)*(a : ℝ)*(2*((a : ℝ)-1)) ≠ 0 by
      apply mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero _ _) <;> apply ne_of_gt <;> linarith))
  have H₃ : ((a : ℂ)+2)*(-(a : ℂ)-2)*(4*(a : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show ((a : ℝ)+2)*(-(a : ℝ)-2)*(4*(a : ℝ)) ≠ 0 by
      apply mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero _ _) <;> first | (apply ne_of_gt; linarith) | (apply ne_of_lt; linarith)))
  have H₄ : ((a : ℂ)+2)*(-(a : ℂ))*((a : ℂ)-1) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show ((a : ℝ)+2)*(-(a : ℝ))*((a : ℝ)-1) ≠ 0 by
      apply mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero _ _) <;> first | (apply ne_of_gt; linarith) | (apply ne_of_lt; linarith)))
  rw [hQ]
  exact (((coprime_linear _ _ (h₁ ▸ H₁)).mul_left
    (coprime_linear _ _ (h₂ ▸ H₂))).mul_left (coprime_linear _ _ (h₃ ▸ H₃))).mul_left
    (coprime_linear _ _ (h₄ ▸ H₄))

theorem transpose_seeds_coprime (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    IsCoprime (transposeQ a) (transposeR a) := by
  let A := (a : ℂ)
  change IsCoprime (transposeQ A) (transposeR A)
  have hQ : transposeQ A = (X - C (-2)) * (X - C (A-1)) * (X - C (A+1)) := by
    simp only [transposeQ, map_neg, map_ofNat]
    ring
  have hA := real_nat_one a ha
  have h₁ : (transposeR A).eval (-2) = (A+2)*(A+2) := by
    simp only [transposeR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_X, eval_C, eval_one]
    ring
  have h₂ : (transposeR A).eval (A-1) = -(A+2)*A := by
    simp only [transposeR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_X, eval_C, eval_one]
    ring
  have h₃ : (transposeR A).eval (A+1) = (A+2)*(A+2) := by
    simp only [transposeR, eval_mul, eval_add, eval_sub, eval_X, eval_C, eval_one]
    ring
  have H₁ : ((a : ℂ)+2)*((a : ℂ)+2) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show ((a : ℝ)+2)*((a : ℝ)+2) ≠ 0 by positivity))
  have H₂ : -((a : ℂ)+2)*(a : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (complex_real_ne (show -((a : ℝ)+2)*(a : ℝ) ≠ 0 by
      apply mul_ne_zero <;> first | (apply ne_of_gt; linarith) | (apply ne_of_lt; linarith)))
  rw [hQ]
  exact ((coprime_linear _ _ (h₁ ▸ H₁)).mul_left
    (coprime_linear _ _ (h₂ ▸ H₂))).mul_left (coprime_linear _ _ (h₃ ▸ H₁))


noncomputable def seedP (Q R : ℂ[X]) (k : ℤ) : ℂ[X] :=
  Q * nativeU (k + 2) - R * nativeU (k + 1)

theorem seedP_step (Q R : ℂ[X]) (k : ℤ) :
    seedP Q R (k + 2) = X * seedP Q R (k + 1) - seedP Q R k := by
  have h₄ := nativeU_step (k+2)
  have h₃ := nativeU_step (k+1)
  have h₂ := nativeU_step k
  rw [show k+2+2=k+4 by omega, show k+2+1=k+3 by omega] at h₄
  rw [show k+1+2=k+3 by omega, show k+1+1=k+2 by omega] at h₃
  simp only [seedP, show k+2+2=k+4 by omega, show k+2+1=k+3 by omega,
    show k+1+2=k+3 by omega, show k+1+1=k+2 by omega, h₄, h₃, h₂]
  ring

theorem nativeU_degree (k : ℕ) : IsMonicOfDegree (nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1)) k := by
  induction k using Nat.twoStepInduction with
  | zero => simp
  | one =>
    norm_num
    exact ⟨natDegree_X, monic_X⟩
  | more k h₀ h₁ =>
    have hx : IsMonicOfDegree (X : ℂ[X]) 1 := ⟨natDegree_X, monic_X⟩
    have hm := hx.mul h₁
    have hb : (nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1)).natDegree < 1+(k+1) := by
      rw [h₀.natDegree_eq]; omega
    have he := hm.sub hb
    have hs := nativeU_step ((k : ℤ)+1)
    have heq : nativeU (((k+2 : ℕ) : ℤ)+1) =
        X * nativeU (((k+1 : ℕ) : ℤ)+1) - nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1) := by
      push_cast
      rw [show (k : ℤ)+2+1 = ((k : ℤ)+1)+2 by omega]
      exact hs
    rw [heq, show k+2 = 1+(k+1) by omega]
    exact he

theorem seedP_degree (Q R : ℂ[X]) (d : ℕ)
    (hQ : IsMonicOfDegree Q d) (hR : R.natDegree ≤ d) (k : ℕ) :
    IsMonicOfDegree (seedP Q R k) (d+k+1) := by
  have hu := nativeU_degree (k+1)
  have hu' := nativeU_degree k
  have hm := hQ.mul hu
  have hlt : (R * nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1)).natDegree < d+(k+1) := by
    have hh := natDegree_mul_le (p := R) (q := nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1))
    rw [hu'.natDegree_eq] at hh
    omega
  have he := hm.sub hlt
  change IsMonicOfDegree (Q * nativeU ((k : ℤ)+2) - R * nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1)) (d+k+1)
  simpa only [Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one, add_assoc, one_add_one_eq_two] using he

private theorem coprime_step (p q : ℂ[X]) :
    IsCoprime p q ↔ IsCoprime q (X*q-p) := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨u,v,h⟩
    refine ⟨u*X+v, -u, ?_⟩
    calc
      (u*X+v)*q + (-u)*(X*q-p) = u*p+v*q := by ring
      _ = 1 := h
  · rintro ⟨u,v,h⟩
    refine ⟨-v, u+v*X, ?_⟩
    calc
      (-v)*p+(u+v*X)*q = u*q+v*(X*q-p) := by ring
      _ = 1 := h

private theorem coprime_all (P : ℤ → ℂ[X])
    (hs : ∀ k, P (k+2) = X * P (k+1) - P k)
    (hm : IsCoprime (P (-1)) (P 0)) (k : ℤ) :
    IsCoprime (P k) (P (k+1)) := by
  have hstep (k : ℤ) : IsCoprime (P k) (P (k+1)) ↔ IsCoprime (P (k+1)) (P (k+1+1)) := by
    rw [show k+1+1=k+2 by omega, hs]
    exact coprime_step _ _
  have hbase := hstep (-1)
  rw [show (-1 : ℤ)+1=0 by decide] at hbase
  refine Int.induction_on (motive := fun k => IsCoprime (P k) (P (k+1))) k ?_ ?_ ?_
  · exact hbase.mp hm
  · intro i hi
    exact (hstep i).mp hi
  · intro i hi
    apply (hstep (-(i : ℤ)-1)).mpr
    rwa [show -(i : ℤ)-1+1 = -(i : ℤ) by omega]

theorem seedP_coprime (Q R : ℂ[X]) (h : IsCoprime Q R) (k : ℤ) :
    IsCoprime (seedP Q R k) (seedP Q R (k+1)) := by
  apply coprime_all _ (seedP_step Q R) _ k
  have hh : IsCoprime Q (X*Q-R) := (coprime_step R Q).mp h.symm
  simpa [seedP, mul_comm] using hh

theorem ordinaryP_monic_degree (a k : ℕ) :
    IsMonicOfDegree (printedOrdinaryP a k) (k+5) := by
  rw [ordinaryP_seed_identity]
  simpa [seedP, Nat.add_comm, Nat.add_left_comm, Nat.add_assoc] using
    seedP_degree (ordinaryQ a) (ordinaryR a) 4 (ordinaryQ_degree _) 
      ((ordinaryR_degree_bound _).trans (by decide : 3 ≤ 4)) k

theorem transposeP_monic_degree (a k : ℕ) :
    IsMonicOfDegree (correctedTransposeP a k) (k+4) := by
  rw [transposeP_seed_identity]
  simpa [seedP, Nat.add_comm, Nat.add_left_comm, Nat.add_assoc] using
    seedP_degree (transposeQ a) (transposeR a) 3 (transposeQ_degree _)
      ((transposeR_degree_bound _).trans (by decide : 2 ≤ 3)) k

theorem ordinaryP_coprime (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) (k : ℤ) :
    IsCoprime (printedOrdinaryP a k) (printedOrdinaryP a (k+1)) := by
  simp_rw [ordinaryP_seed_identity]
  exact seedP_coprime _ _ (ordinary_seeds_coprime a ha) k

theorem transposeP_coprime (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) (k : ℤ) :
    IsCoprime (correctedTransposeP a k) (correctedTransposeP a (k+1)) := by
  simp_rw [transposeP_seed_identity]
  exact seedP_coprime _ _ (transpose_seeds_coprime a ha) k

#print axioms nativeU_degree
#print axioms seedP_step
#print axioms seedP_degree
#print axioms seedP_coprime
#print axioms ordinaryP_monic_degree
#print axioms transposeP_monic_degree
#print axioms ordinaryP_coprime
#print axioms transposeP_coprime

#print axioms ordinary_residues_polynomial
#print axioms transpose_residues_polynomial
#print axioms ordinaryQ_degree
#print axioms transposeQ_degree
#print axioms ordinaryR_degree_bound
#print axioms transposeR_degree_bound
#print axioms ordinary_seeds_coprime
#print axioms transpose_seeds_coprime

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import SeedAlgebra
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.Determinant.Basic

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

/-- Path matrix with first diagonal y, remaining diagonals x and unit edges. -/
def scalarPath {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) R :=
  fun i j =>
    if i.val = j.val then (if i.val = 0 then y else x)
    else if i.val+1 = j.val ∨ j.val+1 = i.val then 1 else 0

private theorem first_path_minor {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath x y (n+1)).submatrix Fin.succ Fin.succ =
      scalarPath x x n := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  simp only [Matrix.submatrix_apply, scalarPath, Fin.val_succ,
    Nat.add_right_cancel_iff, ite_self, Nat.add_eq_zero_iff, Nat.one_ne_zero, and_false, ite_false]

private theorem path_row0_first {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    scalarPath x y (n+1) 0 0 = y := by rfl

private theorem path_row0_second {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    scalarPath x y (n+2) 0 (1 : Fin (n+2)) = 1 := by rfl

private theorem path_row0_later {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) (j : Fin n) :
    scalarPath x y (n+2) 0 j.succ.succ = 0 := by
  simp [scalarPath, Fin.val_succ]

private theorem path_minor_col0 {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) (i : Fin (n+1)) :
    ((scalarPath x y (n+2)).submatrix Fin.succ (1 : Fin (n+2)).succAbove) i 0 =
      if i = 0 then 1 else 0 := by
  have hu : (1 : Fin (n+2)).succAbove (0 : Fin (n+1)) = (0 : Fin (n+2)) := by
    calc
      _ = (0 : Fin (n+1)).castSucc :=
        Fin.succAbove_of_castSucc_lt _ _ (by simp)
      _ = _ := rfl
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, hu]
  by_cases h : i = 0
  · subst i
    rfl
  · simp [scalarPath, Fin.val_succ, h]

private theorem path_minor_twice {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    Matrix.submatrix ((scalarPath x y (n+2)).submatrix Fin.succ (1 : Fin (n+2)).succAbove)
      Fin.succ Fin.succ = scalarPath x x n := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  have hj : (1 : Fin (n+2)).succAbove j.succ = j.succ.succ :=
    Fin.succAbove_of_le_castSucc _ _ (by
      simp [Fin.le_def, Fin.val_succ])
  simp only [Matrix.submatrix_apply, hj, scalarPath, Fin.val_succ,
    Nat.add_right_cancel_iff, ite_self, Nat.add_eq_zero_iff, Nat.one_ne_zero, and_false, ite_false]

private theorem second_path_minor_det {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    ((scalarPath x y (n+2)).submatrix Fin.succ (1 : Fin (n+2)).succAbove).det =
      (scalarPath x x n).det := by
  rw [Matrix.det_succ_column_zero, Fin.sum_univ_succ]
  simp only [path_minor_col0]
  simp -singlePass only [Fin.val_zero, pow_zero, ite_true, one_mul, zero_mul, mul_zero,
    Fin.succ_ne_zero, ite_false, Finset.sum_const_zero, add_zero, Fin.succAbove_zero]
  rw [path_minor_twice]

theorem scalarPath_det_step {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath x y (n+2)).det =
      y * (scalarPath x x (n+1)).det - (scalarPath x x n).det := by
  rw [Matrix.det_succ_row_zero, Fin.sum_univ_succ, Fin.sum_univ_succ]
  simp [path_row0_first, path_row0_second, path_row0_later, first_path_minor,
    second_path_minor_det, sub_eq_add_neg]

theorem scalarPath_nat_chebyshev (n : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath (X : ℂ[X]) X n).det = nativeU ((n : ℤ)+1) := by
  induction n using Nat.twoStepInduction with
  | zero => simp
  | one => rw [Matrix.det_fin_one, path_row0_first]; exact nativeU_two.symm
  | more n hn hn' =>
    rw [scalarPath_det_step, hn, hn']
    have h := nativeU_step ((n : ℤ)+1)
    push_cast
    rw [show (n : ℤ)+2+1 = ((n : ℤ)+1)+2 by omega]
    exact h.symm

theorem scalarPath_modified_det (y : ℂ[X]) (k : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath X y (k+1)).det = nativeU ((k : ℤ)+2) + (y-X)*nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1) := by
  cases k with
  | zero =>
    rw [Matrix.det_fin_one, path_row0_first]
    norm_num
  | succ k =>
    rw [scalarPath_det_step, scalarPath_nat_chebyshev, scalarPath_nat_chebyshev]
    push_cast
    rw [nativeU_step ((k : ℤ)+1)]
    ring

/-- Pair of indices uses the native tail-site first order. -/
def fiberPath {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n d : ℕ) :
    Matrix (Fin n × Fin d) (Fin n × Fin d) R :=
  fun i j => if i.2 = j.2 then scalarPath x y n i.1 j.1 else 0

theorem fiberPath_det {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (x y : R) (n d : ℕ) :
    (fiberPath x y n d).det = (scalarPath x y n).det ^ d := by
  have h : fiberPath x y n d = Matrix.blockDiagonal (fun _ : Fin d => scalarPath x y n) := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    rfl
  rw [h, Matrix.det_blockDiagonal]
  simp

#print axioms scalarPath_det_step
#print axioms scalarPath_nat_chebyshev
#print axioms scalarPath_modified_det
#print axioms fiberPath_det

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import NativeCore
import Continuant

open Polynomial
open scoped BigOperators

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev CoreIndex (a : ℕ) := CoreCup a × Fin (a+1)
abbrev TailIndex (a k : ℕ) := Fin (k+1) × Fin (a+1)

noncomputable def coreTailIndex (a k : ℕ) :
    CoreIndex a ⊕ TailIndex a k ≃ NativeIndex a k :=
  (Equiv.sumProdDistrib (CoreCup a) (Fin (k+1)) (Fin (a+1))).symm.trans
    (Equiv.prodCongr (coreTailEquiv a k) (Equiv.refl _))

def coreInc (a k : ℕ) (i : CoreIndex a) : NativeIndex a k :=
  (embedCore a k i.1, i.2)

def tailInc (a k : ℕ) (i : TailIndex a k) : NativeIndex a k :=
  (embedTail a k i.1, i.2)

@[simp] theorem coreTailIndex_core (a k : ℕ) (i : CoreIndex a) :
    coreTailIndex a k (.inl i) = coreInc a k i := rfl

@[simp] theorem coreTailIndex_tail (a k : ℕ) (i : TailIndex a k) :
    coreTailIndex a k (.inr i) = tailInc a k i := rfl

noncomputable def normalMatrix (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (NativeIndex a k) (NativeIndex a k) ℂ[X] :=
  Matrix.charmatrix (-interaction a k transpose)

theorem normalMatrix_entry (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i j : NativeIndex a k) :
    normalMatrix a k transpose i j =
      (if i = j then X else 0) + C (nativeMove a k transpose j.1 i.1 j.2 i.2) := by
  classical
  rw [normalMatrix, Matrix.charmatrix_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply]
  simp [interaction]

noncomputable def coreMatrix (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ[X] :=
  (normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreInc a k) (coreInc a k)

noncomputable def boundaryIn (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreIndex a) (Fin (a+1)) ℂ[X] :=
  (normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreInc a k) (fun i => tailInc a k (0,i))

noncomputable def boundaryOut (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (CoreIndex a) ℂ[X] :=
  (normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (fun i => tailInc a k (0,i)) (coreInc a k)

def padIn {R : Type*} [Zero R] {ι : Type*} {n d : ℕ}
    (B : Matrix ι (Fin d) R) : Matrix ι (Fin (n+1) × Fin d) R :=
  fun i j => if j.1 = 0 then B i j.2 else 0

def padOut {R : Type*} [Zero R] {ι : Type*} {n d : ℕ}
    (C : Matrix (Fin d) ι R) : Matrix (Fin (n+1) × Fin d) ι R :=
  fun i j => if i.1 = 0 then C i.2 j else 0

private theorem coreTailIdx_ne (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreIndex a) (q : TailIndex a k) :
    coreInc a k p ≠ tailInc a k q := by
  intro h
  exact core_tail_ne a k p.1 q.1 (congrArg Prod.fst h)

theorem native_tail_block (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i j : TailIndex a k) :
    normalMatrix a k transpose (tailInc a k i) (tailInc a k j) =
    fiberPath X X (k+1) (a+1) i j := by
  rw [normalMatrix_entry]
  have hEq : tailInc a k i = tailInc a k j ↔ i.1.val = j.1.val ∧ i.2 = j.2 := by
    simp only [tailInc, Prod.ext_iff, (tail_injective a k).eq_iff, Fin.ext_iff]
  change ((if tailInc a k i = tailInc a k j then X else 0) +
    C (nativeMove a k transpose (embedTail a k j.1) (embedTail a k i.1) j.2 i.2)) = _
  rw [nativeMove_tail]
  simp only [hEq]
  by_cases h₁ : i.1.val = j.1.val
  · simp [fiberPath, scalarPath, h₁]
  · by_cases h₂ : i.2 = j.2
    · simp only [h₂, h₁, false_and, ite_false, zero_add, fiberPath, scalarPath, ite_true]
      by_cases h₃ : i.1.val + 1 = j.1.val ∨ j.1.val + 1 = i.1.val
      · have h₄ := h₃.symm
        simp [h₃, h₄, delta]
      · have h₄ : ¬ (j.1.val+1 = i.1.val ∨ i.1.val+1 = j.1.val) := fun h => h₃ h.symm
        simp [h₃, h₄]
    · simp only [h₁, h₂, false_and, ite_false, zero_add, fiberPath]
      split
      · simp [delta, h₂]
      · simp

theorem native_in_block (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i : CoreIndex a) (j : TailIndex a k) :
    normalMatrix a k transpose (coreInc a k i) (tailInc a k j) =
    padIn (n := k) (boundaryIn a k transpose) i j := by
  by_cases h : j.1 = 0
  · rw [show j = (0,j.2) from Prod.ext h rfl]
    simp [padIn, boundaryIn]
  · rw [normalMatrix_entry]
    have hj : 0 < j.1.val := by
      apply Nat.pos_of_ne_zero
      exact fun hval => h (Fin.ext hval)
    simp [padIn, h, core_tail_ne a k i.1 j.1, coreInc, tailInc,
      nativeMove_tail_core_late a k transpose i.1 j.1 hj]

theorem native_out_block (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (i : TailIndex a k) (j : CoreIndex a) :
    normalMatrix a k transpose (tailInc a k i) (coreInc a k j) =
    padOut (n := k) (boundaryOut a k transpose) i j := by
  by_cases h : i.1 = 0
  · rw [show i = (0,i.2) from Prod.ext h rfl]
    simp [padOut, boundaryOut]
  · rw [normalMatrix_entry]
    have hi : 0 < i.1.val := by
      apply Nat.pos_of_ne_zero
      exact fun hval => h (Fin.ext hval)
    simp [padOut, h, Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a k j.1 i.1), coreInc, tailInc,
      nativeMove_core_tail_late a k transpose j.1 i.1 hi]

theorem native_normal_blocks (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    (normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreTailIndex a k) (coreTailIndex a k) =
      Matrix.fromBlocks (coreMatrix a k transpose)
        (padIn (n := k) (boundaryIn a k transpose))
        (padOut (n := k) (boundaryOut a k transpose))
        (fiberPath X X (k+1) (a+1)) := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  cases i <;> cases j <;>
    simp only [Matrix.submatrix_apply, coreTailIndex_core, coreTailIndex_tail,
      Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₁₁, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₁₂, Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₁,
      Matrix.fromBlocks_apply₂₂, native_tail_block, native_in_block, native_out_block, coreMatrix]

theorem coreMatrix_is_charmatrix (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    coreMatrix a k transpose =
      Matrix.charmatrix (-((interaction a k transpose).submatrix (coreInc a k) (coreInc a k))) := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  have hinj : Function.Injective (coreInc a k) := by
    intro p q h
    exact Prod.ext (core_injective a k (congrArg (fun i : NativeIndex a k => i.1) h))
      (congrArg (fun i : NativeIndex a k => i.2) h)
  simp [coreMatrix, normalMatrix, Matrix.charmatrix_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply, hinj.eq_iff]

theorem coreMatrix_det_monic (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    (coreMatrix a k transpose).det.Monic := by
  rw [coreMatrix_is_charmatrix, ← Matrix.charpoly]
  exact Matrix.charpoly_monic _

#print axioms native_tail_block
#print axioms native_in_block
#print axioms native_out_block
#print axioms native_normal_blocks
#print axioms coreMatrix_is_charmatrix
#print axioms coreMatrix_det_monic

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import NativeBlocks
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.SchurComplement
import Mathlib.RingTheory.Localization.FractionRing

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev LoopField := FractionRing ℂ[X]
noncomputable abbrev lp : ℂ[X] →+* LoopField := algebraMap ℂ[X] LoopField

theorem lp_injective : Function.Injective lp := IsFractionRing.injective _ _

theorem map_scalarPath {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S)
    (x y : R) (n : ℕ) :
    f.mapMatrix (scalarPath x y n) = scalarPath (f x) (f y) n := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  change f (scalarPath x y n i j) = scalarPath (f x) (f y) n i j
  by_cases hij : i.val = j.val
  · by_cases hj : j.val = 0 <;> simp [scalarPath, hij, hj]
  · by_cases hn : i.val+1 = j.val ∨ j.val+1 = i.val <;> simp [scalarPath, hij, hn]

theorem map_fiberPath {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S)
    (x y : R) (n d : ℕ) :
    f.mapMatrix (fiberPath x y n d) = fiberPath (f x) (f y) n d := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  have hm := congrArg (fun (A : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) S) => A i.1 j.1)
    (map_scalarPath f x y n)
  change f (scalarPath x y n i.1 j.1) = _ at hm
  by_cases h : i.2 = j.2 <;> simp [fiberPath, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, h, hm]

theorem map_padIn {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S)
    {ι : Type*} {k d : ℕ} (B : Matrix ι (Fin d) R) :
    (padIn (n := k) B).map f = padIn (n := k) (B.map f) := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : j.1 = 0 <;> simp [padIn, Matrix.map_apply, h]

theorem map_padOut {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S)
    {ι : Type*} {k d : ℕ} (B : Matrix (Fin d) ι R) :
    (padOut (n := k) B).map f = padOut (n := k) (B.map f) := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i.1 = 0 <;> simp [padOut, Matrix.map_apply, h]

theorem frac_scalarPath_uniform (n : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath (lp X) (lp X) n).det = lp (nativeU ((n : ℤ)+1)) := by
  rw [← map_scalarPath lp, ← RingHom.map_det, scalarPath_nat_chebyshev]

theorem frac_scalarPath_modified (y : LoopField) (k : ℕ) :
    (scalarPath (lp X) y (k+1)).det =
      lp (nativeU ((k : ℤ)+2)) + (y-lp X)*lp (nativeU ((k : ℤ)+1)) := by
  cases k with
  | zero =>
    rw [Matrix.det_fin_one]
    change y = _
    norm_num
  | succ k =>
    rw [scalarPath_det_step, frac_scalarPath_uniform, frac_scalarPath_uniform]
    push_cast
    rw [nativeU_step ((k : ℤ)+1), map_sub, map_mul]
    ring

theorem padded_compression {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] {k d : ℕ}
    (A : Matrix ι ι LoopField) (B : Matrix ι (Fin d) LoopField)
    (C₀ : Matrix (Fin d) ι LoopField) (i j : Fin (k+1) × Fin d) :
    (padOut (n := k) C₀ * A * padIn (n := k) B) i j =
      if i.1 = 0 ∧ j.1 = 0 then (C₀ * A * B) i.2 j.2 else 0 := by
  by_cases hi : i.1 = 0 <;> by_cases hj : j.1 = 0 <;>
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, padOut, padIn, hi, hj]

theorem subtract_marked_block {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] {k d : ℕ}
    (x t : LoopField) (A : Matrix ι ι LoopField) (B : Matrix ι (Fin d) LoopField)
    (C₀ : Matrix (Fin d) ι LoopField) (h : C₀ * A * B = Matrix.scalar (Fin d) t) :
    fiberPath x x (k+1) d - padOut (n := k) C₀ * A * padIn (n := k) B =
      fiberPath x (x-t) (k+1) d := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, padded_compression, h]
  by_cases hi : i.1 = 0 <;> by_cases hj : j.1 = 0 <;> by_cases hd : i.2 = j.2
  all_goals
    have hvi : i.1.val = 0 ↔ i.1 = 0 :=
      ⟨fun h => Fin.ext h, fun h => congrArg Fin.val h⟩
    have hvj : j.1.val = 0 ↔ j.1 = 0 :=
      ⟨fun h => Fin.ext h, fun h => congrArg Fin.val h⟩
    simp [fiberPath, scalarPath, Matrix.scalar_apply,
      hi, hj, hd, hvi, hvj]
  have hz : 0 ≠ j.1.val := fun hh => hj (Fin.ext hh.symm)
  simp [hz]

noncomputable def boundaryCompression (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField :=
  (boundaryOut a k transpose).map lp * (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a k transpose))⁻¹ *
    (boundaryIn a k transpose).map lp

theorem core_field_det_ne (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a k transpose)).det ≠ 0 := by
  rw [← RingHom.map_det]
  intro h
  have h' := lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm)
  exact (coreMatrix_det_monic a k transpose).ne_zero h'

private theorem map_native_blocks (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    lp.mapMatrix ((normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreTailIndex a k) (coreTailIndex a k)) =
       Matrix.fromBlocks (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a k transpose))
        (padIn (n := k) ((boundaryIn a k transpose).map lp))
        (padOut (n := k) ((boundaryOut a k transpose).map lp))
        (fiberPath (lp X) (lp X) (k+1) (a+1)) := by
  change ((normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreTailIndex a k) (coreTailIndex a k)).map lp = _
  have hpath : (fiberPath X X (k+1) (a+1)).map lp =
      fiberPath (lp X) (lp X) (k+1) (a+1) := map_fiberPath lp X X _ _
  rw [native_normal_blocks, Matrix.fromBlocks_map, map_padIn, map_padOut, hpath]
  rfl

theorem native_schur_path (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (s : LoopField)
    (h : boundaryCompression a k transpose = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) s) :
    lp (normalMatrix a k transpose).det =
      lp (coreMatrix a k transpose).det *
        (scalarPath (lp X) (lp X-s) (k+1)).det^(a+1) := by
  classical
  let A := lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a k transpose)
  let inst : Invertible A := Matrix.invertibleOfIsUnitDet A (core_field_det_ne a k transpose).isUnit
  have hdet := congrArg Matrix.det (map_native_blocks a k transpose)
  rw [← RingHom.map_det] at hdet
  have he : ((normalMatrix a k transpose).submatrix (coreTailIndex a k)
      (coreTailIndex a k)).det = (normalMatrix a k transpose).det :=
    Matrix.det_submatrix_equiv_self _ _
  rw [he, Matrix.det_fromBlocks₁₁, Matrix.invOf_eq_nonsing_inv] at hdet
  rw [hdet, subtract_marked_block _ _ _ _ _ h, fiberPath_det, ← RingHom.map_det]

/-- Descent from the fraction field for the actual native one-cup Gram matrix.
The two inputs are the exact core determinant and marked-fiber compression. -/
theorem native_det_of_core (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (fac Q R : ℂ[X]) (hq : Q ≠ 0)
    (hcore : (coreMatrix a k transpose).det = fac * Q^(a+1))
    (hbound : boundaryCompression a k transpose = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (lp R / lp Q)) :
    nativeDet a k transpose =
      C (cupMetric a k).det * (fac * (seedP Q R k)^(a+1)) := by
  have hq' : lp Q ≠ 0 := fun h => hq (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  let D := (scalarPath (lp X) (lp X - lp R / lp Q) (k+1)).det
  have hD : D * lp Q = lp (seedP Q R k) := by
    dsimp only [D]
    rw [frac_scalarPath_modified, seedP, map_sub, map_mul, map_mul]
    field_simp
    ring
  have hx : (normalMatrix a k transpose).det = fac * (seedP Q R k)^(a+1) := by
    apply lp_injective
    rw [native_schur_path a k transpose _ hbound, hcore, map_mul, map_pow, map_mul, map_pow]
    change (lp fac * lp Q ^ (a+1)) * D^(a+1) = lp fac * (lp (seedP Q R k))^(a+1)
    rw [← hD, mul_pow]
    ring
  rw [nativeDet_eq, Matrix.charpoly]
  rw [← normalMatrix, hx]

#print axioms frac_scalarPath_modified
#print axioms padded_compression
#print axioms subtract_marked_block
#print axioms native_schur_path
#print axioms native_det_of_core

end BrauerHook
```

## 12. `CoreStability` (inherited, 136 lines, sha256 `27d098c0cd2536f3d0cddfaf3e47f1170dee6b67f2db4f9f31cee33607b7b82a`)

```lean
import NativeSchur

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

def liftSeedSite (a k : ℕ) (x : SeedSite a) : Fin (Rank a k) :=
  ⟨x.val, by have h := x.isLt; simp only [Rank] at h ⊢; omega⟩

theorem liftSeedSite_injective (a k : ℕ) : Function.Injective (liftSeedSite a k) :=
  fun _ _ h => Fin.ext (congrArg (fun i : Fin (Rank a k) => i.val) h)

def extendSeedCup (a k : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) : Cup a (Rank a k) :=
  ⟨(liftSeedSite a k (left c), liftSeedSite a k (right c)), c.property⟩

theorem extendSeedCup_injective (a k : ℕ) : Function.Injective (extendSeedCup a k) := by
  intro c d h
  apply (loopEdges_iff _ _).mp
  constructor
  · exact liftSeedSite_injective a k (congrArg left h)
  · exact liftSeedSite_injective a k (congrArg right h)

theorem touch_extend (a k : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (z : SeedSite a) :
    touches (extendSeedCup a k c) (liftSeedSite a k z) ↔ touches c z := by
  simp only [touches, left, right, extendSeedCup, (liftSeedSite_injective a k).eq_iff]

theorem lift_touched (a k : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (z : Fin (Rank a k))
    (h : touches (extendSeedCup a k c) z) :
    ∃ w : SeedSite a, liftSeedSite a k w = z := by
  rcases h with h | h
  · exact ⟨left c, h.symm⟩
  · exact ⟨right c, h.symm⟩

theorem extend_shared {a : ℕ} (k : ℕ) {c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)} {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) :
    Shared (extendSeedCup a k c) (extendSeedCup a k d)
      (liftSeedSite a k b) (liftSeedSite a k u) (liftSeedSite a k v) := by
  have hinj := liftSeedSite_injective a k
  have he (p : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (x y : SeedSite a)
      (hp : ∀ z, touches p z ↔ z=x ∨ z=y) (z : Fin (Rank a k)) :
      touches (extendSeedCup a k p) z ↔
        z = liftSeedSite a k x ∨ z = liftSeedSite a k y := by
    constructor
    · intro hz
      obtain ⟨w, rfl⟩ := lift_touched a k p z hz
      rw [hinj.eq_iff, hinj.eq_iff, ← hp]
      exact (touch_extend a k p w).mp hz
    · rintro (rfl | rfl)
      · exact (touch_extend a k p x).mpr ((hp x).mpr (Or.inl rfl))
      · exact (touch_extend a k p y).mpr ((hp y).mpr (Or.inr rfl))
  exact ⟨fun h => s.distinctBU (hinj h), fun h => s.distinctBV (hinj h),
    fun h => s.distinctUV (hinj h), he c b v s.source, he d b u s.target⟩

theorem extend_disjoint {a : ℕ} (k : ℕ) {c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)}
    (hd : ∀ z, touches d z → ¬ touches c z) :
    ∀ z, touches (extendSeedCup a k d) z → ¬ touches (extendSeedCup a k c) z := by
  intro z hz hc
  obtain ⟨w, rfl⟩ := lift_touched a k d z hz
  exact hd w ((touch_extend a k d w).mp hz) ((touch_extend a k c w).mp hc)

theorem extendSeed_core (a k : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    extendSeedCup a k (embedCore a 0 p) = embedCore a k p := rfl

theorem extendSeed_tail (a k : ℕ) :
    extendSeedCup a k (embedTail a 0 0) = embedTail a k 0 := rfl

theorem nativeMove_rank_stable (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool)
    (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a k transpose (extendSeedCup a k c) (extendSeedCup a k d) j =
      nativeMove a 0 transpose c d j := by
  obtain he | hd | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy c d
  · subst d
    simp [nativeMove]
  · have he : c ≠ d := by
      intro h
      subst d
      exact hd (left c) (Or.inl rfl) (Or.inl rfl)
    have hn : extendSeedCup a k c ≠ extendSeedCup a k d :=
      fun h => he (extendSeedCup_injective a k h)
    simp [nativeMove, he, hn, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := k) (extend_disjoint k hd),
      mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd]
  · have he := shared_ne s
    have hn : extendSeedCup a k c ≠ extendSeedCup a k d :=
      fun h => he (extendSeedCup_injective a k h)
    have ht := transport_independent_of_rank (extend_shared k s) s rfl rfl rfl rfl rfl rfl
    simp [nativeMove, he, hn, mixedTransport_shared (extend_shared k s), mixedTransport_shared s, ht]

private theorem stable_move_core (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool)
    (p q : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a k transpose (embedCore a k p) (embedCore a k q) j =
    nativeMove a 0 transpose (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j := by
  simpa only [extendSeed_core] using
    nativeMove_rank_stable a k transpose (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j

theorem coreMatrix_stable (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    coreMatrix a k transpose = coreMatrix a 0 transpose := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  classical
  simp only [coreMatrix, Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, coreInc, stable_move_core,
    Prod.ext_iff, (core_injective a k).eq_iff, (core_injective a 0).eq_iff]

theorem boundaryIn_stable (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    boundaryIn a k transpose = boundaryIn a 0 transpose := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  have he (l : ℕ) : coreInc a l i ≠ tailInc a l (0,j) :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a l i.1 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)
  simp only [boundaryIn, Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, he, ite_false, zero_add]
  have ht := nativeMove_rank_stable a k transpose (embedTail a 0 0) (embedCore a 0 i.1) j
  rw [extendSeed_tail, extendSeed_core] at ht
  exact congrArg (fun f => C (f i.2)) ht

theorem boundaryOut_stable (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    boundaryOut a k transpose = boundaryOut a 0 transpose := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  have he (l : ℕ) : tailInc a l (0,i) ≠ coreInc a l j :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a l j.1 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)
  simp only [boundaryOut, Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, he, ite_false, zero_add]
  have ht := nativeMove_rank_stable a k transpose (embedCore a 0 j.1) (embedTail a 0 0) j.2
  rw [extendSeed_core, extendSeed_tail] at ht
  exact congrArg (fun f => C (f i)) ht

theorem boundaryCompression_stable (a k : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) :
    boundaryCompression a k transpose = boundaryCompression a 0 transpose := by
  rw [boundaryCompression, coreMatrix_stable, boundaryIn_stable, boundaryOut_stable]
  rfl

#print axioms nativeMove_rank_stable
#print axioms coreMatrix_stable
#print axioms boundaryIn_stable
#print axioms boundaryOut_stable
#print axioms boundaryCompression_stable

end BrauerHook
```

## 13. `TransposedFiber` (inherited, 223 lines, sha256 `5f7a1a76ae69d6281d536a036cc5f8b121b4f052e815316167a11d2ac8613462`)

```lean
import HookFiber

open scoped BigOperators

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev TransLetters (a : ℕ) := Letters (a + 1)
abbrev Tableau (a : ℕ) := Equiv.Perm (TransLetters a)

def firstPos (a : ℕ) : TransLetters a := 0
def secondPos (a : ℕ) : TransLetters a := Fin.last (a + 1)

theorem distinct_positions (a : ℕ) : firstPos a ≠ secondPos a := by
  intro h
  have he := congrArg Fin.val h
  change 0 = a + 1 at he
  exact Nat.noConfusion he

def rowSwap (a : ℕ) : Tableau a := Equiv.swap (firstPos a) (secondPos a)

theorem rowSwap_first (a : ℕ) : rowSwap a (firstPos a) = secondPos a :=
  Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _

theorem rowSwap_second (a : ℕ) : rowSwap a (secondPos a) = firstPos a :=
  Equiv.swap_apply_right _ _

theorem rowSwap_sign (a : ℕ) : permSign (rowSwap a) = -1 := by
  simp [permSign, rowSwap, distinct_positions]

/-- Row tabloids of (2,1^a): potential orientations differ by swapping the
first two row positions, while the remaining rows have one entry each. -/
def TransTabloid (a : ℕ) : Submodule ℂ (Tableau a → ℂ) where
  carrier := {F | ∀ t, F (t * rowSwap a) = F t}
  zero_mem' _ := rfl
  add_mem' {F G} hf hg t := by
    change F (t * rowSwap a) + G (t * rowSwap a) = F t + G t
    rw [hf t, hg t]
  smul_mem' c F hf t := by
    change c * F (t * rowSwap a) = c * F t
    rw [hf t]

/-- Oriented coefficient on the row tabloid represented by t. -/
noncomputable def transImage (a : ℕ) : TabloidModule (a+1) →ₗ[ℂ] (Tableau a → ℂ) where
  toFun f t := permSign t * (f (t (secondPos a)) - f (t (firstPos a)))
  map_add' f g := by
    funext t
    change permSign t * ((f (t (secondPos a)) + g (t (secondPos a))) -
      (f (t (firstPos a)) + g (t (firstPos a)))) =
      permSign t * (f (t (secondPos a)) - f (t (firstPos a))) +
      permSign t * (g (t (secondPos a)) - g (t (firstPos a)))
    ring
  map_smul' c f := by
    funext t
    change permSign t * (c * f (t (secondPos a)) - c * f (t (firstPos a))) =
      c * (permSign t * (f (t (secondPos a)) - f (t (firstPos a))))
    ring

theorem transImage_row_invariant (a : ℕ) (f : TabloidModule (a+1)) :
    transImage a f ∈ TransTabloid a := by
  intro t
  change permSign (t * rowSwap a) *
      (f ((t * rowSwap a) (secondPos a)) - f ((t * rowSwap a) (firstPos a))) =
    permSign t * (f (t (secondPos a)) - f (t (firstPos a)))
  rw [sign_mul, rowSwap_sign]
  simp only [Equiv.Perm.mul_apply, rowSwap_first, rowSwap_second]
  ring

/-- The two permutations representing the output row tabloid are t and t·swap.
A signed column-orbit term from q contributes precisely when the input and
output singleton second-column positions agree. -/
noncomputable def transposePolytabloid (a : ℕ) (q : Tableau a) : Tableau a → ℂ :=
  fun t => (if t (secondPos a) = q (secondPos a) then permSign (t * q⁻¹) else 0) +
    (if (t * rowSwap a) (secondPos a) = q (secondPos a)
      then permSign ((t * rowSwap a) * q⁻¹) else 0)

theorem sign_inv {d : ℕ} (q : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) : permSign q⁻¹ = permSign q := by
  have h := sign_mul q q⁻¹
  rw [mul_inv_cancel, sign_one] at h
  have h' := sign_sq q
  calc
    permSign q⁻¹ = (permSign q * permSign q) * permSign q⁻¹ := by rw [h', one_mul]
    _ = permSign q := by rw [mul_assoc, ← h, mul_one]

theorem transposePolytabloid_eq (a : ℕ) (q : Tableau a) :
    transposePolytabloid a q = permSign q • transImage a (tabloid (q (secondPos a))) := by
  funext t
  change _ = permSign q * (permSign t *
    (tabloid (q (secondPos a)) (t (secondPos a)) -
     tabloid (q (secondPos a)) (t (firstPos a))))
  simp only [transposePolytabloid, Equiv.Perm.mul_apply, rowSwap_second, sign_mul,
    rowSwap_sign, sign_inv, tabloid]
  split <;> (try split) <;> ring

noncomputable def transTwist (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (f : TabloidModule (a+1)) :
    TabloidModule (a+1) := permSign p • permute p f

theorem transImage_equivariant (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (f : TabloidModule (a+1))
    (t : Tableau a) :
    transImage a (transTwist a p f) t = transImage a f (p⁻¹ * t) := by
  change permSign t * (permSign p * f (p.symm (t (secondPos a))) -
    permSign p * f (p.symm (t (firstPos a)))) =
      permSign (p⁻¹ * t) * (f ((p⁻¹ * t) (secondPos a)) - f ((p⁻¹ * t) (firstPos a)))
  rw [sign_mul, sign_inv]
  change permSign t * (permSign p * f (p.symm (t (secondPos a))) -
    permSign p * f (p.symm (t (firstPos a)))) =
      (permSign p * permSign t) * (f (p.symm (t (secondPos a))) - f (p.symm (t (firstPos a))))
  ring

private theorem centered_total (a : ℕ) (b : TransLetters a) :
    tabloid b - (fun _ => ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹) ∈ Standard (a+1) := by
  have hne : (a : ℂ) + 2 ≠ 0 := by
    have h : (((a+2 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero (a+1))
    simpa using h
  change total (tabloid b - _) = 0
  rw [map_sub, total_tabloid]
  change 1 - (∑ _ : TransLetters a, ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹) = 0
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
    nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
  rw [show (a : ℂ) + 1 + 1 = (a : ℂ) + 2 by ring, mul_inv_cancel₀ hne, sub_self]

theorem transpose_span_eq (a : ℕ) :
    Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (transposePolytabloid a)) =
      (Standard (a+1)).map (transImage a) := by
  apply le_antisymm
  · apply Submodule.span_le.mpr
    rintro y ⟨q, rfl⟩
    rw [transposePolytabloid_eq]
    apply Submodule.smul_mem
    refine ⟨_, centered_total a (q (secondPos a)), ?_⟩
    funext t
    change permSign t * ((_ - _) - (_ - _)) = permSign t * (_ - _)
    ring
  · have hmem (b : TransLetters a) :
        transImage a (tabloid b) ∈ Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (transposePolytabloid a)) := by
      let q := Equiv.swap (secondPos a) b
      have hq : q (secondPos a) = b := Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _
      have hm := Submodule.subset_span (R := ℂ) (s := Set.range (transposePolytabloid a))
        (Set.mem_range_self q)
      rw [transposePolytabloid_eq, hq] at hm
      have hh := Submodule.smul_mem _ (permSign q) hm
      rwa [smul_smul, sign_sq, one_smul] at hh
    rintro y ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
    rw [standard_eq_polytabloid_span] at hv
    induction hv using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem v hv =>
        obtain ⟨top, bottom, _, rfl⟩ := hv
        rw [hookPolytabloid, map_sub]
        exact Submodule.sub_mem _ (hmem bottom) (hmem top)
    | zero => simpa only [map_zero] using
        (Submodule.zero_mem (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (transposePolytabloid a))))
    | add u v _ _ hu hv => exact map_add (transImage a) u v ▸ Submodule.add_mem _ hu hv
    | smul c v _ h => exact map_smul (transImage a) c v ▸ Submodule.smul_mem _ c h

private theorem exists_tableau_pair (a : ℕ) (x y : TransLetters a) (h : x ≠ y) :
    ∃ t : Tableau a, t (firstPos a) = x ∧ t (secondPos a) = y := by
  let p := Equiv.swap (firstPos a) x
  have hp : p (firstPos a) = x := Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _
  have hn : p (secondPos a) ≠ x := fun he =>
    (distinct_positions a) (p.injective (hp.trans he.symm))
  let q := Equiv.swap (p (secondPos a)) y
  exact ⟨q * p, by
    change q (p (firstPos a)) = x
    rw [hp]
    exact Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hn.symm h,
    Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _⟩

theorem transImage_injective_standard (a : ℕ) :
    Function.Injective (fun f : Standard (a+1) => transImage a f.val) := by
  intro f g he
  change transImage a f.val = transImage a g.val at he
  have hzero : transImage a (f.val - g.val) = 0 := by rw [map_sub, he, sub_self]
  have hf : f.val - g.val ∈ Standard (a+1) :=
    Submodule.sub_mem _ f.property g.property
  let v := f.val - g.val
  have hconst (x y : TransLetters a) : v x = v y := by
    by_cases h : x = y
    · rw [h]
    obtain ⟨t, ht₁, ht₂⟩ := exists_tableau_pair a x y h
    have hz := congrFun hzero t
    change permSign t * (v (t (secondPos a)) - v (t (firstPos a))) = 0 at hz
    rw [ht₁, ht₂] at hz
    have hne : permSign t ≠ 0 := by
      intro h
      have hs := sign_sq t
      rw [h, zero_mul] at hs
      exact zero_ne_one hs
    exact (sub_eq_zero.mp ((mul_eq_zero.mp hz).resolve_left hne)).symm
  have hsum : ∑ x, v x = 0 := hf
  simp_rw [hconst _ (firstPos a)] at hsum
  simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul] at hsum
  have hne : (↑(a + 1 + 1) : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero _)
  have hv := (mul_eq_zero.mp hsum).resolve_left hne
  apply Subtype.ext
  funext x
  exact sub_eq_zero.mp (show v x = 0 by rw [hconst x (firstPos a), hv])

/-- Explicit equivalence between the sign-twisted standard space and the
native transposed-hook polytabloid span. -/
noncomputable def transposeCoords (a : ℕ) :
    Standard (a+1) ≃ₗ[ℂ] Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (transposePolytabloid a)) :=
  LinearEquiv.ofBijective
    { toFun := fun f => ⟨transImage a f.val, by rw [transpose_span_eq]; exact ⟨_, f.property, rfl⟩⟩
      map_add' := fun f g => Subtype.ext (map_add _ f.val g.val)
      map_smul' := fun c f => Subtype.ext (map_smul _ c f.val) }
    ⟨fun _ _ h => transImage_injective_standard a (congrArg Subtype.val h),
      fun y => by
        have h : y.val ∈ (Standard (a+1)).map (transImage a) :=
          (transpose_span_eq a) ▸ y.property
        obtain ⟨v,hv,he⟩ := h
        exact ⟨⟨v,hv⟩, Subtype.ext he⟩⟩

theorem transpose_dim (a : ℕ) :
    Module.finrank ℂ (Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range (transposePolytabloid a))) = a + 1 := by
  rw [← (transposeCoords a).finrank_eq, standard_dim]

#print axioms transposePolytabloid_eq
#print axioms transImage_row_invariant
#print axioms transImage_equivariant
#print axioms transpose_span_eq
#print axioms transposeCoords
#print axioms transpose_dim

end BrauerHook
```

## 14. `PairingBridge` (inherited, 150 lines, sha256 `b348611d1b48d8e86e3ff77e35a0eeef989f176071c9285bb10093cbe1e96465`)

```lean
import NativeGram
import TransposedFiber

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev HFiber (a : ℕ) := Standard (a+1)

noncomputable def basisVector (a : ℕ) (i : Fin (a+1)) : HFiber a :=
  standardCoords (a+1) (delta i)

noncomputable def centered (a : ℕ) (b : TransLetters a) : HFiber a :=
  ⟨tabloid b - (fun _ => ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹), by
    have hne : (a : ℂ) + 2 ≠ 0 := by
      have h : (((a+2 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero (a+1))
      simpa using h
    change total (tabloid b - _) = 0
    rw [map_sub, total_tabloid]
    change 1 - (∑ _ : TransLetters a, ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹) = 0
    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin,
      nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
    rw [show (a : ℂ) + 1 + 1 = (a : ℂ) + 2 by ring, mul_inv_cancel₀ hne, sub_self]⟩

def stdRelabel (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (v : HFiber a) : HFiber a :=
  ⟨permute p v.val, permute_mem p v⟩

theorem relabel_centered (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (b : TransLetters a) :
    stdRelabel a p (centered a b) = centered a (p b) := by
  apply Subtype.ext
  change permute p (tabloid b - _) = tabloid (p b) - _
  funext i
  have h := congrFun (permute_tabloid p b) i
  change tabloid b (p.symm i) - _ = tabloid (p b) i - _
  change tabloid b (p.symm i) = tabloid (p b) i at h
  rw [h]

theorem basis_centered (a : ℕ) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    basisVector a i = centered a i.castSucc - centered a (Fin.last (a+1)) := by
  apply Subtype.ext
  change expand (delta i) = _
  change expand (fun j => if j = i then 1 else 0) = _
  rw [expand_delta]
  funext t
  change tabloid i.castSucc t - tabloid (Fin.last (a+1)) t =
    (tabloid i.castSucc t - ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹) -
      (tabloid (Fin.last (a+1)) t - ((a : ℂ) + 2)⁻¹)
  ring

theorem std_basis_coordinates (a : ℕ) (v : HFiber a) :
    v = ∑ i : Fin (a+1), (v.val i.castSucc) • basisVector a i := by
  have h : (standardCoords (a+1)).symm v =
      ∑ i : Fin (a+1), v.val i.castSucc • delta i := by
    funext j
    change v.val j.castSucc = _
    simp [delta]
  have he := congrArg (standardCoords (a+1)) h
  rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply, map_sum] at he
  simp_rw [map_smul] at he
  exact he

theorem transAct_coordinates (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (f : Fin (a+1) → ℂ) (t : Tableau a) :
    transImage a (expand (hookAct true p f)) t =
      transImage a (expand f) (p⁻¹ * t) := by
  rw [expand_hookAct]
  exact transImage_equivariant a p _ t

theorem ordinaryAct_coordinates (a : ℕ) (p : Tableau a) (f : Fin (a+1) → ℂ) :
    expand (hookAct false p f) = permute p (expand f) := by
  rw [expand_hookAct]
  simp

private theorem exists_relabel_pair (a : ℕ) (i j i' j' : TransLetters a)
    (h : i ≠ j) (h' : i' ≠ j') :
    ∃ p : Tableau a, p i = i' ∧ p j = j' := by
  let u := Equiv.swap i i'
  have hu : u i = i' := Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _
  have hne : u j ≠ i' := fun hh => h (u.injective (hu.trans hh.symm))
  let v := Equiv.swap (u j) j'
  exact ⟨v*u, by
    change v (u i) = i'
    rw [hu]
    exact Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hne.symm h',
    Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _⟩

/-- Invariant nonzero forms in the native hook coordinates differ by a scalar.
For the transposed action its two sign factors cancel, so the same
invariance condition applies after the identification by transposeCoords. -/
theorem invariant_hook_pairing (a : ℕ) (B : HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)
    (hInv : ∀ (p : Tableau a) v w, B (stdRelabel a p v) (stdRelabel a p w) = B v w)
    (hB : B ≠ 0) :
    ∃ s : ℂ, s ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i j : Fin (a+1),
      B (basisVector a i) (basisVector a j) = s * (delta j i + 1) := by
  let p₀ := firstPos a
  let p₁ := secondPos a
  let D := B (centered a p₀) (centered a p₀)
  let E := B (centered a p₀) (centered a p₁)
  have hD (i : TransLetters a) : B (centered a i) (centered a i) = D := by
    let p := Equiv.swap p₀ i
    have hp : p p₀ = i := Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _
    simpa only [relabel_centered, hp] using hInv p (centered a p₀) (centered a p₀)
  have hE (i j : TransLetters a) (h : i ≠ j) : B (centered a i) (centered a j) = E := by
    obtain ⟨p, hp₀, hp₁⟩ := exists_relabel_pair a p₀ p₁ i j (distinct_positions a) h
    simpa only [relabel_centered, hp₀, hp₁] using hInv p (centered a p₀) (centered a p₁)
  let s := D - E
  have hval (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
      B (basisVector a i) (basisVector a j) = s * (delta j i + 1) := by
    simp only [basis_centered, map_sub, LinearMap.sub_apply]
    rw [hE _ _ (Fin.castSucc_ne_last _), hE _ _ (Fin.castSucc_ne_last _).symm, hD]
    by_cases h : i = j
    · subst j
      rw [hD]
      simp only [delta, ite_true, s]
      ring
    · rw [hE _ _ (fun hh => h (Fin.castSucc_injective _ hh))]
      simp only [delta, ite_eq_right h, s]
      ring
  have hs : s ≠ 0 := by
    intro he
    apply hB
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro v
    apply LinearMap.ext
    intro w
    have hsumv := std_basis_coordinates a v
    have hsumw := std_basis_coordinates a w
    calc
      B v w = B (∑ i, v.val i.castSucc • basisVector a i)
          (∑ i, w.val i.castSucc • basisVector a i) :=
        congrArg₂ (fun v w => B v w) hsumv hsumw
      _ = (0 : HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) v w := by
        simp [map_sum, map_smul, LinearMap.sum_apply, LinearMap.smul_apply, hval, he]
  exact ⟨s, hs, hval⟩

theorem transpose_signs_cancel (a : ℕ) (B : HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] HFiber a →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)
    (hInv : ∀ (p : Tableau a) v w,
      B (permSign p • stdRelabel a p v) (permSign p • stdRelabel a p w) = B v w) :
    ∀ (p : Tableau a) v w, B (stdRelabel a p v) (stdRelabel a p w) = B v w := by
  intro p v w
  have h := hInv p v w
  simp only [map_smul, LinearMap.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, ← mul_assoc, sign_sq, one_mul] at h
  exact h

#print axioms transAct_coordinates
#print axioms ordinaryAct_coordinates
#print axioms invariant_hook_pairing
#print axioms transpose_signs_cancel

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import CoreStability
import PairingBridge

open scoped BigOperators

namespace BrauerHook

def ghostSite (a : ℕ) : SeedSite a := ⟨a+3, by simp [Rank]⟩

theorem core_ghost_slot (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    ((throughOrder (embedCore a 0 p)) (seedSlot a (Fin.last (a+1)))).val =
      ghostSite a := by
  apply Fin.ext
  change slotValue (embedCore a 0 p) (a+1) = a+3
  have hr := p.val.2.isLt
  have hl := p.property
  simp only [Fin.lt_def] at hl
  simp only [slotValue, left_core_value, right_core_value]
  have h₁ : ¬ a+1 < p.val.1.val := by omega
  have h₂ : ¬ a+1+1 < p.val.2.val := by omega
  simp [h₁,h₂]

theorem core_swap_fixes_ghost (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    siteSwapPerm a p (ghostSite a) = ghostSite a := by
  apply (mate_eq_self_iff _ _).mpr
  intro h
  have hr := p.val.2.isLt
  have hl := p.val.1.isLt
  rcases h with h | h <;>
    have e := congrArg Fin.val h <;>
    simp only [ghostSite, left_core_value, right_core_value] at e <;> omega

theorem coreRelabel_fixes_last (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) :
    coreRelabel a e p (Fin.last (a+1)) = Fin.last (a+1) := by
  have h := coreRelabel_site a e p (Fin.last (a+1))
  rw [core_ghost_slot, core_swap_fixes_ghost, ← core_ghost_slot a (swapCup a e p)] at h
  exact (seedSlot a).injective ((throughOrder _).injective (Subtype.ext h))

theorem shared_core_fixes_last (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) b u v) :
    activeTransport s (Fin.last (a+1)) = Fin.last (a+1) := by
  obtain ⟨e,he,hu⟩ := shared_has_swap a p q s
  rw [← coreRelabel_shared a e p q he s hu, coreRelabel_fixes_last]

theorem hookAct_last_sum {d : ℕ} (transpose : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d))
    (hp : p (Fin.last d) = Fin.last d) (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    (∑ j, hookAct transpose p f j) =
      (if transpose then permSign p else 1) * ∑ j, f j := by
  have h := congrFun (expand_hookAct transpose p f) (Fin.last d)
  have hp' : p.symm (Fin.last d) = Fin.last d := by
    apply p.injective
    rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply, hp]
  simp only [expand_last, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, permute, hp', mul_neg] at h
  exact neg_injective h

theorem hookAct_constant {d : ℕ} (transpose : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d))
    (hp : p (Fin.last d) = Fin.last d) (z : ℂ) :
    hookAct transpose p (fun _ => z) = fun _ => (if transpose then permSign p else 1)*z := by
  funext i
  have hn : p.symm i.castSucc ≠ Fin.last d := by
    intro h
    exact i.castSucc_ne_last ((p.apply_symm_apply i.castSucc).symm.trans
      ((congrArg p h).trans hp))
  have hp' : ∃ j : Fin d, j.castSucc = p.symm i.castSucc := by
    have H (t : Letters d) : t ≠ Fin.last d → ∃ j : Fin d, j.castSucc = t :=
      Fin.lastCases (fun h => (h rfl).elim) (fun j _ => ⟨j,rfl⟩) t
    exact H _ hn
  obtain ⟨j,hj⟩ := hp'
  simp [hookAct, permute, ← hj]

/-- Split the ordinary through standard fiber on restriction to permutations of
the first a+1 sites. These are the constant line and the sum-zero part. -/
theorem core_fiber_split (a : ℕ) (f : Fin (a+1) → ℂ) :
    ∃ z : ℂ, ∃ g : Fin (a+1) → ℂ,
      (∑ i, g i) = 0 ∧ f = fun i => z + g i := by
  let s := ∑ i, f i
  let z := s / ((a : ℂ)+1)
  let g := fun i => f i - z
  refine ⟨z,g,?_,?_⟩
  · have hn : (a : ℂ)+1 ≠ 0 := by
      have h : (((a+1 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero _)
      simpa using h
    simp only [g, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ,
      Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
    change s - ((a : ℂ)+1) * (s / ((a : ℂ)+1)) = 0
    rw [mul_div_left_comm, div_self hn, mul_one, sub_self]
  · funext i
    simp [g]

theorem core_relabel_preserves_sum_zero (a : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (e p : CoreCup a)
    (f : Fin (a+1) → ℂ) (hf : (∑ i, f i) = 0) :
    (∑ i, hookAct transpose (coreRelabel a e p) f i) = 0 := by
  rw [hookAct_last_sum transpose _ (coreRelabel_fixes_last a e p), hf, mul_zero]

theorem core_relabel_ordinary_line (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (z : ℂ) :
    hookAct false (coreRelabel a e p) (fun _ => z) = fun _ => z := by
  rw [hookAct_constant false _ (coreRelabel_fixes_last a e p)]
  simp

theorem core_relabel_transpose_line (a : ℕ) (e p : CoreCup a) (z : ℂ) :
    hookAct true (coreRelabel a e p) (fun _ => z) = fun _ => permSign (coreRelabel a e p)*z := by
  rw [hookAct_constant true _ (coreRelabel_fixes_last a e p)]
  rfl

#print axioms core_ghost_slot
#print axioms coreRelabel_fixes_last
#print axioms shared_core_fixes_last
#print axioms core_fiber_split
#print axioms core_relabel_preserves_sum_zero
#print axioms core_relabel_ordinary_line
#print axioms core_relabel_transpose_line

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import CoreRestriction

namespace BrauerHook

abbrev LabelSlots (a : ℕ) := Letters (a+1) ⊕ Bool

def labeledSite (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) : LabelSlots a → SeedSite a
  | .inl i => ((throughOrder c) (seedSlot a i)).val
  | .inr false => left c
  | .inr true => right c

noncomputable def labelCup (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) :
    LabelSlots a ≃ SeedSite a :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (labeledSite a c) ⟨by
    intro i j he
    cases i with
    | inl i =>
      cases j with
      | inl j =>
        congr 1
        exact (seedSlot a).injective ((throughOrder c).injective (Subtype.ext he))
      | inr j =>
        have hfree := (mem_free c _).mp ((throughOrder c) (seedSlot a i)).property
        cases j
        · exact (hfree (Or.inl he)).elim
        · exact (hfree (Or.inr he)).elim
    | inr i =>
      cases j with
      | inl j =>
        have hfree := (mem_free c _).mp ((throughOrder c) (seedSlot a j)).property
        cases i
        · exact (hfree (Or.inl he.symm)).elim
        · exact (hfree (Or.inr he.symm)).elim
      | inr j =>
        cases i <;> cases j
        · rfl
        · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) he).elim
        · exact ((ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)).symm he).elim
        · rfl,
    by
      intro x
      by_cases h : touches c x
      · rcases h with h | h
        · exact ⟨Sum.inr false, h.symm⟩
        · exact ⟨Sum.inr true, h.symm⟩
      · let y : ↥(free c) := ⟨x, (mem_free c x).mpr h⟩
        refine ⟨Sum.inl ((seedSlot a).symm ((throughOrder c).symm y)), ?_⟩
        change ((throughOrder c) (seedSlot a ((seedSlot a).symm ((throughOrder c).symm y)))).val = x
        rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply, OrderIso.apply_symm_apply]⟩

def endpointSite (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (i : Bool) : SeedSite a :=
  if i then right c else left c

theorem endpoint_unique (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) :
    Function.Injective (endpointSite a c) := by
  intro i j he
  cases i <;> cases j
  · rfl
  · exact (ne_of_lt (ends_lt c) he).elim
  · exact ((ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)).symm he).elim
  · rfl

noncomputable def endpointPosition (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0))
    (b : SeedSite a) : Bool := by
  classical
  exact if b = left c then false else true

theorem endpoint_at (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) {b : SeedSite a}
    (h : touches c b) : endpointSite a c (endpointPosition a c b) = b := by
  rcases h with h | h
  · subst b
    simp [endpointPosition, endpointSite]
  · subst b
    simp [endpointPosition, endpointSite, (ne_of_lt (ends_lt c)).symm]

theorem endpoint_other (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) {b v : SeedSite a}
    (hb : touches c b) (hv : touches c v) (hn : b ≠ v) :
    endpointSite a c (!(endpointPosition a c b)) = v := by
  have hc := endpoint_at a c hb
  have hd := endpoint_at a c hv
  have hp : endpointPosition a c b ≠ endpointPosition a c v := by
    intro h
    exact hn ((hc.symm.trans (congrArg (endpointSite a c) h)).trans hd)
  have hnot : Bool.not (endpointPosition a c b) = endpointPosition a c v := by
    generalize he : endpointPosition a c b = x at hp ⊢
    generalize he' : endpointPosition a c v = y at hp ⊢
    cases x <;> cases y <;> simp_all
  rw [hnot, hd]

/-- The permutation on the two endpoint labels of the cup, after physically
swapping the two unmatched sites. -/
noncomputable def endpointPerm (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (b : SeedSite a) :
    Equiv.Perm Bool :=
  Equiv.swap (endpointPosition a c b) (endpointPosition a d b)

theorem endpointPerm_common (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (b : SeedSite a) :
    endpointPerm a c d b (endpointPosition a c b) = endpointPosition a d b :=
  Equiv.swap_apply_left _ _

theorem endpointPerm_other (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (b : SeedSite a) :
    endpointPerm a c d b (!(endpointPosition a c b)) = !(endpointPosition a d b) := by
  let x := endpointPosition a c b
  let y := endpointPosition a d b
  change Equiv.swap x y (!x) = !y
  cases x <;> cases y <;> decide

theorem endpoint_gluing (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) {b u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared c d b u v) (i : Bool) :
    Equiv.swap u v (endpointSite a c i) = endpointSite a d (endpointPerm a c d b i) := by
  have hc := (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hd := (s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hv := (s.source v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  have hu := (s.target u).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
  by_cases hi : i = endpointPosition a c b
  · rw [hi, endpointPerm_common, endpoint_at a c hc, endpoint_at a d hd]
    exact Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne s.distinctBU s.distinctBV
  · have hh : i = Bool.not (endpointPosition a c b) := by
      generalize he : endpointPosition a c b = x at hi ⊢
      cases i <;> cases x <;> simp_all
    rw [hh, endpointPerm_other, endpoint_other a c hc hv s.distinctBV,
      endpoint_other a d hd hu s.distinctBU, Equiv.swap_apply_right]

theorem transport_label_decomposition (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0))
    {b u v : SeedSite a} (s : Shared c d b u v) :
    (labelCup a c).trans ((Equiv.swap u v).trans (labelCup a d).symm) =
      Equiv.sumCongr (activeTransport s) (endpointPerm a c d b) := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro z
  apply (labelCup a d).injective
  rw [Equiv.trans_apply, Equiv.trans_apply, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
  cases z with
  | inl i =>
    change Equiv.swap u v ((throughOrder c) (seedSlot a i)).val =
      ((throughOrder d) (seedSlot a (activeTransport s i))).val
    have h := transport_site s (liftSlot a 0 i)
    rw [← activeTransport_commutes] at h
    exact h.symm
  | inr i =>
    have ht (p : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (k : Bool) :
        labeledSite a p (.inr k) = endpointSite a p k := by cases k <;> rfl
    change Equiv.swap u v (labeledSite a c (.inr i)) =
      labeledSite a d (.inr (endpointPerm a c d b i))
    rw [ht,ht]
    exact endpoint_gluing a c d s i


noncomputable def signC {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] (p : Equiv.Perm α) : ℂ :=
  ((p.sign : ℤ) : ℂ)

theorem signC_mul {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] (p q : Equiv.Perm α) :
    signC (p*q) = signC p * signC q := by simp [signC]

theorem signC_trans {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] (p q : Equiv.Perm α) :
    signC (p.trans q) = signC q * signC p := signC_mul q p

theorem signC_sq {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] (p : Equiv.Perm α) :
    signC p * signC p = 1 := by
  unfold signC
  rw [← Int.cast_mul]
  have h := Int.units_eq_one_or p.sign
  rcases h with h | h <;> simp [h]

theorem signC_symm {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] (p : Equiv.Perm α) :
    signC p.symm = signC p := by
  have h := signC_mul p p⁻¹
  rw [mul_inv_cancel] at h
  have h₁ : signC (1 : Equiv.Perm α) = 1 := by simp [signC]
  rw [h₁] at h
  change signC p⁻¹ = signC p
  calc
    signC p⁻¹ = (signC p * signC p)*signC p⁻¹ := by rw [signC_sq, one_mul]
    _ = signC p := by rw [mul_assoc, ← h, mul_one]

theorem signC_sumCongr {α β : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β]
    (p : Equiv.Perm α) (q : Equiv.Perm β) :
    signC (Equiv.sumCongr p q) = signC p * signC q := by simp [signC, Equiv.Perm.sign_sumCongr]

theorem signC_swap_ne {α : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] {u v : α} (hn : u ≠ v) :
    signC (Equiv.swap u v) = -1 := by simp [signC,hn]

noncomputable def referenceLabels (a : ℕ) : LabelSlots a ≃ SeedSite a :=
  labelCup a (embedTail a 0 0)

noncomputable def orientPerm (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) : Equiv.Perm (LabelSlots a) :=
  (referenceLabels a).trans (labelCup a c).symm

noncomputable def cupOrientation (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) : ℂ :=
  signC (orientPerm a c)

theorem cupOrientation_sq (a : ℕ) (c : Cup a (Rank a 0)) :
    cupOrientation a c * cupOrientation a c = 1 := signC_sq _

theorem orientation_first_tail (a : ℕ) : cupOrientation a (embedTail a 0 0) = 1 := by
  unfold cupOrientation orientPerm referenceLabels
  rw [Equiv.self_trans_symm]
  simp [signC]

private theorem swap_in_ref (a : ℕ) (u v : SeedSite a) :
    (referenceLabels a).trans ((Equiv.swap u v).trans (referenceLabels a).symm) =
      Equiv.swap ((referenceLabels a).symm u) ((referenceLabels a).symm v) := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro z
  apply (referenceLabels a).injective
  rw [Equiv.trans_apply, Equiv.trans_apply, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
  by_cases hu : z = (referenceLabels a).symm u
  · subst z
    simp
  · by_cases hv : z = (referenceLabels a).symm v
    · subst z
      simp
    · have hu' : referenceLabels a z ≠ u := fun h => hu ((Equiv.apply_eq_iff_eq _).mp
        (h.trans ((referenceLabels a).apply_symm_apply u).symm))
      have hv' : referenceLabels a z ≠ v := fun h => hv ((Equiv.apply_eq_iff_eq _).mp
        (h.trans ((referenceLabels a).apply_symm_apply v).symm))
      rw [Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hu hv, Equiv.swap_apply_of_ne_of_ne hu' hv']

/-- Sign of the through permutation in the native transposed hook, including
the orientation correction from the endpoint positions. -/
theorem through_sign_orientation (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0))
    {b u v : SeedSite a} (s : Shared c d b u v) :
    permSign (activeTransport s) =
      -(cupOrientation a d) * cupOrientation a c * signC (endpointPerm a c d b) := by
  let ref := referenceLabels a
  let sRef : Equiv.Perm (LabelSlots a) := ref.trans ((Equiv.swap u v).trans ref.symm)
  have hr : signC sRef = -1 := by
    change signC ((referenceLabels a).trans ((Equiv.swap u v).trans (referenceLabels a).symm)) = _
    rw [swap_in_ref, signC_swap_ne (fun he => s.distinctUV (ref.symm.injective he))]
  have h :
      (labelCup a c).trans ((Equiv.swap u v).trans (labelCup a d).symm) =
      ((orientPerm a c).symm.trans sRef).trans (orientPerm a d) := by
    apply Equiv.ext
    intro z
    simp [orientPerm,sRef,ref]
  rw [transport_label_decomposition a c d s] at h
  have hh := congrArg signC h
  rw [signC_sumCongr, signC_trans, signC_trans, hr, signC_symm] at hh
  change permSign (activeTransport s) * signC (endpointPerm a c d b) =
    cupOrientation a d * (-1 * cupOrientation a c) at hh
  calc
    permSign (activeTransport s) =
        (permSign (activeTransport s) * signC (endpointPerm a c d b)) *
          signC (endpointPerm a c d b) := by rw [mul_assoc, signC_sq, mul_one]
    _ = _ := by rw [hh]; ring

#print axioms cupOrientation_sq
#print axioms orientation_first_tail
#print axioms through_sign_orientation

#print axioms labelCup
#print axioms endpoint_gluing
#print axioms transport_label_decomposition

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import CoreRestriction
import Mathlib.Data.Finset.Powerset
import Mathlib.Data.Nat.Choose.Basic

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

def incident {a : ℕ} (c : CoreCup a) (i : CoreSite a) : Prop :=
  i = c.val.1 ∨ i = c.val.2

instance {a : ℕ} (c : CoreCup a) : DecidablePred (incident c) :=
  fun i => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (i = c.val.1 ∨ i = c.val.2))

theorem incident_mem {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) :
    incident p z ↔ z ∈ ({p.val.1,p.val.2} : Finset (CoreSite a)) := by
  simp only [incident, Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton]

theorem incident_embed {a : ℕ} (c : CoreCup a) (i : CoreSite a) :
    touches (embedCore a 0 c) (injectCoreSite a i) ↔ incident c i := by
  change (injectCoreSite a i = injectCoreSite a c.val.1 ∨
    injectCoreSite a i = injectCoreSite a c.val.2) ↔ _
  simp only [(injectCoreSite_inj a).eq_iff, incident]

theorem incident_sorted {a : ℕ} {i j : CoreSite a} (h : i ≠ j) (z : CoreSite a) :
    incident (sortedCorePair a i j h) z ↔ z=i ∨ z=j := by
  rw [← incident_embed, sortedCorePair_touches]
  simp only [(injectCoreSite_inj a).eq_iff]

theorem core_sites_touch_iff {a : ℕ} (p q : CoreCup a) :
    (∃ z : SeedSite a, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z ∧ touches (embedCore a 0 q) z) ↔
      ∃ z : CoreSite a, incident p z ∧ incident q z := by
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨z, hp, hq⟩
    rcases hp with hp | hp
    · subst z
      exact ⟨p.val.1, Or.inl rfl, (incident_embed q _).mp hq⟩
    · subst z
      exact ⟨p.val.2, Or.inr rfl, (incident_embed q _).mp hq⟩
  · rintro ⟨z,hp,hq⟩
    exact ⟨injectCoreSite a z, (incident_embed p z).mpr hp, (incident_embed q z).mpr hq⟩

theorem core_pair_by_both {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) {i j : CoreSite a}
    (hn : i ≠ j) (hi : incident p i) (hj : incident p j) :
    p = sortedCorePair a i j hn := by
  apply coreCup_eq_touches
  intro z
  rw [sortedCorePair_touches]
  rcases hi with hi | hi <;> rcases hj with hj | hj
  · exact (hn (hi.trans hj.symm)).elim
  · rw [hi,hj]; rfl
  · rw [hi,hj]; exact or_comm
  · exact (hn (hi.trans hj.symm)).elim

noncomputable def corePairSetEquiv (a : ℕ) :
    CoreCup a ≃ ↥((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreSite a)).powersetCard 2) :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (fun p => ⟨{p.val.1,p.val.2}, by
    simp [Finset.mem_powersetCard, ne_of_lt p.property]⟩)
    ⟨by
      intro p q h
      have hset : ({p.val.1,p.val.2} : Finset (CoreSite a)) = {q.val.1,q.val.2} :=
        congrArg Subtype.val h
      have h₁ : incident q p.val.1 := by
        rw [incident_mem]
        rw [← hset]; simp
      have h₂ : incident q p.val.2 := by
        rw [incident_mem]
        rw [← hset]; simp
      exact (core_pair_by_both p (ne_of_lt p.property) (Or.inl rfl) (Or.inr rfl)).trans
        (core_pair_by_both q (ne_of_lt p.property) h₁ h₂).symm,
      by
        intro q
        obtain ⟨i,j,hn,hq⟩ := Finset.card_eq_two.mp (Finset.mem_powersetCard.mp q.property).2
        refine ⟨sortedCorePair a i j hn, Subtype.ext ?_⟩
        ext z
        rw [hq]
        rw [← incident_mem]
        simpa only [Finset.mem_insert, Finset.mem_singleton] using incident_sorted hn z⟩

theorem card_core (a : ℕ) : Fintype.card (CoreCup a) = (a+3).choose 2 := by
  rw [Fintype.card_congr (corePairSetEquiv a), Fintype.card_coe, Finset.card_powersetCard]
  simp

def pairIncidence (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) ℂ :=
  fun c i => if incident c i then 1 else 0

noncomputable def lineAdj (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ := by
  classical
  exact fun p q => if p = q then 0 else if ∃ s : CoreSite a, incident p s ∧ incident q s then 1 else 0

theorem pair_incidence_rows (a : ℕ) :
    pairIncidence a * (pairIncidence a).transpose = (2 : ℂ) • 1 + lineAdj a := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro p q
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  by_cases he : p = q
  · subst q
    have hn := ne_of_lt p.property
    have hs : (∑ i : CoreSite a, if incident p i then (1:ℂ) else 0) = 2 := by
      rw [← Finset.sum_filter]
      have hs : ((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreSite a)).filter (incident p)) = {p.val.1,p.val.2} := by
        ext z
        simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, incident_mem]
      rw [hs]
      simp [hn]
    have h : (∑ i : CoreSite a, pairIncidence a p i * (pairIncidence a).transpose i p) =
        ∑ i : CoreSite a, if incident p i then (1:ℂ) else 0 := by
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro i _
      by_cases hi : incident p i <;> simp [pairIncidence, hi]
    rw [h,hs]
    simp [lineAdj]
  · by_cases h : ∃ i : CoreSite a, incident p i ∧ incident q i
    · obtain ⟨i,hi,hj⟩ := h
      rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
      · simp [pairIncidence, lineAdj, he, hi, hj, show ∃ x, incident p x ∧ incident q x from ⟨i,hi,hj⟩]
      · intro z _ hz
        by_cases hp : incident p z
        · have hq : ¬ incident q z := fun hq => he ((core_pair_by_both p hz hp hi).trans
            (core_pair_by_both q hz hq hj).symm)
          simp [pairIncidence,hq]
        · simp [pairIncidence,hp]
      · simp
    · have term (z : CoreSite a) : pairIncidence a p z * (pairIncidence a).transpose z q = 0 := by
        by_cases hp : incident p z
        · have hq : ¬ incident q z := fun hq => h ⟨z,hp,hq⟩
          simp [pairIncidence, hq]
        · simp [pairIncidence,hp]
      simp only [term, Finset.sum_const_zero]
      simp [lineAdj, he, h]

private theorem incident_count (a : ℕ) (i : CoreSite a) :
    ((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreCup a)).filter (fun p => incident p i)).card = a+2 := by
  classical
  let E : {j : CoreSite a // j ≠ i} ≃
      ↥((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreCup a)).filter (fun p => incident p i)) :=
    Equiv.ofBijective (fun j => ⟨sortedCorePair a j.val i j.property, by
      simp [incident_sorted]⟩) ⟨by
        intro j l h
        have he : sortedCorePair a j.val i j.property = sortedCorePair a l.val i l.property :=
          congrArg Subtype.val h
        apply Subtype.ext
        have hj : incident (sortedCorePair a l.val i l.property) j.val := by
          rw [← he, incident_sorted]
          exact Or.inl rfl
        exact ((incident_sorted l.property _).mp hj).resolve_right j.property,
      by
        intro p
        have hp := (Finset.mem_filter.mp p.property).2
        rcases hp with hl | hr
        · have hn : p.val.val.2 ≠ i := fun hh => (ne_of_lt p.val.property) (hl.symm.trans hh.symm)
          exact ⟨⟨p.val.val.2,hn⟩, Subtype.ext
            (core_pair_by_both p.val hn (Or.inr rfl) (Or.inl hl)).symm⟩
        · have hn : p.val.val.1 ≠ i := fun hh => (ne_of_lt p.val.property) (hh.trans hr)
          exact ⟨⟨p.val.val.1,hn⟩, Subtype.ext
            (core_pair_by_both p.val hn (Or.inl rfl) (Or.inr hr)).symm⟩⟩
  rw [← Fintype.card_coe, ← Fintype.card_congr E, Fintype.card_subtype_compl]
  simp

theorem pair_incidence_cols (a : ℕ) :
    (pairIncidence a).transpose * pairIncidence a =
      ((a : ℂ)+1) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) ℂ) +
        Matrix.of (fun _ _ : CoreSite a => (1 : ℂ)) := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  by_cases h : i = j
  · subst j
    have hc : (∑ p : CoreCup a, if incident p i then (1:ℂ) else 0) = a+2 := by
      rw [← Finset.sum_filter]
      simp [incident_count, Nat.cast_add]
    have he : (∑ p : CoreCup a, (pairIncidence a).transpose i p * pairIncidence a p i) =
        ∑ p : CoreCup a, if incident p i then (1:ℂ) else 0 := by
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro p _
      by_cases hp : incident p i <;> simp [pairIncidence,hp]
    rw [he,hc]
    simp
    ring
  · let p := sortedCorePair a i j h
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single p]
    · have hi : incident p i := (incident_sorted h i).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
      have hj : incident p j := (incident_sorted h j).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
      simp [pairIncidence,hi,hj,h]
    · intro q _ hq
      by_cases hi : incident q i
      · have hj : ¬incident q j := fun hj => hq (core_pair_by_both q h hi hj)
        simp [pairIncidence,hj]
      · simp [pairIncidence,hi]
    · simp

#print axioms card_core
#print axioms pair_incidence_rows
#print axioms pair_incidence_cols

end BrauerHook
```
