```lean
import CoreIncidence

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

private theorem expand_add_coord {d : ℕ} (f g : Fin d → ℂ) :
    expand (f + g) = expand f + expand g :=
  congrArg Subtype.val ((standardCoords d).map_add f g)

private theorem expand_smul_coord {d : ℕ} (c : ℂ) (f : Fin d → ℂ) :
    expand (c • f) = c • expand f :=
  congrArg Subtype.val ((standardCoords d).map_smul c f)

noncomputable def hookLinear {d : ℕ} (b : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) :
    (Fin d → ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] (Fin d → ℂ) where
  toFun := hookAct b p
  map_add' f g := by
    funext i
    simp [hookAct, permute, expand_add_coord, mul_add]
  map_smul' c f := by
    funext i
    simp [hookAct, permute, expand_smul_coord, mul_left_comm]

theorem hookAct_deltas_sum {d : ℕ} (b : Bool) (p : Equiv.Perm (Letters d)) (i : Fin d) :
    (∑ j, hookAct b p (delta j) i) = hookAct b p (fun _ => 1) i := by
  classical
  have h : (∑ j : Fin d, delta j) = fun _ => (1 : ℂ) := by
    funext q
    simp [delta]
  have he := congrArg (hookLinear b p) h
  rw [map_sum] at he
  have hi := congrFun he i
  simpa only [Finset.sum_apply, hookLinear, LinearMap.coe_mk, AddHom.coe_mk] using hi

theorem ordinary_line_entries (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (∑ j : Fin (a+1), nativeMove a 0 false (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i) =
      lineAdj a q p := by
  classical
  by_cases he : p = q
  · subst q
    simp [nativeMove, lineAdj]
  · have hn : embedCore a 0 p ≠ embedCore a 0 q := fun h => he (core_injective a 0 h)
    obtain h | hd | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q)
    · exact (hn h).elim
    · have hh : ¬ ∃ z : CoreSite a, incident q z ∧ incident p z := by
        rw [← core_sites_touch_iff]
        rintro ⟨z,hq,hp⟩
        exact hd z hq hp
      simp [nativeMove, hn, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd, lineAdj, Ne.symm he, hh]
    · have hh : ∃ z : CoreSite a, incident q z ∧ incident p z := by
        rw [← core_sites_touch_iff]
        exact ⟨b,(s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl), (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)⟩
      simp only [nativeMove, hn, ite_false, mixedTransport_shared s, lineAdj, Ne.symm he,
        hh, ite_true]
      rw [hookAct_deltas_sum, hookAct_constant false _ (shared_core_fixes_last a p q s)]
      simp

noncomputable def lineInclusion (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreCup a) ℂ :=
  fun i p => if i.1 = p then 1 else 0

noncomputable def actualCore (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ :=
  (interaction a 0 b).submatrix (coreInc a 0) (coreInc a 0)

/-- The ordinary constant-line summand of the actual native core intertwines
with the incidence-derived Johnson adjacency. -/
theorem ordinary_line_intertwines (a : ℕ) :
    actualCore a false * lineInclusion a = lineInclusion a * lineAdj a := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i p
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Fintype.sum_prod_type, Matrix.mul_apply]
  have L (q : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
      actualCore a false i (q,j) * lineInclusion a (q,j) p =
        if q = p then nativeMove a 0 false (embedCore a 0 q) (embedCore a 0 i.1) j i.2 else 0 := by
    by_cases h : q = p <;> simp [lineInclusion, actualCore, interaction, coreInc, h]
  have R (q : CoreCup a) : lineInclusion a i q * lineAdj a q p =
      if i.1 = q then lineAdj a q p else 0 := by
    by_cases h : i.1 = q <;> simp [lineInclusion,h]
  simp_rw [L, R, Finset.sum_ite_irrel, Finset.sum_const_zero]
  have hL : (∑ q : CoreCup a, if q=p then
      ∑ j : Fin (a+1), nativeMove a 0 false (embedCore a 0 q) (embedCore a 0 i.1) j i.2 else 0) =
        ∑ j : Fin (a+1), nativeMove a 0 false (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 i.1) j i.2 := by
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single p]
    · simp
    · intro b _ hb; simp [hb]
    · simp
  have hR : (∑ q : CoreCup a, if i.1 = q then lineAdj a q p else 0) = lineAdj a i.1 p := by
    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i.1]
    · simp
    · intro b _ hb; simp [Ne.symm hb]
    · simp
  rw [hL,hR,ordinary_line_entries]

section DeterminantTransfer
variable {K α β : Type*} [Field K]
variable [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β]

private theorem shifted_product_det (A : Matrix α β K) (B : Matrix β α K)
    (x : K) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + A*B).det * x^(Fintype.card β) =
      (x • (1 : Matrix β β K) + B*A).det * x^(Fintype.card α) := by
  have hA : x • (1 + (x⁻¹ • A) * B) = x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + A * B := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.smul_mul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  have hB : x • (1 + B * (x⁻¹ • A)) = x • (1 : Matrix β β K) + B * A := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.mul_smul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  rw [← hA, ← hB, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_one_add_mul_comm]
  ring

private theorem shifted_ones_det [Nonempty α] (x : K) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1 : K))).det =
      x^(Fintype.card α-1) * (x + (Fintype.card α : K)) := by
  let A : Matrix α Unit K := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)
  let B : Matrix Unit α K := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)
  have hAB : A*B = Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1:K)) := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply,A,B]
  have hBA : B*A = Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (Fintype.card α : K)) := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply,A,B]
  have hs := shifted_product_det A B x hx
  rw [hAB,hBA] at hs
  simp only [Fintype.card_unit, pow_one, Matrix.det_unique, Matrix.add_apply,
    Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, ite_true, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, Matrix.of_apply] at hs
  have hp : 0 < Fintype.card α := Fintype.card_pos
  have he : x^(Fintype.card α) = x^(Fintype.card α-1)*x := by
    nth_rw 1 [show Fintype.card α = (Fintype.card α-1)+1 by omega]
    rw [pow_succ]
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ hx
  rw [hs, he]
  ring

end DeterminantTransfer

noncomputable def lineChar (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X] :=
  (X : ℂ[X]) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X]) + C.mapMatrix (lineAdj a)

private theorem map_smul_one {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S]
    {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] (f : R →+* S) (r : R) :
    f.mapMatrix (r • (1 : Matrix ι ι R)) = f r • (1 : Matrix ι ι S) := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j <;> simp [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, h]

private theorem map_pair_incidence_rows (a : ℕ) :
    (pairIncidence a).map (fun x => lp (C x)) *
      (pairIncidence a).transpose.map (fun x => lp (C x)) =
      (lp (2 : ℂ[X])) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) +
        lp.mapMatrix (C.mapMatrix (lineAdj a)) := by
  let f : ℂ →+* LoopField := lp.comp C
  change (pairIncidence a).map f * (pairIncidence a).transpose.map f =
    lp (2 : ℂ[X]) • _ + _
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := f), pair_incidence_rows]
  change f.mapMatrix ((2 : ℂ) • 1 + lineAdj a) = _
  rw [map_add, map_smul_one]
  simp [f, map_ofNat]

private theorem map_pair_incidence_cols (a : ℕ) :
    (pairIncidence a).transpose.map (fun x => lp (C x)) *
      (pairIncidence a).map (fun x => lp (C x)) =
      (lp (C ((a:ℂ)+1))) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) +
        Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1 : LoopField)) := by
  let f : ℂ →+* LoopField := lp.comp C
  change (pairIncidence a).transpose.map f * (pairIncidence a).map f =
    lp (C ((a:ℂ)+1)) • _ + _
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := f), pair_incidence_cols]
  change f.mapMatrix (((a : ℂ)+1) • 1 + Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)) = _
  rw [map_add, map_smul_one]
  congr 1
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  simp [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply]

/-- Exact characteristic factor of the ordinary constant-line native component. -/
theorem ordinary_line_charpoly (a : ℕ) :
    (lineChar a).det =
      (X+C (2*((a : ℂ)+1))) * (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1))^(a+2) *
        (X-2)^((a+3)*a/2) := by
  classical
  let x := lp (X-2)
  let y := lp (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1))
  have hx : x ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact (monic_X_sub_C (2:ℂ)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  have hy : y ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact (monic_X_add_C ((a:ℂ)-1)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  let L := (pairIncidence a).map (fun x => lp (C x))
  let R := (pairIncidence a).transpose.map (fun x => lp (C x))
  have h₁ : x • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) + L*R =
      lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) := by
    rw [show L*R = _ from map_pair_incidence_rows a, lineChar, map_add,
      map_smul_one lp (X : ℂ[X]), ← add_assoc, ← add_smul]
    congr 1
    apply congrArg (fun t : LoopField => t • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField))
    change lp (X-2) + lp (2 : ℂ[X]) = lp X
    rw [← map_add, sub_add_cancel]
  have h₂ : x • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) + R*L =
      y • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) + Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1) := by
    rw [show R*L = _ from map_pair_incidence_cols a, ← add_assoc, ← add_smul]
    congr 1
    apply congrArg (fun t : LoopField => t • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField))
    change lp (X-2) + lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)) = lp (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1))
    rw [← map_add]
    congr 1
    simp only [map_sub, map_add, map_one]
    ring
  have hcard : Fintype.card (CoreCup a) = (a+3)+((a+3)*a/2) := by
    rw [card_core, Nat.choose_two_right, show a+3-1 = a+2 by omega]
    have e : (a+3)*(a+2) = (a+3)*a + 2*(a+3) := by ring
    omega
  have hs := shifted_product_det L R x hx
  rw [h₁,h₂,shifted_ones_det y hy, ← RingHom.map_det,
    Fintype.card_fin, hcard, pow_add x (a+3) ((a+3)*a/2)] at hs
  have ht : lp (lineChar a).det =
      y^(a+3-1) * (y + ((a+3 : ℕ) : LoopField)) * x^((a+3)*a/2) := by
    apply mul_right_cancel₀ (pow_ne_zero (a+3) hx)
    rw [hs]
    ring
  apply lp_injective
  rw [ht, map_mul, map_mul, map_pow, map_pow]
  have he : y + ((a+3 : ℕ) : LoopField) = lp (X+C (2*((a : ℂ)+1))) := by
    change lp (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1)) + _ = _
    have hn : (((a+3 : ℕ) : LoopField)) = lp (C (((a+3 : ℕ) : ℂ))) := by
      rw [map_natCast, map_natCast]
    rw [hn, ← map_add]
    apply congrArg lp
    rw [add_assoc, ← C_add]
    congr 1
    push_cast
    ring
  rw [he, show a+3-1=a+2 by omega]
  change y^(a+2) * _ * x^((a+3)*a/2) = _ * y^(a+2) * x^((a+3)*a/2)
  rw [mul_comm (y^(a+2))]

#print axioms ordinary_line_intertwines
#print axioms ordinary_line_charpoly

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import OrdinaryLine

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

def lastCore (a : ℕ) : CoreSite a := Fin.last (a+2)

def isMarked {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) : Prop :=
  p.val.2 = lastCore a

instance {a : ℕ} : DecidablePred (@isMarked a) :=
  fun p => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (p.val.2 = lastCore a))

def markedPair (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) : CoreCup a :=
  ⟨(t.castSucc, lastCore a), by
    change t.val < a+2
    exact t.isLt⟩

def markedEmbedding (a : ℕ) : Fin (a+2) ↪ CoreCup a :=
  ⟨markedPair a, fun _ _ h => Fin.ext (congrArg (fun p : CoreCup a => p.val.1.val) h)⟩

theorem image_marked_pair (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    isMarked p ↔ ∃ i : Fin (a+2), markedPair a i = p := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    have hlt := p.property
    change p.val.2 = lastCore a at h
    rw [h] at hlt
    let i : Fin (a+2) := ⟨p.val.1.val, hlt⟩
    exact ⟨i, Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (Fin.ext rfl) h.symm)⟩
  · rintro ⟨i,rfl⟩
    rfl

theorem sum_marked_pairs {K : Type*} [AddCommMonoid K] (a : ℕ) (f : CoreCup a → K) :
    (∑ p : CoreCup a, if isMarked p then f p else 0) =
      ∑ i : Fin (a+2), f (markedPair a i) := by
  classical
  rw [← Finset.sum_filter]
  have he : ((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreCup a)).filter isMarked) =
      (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (a+2))).map (markedEmbedding a) := by
    ext p
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, Finset.mem_map]
    exact image_marked_pair a p
  rw [he, Finset.sum_map]
  rfl

def incidenceOver {K : Type*} [Zero K] [One K] (a : ℕ) :
    Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) K :=
  fun p i => if incident p i then 1 else 0

def markedLift {K : Type*} [Zero K] (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → K) : CoreCup a → K :=
  fun p => if isMarked p then v p.val.1 else 0

theorem incidence_mulVec_eval {K : Type*} [CommRing K] (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → K)
    (p : CoreCup a) :
    (incidenceOver a *ᵥ v) p = v p.val.1 + v p.val.2 := by
  classical
  change (∑ j : CoreSite a, (if incident p j then (1:K) else 0)*v j) = _
  simp_rw [ite_mul, one_mul, zero_mul]
  rw [← Finset.sum_filter]
  have h : ((Finset.univ : Finset (CoreSite a)).filter (incident p)) = {p.val.1,p.val.2} := by
    ext z
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_univ, true_and, incident_mem]
  rw [h]
  simp [ne_of_lt p.property]

/-- Actual marked cups lift a sum-zero vector on the old sites. Its
incidence push-forward is exactly that vector, extended by zero at m. -/
theorem incidence_transpose_marked {K : Type*} [CommRing K] (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → K)
    (hg : v (lastCore a) = 0) (hz : (∑ t, v t) = 0) :
    (incidenceOver (K := K) a).transpose *ᵥ markedLift a v = v := by
  classical
  funext i
  change (∑ p : CoreCup a, (if incident p i then (1:K) else 0) *
      (if isMarked p then v p.val.1 else 0)) = v i
  have h (p : CoreCup a) :
      (if incident p i then (1:K) else 0) * (if isMarked p then v p.val.1 else 0) =
        if isMarked p then (if incident p i then v p.val.1 else 0) else 0 := by
    by_cases h₁ : incident p i <;> by_cases h₂ : isMarked p <;> simp [h₁,h₂]
  simp_rw [h, sum_marked_pairs]
  have H (j : Fin (a+2)) : incident (markedPair a j) i ↔ i = j.castSucc ∨ i = lastCore a := Iff.rfl
  simp_rw [H]
  by_cases hi : i = lastCore a
  · rw [hi]
    simp only [or_true, ite_true]
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc] at hz
    change v (Fin.last (a+2)) = 0 at hg
    rw [hg, add_zero] at hz
    exact hz.trans hg.symm
  · simp only [hi, or_false]
    have H' (j : CoreSite a) : j ≠ lastCore a → ∃ t : Fin (a+2), t.castSucc = j :=
      Fin.lastCases (fun h => (h rfl).elim) (fun j _ => ⟨j,rfl⟩) j
    obtain ⟨t,rfl⟩ := H' i hi
    simp [(Fin.castSucc_injective _).eq_iff, markedPair]

/-- The incidence pair of test vectors on the ordinary marked boundary.
This identifies the two-dimensional space in which the boundary seed
is solved on the constant-line summand. -/
theorem marked_boundary_test_vectors (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → LoopField)
    (hg : v (lastCore a) = 0) (hz : (∑ t, v t) = 0) :
    let L := incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a
    L.transpose *ᵥ markedLift a v = v ∧
    ∀ i : Fin (a+2), (L *ᵥ v) (markedPair a i) = v i.castSucc := by
  refine ⟨incidence_transpose_marked a v hg hz, ?_⟩
  intro i
  have h := incidence_mulVec_eval a v (markedPair a i)
  simpa only [markedPair, hg, add_zero] using h


private theorem incidence_loop_cols (a : ℕ) :
    (incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a).transpose * incidenceOver a =
      lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) +
        Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1 : LoopField)) := by
  let f : ℂ →+* LoopField := lp.comp C
  have ho : incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a = (pairIncidence a).map f := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro p i
    by_cases h : incident p i <;> simp [incidenceOver, pairIncidence, Matrix.map_apply, h]
  have ht : ((pairIncidence a).map f).transpose = (pairIncidence a).transpose.map f := rfl
  rw [ho, ht, ← Matrix.map_mul (f := f), pair_incidence_cols]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j <;> simp [Matrix.map_apply, h,f]

theorem incidence_cols_sumzero (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → LoopField)
    (hz : (∑ i, v i) = 0) :
    ((incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a).transpose * incidenceOver a) *ᵥ v =
      lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)) • v := by
  rw [incidence_loop_cols, Matrix.add_mulVec, Matrix.smul_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec]
  have h : (Matrix.of (fun _ _ : CoreSite a => (1 : LoopField))) *ᵥ v = 0 := by
    funext i
    change (∑ j, 1 * v j) = 0
    simpa only [one_mul] using hz
  rw [h, add_zero]

/-- Resolvent solution on the actual ordinary marked-pair incidence plane. -/
theorem ordinary_marked_line_solution (a : ℕ) (v : CoreSite a → LoopField)
    (hg : v (lastCore a) = 0) (hz : (∑ i, v i) = 0)
    (x : LoopField) (hx : x ≠ 0) (hw : x + lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)) ≠ 0) :
    let L := incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a
    let w := x + lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1))
    let g := x⁻¹ • markedLift a v - (x*w)⁻¹ • (L *ᵥ v)
    ((x • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) + L*L.transpose) *ᵥ g =
      markedLift a v) ∧
      ∀ i : Fin (a+2), g (markedPair a i) = ((w-1)/(x*w)) * v i.castSucc := by
  dsimp only
  let L := incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a
  let μ := lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1))
  let w := x + μ
  let D := x • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) + L*L.transpose
  have hA : D *ᵥ markedLift a v = x • markedLift a v + L *ᵥ v := by
    dsimp only [D]
    rw [Matrix.add_mulVec, Matrix.smul_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec,
      ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, incidence_transpose_marked a v hg hz]
  have hB : D *ᵥ (L *ᵥ v) = x • (L *ᵥ v) + μ • (L *ᵥ v) := by
    dsimp only [D]
    rw [Matrix.add_mulVec, Matrix.smul_mulVec, Matrix.one_mulVec,
      ← Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec _ L.transpose L,
      incidence_cols_sumzero a v hz, Matrix.mulVec_smul]
  refine ⟨?_,?_⟩
  · change D *ᵥ (x⁻¹ • markedLift a v - (x*w)⁻¹ • (L *ᵥ v)) = markedLift a v
    rw [Matrix.mulVec_sub, Matrix.mulVec_smul, Matrix.mulVec_smul, hA,hB]
    funext i
    simp only [Pi.sub_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, Pi.add_apply]
    have hw' : w ≠ 0 := hw
    have he : x + μ = w := rfl
    rw [← add_mul, he]

    have h := (marked_boundary_test_vectors a v hg hz).2 i

    simp only [Pi.sub_apply, Pi.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, h, hm]

#print axioms sum_marked_pairs
#print axioms incidence_transpose_marked
#print axioms marked_boundary_test_vectors

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import OrdinaryMarked

open scoped BigOperators
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

theorem activeTransport_inverse {a k : ℕ} {c d : Cup a (Rank a k)}
    {b u v : Fin (Rank a k)} (s : Shared c d b u v) (t : Shared d c b v u) :
    activeTransport t = (activeTransport s).symm := by
  apply Equiv.ext
  intro i
  apply (activeTransport s).injective
  rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
  apply liftSlot_injective a k
  rw [activeTransport_commutes, activeTransport_commutes, transport_reverse s t,
    Equiv.apply_symm_apply]

theorem markedPair_right_value (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) :
    (markedPair a t).val.2.val = a+2 := rfl

theorem marked_ghost_image (a k : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) :
    activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a k (markedPair a t)
      (markedPair_right_value a t)) (Fin.last (a+1)) = t := by
  apply Fin.ext
  rw [marked_to_first_tail a k _ (markedPair_right_value a t)]
  have h := t.isLt
  change (if a+1 < t.val then a+1 else
      if a+1 < a+1 then a+1+1 else t.val) = t.val
  have hn : ¬ a+1 < t.val := by omega
  simp [hn]

private theorem entry_in_marked (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2))
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
    boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t,i) j =
      C (hookAct false (activeTransport s).symm (delta j) i) := by
  dsimp only
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  let sr := Shared.mk s.distinctBV s.distinctBU s.distinctUV.symm s.target s.source
  unfold boundaryIn
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp only [zero_add,coreInc,tailInc]
    rw [nativeMove, ite_eq_right (Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0)),
      mixedTransport_shared sr, activeTransport_inverse s sr]
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)

private theorem entry_out_marked (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2))
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
    boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t,j) =
      C (hookAct false (activeTransport s) (delta j) i) := by
  dsimp only
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  unfold boundaryOut
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp only [zero_add,coreInc,tailInc]
    rw [nativeMove, ite_eq_right (core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0), mixedTransport_shared s]
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)

theorem ordinary_marked_in_column (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    (∑ i : Fin (a+1), boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t,i) j) =
      -C (expand (delta j) t) := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  simp_rw [entry_in_marked, ← map_sum]
  have ht := congrFun (expand_hookAct false (activeTransport s).symm (delta j)) (Fin.last (a+1))
  simp only [expand_last, Bool.false_eq_true, ite_false, one_smul, permute,
    Equiv.symm_symm, marked_ghost_image] at ht
  rw [← map_neg]
  exact congrArg C (by simpa only [neg_neg] using congrArg Neg.neg ht)

theorem ordinary_marked_out_row (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (∑ j : Fin (a+1), boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t,j)) =
      C (if i.castSucc = t then -((a : ℂ)+1) else 1) := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  let p := activeTransport s
  simp_rw [entry_out_marked, ← map_sum, hookAct_deltas_sum]
  congr 1
  simp only [hookAct, Bool.false_eq_true, ite_false, one_mul, permute]
  change expand (fun _ : Fin (a+1) => (1:ℂ)) (p.symm i.castSucc) = _
  have he : p.symm i.castSucc = Fin.last (a+1) ↔ i.castSucc = t := by
    rw [Equiv.symm_apply_eq]
    exact Eq.congr_right (marked_ghost_image a 0 t)
  by_cases hi : i.castSucc = t
  · rw [ite_eq_left hi, he.mpr hi, expand_last]
    simp [Nat.cast_add]
  · have hn : p.symm i.castSucc ≠ Fin.last (a+1) := fun hh => hi (he.mp hh)
    have H (x : Letters (a+1)) : x ≠ Fin.last (a+1) → ∃ j : Fin (a+1), j.castSucc = x :=
      Fin.lastCases (fun h => (h rfl).elim) (fun j _ => ⟨j,rfl⟩) x
    obtain ⟨j,hj⟩ := H _ hn
    rw [ite_eq_right hi, ← hj, expand_castSucc]

theorem ordinary_marked_in_zero (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p)
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) : boundaryIn a 0 false (p,i) j = 0 := by
  have hlt : p.val.2.val < a+2 := by
    have h := p.val.2.isLt
    have he : p.val.2.val ≠ a+2 := fun h => hp (Fin.ext h)
    omega
  have hd := core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked a 0 p hlt
  have hd' : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedTail a 0 0) z :=
    fun z hc ht => hd z ht hc
  unfold boundaryIn
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp [coreInc,tailInc,nativeMove,Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a 0 p 0),
      mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd']
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 p 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)

theorem ordinary_marked_out_zero (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p)
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) : boundaryOut a 0 false i (p,j) = 0 := by
  have hlt : p.val.2.val < a+2 := by
    have h := p.val.2.isLt
    have he : p.val.2.val ≠ a+2 := fun h => hp (Fin.ext h)
    omega
  have hd := core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked a 0 p hlt
  unfold boundaryOut
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp [coreInc,tailInc,nativeMove,core_tail_ne a 0 p 0,
      mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd]
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 p 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)

#print axioms marked_ghost_image
#print axioms ordinary_marked_in_column
#print axioms ordinary_marked_out_row
#print axioms ordinary_marked_in_zero
#print axioms ordinary_marked_out_zero

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import CupOrientation
import LineBoundary

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

namespace BrauerHook

def orientedIncidence (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) ℂ :=
  fun p j => if j = p.val.1 then -1 else if j = p.val.2 then 1 else 0

def endpointWeight (b : Bool) : ℂ := if b then 1 else -1

theorem sign_endpointPerm (a : ℕ) (c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)) (b : SeedSite a) :
    signC (endpointPerm a c d b) =
      endpointWeight (endpointPosition a c b) * endpointWeight (endpointPosition a d b) := by
  unfold endpointPerm
  generalize endpointPosition a c b = x
  generalize endpointPosition a d b = y
  cases x <;> cases y <;> simp [signC, endpointWeight]

theorem orientation_at_site (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (j : CoreSite a)
    (h : incident p j) :
    orientedIncidence a p j =
      endpointWeight (endpointPosition a (embedCore a 0 p) (injectCoreSite a j)) := by
  rcases h with h | h
  · subst j
    change orientedIncidence a p p.val.1 = endpointWeight (endpointPosition a (embedCore a 0 p) (left (embedCore a 0 p)))
    simp [orientedIncidence,endpointWeight, endpointPosition]
  · subst j
    have hn := ne_of_lt (ends_lt (embedCore a 0 p))
    have hn' := (ne_of_lt p.property).symm
    change orientedIncidence a p p.val.2 =
      endpointWeight (endpointPosition a (embedCore a 0 p) (right (embedCore a 0 p)))
    simp [orientedIncidence, endpointWeight, endpointPosition, hn.symm, hn']

private theorem signed_zero (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (j : CoreSite a)
    (h : ¬ incident p j) : orientedIncidence a p j = 0 := by
  have h₁ : j ≠ p.val.1 := fun h' => h (Or.inl h')
  have h₂ : j ≠ p.val.2 := fun h' => h (Or.inr h')
  simp [orientedIncidence,h₁,h₂]

private theorem signed_rows_diag (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (orientedIncidence a * (orientedIncidence a).transpose) p p = 2 := by
  classical
  have h : (orientedIncidence a * (orientedIncidence a).transpose) p p =
      (pairIncidence a * (pairIncidence a).transpose) p p := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply,Matrix.mul_apply]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro j _
    by_cases h₁ : j = p.val.1
    · simp [Matrix.transpose_apply, orientedIncidence, pairIncidence, incident, h₁]
    · by_cases h₂ : j = p.val.2
      · subst j
        simp [orientedIncidence,pairIncidence,incident,(ne_of_lt p.property).symm]
      · simp [orientedIncidence,pairIncidence,incident,h₁,h₂]
  rw [h,pair_incidence_rows]
  simp [lineAdj]

private theorem signed_rows_shared (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) (h : p ≠ q)
    {b u v : SeedSite a} (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) b u v) :
    (orientedIncidence a * (orientedIncidence a).transpose) q p =
      signC (endpointPerm a (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) b) := by
  classical
  have hb := (s.source b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hb' := (s.target b).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
  have hbi : b.val < a+3 := by
    rcases hb with rfl | rfl
    · exact p.val.1.isLt
    · exact p.val.2.isLt
  let i : CoreSite a := ⟨b.val,hbi⟩
  have hi : injectCoreSite a i = b := rfl
  have hp : incident p i := (incident_embed p i).mp (hi.symm ▸ hb)
  have hq : incident q i := (incident_embed q i).mp (hi.symm ▸ hb')
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
  · rw [Matrix.transpose_apply, orientation_at_site a q i hq, orientation_at_site a p i hp,
      hi, sign_endpointPerm, mul_comm]
  · intro j _ hj
    by_cases hqj : incident q j
    · have hpj : ¬ incident p j := fun hh =>
        h ((core_pair_by_both p hj hh hp).trans (core_pair_by_both q hj hqj hq).symm)
      simp [signed_zero a p j hpj]
    · simp [signed_zero a q j hqj]
  · simp

private theorem signed_rows_disjoint (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a)
    (hd : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 q) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 p) z) :
    (orientedIncidence a * (orientedIncidence a).transpose) q p = 0 := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  have hz (j : CoreSite a) : orientedIncidence a q j * (orientedIncidence a).transpose j p = 0 := by
    by_cases h : incident q j
    · have hp : ¬ incident p j := fun hc =>

theorem oriented_cols (a : ℕ) :
    (orientedIncidence a).transpose * orientedIncidence a =
      ((a : ℂ)+3) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) ℂ) -
        Matrix.of (fun _ _ : CoreSite a => (1 : ℂ)) := by

  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j
  · subst j

      intro p _

      · simp [orientedIncidence,pairIncidence,incident,h₁]

    · have he₁ : incident p i := (incident_sorted h i).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
      have he₂ : incident p j := (incident_sorted h j).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
      have hn := ne_of_lt p.property
      rcases he₁ with hi | hi <;> rcases he₂ with hj | hj

      · simp [orientedIncidence,hi,hj,hn,hn.symm]
      · simp [orientedIncidence,hi,hj,hn.symm]
      · exact (h (hi.trans hj.symm)).elim

      by_cases hi : incident q i
      · have hj : ¬ incident q j := fun hj => hq (core_pair_by_both q h hi hj)
        simp [signed_zero a q j hj]
      · simp [signed_zero a q i hi]
    · simp

noncomputable def orientedLine (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ :=
  (2 : ℂ) • 1 - (orientedIncidence a * (orientedIncidence a).transpose)

noncomputable def coreOrientation (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) : ℂ :=
  cupOrientation a (embedCore a 0 p)

/-- Once each transposed cup is oriented, its constant-line core block is
2I minus the oriented incidence product. -/
theorem transposed_line_entries (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (∑ j : Fin (a+1), nativeMove a 0 true (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i) =
      coreOrientation a q * orientedLine a q p * coreOrientation a p := by

  by_cases he : p = q
  · subst q
    simp [nativeMove,orientedLine,signed_rows_diag]
  · have hn : embedCore a 0 p ≠ embedCore a 0 q := fun h => he (core_injective a 0 h)
    obtain h | hd | ⟨b,u,v,s⟩ := overlap_trichotomy (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q)
    · exact (hn h).elim
    · simp [nativeMove, hn, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd, orientedLine,
        Ne.symm he, signed_rows_disjoint a p q hd]

        simp [Ne.symm he]
      rw [h₀]
      simp only [ite_true,mul_one,zero_sub, coreOrientation]

theorem coreOrientation_sq (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    coreOrientation a p * coreOrientation a p = 1 := cupOrientation_sq _ _

theorem marked_orientation_sign (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) :
    permSign (activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t)

  let d := embedTail a 0 0

    change endpointPosition a c (right c) = true

section DeterminantTransfer
variable {K α β : Type*} [Field K]
variable [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β]

private theorem signed_transfer_det (A : Matrix α β K) (B : Matrix β α K)
    (x : K) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + A*B).det * x^(Fintype.card β) =
      (x • (1 : Matrix β β K) + B*A).det * x^(Fintype.card α) := by
  have hA : x • (1 + (x⁻¹ • A) * B) = x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + A * B := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.smul_mul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  have hB : x • (1 + B * (x⁻¹ • A)) = x • (1 : Matrix β β K) + B * A := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.mul_smul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  rw [← hA, ← hB, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_one_add_mul_comm]
  ring

private theorem signed_ones_det [Nonempty α] (x : K) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (x • (1 : Matrix α α K) + Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1 : K))).det =
      x^(Fintype.card α-1) * (x + (Fintype.card α : K)) := by
  let A : Matrix α Unit K := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)
  let B : Matrix Unit α K := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)
  have hAB : A*B = Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (1:K)) := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply,A,B]
  have hBA : B*A = Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (Fintype.card α : K)) := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply,A,B]
  have hs := signed_transfer_det A B x hx
  rw [hAB,hBA] at hs
  simp only [Fintype.card_unit, pow_one, Matrix.det_unique, Matrix.add_apply,
    Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, ite_true, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, Matrix.of_apply] at hs
  have hp : 0 < Fintype.card α := Fintype.card_pos
  have he : x^(Fintype.card α) = x^(Fintype.card α-1)*x := by
    nth_rw 1 [show Fintype.card α = (Fintype.card α-1)+1 by omega]
    rw [pow_succ]
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ hx
  rw [hs, he]
  ring

end DeterminantTransfer


open Polynomial

noncomputable def transLineChar (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X] :=
  (X : ℂ[X]) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X]) + C.mapMatrix (orientedLine a)

private theorem transfer_rows (a : ℕ) :
    lp.mapMatrix (transLineChar a) =
      lp (X+2) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) -
        (orientedIncidence a).map (lp.comp C) * (orientedIncidence a).transpose.map (lp.comp C) := by
  classical
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := lp.comp C)]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j
  · subst j
    simp [transLineChar,orientedLine,RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply,map_ofNat]
    ring
  · simp [transLineChar,orientedLine,RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply,h]

private theorem transfer_cols (a : ℕ) :
    (orientedIncidence a).transpose.map (lp.comp C) * (orientedIncidence a).map (lp.comp C) =
      lp (C ((a:ℂ)+3)) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) -
        Matrix.of (fun _ _ : CoreSite a => (1 : LoopField)) := by
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := lp.comp C), oriented_cols]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j <;> simp [Matrix.map_apply,h]

theorem transposed_line_charpoly (a : ℕ) :
    (transLineChar a).det =
      (X-C ((a:ℂ)+1))^(a+2) * (X+2)^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) := by
  classical
  let x := lp (X+2)
  let y := lp (X-C ((a:ℂ)+1))
  have hx : x ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact (monic_X_add_C (2:ℂ)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  have hy : y ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    exact (monic_X_sub_C ((a:ℂ)+1)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  let L := (orientedIncidence a).map (lp.comp C)
  let R := (orientedIncidence a).transpose.map (lp.comp C)
  have h₁ : x • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) + L*(-R) =
      lp.mapMatrix (transLineChar a) := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_neg, ← sub_eq_add_neg, transfer_rows]
  have h₂ : x • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) + (-R)*L =
      y • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) + Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1) := by
    rw [Matrix.neg_mul, ← sub_eq_add_neg, show R*L = _ from transfer_cols a,
      sub_sub_eq_add_sub, add_comm (x • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField)),
      add_sub_assoc, ← sub_smul, add_comm]
    congr 1
    apply congrArg (fun k : LoopField => k • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) LoopField))
    change lp (X+2) - lp (C ((a:ℂ)+3)) = lp (X-C ((a:ℂ)+1))
    rw [← map_sub]
    apply congrArg lp
    simp only [map_add,map_ofNat,map_one]
    ring
  have hcard : Fintype.card (CoreCup a) = (a+3)+((a+3)*a/2) := by
    rw [card_core, Nat.choose_two_right, show a+3-1 = a+2 by omega]
    have e : (a+3)*(a+2) = (a+3)*a + 2*(a+3) := by ring
    omega
  have hsum : y + ((a+3:ℕ):LoopField) = x := by
    have hn : (((a+3 : ℕ) : LoopField)) = lp (C (((a+3 : ℕ) : ℂ))) := by
      rw [map_natCast,map_natCast]
    rw [hn]
    change lp (X-C ((a:ℂ)+1)) + lp (C (((a+3:ℕ):ℂ))) = lp (X+2)
    rw [← map_add]
    apply congrArg lp
    have ha : ((a+3:ℕ):ℂ) = a+3 := by push_cast; rfl
    rw [ha]
    simp only [map_add,map_ofNat,map_one]
    ring
  have hs := signed_transfer_det L (-R) x hx
  rw [h₁,h₂,signed_ones_det y hy, ← RingHom.map_det,
    Fintype.card_fin, hcard, pow_add x (a+3) ((a+3)*a/2), hsum] at hs
  apply lp_injective
  rw [map_mul,map_pow,map_pow]
  change lp (transLineChar a).det = y^(a+2) * x^((a+2)*(a+1)/2)
  have he : (a+2)*(a+1)/2 = ((a+3)*a/2)+1 := by
    have h : (a+2)*(a+1) = (a+3)*a+2 := by ring
    omega
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ (pow_ne_zero (a+3) hx)
  rw [hs,he,pow_succ,show a+3-1=a+2 by omega]
  ring

#print axioms transposed_line_charpoly
#print axioms marked_orientation_sign

#print axioms oriented_cols
#print axioms transposed_line_entries

end BrauerHook
```

## 22. `OrdinaryLineResolvent` (inherited, 189 lines, sha256 `7fd7ba253986120159a0736c0a6559de0874ab301427e1846d5217f93baf25b4`)

```lean
import LineBoundary

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

noncomputable def lineInput (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField :=
  fun p j => (lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)))⁻¹ * lp (∑ i, boundaryIn a 0 false (p,i) j)

noncomputable def lineOutput (a : ℕ) : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (CoreCup a) LoopField :=
  fun i p => lp (∑ j, boundaryOut a 0 false i (p,j))

noncomputable def oldVector (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : CoreSite a → LoopField :=
  Fin.lastCases 0 (fun i : Fin (a+2) => lp (C (expand (delta j) i)))

theorem oldVector_sum (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : (∑ i, oldVector a j i) = 0 := by
  rw [Fin.sum_univ_castSucc]
  simp only [oldVector, Fin.lastCases_castSucc, Fin.lastCases_last, add_zero]
  rw [← map_sum, ← map_sum]
  have h : (∑ i, expand (delta j) i) = 0 := expand_mem _
  rw [h, map_zero, map_zero]

theorem oldVector_ghost (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : oldVector a j (lastCore a) = 0 := by
  simp [oldVector,lastCore]

noncomputable def dLoop (a : ℕ) : LoopField := lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1))
noncomputable def xLoop : LoopField := lp (X-2)
noncomputable def wLoop (a : ℕ) : LoopField := lp (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1))

theorem xLoop_ne : xLoop ≠ 0 := by
  intro h
  exact (monic_X_sub_C (2 : ℂ)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))

theorem wLoop_ne (a : ℕ) : wLoop a ≠ 0 := by
  intro h
  exact (monic_X_add_C ((a : ℂ)-1)).ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))

theorem dLoop_ne (a : ℕ) : dLoop a ≠ 0 := by
  intro h
  have h' : C ((a : ℂ)+1) = 0 := lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm)
  have hc := C_eq_zero.mp h'
  have hn : (((a+1 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero _)
  exact hn (by simpa using hc)

theorem wLoop_add (a : ℕ) : xLoop + lp (C ((a : ℂ)+1)) = wLoop a := by
  unfold xLoop wLoop
  rw [← map_add]
  apply congrArg lp
  simp only [map_add,map_sub,map_one]
  ring

private theorem incidence_loop_rows (a : ℕ) :
    lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) =
      xLoop • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) +
        (incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a) * (incidenceOver a).transpose := by
  let f : ℂ →+* LoopField := lp.comp C
  have ho : incidenceOver (K := LoopField) a = (pairIncidence a).map f := by
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro p i
    by_cases h : incident p i <;> simp [incidenceOver,pairIncidence,Matrix.map_apply,h]
  have ht : ((pairIncidence a).map f).transpose = (pairIncidence a).transpose.map f := rfl
  rw [ho,ht,← Matrix.map_mul (f := f),pair_incidence_rows]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  by_cases h : i=j
  · subst j
    simp [lineChar, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, f, xLoop, map_ofNat]
    ring
  · simp [lineChar, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, f, h]

private theorem line_input_formula (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    lineInput a p j = -(dLoop a)⁻¹ * markedLift a (oldVector a j) p := by
  by_cases hp : isMarked p
  · obtain ⟨i,rfl⟩ := (image_marked_pair a p).mp hp
    have h : isMarked (markedPair a i) := rfl
    have hm : markedLift a (oldVector a j) (markedPair a i) =
        lp (C (expand (delta j) i)) := by
      unfold markedLift
      rw [ite_eq_left h]
      exact Fin.lastCases_castSucc _
    unfold lineInput
    rw [ordinary_marked_in_column, map_neg, hm]
    change (dLoop a)⁻¹ * (-lp (C (expand (delta j) i))) =
      -(dLoop a)⁻¹ * lp (C (expand (delta j) i))
    ring
  · have hz : (∑ i, boundaryIn a 0 false (p,i) j) = 0 := by
      simp [ordinary_marked_in_zero a p hp]
    simp [lineInput, hz, markedLift, hp]

noncomputable def markedSolution (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : CoreCup a → LoopField :=
  xLoop⁻¹ • markedLift a (oldVector a j) -
    (xLoop * wLoop a)⁻¹ • (incidenceOver a *ᵥ oldVector a j)

theorem markedSolution_equation (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) *ᵥ markedSolution a j =
      markedLift a (oldVector a j) := by
  rw [incidence_loop_rows]
  have h := ordinary_marked_line_solution a (oldVector a j) (oldVector_ghost a j)
    (oldVector_sum a j) xLoop xLoop_ne (by rw [wLoop_add]; exact wLoop_ne a)
  simpa only [wLoop_add, markedSolution] using h.1

theorem markedSolution_value (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) (t : Fin (a+2)) :
    markedSolution a j (markedPair a t) =
      ((wLoop a-1)/(xLoop*wLoop a)) * lp (C (expand (delta j) t)) := by
  have h := ordinary_marked_line_solution a (oldVector a j) (oldVector_ghost a j)
    (oldVector_sum a j) xLoop xLoop_ne (by rw [wLoop_add]; exact wLoop_ne a)
  simpa only [wLoop_add, markedSolution, oldVector, Fin.lastCases_castSucc] using h.2 t

noncomputable def lineResponse (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField :=
  fun p j => -(dLoop a)⁻¹ * markedSolution a j p

theorem lineResponse_equation (a : ℕ) :
    lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) * lineResponse a = lineInput a := by
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro p j
  have h := congrFun (markedSolution_equation a j) p
  change (∑ q, lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) p q * markedSolution a j q) =
    markedLift a (oldVector a j) p at h
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, line_input_formula]
  change (∑ q, lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a) p q * (-(dLoop a)⁻¹ * markedSolution a j q)) = _
  simp_rw [mul_left_comm _ (-(dLoop a)⁻¹), ← Finset.mul_sum, h]

theorem lineResponse_inverse (a : ℕ) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a))⁻¹ * lineInput a = lineResponse a := by
  have hdet : (lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a)).det ≠ 0 := by
    rw [← RingHom.map_det, ordinary_line_charpoly]
    have h : ((X+C (2*((a : ℂ)+1))) * (X+C ((a : ℂ)-1))^(a+2) *
        (X-2)^((a+3)*a/2)).Monic := by monicity!
    intro hz
    exact h.ne_zero (lp_injective (hz.trans lp.map_zero.symm))
  rw [← lineResponse_equation, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.nonsing_inv_mul _ hdet.isUnit]
  simp

/-- First summand of the ordinary normalized marked resolvent, obtained from
the actual boundary rows and columns and the core constant-line action. -/
theorem ordinary_line_boundary_resolvent (a : ℕ) :
    lineOutput a * (lp.mapMatrix (lineChar a))⁻¹ * lineInput a =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) ((dLoop a+1) / dLoop a *
        ((wLoop a-1)/(xLoop*wLoop a))) := by
  classical
  rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, lineResponse_inverse]
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  have h (p : CoreCup a) : lineOutput a i p * lineResponse a p j =
      if isMarked p then lineOutput a i p * lineResponse a p j else 0 := by
    by_cases hp : isMarked p
    · simp [hp]
    · simp [lineOutput, hp, ordinary_marked_out_zero a p hp]
  conv_lhs => arg 2; intro p; rw [h p]
  rw [sum_marked_pairs]
  let c := (wLoop a-1)/(xLoop*wLoop a)
  let v := fun t : Fin (a+2) => lp (C (expand (delta j) t))
  have hz : (∑ t, v t) = 0 := by
    rw [← map_sum, ← map_sum]
    have he : (∑ t, expand (delta j) t) = 0 := expand_mem _
    rw [he, map_zero, map_zero]
  have ho (t : Fin (a+2)) :
      lineOutput a i (markedPair a t) * lineResponse a (markedPair a t) j =
        (if i.castSucc = t then (dLoop a+1) * ((dLoop a)⁻¹*c*v t) else 0) -
          (dLoop a)⁻¹*c*v t := by
    simp only [lineOutput, ordinary_marked_out_row, lineResponse, markedSolution_value,
      c,v]
    by_cases hh : i.castSucc = t
    · simp only [hh, ite_true, map_neg, dLoop]
      ring
    · simp only [hh, ite_false, map_one, one_mul, zero_sub]
      ring
  simp_rw [ho, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hz, mul_zero, sub_zero]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i.castSucc]
  · simp only [ite_true, Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply]
    change _ = if i=j then _ else 0
    have he : v i.castSucc = if i=j then 1 else 0 := by
      simp only [v, expand_castSucc, delta]
      by_cases hh : i=j <;> simp [hh]
    rw [he]
    by_cases hh : i=j
    · simp [hh, div_eq_mul_inv, c, mul_assoc]
    · simp [hh]
  · intro b _ hb
    simp [Ne.symm hb]
  · simp

#print axioms lineResponse_equation
#print axioms ordinary_line_boundary_resolvent

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import TransposedLine
import OrdinaryLineResolvent

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

private theorem move_twist_shared (a : ℕ) {c d : Cup a (Rank a 0)}
    {b u v : SeedSite a} (s : Shared c d b u v) (j i : Fin (a+1)) :
    nativeMove a 0 true c d j i =
      permSign (activeTransport s) * nativeMove a 0 false c d j i := by
  simp [nativeMove,shared_ne s,mixedTransport_shared s,hookAct]

theorem transposed_marked_input_action (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryIn a 0 true (markedPair a t,i) j =
      C (coreOrientation a (markedPair a t)) * boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t,i) j := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  let sr := Shared.mk s.distinctBV s.distinctBU s.distinctUV.symm s.target s.source
  have hn : coreInc a 0 (markedPair a t,i) ≠ tailInc a 0 (0,j) :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)
  simp only [boundaryIn,Matrix.submatrix_apply,normalMatrix_entry,hn,ite_false,zero_add]
  simp only [coreInc,tailInc]
  rw [move_twist_shared a sr, activeTransport_inverse s sr]
  have hs : permSign (activeTransport s).symm = permSign (activeTransport s) := sign_inv _
  rw [hs]
  change C (permSign (activeTransport s) * _) = _
  rw [marked_orientation_sign, map_mul]

theorem transposed_marked_output_action (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryOut a 0 true i (markedPair a t,j) =
      C (coreOrientation a (markedPair a t)) * boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t,j) := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  have hn : tailInc a 0 (0,i) ≠ coreInc a 0 (markedPair a t,j) :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)
  simp only [boundaryOut,Matrix.submatrix_apply,normalMatrix_entry,hn,ite_false,zero_add]
  simp only [coreInc,tailInc]
  rw [move_twist_shared a s]
  change C (permSign (activeTransport s) * _) = _
  rw [marked_orientation_sign, map_mul]

theorem transposed_input_unmarked (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p)
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) : boundaryIn a 0 true (p,i) j = 0 := by
  have hlt : p.val.2.val < a+2 := by
    have h := p.val.2.isLt
    have he : p.val.2.val ≠ a+2 := fun h => hp (Fin.ext h)
    omega
  have hd := core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked a 0 p hlt
  have hd' : ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 p) z → ¬ touches (embedTail a 0 0) z :=
    fun z hc ht => hd z ht hc
  unfold boundaryIn
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp [coreInc,tailInc,nativeMove,Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a 0 p 0),
      mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd']
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 p 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)

theorem transposed_output_unmarked (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p)
    (i j : Fin (a+1)) : boundaryOut a 0 true i (p,j) = 0 := by
  have hlt : p.val.2.val < a+2 := by
    have h := p.val.2.isLt
    have he : p.val.2.val ≠ a+2 := fun h => hp (Fin.ext h)
    omega
  have hd := core_first_tail_disjoint_unmarked a 0 p hlt
  unfold boundaryOut
  rw [Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, ite_eq_right]
  · simp [coreInc,tailInc,nativeMove,core_tail_ne a 0 p 0,
      mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) hd]
  · intro h
    exact core_tail_ne a 0 p 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)

/-- The orientation of the core line changes each marked boundary block by
exactly the same sign as the gauge on that core fiber. -/
theorem transpose_line_input_eq (a : ℕ) :
    (fun (p : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) =>
      (dLoop a)⁻¹ * lp (C (coreOrientation a p) *
        (∑ i, boundaryIn a 0 true (p,i) j))) = lineInput a := by
  funext p j
  by_cases hp : isMarked p
  · obtain ⟨t,rfl⟩ := (image_marked_pair a p).mp hp
    simp only [transposed_marked_input_action, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← mul_assoc,
      ← C_mul, coreOrientation_sq, C_1, one_mul, lineInput, dLoop]
  · simp [lineInput,dLoop,transposed_input_unmarked a p hp,ordinary_marked_in_zero a p hp]

theorem transpose_line_output_eq (a : ℕ) :
    (fun (i : Fin (a+1)) (p : CoreCup a) =>
      lp (C (coreOrientation a p) * (∑ j, boundaryOut a 0 true i (p,j)))) = lineOutput a := by
  funext i p
  by_cases hp : isMarked p
  · obtain ⟨t,rfl⟩ := (image_marked_pair a p).mp hp
    simp only [transposed_marked_output_action, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← mul_assoc,
      ← C_mul, coreOrientation_sq, C_1, one_mul, lineOutput]
  · simp [lineOutput,transposed_output_unmarked a p hp,ordinary_marked_out_zero a p hp]

#print axioms transposed_marked_input_action
#print axioms transposed_marked_output_action
#print axioms transpose_line_input_eq
#print axioms transpose_line_output_eq

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import TransposedBoundary

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

noncomputable def signedIncFrac (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField :=
  (orientedIncidence a).map (lp.comp C)

private theorem signedInc_split (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : CoreSite a) :
    signedIncFrac a p i =
      (if i = p.val.1 then -1 else 0) + (if i = p.val.2 then 1 else 0) := by
  unfold signedIncFrac

    simp [orientedIncidence,Matrix.map_apply,ne_of_lt p.property]
  · by_cases h₂ : i = p.val.2
    · subst i
      simp [orientedIncidence,Matrix.map_apply,(ne_of_lt p.property).symm]
    · simp [orientedIncidence,Matrix.map_apply,h₁,h₂]

    (signedIncFrac a *ᵥ v) p = -v p.val.1 + v p.val.2 := by
  classical
  change (∑ i, signedIncFrac a p i * v i) = _
  simp_rw [signedInc_split,add_mul,Finset.sum_add_distrib,ite_mul,zero_mul,
    neg_one_mul,one_mul]

  let v := fun t : Fin (a+2) => lp (C (expand (delta j) t))
  have hz : (∑ t, v t) = 0 := by
    rw [← map_sum, ← map_sum]
    have he : (∑ t, expand (delta j) t) = 0 := expand_mem _
    rw [he, map_zero, map_zero]
  have ho (t : Fin (a+2)) :
      lineOutput a i (markedPair a t) * transResponse a (markedPair a t) j =
        (if i.castSucc = t then (dLoop a+1) * ((dLoop a)⁻¹*c*v t) else 0) -
          (dLoop a)⁻¹*c*v t := by
    simp only [lineOutput, ordinary_marked_out_row, transResponse, transSolution_value,
      c,v]
    by_cases hh : i.castSucc = t
    · simp only [hh, ite_true, map_neg, dLoop]
      ring
    · simp only [hh, ite_false, map_one, one_mul, zero_sub]
      ring
  simp_rw [ho, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, ← Finset.mul_sum, hz, mul_zero, sub_zero]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i.castSucc]
  · simp only [ite_true, Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply]
    change _ = if i=j then _ else 0
    have he : v i.castSucc = if i=j then 1 else 0 := by
      simp only [v, expand_castSucc, delta]
      by_cases hh : i=j <;> simp [hh]
    rw [he]
    by_cases hh : i=j
    · simp [hh, div_eq_mul_inv, c, mul_assoc]
    · simp [hh]
  · intro b _ hb
    simp [Ne.symm hb]
  · simp

#print axioms transSolution_equation
#print axioms transSolution_value
#print axioms transposed_line_boundary_resolvent

end BrauerHook
```

## 25. `LiteralNegation` (inherited, 85 lines, sha256 `bc6fc8292eb8554032ca826c1d83badf1aaf2ade0d73b4bdcd53b2e850c9804b`)

```lean
import NativeGram
import PrintedHook
import Mathlib.Tactic.ComputeDegree

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

set_option maxRecDepth 5000

theorem printedC_five_factors :
    ordinaryCPrinted 5 =
      (X - (3 : ℂ[X]))^64 * (X - 2)^14 * X^20 * (X + 2)^15 *
        (X + 4)^21 * (X + 10) * (X + 12) := by
  norm_num [ordinaryCPrinted, map_ofNat]

theorem printedC_five_degree : (ordinaryCPrinted 5).natDegree = 136 := by
  rw [printedC_five_factors]
  compute_degree!

theorem printedP_five_polynomial :
    printedOrdinaryP 5 0 =
      X^5 + (14 : ℂ[X])*X^4 + 29*X^3 - 126*X^2 - 237*X + 210 := by
  rw [ordinaryP_seed_identity]
  norm_num [ordinaryQ, ordinaryR, map_ofNat]
  ring

theorem printedP_five_degree : (printedOrdinaryP 5 0).natDegree = 5 := by
  rw [printedP_five_polynomial]
  compute_degree!

theorem printed_height_five_degree (s : ℂ) (hs : s ≠ 0) :
    (C s * (ordinaryCPrinted 5 * (printedOrdinaryP 5 0)^6)).natDegree = 166 := by
  have hp : printedOrdinaryP 5 0 ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hh := printedP_five_degree
    rw [h, natDegree_zero] at hh
    exact (by decide : 0 ≠ 5) hh
  have hc : ordinaryCPrinted 5 ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have hh := printedC_five_degree
    rw [h, natDegree_zero] at hh
    exact (by decide : 0 ≠ 136) hh
  rw [natDegree_C_mul hs, Polynomial.natDegree_mul hc (pow_ne_zero _ hp),
    Polynomial.natDegree_pow, printedC_five_degree, printedP_five_degree]

/-- The witness rules out every nonzero constant normalization of the printed product. -/
theorem height_five_printed_negation :
    ¬ ∃ s : ℂ, s ≠ 0 ∧
      nativeDet 5 0 false = C s * (ordinaryCPrinted 5 * (printedOrdinaryP 5 0)^6) := by
  rintro ⟨s, hs, h⟩
  have hh := congrArg Polynomial.natDegree h
  rw [native_height_five_degree, printed_height_five_degree s hs] at hh
  exact (by decide : 174 ≠ 166) hh

/-- Theorem 3.19 factors in the native k coordinate, with the recurrence from (3.2.9). -/
def NativeNormalized (a : ℕ) (transpose : Bool) (fac : ℂ[X]) (P : ℤ → ℂ[X]) : Prop :=
  fac.Monic ∧
  (∀ k : ℤ, P (k + 2) = X * P (k + 1) - P k) ∧
  (∀ k : ℤ, IsCoprime (P k) (P (k + 1))) ∧
  (∀ k : ℕ, (P k).Monic ∧ (P (k + 1 : ℤ)).natDegree = (P k).natDegree + 1) ∧
  ∀ k : ℕ, ∃ s : ℂ, s ≠ 0 ∧ nativeDet a k transpose = C s * (fac * (P k)^(a+1))

/-- The two unambiguous ordinary-hook conjuncts of Conjecture 3.31.1 already fail. -/
theorem printed_ordinary_clauses_negation :
    ¬ ∃ fac : ℕ → ℂ[X], ∃ P : ℕ → ℤ → ℂ[X],
      (∀ a : ℕ, 2 ≤ a → NativeNormalized a false (fac a) (P a)) ∧
      (∀ a : ℕ, 4 ≤ a → fac a = ordinaryCPrinted a) ∧
      (∀ (a : ℕ) (k : ℤ), 2 ≤ a → P a k = printedOrdinaryP a k) := by
  rintro ⟨fac, P, hf, hc, hp⟩
  obtain ⟨s, hs, h⟩ := (hf 5 (by decide)).2.2.2.2 0
  change nativeDet 5 0 false = C s * (fac 5 * P 5 0 ^ 6) at h
  rw [hc 5 (by decide), hp 5 0 (by decide)] at h
  exact height_five_printed_negation ⟨s, hs, h⟩

#print axioms printedC_five_factors
#print axioms printedC_five_degree
#print axioms printedP_five_polynomial
#print axioms printedP_five_degree
#print axioms printed_height_five_degree
#print axioms height_five_printed_negation
#print axioms NativeNormalized
#print axioms printed_ordinary_clauses_negation

end BrauerHook
```

## 26. `FactorArithmetic` (inherited, 152 lines, sha256 `3c9b9a178bf5650cb155017803c51fed0038cd86d469d26feeeec58aafdad965`)

```lean
import CoreStability
import LiteralNegation

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

/-- Product from the two reviewed ordinary core multiplicity lists.
Evaluation of the native core determinant against this product is a separate problem. -/
noncomputable def ordinaryCoreProduct (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X + C (2*((a : ℂ)+1))) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-1))^((a+2)+((a+3)*a/2)) *
  (X - 2)^((a+3)*a/2) *
  (X + C (2*(a : ℂ)))^(a+2) *
  (X + C ((a : ℂ)-3))^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) *
  X^((a+3)*(a+2)*(a-2)/6) *
  (X - 3)^((a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1)/3)

noncomputable def transposeCoreProduct (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X - C ((a : ℂ)+1))^((a+2)+((a+3)*a/2)) *
  (X + 2)^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) *
  (X - C ((a : ℂ)-1))^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) *
  (X + 1)^((a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1)/3) *
  (X + 4)^(a*(a+1)*(a+2)/6)

private theorem mult_first (a : ℕ) : a+2+(a+3)*a/2 = (a+1)*(a+2)/2+(a+1) := by
  have e : (a+1)*(a+2) = (a+3)*a+2 := by ring
  omega

private theorem mult_second (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    (a+3)*a/2 = (a-1)*(a+2)/2+(a+1) := by
  have e : (a+3)*a = (a-1)*(a+2)+2*(a+1) := by
    obtain ⟨b, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le ha
    simp only [Nat.add_sub_cancel_left]
    ring
  omega

private theorem mult_third (a : ℕ) : (a+2)*(a+1)/2 = a*(a+1)/2+(a+1) := by
  have e : (a+2)*(a+1) = a*(a+1)+2*(a+1) := by ring
  omega


private theorem ordinary_rearrange {M : Type*} [CommMonoid M]
    (A B C D E F G : M) (d u v w z t : ℕ) :
    A * B^(u+d) * C^(v+d) * D^(d+1) * E^(w+d) * F^z * G^t =
      (G^t * C^v * F^z * E^w * B^u * D * A) * (B*C*D*E)^d := by
  simp only [pow_add, pow_one, mul_pow]
  ac_rfl

private theorem transpose_rearrange {M : Type*} [CommMonoid M]
    (A B C D E : M) (d u v w z : ℕ) :
    A^(u+d) * B^(v+d) * C^(v+d) * D^w * E^z =
      (E^z * B^v * D^w * C^v * A^u) * (B*C*A)^d := by
  simp only [pow_add, mul_pow]
  ac_rfl

theorem ordinary_core_factor_algebra (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    ordinaryCoreProduct a = ordinaryCCorrected a * (ordinaryQ a)^(a+1) := by
  unfold ordinaryCoreProduct ordinaryCCorrected ordinaryQ
  rw [mult_first, mult_second a (by omega), mult_third]
  rw [show a+2 = a+1+1 by omega]
  rw [show (a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1) = (a-1)*(a+1)*(a+3) by ring]
  exact ordinary_rearrange _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

theorem transpose_core_factor_algebra (a : ℕ) :
    transposeCoreProduct a = transposeCPrinted a * (transposeQ a)^(a+1) := by
  unfold transposeCoreProduct transposeCPrinted transposeQ
  rw [mult_first, mult_third]
  rw [show (a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1) = (a-1)*(a+1)*(a+3) by ring]
  exact transpose_rearrange _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

theorem ordinary_two_overlap :
    ordinaryCCorrected 2 =
      (X + (6 : ℂ[X])) * (X - 3)^5 * (X + 4) * (X + 1)^6 * (X - 2)^2 * (X - 1)^3 := by
  norm_num [ordinaryCCorrected, map_neg, map_ofNat, sub_eq_add_neg]
  ring

theorem ordinary_three_overlap :
    ordinaryCCorrected 3 =
      (X - (3 : ℂ[X]))^16 * (X - 2)^5 * X^11 * (X + 2)^10 * (X + 6) * (X + 8) := by
  norm_num [ordinaryCCorrected, map_ofNat, sub_eq_add_neg]
  ring_nf
  simp

theorem transpose_one_overlap :
    transposeCPrinted 1 = (X - (2 : ℂ[X]))^3 * (X + 2) * X * (X + 4) := by
  norm_num [transposeCPrinted, map_ofNat, sub_eq_add_neg]
  ring

theorem ordinaryCorrected_monic (a : ℕ) : (ordinaryCCorrected a).Monic := by
  unfold ordinaryCCorrected
  monicity!

theorem transposePrinted_monic (a : ℕ) : (transposeCPrinted a).Monic := by
  unfold transposeCPrinted
  monicity!

/-- The remaining two exact ordinary inputs are the native core spectrum and
marked-fiber resolvent, both at seed rank a+4. -/
theorem ordinary_normalized_of_seed (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a)
    (hcore : (coreMatrix a 0 false).det = ordinaryCoreProduct a)
    (hbound : boundaryCompression a 0 false =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (lp (ordinaryR a) / lp (ordinaryQ a))) :
    NativeNormalized a false (ordinaryCCorrected a) (printedOrdinaryP a) := by
  refine ⟨ordinaryCorrected_monic a, ?_, ordinaryP_coprime a ha, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro k
    simp_rw [ordinaryP_seed_identity]
    exact seedP_step _ _ k
  · intro k
    refine ⟨(ordinaryP_monic_degree a k).monic, ?_⟩
    have h := (ordinaryP_monic_degree a (k+1)).natDegree_eq
    push_cast at h
    rw [h, (ordinaryP_monic_degree a k).natDegree_eq]
  · intro k
    refine ⟨(cupMetric a k).det, metric_det_ne a k, ?_⟩
    rw [native_det_of_core a k false _ (ordinaryQ a) (ordinaryR a) (ordinaryQ_degree a).monic.ne_zero
      (by rw [coreMatrix_stable, hcore, ordinary_core_factor_algebra a ha])
      (by rw [boundaryCompression_stable, hbound])]
    rw [ordinaryP_seed_identity]
    rfl

theorem transpose_normalized_of_seed (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a)
    (hcore : (coreMatrix a 0 true).det = transposeCoreProduct a)
    (hbound : boundaryCompression a 0 true =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (lp (transposeR a) / lp (transposeQ a))) :
    NativeNormalized a true (transposeCPrinted a) (correctedTransposeP a) := by
  refine ⟨transposePrinted_monic a, ?_, transposeP_coprime a ha, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro k
    simp_rw [transposeP_seed_identity]
    exact seedP_step _ _ k
  · intro k
    refine ⟨(transposeP_monic_degree a k).monic, ?_⟩
    have h := (transposeP_monic_degree a (k+1)).natDegree_eq
    push_cast at h
    rw [h, (transposeP_monic_degree a k).natDegree_eq]
  · intro k
    refine ⟨(cupMetric a k).det, metric_det_ne a k, ?_⟩
    rw [native_det_of_core a k true _ (transposeQ a) (transposeR a) (transposeQ_degree a).monic.ne_zero
      (by rw [coreMatrix_stable, hcore, transpose_core_factor_algebra])
      (by rw [boundaryCompression_stable, hbound])]
    rw [transposeP_seed_identity]
    rfl

#print axioms ordinary_core_factor_algebra
#print axioms transpose_core_factor_algebra
#print axioms ordinary_two_overlap
#print axioms ordinary_three_overlap
#print axioms transpose_one_overlap
#print axioms ordinary_normalized_of_seed
#print axioms transpose_normalized_of_seed

end BrauerHook
```

## 27. `LineSplitting` (inherited, 224 lines, sha256 `834088313e5f9f134e06f2117438e607eec3809fa75728752e6568c633959c97`)

```lean
import TransposedLineResolvent
import FactorArithmetic

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

section LineConjugacy
variable {R U V : Type*} [CommRing R]
variable [Fintype U] [DecidableEq U] [Fintype V] [DecidableEq V]

/-- Exact remaining determinant after a split injective invariant summand.
The displayed residual matrix acts identically on the split summand. -/
theorem det_remove_summand (A : Matrix U U R) (D : Matrix V V R)
    (J : Matrix U V R) (L : Matrix V U R)
    (hJ : A*J=J*D) (hL : L*J=1) :
    A.det = D.det * (A*(1-J*L)+J*L).det := by
  let P := J*L
  let Q := 1-P
  have hPQ : P*Q = 0 := by
    dsimp only [P,Q]
    rw [Matrix.mul_sub,Matrix.mul_one]
    have h : (J*L)*(J*L) = J*L := by
      rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.mul_assoc J L J, hL, Matrix.mul_one]
    rw [h,sub_self]
  have hQQ : Q*Q = Q := by
    dsimp only [Q]
    rw [Matrix.sub_mul,Matrix.one_mul,hPQ,sub_zero]
  have hQJ : Q*J = 0 := by
    dsimp only [Q,P]
    rw [Matrix.sub_mul,Matrix.one_mul,Matrix.mul_assoc,hL,Matrix.mul_one,sub_self]
  have hPJ : P*J = J := by
    dsimp only [P]
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc,hL,Matrix.mul_one]
  have hA : A = (A*Q+P)*(Q+(J*D)*L) := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc A Q Q, hQQ, hPQ, add_zero,
      ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc A Q J,
      hQJ, Matrix.mul_zero, hPJ, zero_add, ← hJ, Matrix.mul_assoc]
    change A = A*(1-P)+A*P
    rw [← Matrix.mul_add, sub_add_cancel, Matrix.mul_one]
  have hD : (Q+(J*D)*L).det = D.det := by
    have he : Q+(J*D)*L = 1+(J*(D-1))*L := by
      dsimp only [Q,P]
      rw [Matrix.mul_sub,Matrix.mul_one,Matrix.sub_mul]
      abel
    rw [he,Matrix.det_one_add_mul_comm,← Matrix.mul_assoc,hL]
    simp
  change A.det = D.det * (A*Q+P).det
  calc
    A.det = ((A*Q+P)*(Q+(J*D)*L)).det := congrArg Matrix.det hA
    _ = _ := by rw [Matrix.det_mul,hD,mul_comm]

theorem inverse_summand (A : Matrix U U R) (D : Matrix V V R)
    (J : Matrix U V R) (h : A*J=J*D) (ha : IsUnit A.det) (hd : IsUnit D.det) :
    A⁻¹ * J = J * D⁻¹ := by
  calc
    A⁻¹*J = (A⁻¹*J)*(D*D⁻¹) := by
      rw [Matrix.mul_nonsing_inv _ hd,Matrix.mul_one]
    _ = A⁻¹ * (J*D) * D⁻¹ := by simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    _ = _ := by rw [← h, ← Matrix.mul_assoc A⁻¹ A,Matrix.nonsing_inv_mul _ ha,Matrix.one_mul]

end LineConjugacy

noncomputable def gauge (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) : ℂ :=
  if b then coreOrientation a p else 1

theorem gauge_sq (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) :
    gauge a b p * gauge a b p = 1 := by
  cases b <;> simp [gauge,coreOrientation_sq]

noncomputable def lineAdjacency (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ :=
  if b then orientedLine a else lineAdj a

theorem core_line_entries (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p q : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (∑ j : Fin (a+1), nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i) =
      gauge a b q * lineAdjacency a b q p * gauge a b p := by
  cases b
  · simpa [gauge,lineAdjacency] using ordinary_line_entries a p q i
  · exact transposed_line_entries a p q i

noncomputable def lineJ (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreCup a) ℂ :=
  fun i p => if i.1 = p then gauge a b p else 0

theorem core_line_mul (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    actualCore a b * lineJ a b = lineJ a b * lineAdjacency a b := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i p
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply,Fintype.sum_prod_type,Matrix.mul_apply]
  have hL (q : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
      actualCore a b i (q,j) * lineJ a b (q,j) p =
        if q=p then nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 q) (embedCore a 0 i.1) j i.2 * gauge a b p else 0 := by
    by_cases h : q=p <;> simp [lineJ,actualCore,interaction,coreInc,h]
  have hR (q : CoreCup a) : lineJ a b i q * lineAdjacency a b q p =
      if i.1=q then gauge a b q * lineAdjacency a b q p else 0 := by
    by_cases h : i.1=q <;> simp [lineJ,h]
  simp_rw [hL,hR,Finset.sum_ite_irrel,Finset.sum_const_zero]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single p]
  · rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i.1]
    · simp only [ite_true, ← Finset.sum_mul, core_line_entries]
      rw [mul_assoc,gauge_sq,mul_one]
    · intro q _ hq
      simp [Ne.symm hq]
    · simp
  · intro q _ hq
    simp [hq]
  · simp

noncomputable def linePolynomial (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X] :=
  if b then transLineChar a else lineChar a

noncomputable def lineJPoly (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X] := (lineJ a b).map C

theorem coreMatrix_line (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    coreMatrix a 0 b * lineJPoly a b = lineJPoly a b * linePolynomial a b := by
  have h₁ : coreMatrix a 0 b =
      (X : ℂ[X]) • (1 : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ[X]) + C.mapMatrix (actualCore a b) := by
    rw [coreMatrix_is_charmatrix]
    apply Matrix.ext
    intro i j
    by_cases h : i=j <;> simp [actualCore,
      RingHom.mapMatrix_apply,h]
  have h₂ : linePolynomial a b =
      (X : ℂ[X]) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X]) + C.mapMatrix (lineAdjacency a b) := by
    cases b <;> rfl
  rw [h₁,h₂,Matrix.add_mul,Matrix.mul_add,Matrix.smul_mul,Matrix.mul_smul,Matrix.one_mul,
    Matrix.mul_one]
  congr 1
  change (actualCore a b).map C * (lineJ a b).map C =
      (lineJ a b).map C * (lineAdjacency a b).map C
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := C), ← Matrix.map_mul (f := C), core_line_mul]


noncomputable def lineL (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ :=
  fun p i => if p=i.1 then ((a : ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p else 0

theorem lineL_leftInverse (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lineL a b * lineJ a b = 1 := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro p q
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  change (∑ r : CoreCup a, ∑ _ : Fin (a+1),
      (if p=r then ((a : ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p else 0) *
        (if r=q then gauge a b q else 0)) = _
  have hdiag (r : CoreCup a) :
      (∑ _ : Fin (a+1), (if p=r then ((a : ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p else 0) *
        (if r=q then gauge a b q else 0)) =
        if p=r then ∑ _ : Fin (a+1), (((a : ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p) *
          (if r=q then gauge a b q else 0) else 0 := by
    by_cases h : p=r <;> simp [h]
  simp_rw [hdiag]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single p]
  · by_cases h : p=q
    · subst q
      have hn : (a : ℂ)+1 ≠ 0 := by
        have hh : (((a+1 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero _)
        simpa using hh
      simp only [ite_true, mul_assoc, gauge_sq, mul_one, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ,
        Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_add, Nat.cast_one]
      rw [mul_inv_cancel₀ hn]
      simp
    · simp [h]
  · intro r _ hr
    simp [Ne.symm hr]
  · simp

noncomputable def lineLPoly (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ[X] := (lineL a b).map C

theorem line_poly_leftInverse (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lineLPoly a b * lineJPoly a b = 1 := by
  unfold lineLPoly lineJPoly
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := C),lineL_leftInverse]
  exact map_one C.mapMatrix

noncomputable def residualCore (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreIndex a) ℂ[X] :=
  coreMatrix a 0 b * (1 - lineJPoly a b * lineLPoly a b) + lineJPoly a b * lineLPoly a b

theorem native_core_split_det (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (coreMatrix a 0 b).det = (linePolynomial a b).det * (residualCore a b).det :=
  det_remove_summand _ _ _ _ (coreMatrix_line a b) (line_poly_leftInverse a b)

noncomputable def lineJFrac (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreCup a) LoopField := (lineJPoly a b).map lp

noncomputable def lineLFrac (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreIndex a) LoopField := (lineLPoly a b).map lp

theorem coreMatrix_line_frac (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b) * lineJFrac a b =
      lineJFrac a b * lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b) := by
  unfold lineJFrac
  change (coreMatrix a 0 b).map lp * (lineJPoly a b).map lp =
    (lineJPoly a b).map lp * (linePolynomial a b).map lp
  rw [← Matrix.map_mul (f := lp), ← Matrix.map_mul (f := lp), coreMatrix_line]

theorem line_polynomial_field_det_ne (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b)).det ≠ 0 := by
  rw [← RingHom.map_det]
  have hm : (linePolynomial a b).det.Monic := by
    cases b
    · change (lineChar a).det.Monic
      rw [ordinary_line_charpoly]
      monicity!
    · change (transLineChar a).det.Monic
      rw [transposed_line_charpoly]
      monicity!
  intro h
  exact hm.ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))

#print axioms lineL_leftInverse
#print axioms native_core_split_det
#print axioms coreMatrix_line_frac

#print axioms det_remove_summand
#print axioms inverse_summand
#print axioms core_line_mul
#print axioms coreMatrix_line

end BrauerHook
```
