## 28. `BoundarySplitting` (inherited, 107 lines, sha256 `fe5545efad3c53fa082072a21579dcfc12582e9d7bdb3fcb7fc9ca67a8ca7495`)

```lean
import LineSplitting

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

private theorem sum_paired_core {R : Type*} [AddCommMonoid R]
    (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (f : CoreCup a → R) :
    (∑ q, if q=p then f q else 0) = f p := by
  classical
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single p]
  · simp
  · intro q _ hq
    simp [hq]
  · simp

theorem boundary_out_line (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (boundaryOut a 0 b).map lp * lineJFrac a b = lineOutput a := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro i p
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply,Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  change (∑ q : CoreCup a, ∑ j : Fin (a+1),
    lp (boundaryOut a 0 b i (q,j)) * lp (C (if q=p then gauge a b p else 0))) = _
  have he (q : CoreCup a) (j : Fin (a+1)) :
      lp (boundaryOut a 0 b i (q,j)) * lp (C (if q=p then gauge a b p else 0)) =
        if q=p then lp (C (gauge a b p)) * lp (boundaryOut a 0 b i (q,j)) else 0 := by
    by_cases h : q=p <;> simp [h,mul_comm]
  simp_rw [he,Finset.sum_ite_irrel,Finset.sum_const_zero]
  rw [sum_paired_core, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← map_sum, ← map_mul]
  cases b
  · simp [gauge,lineOutput]
  · have h := congrFun (congrFun (transpose_line_output_eq a) i) p
    simpa only [gauge,ite_true] using h

theorem boundary_in_line (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lineLFrac a b * (boundaryIn a 0 b).map lp = lineInput a := by
  classical
  apply Matrix.ext
  intro p j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply,Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  change (∑ q : CoreCup a, ∑ i : Fin (a+1),
    lp (C (if p=q then ((a:ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p else 0)) *
      lp (boundaryIn a 0 b (q,i) j)) = _
  have hm : lp (C (((a:ℂ)+1)⁻¹)) = (dLoop a)⁻¹ := by
    exact map_inv₀ (lp.comp C) ((a:ℂ)+1)
  have he (q : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
      lp (C (if p=q then ((a:ℂ)+1)⁻¹ * gauge a b p else 0)) *
      lp (boundaryIn a 0 b (q,i) j) =
        if q=p then (dLoop a)⁻¹ * (lp (C (gauge a b p)) *
          lp (boundaryIn a 0 b (q,i) j)) else 0 := by
    by_cases h : q=p
    · subst q
      simp [map_mul,hm,mul_assoc]
    · simp [h,Ne.symm h]
  simp_rw [he,Finset.sum_ite_irrel,Finset.sum_const_zero]
  rw [sum_paired_core, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← Finset.mul_sum, ← map_sum, ← map_mul]
  cases b
  · simp [gauge,lineInput,dLoop]
  · have h := congrFun (congrFun (transpose_line_input_eq a) p) j
    simpa only [gauge,ite_true] using h

noncomputable def firstResolvent (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : LoopField :=
  (dLoop a+1)/dLoop a *
    (if b then (wTrans a+1)/(xTrans*wTrans a) else (wLoop a-1)/(xLoop*wLoop a))

theorem first_summand_evaluation (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lineOutput a * (lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b))⁻¹ * lineInput a =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (firstResolvent a b) := by
  cases b
  · exact ordinary_line_boundary_resolvent a
  · exact transposed_line_boundary_resolvent a

noncomputable def residualCompression (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField :=
  (boundaryOut a 0 b).map lp * (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b))⁻¹ *
    (1-lineJFrac a b*lineLFrac a b) * (boundaryIn a 0 b).map lp

theorem native_boundary_split (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    boundaryCompression a 0 b =
      residualCompression a b + Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (firstResolvent a b) := by
  classical
  let A := lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b)
  let D := lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b)
  let J := lineJFrac a b
  let L := lineLFrac a b
  let B := (boundaryIn a 0 b).map lp
  let O := (boundaryOut a 0 b).map lp
  have h : A⁻¹*J = J*D⁻¹ := inverse_summand A D J (coreMatrix_line_frac a b)
    (core_field_det_ne a 0 b).isUnit (line_polynomial_field_det_ne a b).isUnit
  have h₁ : O*A⁻¹*(J*L)*B = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (firstResolvent a b) := by
    calc
      O*A⁻¹*(J*L)*B = O*(A⁻¹*J)*(L*B) := by simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
      _ = (O*J)*D⁻¹*(L*B) := by rw [h, ← Matrix.mul_assoc O J D⁻¹]
      _ = _ := by
        dsimp only [O,J,L,B,D]
        rw [boundary_out_line,boundary_in_line,first_summand_evaluation]
  change O*A⁻¹*B = O*A⁻¹*(1-J*L)*B + _
  rw [← h₁, ← Matrix.add_mul, ← Matrix.mul_add, sub_add_cancel, Matrix.mul_one]

#print axioms boundary_out_line
#print axioms boundary_in_line
#print axioms first_summand_evaluation
#print axioms native_boundary_split

end BrauerHook
```

## 29. `ChainIJ` (new, 151 lines, sha256 `0779e6a48c10b90a4b641e4ef4d98a2882647ed336ec5307d41b949ce2c5fb41`)

```lean
import Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.Matrix.SchurComplement
import Mathlib.Data.Matrix.ColumnRowPartitioned
import Mathlib.Tactic.FieldSimp
import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
import Mathlib.Tactic.Module
import Mathlib.Tactic.LinearCombination

/-!
Abstract linear algebra used to evaluate the residual core: a shifted Sylvester
identity, and determinants of matrices in the span of the identity and the
all-ones matrix, including 2×2 block matrices with such blocks.
-/

open scoped Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Chain

variable {F : Type*} [Field F]

section Sylvester
variable {α β : Type*} [Fintype α] [DecidableEq α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq β]

theorem sylvester_shift (A : Matrix α β F) (B : Matrix β α F) (x : F) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (x • (1 : Matrix α α F) + A*B).det * x^(Fintype.card β) =
      (x • (1 : Matrix β β F) + B*A).det * x^(Fintype.card α) := by
  have hA : x • (1 + (x⁻¹ • A) * B) = x • (1 : Matrix α α F) + A * B := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.smul_mul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  have hB : x • (1 + B * (x⁻¹ • A)) = x • (1 : Matrix β β F) + B * A := by
    rw [smul_add, Matrix.mul_smul, smul_smul, mul_inv_cancel₀ hx, one_smul]
  rw [← hA, ← hB, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_one_add_mul_comm]
  ring

end Sylvester

section IJ
variable {P : Type*} [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P]

/-- The all-ones matrix. -/
def J : Matrix P P F := Matrix.of fun _ _ => 1

theorem J_mul_J : (J : Matrix P P F) * J = (Fintype.card P : F) • J := by
  ext i k
  simp [J, Matrix.mul_apply]

/-- Matrices in the span of the identity and the all-ones matrix. -/
def IJ (x y : F) : Matrix P P F := x • 1 + y • J

theorem IJ_mul (x y x' y' : F) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) * IJ x' y' =
      IJ (x*x') (x*y' + y*x' + (Fintype.card P : F)*y*y') := by
  simp only [IJ, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul,
    Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one, J_mul_J, smul_smul]
  module

theorem IJ_add (x y x' y' : F) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) + IJ x' y' = IJ (x+x') (y+y') := by
  simp only [IJ]
  module

theorem IJ_sub (x y x' y' : F) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) - IJ x' y' = IJ (x-x') (y-y') := by
  simp only [IJ]
  module

theorem IJ_smul (c x y : F) :
    c • (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) = IJ (c*x) (c*y) := by
  simp only [IJ]
  module

theorem IJ_one : (1 : Matrix P P F) = IJ 1 0 := by
  simp [IJ]

theorem IJ_zero : (0 : Matrix P P F) = IJ 0 0 := by
  simp [IJ]

/-- Columns killed by the all-ones matrix see `IJ x y` as the scalar `x`. -/
theorem IJ_mul_of_J {ι : Type*} (x y : F) (U : Matrix P ι F) (hU : (J : Matrix P P F) * U = 0) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) * U = x • U := by
  rw [IJ, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, hU, Matrix.one_mul, smul_zero,
    add_zero]

theorem det_IJ [Nonempty P] (x y : F) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F).det =
      x^(Fintype.card P - 1) * (x + (Fintype.card P : F) * y) := by
  let A : Matrix P Unit F := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => y)
  let B : Matrix Unit P F := Matrix.of (fun _ _ => 1)
  have hAB : A*B = y • (J : Matrix P P F) := by
    ext i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, A, B, J]
  have hBA : B*A = Matrix.of (fun _ _ => (Fintype.card P : F) * y) := by
    ext i j
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, A, B]
  have hs := sylvester_shift A B x hx
  rw [hAB, hBA] at hs
  simp only [Fintype.card_unit, pow_one, Matrix.det_unique, Matrix.add_apply,
    Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, ite_true, smul_eq_mul, mul_one, Matrix.of_apply] at hs
  have hp : 0 < Fintype.card P := Fintype.card_pos
  have he : x^(Fintype.card P) = x^(Fintype.card P-1)*x := by
    nth_rw 1 [show Fintype.card P = (Fintype.card P-1)+1 by omega]
    rw [pow_succ]
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ hx
  rw [IJ, hs, he]
  ring

theorem IJ_inverse (x y : F) (hx : x ≠ 0) (hxy : x + (Fintype.card P : F)*y ≠ 0) :
    (IJ x y : Matrix P P F) * IJ x⁻¹ (-y/(x*(x + (Fintype.card P : F)*y))) = 1 := by
  rw [IJ_mul, IJ_one]
  congr 1
  · field_simp
  · rw [div_eq_mul_inv, mul_inv]
    linear_combination (-y * x⁻¹) * mul_inv_cancel₀ hxy

private theorem ne_of_mul_eq (a e c : F) (h : a * e = c) (hc : c ≠ 0) : e ≠ 0 := by
  rintro rfl
  exact hc (by rw [← h, mul_zero])

private theorem pow_mul_aux (a b c d e f : F) (k : ℕ) (h₁ : a * c = e) (h₂ : b * d = f) :
    a^k * b * (c^k * d) = e^k * f := by
  rw [← h₁, ← h₂, mul_pow]
  ring

/-- Determinant of a 2×2 block matrix whose blocks lie in the span of `1` and `J`. -/
theorem det_blocks_IJ [Nonempty P] (x₁ y₁ x₂ y₂ x₃ y₃ x₄ y₄ : F)
    (h₁ : x₁ ≠ 0) (h₂ : x₁ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₁ ≠ 0) (h₃ : x₁*x₄ - x₂*x₃ ≠ 0) :
    (Matrix.fromBlocks (IJ x₁ y₁ : Matrix P P F) (IJ x₂ y₂) (IJ x₃ y₃) (IJ x₄ y₄)).det =
      (x₁*x₄ - x₂*x₃)^(Fintype.card P - 1) *
        ((x₁ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₁)*(x₄ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₄) -
          (x₂ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₂)*(x₃ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₃)) := by
  have hAi : (IJ x₁ y₁ : Matrix P P F) *
      IJ x₁⁻¹ (-y₁/(x₁*(x₁ + (Fintype.card P : F)*y₁))) = 1 := IJ_inverse x₁ y₁ h₁ h₂
  have : Invertible (IJ x₁ y₁ : Matrix P P F) := invertibleOfRightInverse _ _ hAi
  rw [Matrix.det_fromBlocks₁₁, invOf_eq_right_inv hAi, IJ_mul, IJ_mul, IJ_sub, det_IJ _ _ h₁,
    det_IJ _ _ (ne_of_mul_eq x₁ _ _ ?_ h₃)]
  · apply pow_mul_aux
    · field_simp
    · field_simp
      ring
  · field_simp

end IJ

#print axioms sylvester_shift
#print axioms IJ_mul
#print axioms det_IJ
#print axioms det_blocks_IJ

end Chain
end BrauerHook
```

## 30. `ChainDet` (new, 147 lines, sha256 `a5d06eb7e143d99f4412e9578e7d25d46915610dd73a69a875186a53c7e29515`)

```lean
import ChainIJ

/-!
Abstract determinant reduction for a matrix of the form
`Y • 1 + ε • (B * Bᵀ) + Ct * Ctᵀ`, given the incidence identities satisfied by
the native core in site coordinates.
-/

open scoped Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Chain

variable {F : Type*} [Field F]
variable {S O T Q P : Type*}
variable [Fintype S] [DecidableEq S] [Fintype O] [DecidableEq O] [Fintype T] [DecidableEq T]
variable [Fintype Q] [DecidableEq Q] [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P]

/-- The gauged native core in incidence form. -/
def coreForm (B : Matrix S O F) (Ct : Matrix S T F) (ε Y : F) : Matrix S S F :=
  Y • 1 + ε • (B * Bᵀ) + Ct * Ctᵀ

def alpha (ε m Y : F) : F := Y + ε*(m-2) - 1
def beta (ε m Y : F) : F := ε*(m-2-2*ε)/(Y+3)
def gam (Y : F) : F := (Y+3)⁻¹
def delt (ε m Y : F) : F := beta ε m Y / (alpha ε m Y - 2*beta ε m Y)
def gee (ε m : F) : F := m - 1 - ε

theorem eps_cases {ε : F} (hε : ε * ε = 1) : ε = 1 ∨ ε = -1 := by
  have h : (ε - 1) * (ε + 1) = 0 := by linear_combination hε
  rcases mul_eq_zero.mp h with h | h
  · left; linear_combination h
  · right; linear_combination h

/-- The matrix on ordered pairs obtained after removing the triple level. -/
def zForm (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) (ε m Y : F) : Matrix O O F :=
  alpha ε m Y • 1 + H * Hᵀ - beta ε m Y • (D * Dᵀ) - gam Y • ((D*N) * (D*N)ᵀ)

/-- The first factor of the second-level splitting. -/
def zOne (D : Matrix O Q F) (ε m Y : F) : Matrix O O F :=
  alpha ε m Y • 1 - beta ε m Y • (D * Dᵀ)

section Tri
variable (Ct : Matrix S T F) (Y : F)

theorem tri_sq (h2 : Ctᵀ * Ct = (3:F) • 1) : (Ct * Ctᵀ) * (Ct * Ctᵀ) = (3:F) • (Ct * Ctᵀ) := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc Ctᵀ, h2, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul,
    Matrix.mul_smul]

theorem tri_det (h2 : Ctᵀ * Ct = (3:F) • 1) (hY : Y ≠ 0) :
    (Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + Ct * Ctᵀ).det * Y^(Fintype.card T) =
      (Y+3)^(Fintype.card T) * Y^(Fintype.card S) := by
  rw [sylvester_shift Ct Ctᵀ Y hY, h2, ← add_smul, Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_one, mul_one]

def triInv : Matrix S S F := Y⁻¹ • (1 - (Y+3)⁻¹ • (Ct * Ctᵀ))

theorem tri_inv_right (h2 : Ctᵀ * Ct = (3:F) • 1) (hY : Y ≠ 0) (hY3 : Y + 3 ≠ 0) :
    (Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + Ct * Ctᵀ) * triInv Ct Y = 1 := by
  set M := Ct * Ctᵀ with hM
  have hsq : M * M = (3:F) • M := tri_sq Ct h2
  have e : (Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + M) * (1 - (Y+3)⁻¹ • M) = Y • 1 := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.smul_mul,
      Matrix.one_mul, hsq]
    have h : (Y+3)⁻¹ • (Y • M + (3:F) • M) = M := by
      rw [← add_smul, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ hY3, one_smul]
    rw [h]
    abel
  rw [triInv, Matrix.mul_smul, e, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ hY, one_smul]

theorem tri_inv_eq (h2 : Ctᵀ * Ct = (3:F) • 1) (hY : Y ≠ 0) (hY3 : Y + 3 ≠ 0) :
    (Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + Ct * Ctᵀ)⁻¹ = triInv Ct Y :=
  Matrix.inv_eq_right_inv (tri_inv_right Ct Y h2 hY hY3)

end Tri

section Level1
variable (B : Matrix S O F) (Ct : Matrix S T F) (ε Y : F)

/-- The Schur reduction to ordered pairs, before using the incidence identities. -/
theorem schur_pairs_raw (hY : Y ≠ 0) :
    Y • (1 + (ε • Bᵀ) * triInv Ct Y * B) =
      Y • 1 + ε • (Bᵀ * B) - (ε * (Y+3)⁻¹) • ((Bᵀ * Ct) * (Ctᵀ * B)) := by
  simp only [triInv, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul,
    Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one, smul_add, smul_sub,
    smul_smul, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  match_scalars <;> field_simp

end Level1

section Level2
variable (B : Matrix S O F) (Ct : Matrix S T F) (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F)
  (N : Matrix Q P F) (K₂ : Matrix Q T F) (ε m Y : F)

theorem schur_pairs (hε : ε * ε = 1)
    (h1 : Bᵀ * B = (m-2-ε) • 1 + ε • (H * Hᵀ))
    (h3 : Ctᵀ * B = ε • (K₂ᵀ * Dᵀ))
    (h4 : K₂ * K₂ᵀ = (m-2-2*ε) • 1 + ε • (N * Nᵀ)) :
    Y • 1 + ε • (Bᵀ * B) - (ε * (Y+3)⁻¹) • ((Bᵀ * Ct) * (Ctᵀ * B)) = zForm H D N ε m Y := by
  have hBC : Bᵀ * Ct = ε • (D * K₂) := by
    rw [← Matrix.transpose_transpose (Bᵀ * Ct), Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose,
      h3, Matrix.transpose_smul, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose,
      Matrix.transpose_transpose]
  have h4' : K₂ * (K₂ᵀ * Dᵀ) = (m-2-2*ε) • Dᵀ + ε • (N * (Nᵀ * Dᵀ)) := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, h4, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.smul_mul,
      Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  rw [h1, hBC, h3, zForm, alpha, beta, gam]
  simp only [Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul,
    Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one, smul_add, smul_sub,
    smul_smul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.transpose_mul, h4']
  obtain rfl | rfl := eps_cases hε
  · match_scalars <;> ring
  · match_scalars <;> ring

theorem zone_det (hDD : Dᵀ * D = (2:F) • 1) (ha : alpha ε m Y ≠ 0) :
    (zOne D ε m Y).det * (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card Q) =
      (alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y)^(Fintype.card Q) * (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card O) := by
  have e : zOne D ε m Y = alpha ε m Y • 1 + D * (-(beta ε m Y) • Dᵀ) := by
    rw [zOne, Matrix.mul_smul, neg_smul, sub_eq_add_neg]
  rw [e, sylvester_shift D _ _ ha, Matrix.smul_mul, hDD, smul_smul, ← add_smul,
    Matrix.det_smul, Matrix.det_one, mul_one]
  congr 2
  ring

def zOneInv (D : Matrix O Q F) (ε m Y : F) : Matrix O O F :=
  (alpha ε m Y)⁻¹ • (1 + delt ε m Y • (D * Dᵀ))

theorem zone_inv_right (hDD : Dᵀ * D = (2:F) • 1) (ha : alpha ε m Y ≠ 0)
    (hab : alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y ≠ 0) :
    zOne D ε m Y * zOneInv D ε m Y = 1 := by
  have hDD2 : Dᵀ * (D * Dᵀ) = (2:F) • Dᵀ := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, hDD, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
  simp only [zOne, zOneInv, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul,
    Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_one, smul_add, smul_sub,
    smul_smul, Matrix.mul_assoc, hDD2]
  match_scalars
  · field_simp
  · rw [delt]
    field_simp
    linear_combination (beta ε m Y) * mul_inv_cancel₀ hab

end Level2

end Chain
end BrauerHook
```

## 31. `ChainBlock` (new, 116 lines, sha256 `612e598a30716a09efcb33608090f4b16106d9fa640cc20b1d9f8feb4af37fa3`)

```lean
import ChainDet

/-!
Second-level splitting of the ordered-pair matrix, its 2×2 block form on two
copies of the sites, and the assembled abstract determinant formula.
-/

open scoped Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Chain

variable {F : Type*} [Field F]
variable {S O T Q P : Type*}
variable [Fintype S] [DecidableEq S] [Fintype O] [DecidableEq O] [Fintype T] [DecidableEq T]
variable [Fintype Q] [DecidableEq Q] [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P]

def uW (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) : Matrix O (P ⊕ P) F :=
  Matrix.fromCols H (D*N)

def vW (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) (Y : F) :
    Matrix (P ⊕ P) O F :=
  Matrix.fromRows Hᵀ (-(gam Y) • (D*N)ᵀ)

def bx1 (ε m Y : F) : F := alpha ε m Y + m - 1 + delt ε m Y * gee ε m
def by1 (ε m Y : F) : F := delt ε m Y * ε
def bx2 (ε m Y : F) : F := ε * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y) * gee ε m
def by2 (ε m Y : F) : F := 1 + 2 * delt ε m Y
def bx3 (ε m Y : F) : F := -(gam Y * ε * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y) * gee ε m)
def by3 (ε m Y : F) : F := -(gam Y * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y))
def bx4 (ε m Y : F) : F := alpha ε m Y - 2 * gam Y * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y) * gee ε m
def by4 (ε m Y : F) : F := -(2 * gam Y * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y) * ε)

/-- Eigenvalue factor of the sites block on sum-zero vectors. -/
def sZero (ε m Y : F) : F := bx1 ε m Y * bx4 ε m Y - bx2 ε m Y * bx3 ε m Y

/-- Eigenvalue factor of the sites block on the constant vector. -/
def sOne (ε m Y : F) : F :=
  (bx1 ε m Y + m * by1 ε m Y) * (bx4 ε m Y + m * by4 ε m Y) -
    (bx2 ε m Y + m * by2 ε m Y) * (bx3 ε m Y + m * by3 ε m Y)

section Split
variable (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) (ε m Y : F)

theorem zform_split :
    zForm H D N ε m Y = zOne D ε m Y + uW H D N * vW H D N Y := by
  rw [uW, vW, Matrix.fromCols_mul_fromRows, zForm, zOne, Matrix.mul_smul]
  module

variable (hε : ε * ε = 1) (h5 : Hᵀ * H = (m-1) • 1) (h6 : Dᵀ * H = ε • N)
  (h7 : Dᵀ * D = (2:F) • 1)

include h6 in
theorem ht_d : Hᵀ * D = ε • Nᵀ := by
  rw [← Matrix.transpose_transpose (Hᵀ * D), Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose,
    h6, Matrix.transpose_smul]

include h6 in
theorem zinv_h : zOneInv D ε m Y * H =
    (alpha ε m Y)⁻¹ • (H + (delt ε m Y * ε) • (D * N)) := by
  rw [zOneInv, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.smul_mul,
    Matrix.mul_assoc, h6, Matrix.mul_smul, smul_smul]

include h7 in
theorem zinv_dn : zOneInv D ε m Y * (D * N) =
    ((alpha ε m Y)⁻¹ * (1 + 2 * delt ε m Y)) • (D * N) := by
  have e : D * Dᵀ * (D * N) = (2:F) • (D * N) := by
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc Dᵀ, h7, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul,
      Matrix.mul_smul]
  rw [zOneInv, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, e, smul_smul]
  module

include h6 in
theorem ht_dn : Hᵀ * (D * N) = ε • (Nᵀ * N) := by
  rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, ht_d H D N ε h6, Matrix.smul_mul]

include h6 in
theorem dnt_h : (D * N)ᵀ * H = ε • (Nᵀ * N) := by
  rw [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, h6, Matrix.mul_smul]

include h7 in
theorem dnt_dn : (D * N)ᵀ * (D * N) = (2:F) • (Nᵀ * N) := by
  rw [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc Dᵀ, h7, Matrix.smul_mul,
    Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_smul]

include hε h5 h6 h7 in
theorem block_form (h8 : Nᵀ * N = IJ (m-1-ε) ε) (ha : alpha ε m Y ≠ 0) :
    alpha ε m Y • (1 + vW H D N Y * zOneInv D ε m Y * uW H D N) =
      Matrix.fromBlocks (IJ (bx1 ε m Y) (by1 ε m Y)) (IJ (bx2 ε m Y) (by2 ε m Y))
        (IJ (bx3 ε m Y) (by3 ε m Y)) (IJ (bx4 ε m Y) (by4 ε m Y)) := by
  have e1 := zinv_h (H := H) (D := D) (N := N) (ε := ε) (m := m) (Y := Y) (h6 := h6)
  have e2 := zinv_dn (D := D) (N := N) (ε := ε) (m := m) (Y := Y) (h7 := h7)
  have e3 := ht_dn (H := H) (D := D) (N := N) (ε := ε) (h6 := h6)
  have e4 := dnt_h (H := H) (D := D) (N := N) (ε := ε) (h6 := h6)
  have e5 := dnt_dn (D := D) (N := N) (h7 := h7)
  rw [vW, uW, Matrix.fromRows_mul, Matrix.fromRows_mul_fromCols, ← Matrix.fromBlocks_one,
    Matrix.fromBlocks_add, Matrix.fromBlocks_smul]
  simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.smul_mul, e1, e2, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.mul_add,
    smul_add, smul_smul, add_zero, zero_add]
  simp only [← Matrix.mul_assoc, e3, e4, e5, h5, h8, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul,
    smul_smul, smul_add]
  rw [IJ_one]
  simp only [IJ_smul, IJ_add, IJ_zero]
  obtain rfl | rfl := eps_cases hε
  · congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> simp only [bx1, by1, bx2, by2, bx3, by3, bx4, by4, gee] <;>
      field_simp <;> ring
  · congr 1 <;> congr 1 <;> simp only [bx1, by1, bx2, by2, bx3, by3, bx4, by4, gee] <;>
      field_simp <;> ring

end Split

end Chain
end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import ChainBlock

/-!
The assembled abstract determinant formula for the incidence form of the core.
-/

open scoped Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Chain

variable {F : Type*} [Field F]
variable {S O T Q P : Type*}
variable [Fintype S] [DecidableEq S] [Fintype O] [DecidableEq O] [Fintype T] [DecidableEq T]
variable [Fintype Q] [DecidableEq Q] [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P]

/-- Incidence identities used by the reduction. -/
structure Identities (B : Matrix S O F) (Ct : Matrix S T F) (H : Matrix O P F)
    (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) (K₂ : Matrix Q T F) (ε m : F) : Prop where
  hε : ε * ε = 1
  hm : (Fintype.card P : F) = m
  h1 : Bᵀ * B = (m-2-ε) • 1 + ε • (H * Hᵀ)
  h2 : Ctᵀ * Ct = (3:F) • 1
  h3 : Ctᵀ * B = ε • (K₂ᵀ * Dᵀ)
  h4 : K₂ * K₂ᵀ = (m-2-2*ε) • 1 + ε • (N * Nᵀ)
  h5 : Hᵀ * H = (m-1) • 1
  h6 : Dᵀ * H = ε • N
  h7 : Dᵀ * D = (2:F) • 1
  h8 : Nᵀ * N = IJ (m-1-ε) ε

/-- Nonvanishing conditions on the scalars of the reduction. -/
structure Generic (ε m Y : F) : Prop where
  hY : Y ≠ 0
  hY3 : Y + 3 ≠ 0
  ha : alpha ε m Y ≠ 0
  hab : alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y ≠ 0
  hx1 : bx1 ε m Y ≠ 0
  hx1m : bx1 ε m Y + m * by1 ε m Y ≠ 0
  hs0 : sZero ε m Y ≠ 0

section
variable {B : Matrix S O F} {Ct : Matrix S T F} {H : Matrix O P F} {D : Matrix O Q F}
  {N : Matrix Q P F} {K₂ : Matrix Q T F} {ε m Y : F}

theorem pairs_form (I : Identities B Ct H D N K₂ ε m) (hY : Y ≠ 0) :
    1 + (ε • Bᵀ) * triInv Ct Y * B = Y⁻¹ • zForm H D N ε m Y := by
  rw [← schur_pairs B Ct H D N K₂ ε m Y I.hε I.h1 I.h3 I.h4, ← schur_pairs_raw B Ct ε Y hY,
    smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ hY, one_smul]

theorem chain_det [Nonempty P] (I : Identities B Ct H D N K₂ ε m) (G : Generic ε m Y) :
    (coreForm B Ct ε Y).det * Y^(Fintype.card T) * Y^(Fintype.card O) *
        (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card Q) * (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card P + Fintype.card P) =
      (Y+3)^(Fintype.card T) * Y^(Fintype.card S) *
        (alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y)^(Fintype.card Q) * (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card O) *
        (sZero ε m Y ^ (Fintype.card P - 1) * sOne ε m Y) := by
  have hA := tri_det Ct Y I.h2 G.hY
  have hAinv := tri_inv_eq Ct Y I.h2 G.hY G.hY3
  have hcore : coreForm B Ct ε Y = (Y • 1 + Ct * Ctᵀ) + B * (ε • Bᵀ) := by
    rw [coreForm, Matrix.mul_smul]
    abel
  have hAu : IsUnit (Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + Ct * Ctᵀ).det := by
    apply isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr
    intro h
    rw [h, zero_mul] at hA
    exact mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ G.hY3) (pow_ne_zero _ G.hY) hA.symm
  have hZ1 := zone_det D ε m Y I.h7 G.ha
  have hZ1u : IsUnit (zOne D ε m Y).det := by
    apply isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr
    intro h
    rw [h, zero_mul] at hZ1
    exact mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ G.hab) (pow_ne_zero _ G.ha) hZ1.symm
  have hZ1inv : (zOne D ε m Y)⁻¹ = zOneInv D ε m Y :=
    Matrix.inv_eq_right_inv (zone_inv_right D ε m Y I.h7 G.ha G.hab)
  have hblock : 1 + vW H D N Y * zOneInv D ε m Y * uW H D N =
      (alpha ε m Y)⁻¹ • Matrix.fromBlocks (IJ (bx1 ε m Y) (by1 ε m Y))
        (IJ (bx2 ε m Y) (by2 ε m Y)) (IJ (bx3 ε m Y) (by3 ε m Y))
        (IJ (bx4 ε m Y) (by4 ε m Y)) := by
    rw [← block_form H D N ε m Y I.hε I.h5 I.h6 I.h7 I.h8 G.ha, smul_smul,
      inv_mul_cancel₀ G.ha, one_smul]
  have hS := det_blocks_IJ (P := P) (bx1 ε m Y) (by1 ε m Y) (bx2 ε m Y) (by2 ε m Y)
    (bx3 ε m Y) (by3 ε m Y) (bx4 ε m Y) (by4 ε m Y) G.hx1 (by rw [I.hm]; exact G.hx1m)
    G.hs0
  rw [I.hm] at hS
  rw [hcore, Matrix.det_add_mul B (ε • Bᵀ) hAu, hAinv, pairs_form I G.hY, Matrix.det_smul,
    zform_split, Matrix.det_add_mul _ _ hZ1u, hZ1inv, hblock, Matrix.det_smul, hS,
    Fintype.card_sum]
  have eY : (Y⁻¹)^(Fintype.card O) * Y^(Fintype.card O) = 1 := by
    rw [← mul_pow, inv_mul_cancel₀ G.hY, one_pow]
  have eA : ((alpha ε m Y)⁻¹)^(Fintype.card P + Fintype.card P) *
      (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card P + Fintype.card P) = 1 := by
    rw [← mul_pow, inv_mul_cancel₀ G.ha, one_pow]
  unfold sZero sOne
  calc _ = ((Y • (1 : Matrix S S F) + Ct * Ctᵀ).det * Y^(Fintype.card T)) *
        ((Y⁻¹)^(Fintype.card O) * Y^(Fintype.card O)) *
        ((zOne D ε m Y).det * (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card Q)) *
        (((alpha ε m Y)⁻¹)^(Fintype.card P + Fintype.card P) *
          (alpha ε m Y)^(Fintype.card P + Fintype.card P)) *
        ((bx1 ε m Y * bx4 ε m Y - bx2 ε m Y * bx3 ε m Y) ^ (Fintype.card P - 1) *
          ((bx1 ε m Y + m * by1 ε m Y) * (bx4 ε m Y + m * by4 ε m Y) -
            (bx2 ε m Y + m * by2 ε m Y) * (bx3 ε m Y + m * by3 ε m Y))) := by ring
    _ = _ := by
      rw [hA, eY, hZ1, eA]
      ring

end

#print axioms chain_det

end Chain
end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import ChainFinal

/-!
Abstract evaluation of a compression `(B Ω)ᵀ (coreForm)⁻¹ (B w)` by an explicit
solution of the linear system, built from the incidence identities.
-/

open scoped Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Chain

variable {F : Type*} [Field F]
variable {S O T Q P : Type*}
variable [Fintype S] [DecidableEq S] [Fintype O] [DecidableEq O] [Fintype T] [DecidableEq T]
variable [Fintype Q] [DecidableEq Q] [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P]
variable {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι]

/-- Test data for a compression. -/
structure TestData (H : Matrix O P F) (D : Matrix O Q F) (N : Matrix Q P F) (ε : F)
    (w Ω : Matrix O ι F) (U : Matrix P ι F) (Rr : Matrix Q ι F) : Prop where
  f1 : Hᵀ * w = U
  f2 : Dᵀ * w = Rr
  f3 : Nᵀ * Rr = ε • U
  f4 : (J : Matrix P P F) * U = 0
  p0 : Ωᵀ * w = 1
  p1 : (Hᵀ * Ω)ᵀ * U = 1
  p2 : (Nᵀ * (Dᵀ * Ω))ᵀ * U = ε • 1
  p3 : (Dᵀ * Ω)ᵀ * Rr = 1

/-- Scalar equations for the coefficients of the explicit solution. -/
structure SolveScalars (ε m Y d₁ e₂ c₁ : F) : Prop where
  s1 : (alpha ε m Y + m - 1) * d₁ + ε * gee ε m * e₂ + c₁ = 1
  s2 : c₁ * (alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y) = -beta ε m Y
  s3 : (alpha ε m Y - 2 * beta ε m Y - 2 * gam Y * gee ε m) * e₂ -
    (beta ε m Y + gam Y * gee ε m) * ε * d₁ - 2 * gam Y * ε * c₁ = -(gam Y * ε)

section
variable {B : Matrix S O F} {Ct : Matrix S T F} {H : Matrix O P F} {D : Matrix O Q F}
  {N : Matrix Q P F} {K₂ : Matrix Q T F} {ε m Y : F}
  {w Ω : Matrix O ι F} {U : Matrix P ι F} {Rr : Matrix Q ι F} {d₁ e₂ c₁ : F}

theorem zform_solution (I : Identities B Ct H D N K₂ ε m) (Td : TestData H D N ε w Ω U Rr)
    (Sc : SolveScalars ε m Y d₁ e₂ c₁) :
    zForm H D N ε m Y * (w - (d₁ • (H * U) + e₂ • (D * (N * U)) + c₁ • (D * Rr))) =
      alpha ε m Y • w := by
  have hHD : Hᵀ * D = ε • Nᵀ := ht_d H D N ε I.h6
  have r1 : Hᵀ * (H * U) = (m-1) • U := by rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h5, Matrix.smul_mul,
    Matrix.one_mul]
  have r2 : Hᵀ * (D * (N * U)) = ε • (Nᵀ * (N * U)) := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, hHD, Matrix.smul_mul]
  have r3 : Hᵀ * (D * Rr) = ε • (Nᵀ * Rr) := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, hHD, Matrix.smul_mul]
  have r4 : Dᵀ * (H * U) = ε • (N * U) := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h6, Matrix.smul_mul]
  have r5 : Dᵀ * (D * (N * U)) = (2:F) • (N * U) := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h7, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
  have r6 : Dᵀ * (D * Rr) = (2:F) • Rr := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h7, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
  have r7 : Nᵀ * (N * U) = gee ε m • U := by
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h8, IJ_mul_of_J _ _ U Td.f4, gee]
  rw [zForm]
  simp only [Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul,
    Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_smul,
    Td.f1, Td.f2, r1, r2, r3, r4, r5, r6, r7, Td.f3, smul_add, smul_sub, smul_smul]
  have hs1 := Sc.s1
  have hs2 := Sc.s2
  have hs3 := Sc.s3
  obtain rfl | rfl := eps_cases I.hε
  · match_scalars
    · ring
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs1
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs2
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs3
  · match_scalars
    · ring
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs1
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs2
    · linear_combination (-1 : F) * hs3


theorem chain_compress (I : Identities B Ct H D N K₂ ε m) (Td : TestData H D N ε w Ω U Rr)
    (Sc : SolveScalars ε m Y d₁ e₂ c₁) (hY3 : Y + 3 ≠ 0) (ha : alpha ε m Y ≠ 0)
    (hK : IsUnit (coreForm B Ct ε Y).det) :
    (B * Ω)ᵀ * (coreForm B Ct ε Y)⁻¹ * (B * w) =
      (ε * (1 - Y / alpha ε m Y * (1 - d₁ - ε * e₂ - c₁))) • (1 : Matrix ι ι F) := by
  set V := d₁ • (H * U) + e₂ • (D * (N * U)) + c₁ • (D * Rr) with hV
  set y := (alpha ε m Y)⁻¹ • (w - V) with hy
  have hzy : zForm H D N ε m Y * y = w := by
    rw [hy, Matrix.mul_smul, zform_solution I Td Sc, smul_smul, inv_mul_cancel₀ ha, one_smul]
  have hz' := schur_pairs B Ct H D N K₂ ε m Y I.hε I.h1 I.h3 I.h4
  have hCC : ∀ M : Matrix T ι F, Ctᵀ * (Ct * M) = (3:F) • M := by
    intro M
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, I.h2, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
  set X := B * y - (Y+3)⁻¹ • (Ct * (Ctᵀ * (B * y))) with hX
  have hKX : coreForm B Ct ε Y * X = B * w := by
    have e : coreForm B Ct ε Y * X = B * ((Y • 1 + ε • (Bᵀ * B) -
        (ε * (Y+3)⁻¹) • ((Bᵀ * Ct) * (Ctᵀ * B))) * y) := by
      simp only [coreForm, hX, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.smul_mul,
        Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_smul,
        smul_add, smul_sub, smul_smul, hCC (Ctᵀ * (B * y))]
      match_scalars
      · ring
      · ring
      · field_simp
        ring
      · ring
    rw [e, hz', hzy]
  have hinv : (coreForm B Ct ε Y)⁻¹ * (B * w) = X := by
    rw [← hKX, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.nonsing_inv_mul _ hK, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hBX : Bᵀ * X = ε • (w - Y • y) := by
    have e : Bᵀ * X = ε • ((Y • 1 + ε • (Bᵀ * B) -
        (ε * (Y+3)⁻¹) • ((Bᵀ * Ct) * (Ctᵀ * B))) * y - Y • y) := by
      simp only [hX, Matrix.add_mul, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.smul_mul,
        Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_smul,
        smul_add, smul_sub, smul_smul]
      obtain rfl | rfl := eps_cases I.hε
      · match_scalars <;> ring
      · match_scalars <;> ring
    rw [e, hz', hzy]
  have q1 : Ωᵀ * (H * U) = 1 := by
    have h := Td.p1
    simp only [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_assoc] at h
    exact h
  have q2 : Ωᵀ * (D * (N * U)) = ε • 1 := by
    have h := Td.p2
    simp only [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_assoc] at h
    exact h
  have q3 : Ωᵀ * (D * Rr) = 1 := by
    have h := Td.p3
    simp only [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_assoc] at h
    exact h
  rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, hinv, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, hBX, hy, hV]
  simp only [Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.mul_add, Td.p0, q1, q2, q3, smul_sub,
    smul_add, smul_smul]
  match_scalars
  field_simp
  ring

end

#print axioms chain_compress

end Chain
end BrauerHook
```

## 34. `SiteSums` (new, 280 lines, sha256 `2ffb9b8073b6c13085a4e73558b91c58309e01b4a117441ab37bb02c27e9f7e7`)

```lean
import CoreIncidence

/-!
Site configurations of the native core and summation lemmas over cups, ordered
site pairs, site triples and configurations.

A site configuration is a core cup together with one core site not on the cup.
-/

open scoped BigOperators

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

/-- A core cup with one free core site. -/
abbrev SConf (a : ℕ) := {c : CoreCup a × CoreSite a // ¬ incident c.1 c.2}

/-- Ordered pairs of distinct core sites. -/
abbrev OPair (a : ℕ) := {o : CoreSite a × CoreSite a // o.1 ≠ o.2}

/-- Increasing triples of core sites. -/
abbrev Tri (a : ℕ) :=
  {t : CoreSite a × CoreSite a × CoreSite a // t.1 < t.2.1 ∧ t.2.1 < t.2.2}

def memT {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (x : CoreSite a) : Prop :=
  x = t.val.1 ∨ x = t.val.2.1 ∨ x = t.val.2.2

instance {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) : DecidablePred (memT t) :=
  fun x => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (x = t.val.1 ∨ x = t.val.2.1 ∨ x = t.val.2.2))

theorem incident_iff_val {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) :
    incident p x ↔ x.val = p.val.1.val ∨ x.val = p.val.2.val := by
  simp [incident, Fin.ext_iff]

theorem memT_iff_val {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (x : CoreSite a) :
    memT t x ↔ x.val = t.val.1.val ∨ x.val = t.val.2.1.val ∨ x.val = t.val.2.2.val := by
  simp [memT, Fin.ext_iff]

theorem cup_lt {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) : p.val.1.val < p.val.2.val := p.property

theorem tri_lt {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) :
    t.val.1.val < t.val.2.1.val ∧ t.val.2.1.val < t.val.2.2.val := t.property

theorem cup_ext {a : ℕ} {p q : CoreCup a} :
    p = q ↔ p.val.1.val = q.val.1.val ∧ p.val.2.val = q.val.2.val := by
  constructor
  · rintro rfl; exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  · rintro ⟨h₁, h₂⟩
    exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (Fin.ext h₁) (Fin.ext h₂))

theorem tri_ext {a : ℕ} {t u : Tri a} :
    t = u ↔ t.val.1.val = u.val.1.val ∧ t.val.2.1.val = u.val.2.1.val ∧
      t.val.2.2.val = u.val.2.2.val := by
  constructor
  · rintro rfl; exact ⟨rfl, rfl, rfl⟩
  · rintro ⟨h₁, h₂, h₃⟩
    exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext (Fin.ext h₁) (Prod.ext (Fin.ext h₂) (Fin.ext h₃)))

theorem sconf_ext {a : ℕ} {c d : SConf a} :
    c = d ↔ c.val.1 = d.val.1 ∧ c.val.2.val = d.val.2.val := by
  constructor
  · rintro rfl; exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  · rintro ⟨h₁, h₂⟩
    exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h₁ (Fin.ext h₂))

/-- The increasing triple underlying a site configuration. -/
def sort3 {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x) : Tri a :=
  if h₁ : x < p.val.1 then ⟨(x, (p.val.1, p.val.2)), h₁, p.property⟩
  else if h₂ : x < p.val.2 then
    ⟨(p.val.1, (x, p.val.2)), by
      have hx' := (incident_iff_val p x).not.mp hx
      simp only [Fin.lt_def, not_lt] at h₁ h₂ ⊢
      omega, h₂⟩
  else
    ⟨(p.val.1, (p.val.2, x)), p.property, by
      have hx' := (incident_iff_val p x).not.mp hx
      simp only [Fin.lt_def, not_lt] at h₁ h₂ ⊢
      omega⟩

theorem sort3_val {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x) :
    ((sort3 p x hx).val.1.val = if x.val < p.val.1.val then x.val else p.val.1.val) ∧
    ((sort3 p x hx).val.2.1.val = if x.val < p.val.1.val then p.val.1.val else
        if x.val < p.val.2.val then x.val else p.val.2.val) ∧
    ((sort3 p x hx).val.2.2.val = if x.val < p.val.2.val then p.val.2.val else x.val) := by
  unfold sort3
  have hp := cup_lt p
  by_cases h₁ : x < p.val.1
  · have h₁' : x.val < p.val.1.val := h₁
    have h₂' : x.val < p.val.2.val := by omega
    simp [h₁, h₁', h₂']
  · have h₁' : ¬ x.val < p.val.1.val := h₁
    by_cases h₂ : x < p.val.2
    · have h₂' : x.val < p.val.2.val := h₂
      simp [h₁, h₂, h₁', h₂']
    · have h₂' : ¬ x.val < p.val.2.val := h₂
      simp [h₁, h₂, h₁', h₂']

/-- A configuration lies in exactly one triple. -/
theorem memT_sort3_iff {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x)
    (t : Tri a) :
    (memT t p.val.1 ∧ memT t p.val.2 ∧ memT t x) ↔ t = sort3 p x hx := by
  have hv := sort3_val p x hx
  have hp := cup_lt p
  have ht := tri_lt t
  have hx' := (incident_iff_val p x).not.mp hx
  rw [memT_iff_val, memT_iff_val, memT_iff_val, tri_ext]
  constructor
  · intro h
    split_ifs at hv <;> omega
  · intro h
    split_ifs at hv <;> omega

section Sums
variable {M : Type*} [AddCommMonoid M]

theorem sum_sconf {a : ℕ} (G : CoreCup a × CoreSite a → M) :
    ∑ c : SConf a, G c.val =
      ∑ p : CoreCup a, ∑ x : CoreSite a, if incident p x then 0 else G (p, x) := by
  rw [← Finset.sum_subtype (Finset.univ.filter (fun c : CoreCup a × CoreSite a =>
      ¬ incident c.1 c.2)) (by simp) G, Finset.sum_filter, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  simp only [ite_not]

theorem sum_opair {a : ℕ} (G : CoreSite a × CoreSite a → M) :
    ∑ o : OPair a, G o.val =
      ∑ s : CoreSite a, ∑ z : CoreSite a, if s = z then 0 else G (s, z) := by
  rw [← Finset.sum_subtype (Finset.univ.filter (fun o : CoreSite a × CoreSite a =>
      o.1 ≠ o.2)) (by simp) G, Finset.sum_filter, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
  simp only [ne_eq, ite_not]

theorem sum_tri_cover {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x)
    (g : Tri a → M) :
    ∑ t : Tri a, (if memT t p.val.1 ∧ memT t p.val.2 ∧ memT t x then g t else 0) =
      g (sort3 p x hx) := by
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single (sort3 p x hx)]
  · rw [ite_eq_left ((memT_sort3_iff p x hx _).mpr rfl)]
  · intro t _ ht
    rw [ite_eq_right (fun h => ht ((memT_sort3_iff p x hx t).mp h))]
  · simp

/-- Sum over cups containing two given sites. -/
theorem sum_pairs_both {a : ℕ} (u v : CoreSite a) (huv : u ≠ v) (g : CoreCup a → M) :
    ∑ p : CoreCup a, (if incident p u ∧ incident p v then g p else 0) =
      g (sortedCorePair a u v huv) := by
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single (sortedCorePair a u v huv)]
  · rw [ite_eq_left ⟨(incident_sorted huv u).mpr (Or.inl rfl),
      (incident_sorted huv v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)⟩]
  · intro p _ hp
    rw [ite_eq_right]
    rintro ⟨h₁, h₂⟩
    exact hp (core_pair_by_both p huv h₁ h₂)
  · simp

theorem sum_sconf' {a : ℕ} (g : SConf a → M) :
    ∑ c : SConf a, g c =
      ∑ p : CoreCup a, ∑ x : CoreSite a, if h : incident p x then 0 else g ⟨(p, x), h⟩ := by
  have e := sum_sconf (fun c : CoreCup a × CoreSite a =>
    if h : incident c.1 c.2 then (0 : M) else g ⟨c, h⟩)
  have e1 : (∑ c : SConf a, g c) = ∑ c : SConf a, (fun c : CoreCup a × CoreSite a =>
      if h : incident c.1 c.2 then (0 : M) else g ⟨c, h⟩) c.val := by
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro c _
    simp [c.property]
  rw [e1, e]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro p _
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro x _
  by_cases h : incident p x <;> simp [h]

theorem sum_opair' {a : ℕ} (g : OPair a → M) :
    ∑ o : OPair a, g o =
      ∑ s : CoreSite a, ∑ z : CoreSite a, if h : s = z then 0 else g ⟨(s, z), h⟩ := by
  have e := sum_opair (fun o : CoreSite a × CoreSite a =>
    if h : o.1 = o.2 then (0 : M) else g ⟨o, h⟩)
  have e1 : (∑ o : OPair a, g o) = ∑ o : OPair a, (fun o : CoreSite a × CoreSite a =>
      if h : o.1 = o.2 then (0 : M) else g ⟨o, h⟩) o.val := by
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro o _
    simp [o.property]
  rw [e1, e]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro s _
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro z _
  by_cases h : s = z <;> simp [h]

/-- The other endpoint of a cup through a given site. -/
def otherEnd {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) : CoreSite a :=
  if z = p.val.1 then p.val.2 else p.val.1

/-- Sum over cups through a site, reindexed by the other endpoint. -/
theorem sum_pairs_one {a : ℕ} (z : CoreSite a) (g : CoreCup a → M) :
    ∑ p : CoreCup a, (if incident p z then g p else 0) =
      ∑ r : CoreSite a, if h : z = r then 0 else g (sortedCorePair a z r h) := by
  have e1 : ∀ r : CoreSite a, (if h : z = r then (0 : M) else g (sortedCorePair a z r h)) =
      ∑ p : CoreCup a, (if r ≠ z ∧ (incident p z ∧ incident p r) then g p else 0) := by
    intro r
    by_cases h : z = r
    · subst h
      simp
    · simp only [h, dite_false]
      rw [← sum_pairs_both z r h g]
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro p _
      simp [Ne.symm h]
  simp_rw [e1]
  rw [Finset.sum_comm]
  apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
  intro p _
  by_cases hz : incident p z
  · rw [ite_eq_left hz, Finset.sum_eq_single (otherEnd p z)]
    · have hp := cup_lt p
      have ho : otherEnd p z ≠ z ∧ incident p z ∧ incident p (otherEnd p z) := by
        unfold otherEnd
        rw [incident_iff_val] at hz
        split_ifs with h
        · refine ⟨fun e => ?_, (incident_iff_val p z).mpr hz, Or.inr rfl⟩
          rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h e
          omega
        · refine ⟨fun e => ?_, (incident_iff_val p z).mpr hz, Or.inl rfl⟩
          rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h e
          omega
      rw [ite_eq_left ho]
    · intro r _ hr
      apply ite_eq_right
      rintro ⟨h₁, _, h₂⟩
      apply hr
      unfold otherEnd
      rw [incident_iff_val] at hz
      rw [incident_iff_val] at h₂
      have h₁' : r.val ≠ z.val := fun e => h₁ (Fin.ext e)
      have hp := cup_lt p
      split_ifs with h
      · rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h ⊢
        omega
      · rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h ⊢
        omega
    · simp
  · rw [ite_eq_right hz]
    symm
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro r _
    apply ite_eq_right
    rintro ⟨_, h, _⟩
    exact hz h

end Sums

section Counts
variable {R : Type*} [CommRing R]

theorem sum_off_two {a : ℕ} (s z : CoreSite a) (hsz : s ≠ z) (f : CoreSite a → R) :
    ∑ r : CoreSite a, (if r = s ∨ r = z then 0 else f r) = (∑ r, f r) - f s - f z := by
  have e : ∀ r : CoreSite a, (if r = s ∨ r = z then 0 else f r) =
      f r - (if r = s then f r else 0) - (if r = z then f r else 0) := by
    intro r
    by_cases h₁ : r = s
    · subst h₁; simp [hsz]
    · by_cases h₂ : r = z
      · subst h₂; simp [h₁]
      · simp [h₁, h₂]
  simp_rw [e, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true]

theorem sum_off_one {a : ℕ} (z : CoreSite a) (f : CoreSite a → R) :
    ∑ r : CoreSite a, (if r = z then 0 else f r) = (∑ r, f r) - f z := by
  have e : ∀ r : CoreSite a, (if r = z then 0 else f r) = f r - (if r = z then f r else 0) := by
    intro r
    by_cases h : r = z
    · subst h; simp
    · simp [h]
  simp_rw [e, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true]

theorem sum_const_sites {a : ℕ} (c : R) : ∑ _r : CoreSite a, c = ((a : R) + 3) * c := by
  simp [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin]

end Counts

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import SiteSums
import ChainSolve

/-!
Incidence matrices of the native core in site coordinates, over an arbitrary
commutative ring, and the incidence identities that do not involve triples.
The Boolean `b` selects the transposed hook, which introduces endpoint signs.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

variable {R : Type*} [Field R]

def epsR (b : Bool) : R := if b then -1 else 1

theorem epsR_sq (b : Bool) : (epsR b : R) * epsR b = 1 := by
  cases b <;> simp [epsR]

/-- Endpoint weight of a site on a cup: `ε` at the left end and `1` at the right end. -/
def wInc (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) R :=
  fun p z => if z = p.val.1 then epsR b else if z = p.val.2 then 1 else 0

/-- Sign of a configuration in the transposed hook: `-1` when the free site lies
strictly inside the cup. -/
def fSign (b : Bool) {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) : R :=
  if b = true ∧ p.val.1 < x ∧ x < p.val.2 then -1 else 1

/-- Configuration to ordered pair (marked site, cup endpoint). -/
def incB (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R :=
  fun c o => if c.val.2 = o.val.1 then wInc a b c.val.1 o.val.2 else 0

/-- Configuration to its triple. -/
def incT (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) R :=
  fun c t => if memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2 then
    fSign b c.val.1 c.val.2 else 0

/-- Configuration to its cup (the constant line). -/
def incL (a : ℕ) : Matrix (SConf a) (CoreCup a) R :=
  fun c p => if c.val.1 = p then 1 else 0

/-- Cup to triple. -/
def incK (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Tri a) R := (incL a)ᵀ * incT a b

/-- Ordered pair to its first site. -/
def incH (a : ℕ) : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) R :=
  fun o x => if o.val.1 = x then 1 else 0

/-- Ordered pair to the cup with the same sites, signed by orientation. -/
def incD (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) R :=
  fun o p => if o.val.1 = p.val.1 ∧ o.val.2 = p.val.2 then 1 else
    if o.val.1 = p.val.2 ∧ o.val.2 = p.val.1 then epsR b else 0

theorem dif_of_not {c : Prop} [Decidable c] {α : Sort*} {t : c → α} {e : ¬ c → α}
    (h : ¬ c) : dite c t e = e h := by simp [h]

theorem if_of_not {c : Prop} [Decidable c] {α : Sort*} {t e : α} (h : ¬ c) :
    ite c t e = e := by simp [h]

theorem cup_ne {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) : p.val.1 ≠ p.val.2 := ne_of_lt p.property

theorem wInc_sorted (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (z r : CoreSite a) (h : z ≠ r) (y : CoreSite a) :
    (wInc a b (sortedCorePair a z r h) y : R) =
      if y = z then (if z < r then epsR b else 1) else
        if y = r then (if r < z then epsR b else 1) else 0 := by
  rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne h with hl | hl
  · have e : sortedCorePair a z r h = ⟨(z, r), hl⟩ := by
      simp [sortedCorePair, min_eq_left hl.le, max_eq_right hl.le]
    rw [e]
    simp [wInc, hl, not_lt_of_gt hl]
  · have e : sortedCorePair a z r h = ⟨(r, z), hl⟩ := by
      simp [sortedCorePair, min_eq_right hl.le, max_eq_left hl.le]
    rw [e]
    by_cases hy : y = z
    · subst hy
      simp [wInc, hl, not_lt_of_gt hl, h]
    · simp [wInc, hl, not_lt_of_gt hl, hy]

theorem gram_H (a : ℕ) :
    (incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) R)ᵀ * incH a =
      ((a : R) + 3 - 1) • (1 : Matrix (CoreSite a) (CoreSite a) R) := by
  ext x y
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_opair']
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incH, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single x]
  · by_cases hxy : x = y
    · subst hxy
      simp only [ite_true, mul_one, mul_ite, mul_zero]
      have e : ∀ z : CoreSite a, (if h : x = z then (0 : R) else (1 : R)) =
          if z = x then 0 else 1 := by
        intro z
        by_cases h : x = z
        · subst h; simp
        · simp [h, Ne.symm h]
      simp_rw [e]
      rw [sum_off_one, sum_const_sites]
      ring
    · simp [hxy]
  · intro s _ hs
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro z _
    simp [hs]
  · simp

theorem gram_D (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) R)ᵀ * incD a b =
      (2 : R) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) R) := by
  ext p q
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_opair']
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incD, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  have hp := cup_ne p
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_add p.val.1 p.val.2 hp]
  · rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single p.val.2, Fintype.sum_eq_single p.val.1]
    · by_cases hpq : p = q
      · subst hpq
        simp [hp, Ne.symm hp, epsR_sq]
        norm_num
      · have hq : ¬ (p.val.1 = q.val.1 ∧ p.val.2 = q.val.2) := fun h =>
          hpq (Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h.1 h.2))
        have hq' : ¬ (p.val.2 = q.val.2 ∧ p.val.1 = q.val.1) := fun h => hq ⟨h.2, h.1⟩
        have hp1 := cup_lt p
        have hq1 := cup_lt q
        have hq2 : ¬ (p.val.1 = q.val.2 ∧ p.val.2 = q.val.1) := by
          rintro ⟨h₁, h₂⟩
          rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h₁ h₂
          omega
        have hq3 : ¬ (p.val.2 = q.val.1 ∧ p.val.1 = q.val.2) := fun h => hq2 ⟨h.2, h.1⟩
        simp [hp, Ne.symm hp, hq, hq', hq2, hq3, hpq]
    · intro z hz
      by_cases h : p.val.2 = z
      · simp [h]
      · have h1 : ¬ (p.val.2 = p.val.1 ∧ z = p.val.2) := fun e => hp e.1.symm
        have h2 : ¬ (p.val.2 = p.val.2 ∧ z = p.val.1) := fun e => hz e.2
        simp [h, h1, h2, hz]
    · intro z hz
      by_cases h : p.val.1 = z
      · simp [h]
      · have h1 : ¬ (p.val.1 = p.val.1 ∧ z = p.val.2) := fun e => hz e.2
        have h2 : ¬ (p.val.1 = p.val.2 ∧ z = p.val.1) := fun e => hp e.1
        simp [h, h1, h2, hz]
  · intro s hs
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro z _
    have h1 : ¬ (s = p.val.1 ∧ z = p.val.2) := fun e => hs.1 e.1
    have h2 : ¬ (s = p.val.2 ∧ z = p.val.1) := fun e => hs.2 e.1
    by_cases h : s = z <;> simp [h, h1, h2]

theorem incD_H (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) R)ᵀ * (incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) R) =
      (epsR b : R) • (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) R) := by
  ext p x
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_opair']
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incD, incH, Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, wInc]
  have hp := cup_ne p
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single x]
  · by_cases h1 : x = p.val.1
    · rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single p.val.2]
      · simp [h1, hp, epsR_sq]
      · intro z hz
        by_cases h : p.val.1 = z
        · simp [h1, h]
        · simp [h1, h, hz, hp]
    · by_cases h2 : x = p.val.2
      · rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single p.val.1]
        · simp [h2, hp, Ne.symm hp]
        · intro z hz
          by_cases h : p.val.2 = z
          · simp [h2, h]
          · simp [h2, h, hz, Ne.symm hp]
      · simp only [h1, h2, ite_false, mul_zero]
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro z _
        have e1 : ¬ (x = p.val.1 ∧ z = p.val.2) := fun e => h1 e.1
        have e2 : ¬ (x = p.val.2 ∧ z = p.val.1) := fun e => h2 e.1
        simp [e1, e2]
  · intro s hs
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro z _
    simp [hs]

theorem IJ_apply {P : Type*} [Fintype P] [DecidableEq P] (u v : R) (x y : P) :
    (Chain.IJ u v : Matrix P P R) x y = (if x = y then u else 0) + v := by
  simp [Chain.IJ, Chain.J, Matrix.one_apply]

theorem gram_N (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) R)ᵀ * wInc a b =
      Chain.IJ ((a : R) + 3 - 1 - epsR b) (epsR b : R) := by
  ext x y
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, IJ_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply]
  have e : ∀ p : CoreCup a, wInc a b p x * wInc a b p y =
      if incident p x then (wInc a b p x * wInc a b p y : R) else 0 := by
    intro p
    by_cases h : incident p x
    · rw [ite_eq_left h]
    · rw [ite_eq_right h]
      have h1 : x ≠ p.val.1 := fun e => h (Or.inl e)
      have h2 : x ≠ p.val.2 := fun e => h (Or.inr e)
      simp [wInc, h1, h2]
  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun p _ => e p), sum_pairs_one x]
  simp only [wInc_sorted]
  by_cases hxy : x = y
  · subst hxy
    have e2 : ∀ r : CoreSite a, (if h : x = r then (0 : R) else
        (if x < r then epsR b else 1) * (if x < r then epsR b else 1)) =
        if r = x then 0 else 1 := by
      intro r
      by_cases h : x = r
      · subst h; simp
      · rw [dif_of_not h, if_of_not (Ne.symm h)]
        split_ifs <;> simp [epsR_sq]
    simp only [ite_true, e2]
    rw [sum_off_one, sum_const_sites]
    ring
  · rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single y]
    · rw [dif_of_not hxy]
      simp only [ite_true, Ne.symm hxy, ite_false, hxy]
      rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hxy with hl | hl
      · simp [hl, not_lt_of_gt hl]
      · simp [hl, not_lt_of_gt hl]
    · intro r hr
      by_cases h : x = r
      · simp [h]
      · simp [h, Ne.symm hxy, hr, Ne.symm hr]

theorem gram_B (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R)ᵀ * incB a b =
      ((a : R) + 3 - 2 - epsR b) • (1 : Matrix (OPair a) (OPair a) R) +
        (epsR b : R) • ((incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) R) * (incH a)ᵀ) := by
  ext o o'
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_sconf']
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incB, Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.smul_apply,
    Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, incH]
  have hsz := o.property
  have hsz' := o'.property
  -- collapse the free site to the marked site of `o`
  have e1 : ∀ p : CoreCup a, (∑ x : CoreSite a, if h : incident p x then (0 : R) else
      (if x = o.val.1 then wInc a b p o.val.2 else 0) *
        (if x = o'.val.1 then wInc a b p o'.val.2 else 0)) =
      if incident p o.val.1 then 0 else
        (wInc a b p o.val.2 * (if o.val.1 = o'.val.1 then wInc a b p o'.val.2 else 0)) := by
    intro p
    rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single o.val.1]
    · by_cases h : incident p o.val.1 <;> simp [h]
    · intro x hx
      by_cases h : incident p x <;> simp [h, hx]
  simp_rw [e1]
  have eH : (∑ x : CoreSite a, (if o.val.1 = x then (1 : R) else 0) *
      (if o'.val.1 = x then 1 else 0)) = if o.val.1 = o'.val.1 then 1 else 0 := by
    rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single o.val.1]
    · by_cases h : o.val.1 = o'.val.1
      · simp [h]
      · simp [h, Ne.symm h]
    · intro x hx
      simp [Ne.symm hx]
  rw [eH]
  by_cases hs : o.val.1 = o'.val.1
  · have hoo : o = o' ↔ o.val.2 = o'.val.2 := by
      constructor
      · rintro rfl; rfl
      · intro h; exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext hs h)
    simp only [hs, ite_true, mul_one, hoo]
    -- now a sum over cups through the endpoint `o.2`
    have e2 : ∀ p : CoreCup a, (if incident p o'.val.1 then (0 : R) else
        wInc a b p o.val.2 * wInc a b p o'.val.2) =
        if incident p o.val.2 then (if incident p o'.val.1 then 0 else
          wInc a b p o.val.2 * wInc a b p o'.val.2) else 0 := by
      intro p
      by_cases h : incident p o.val.2
      · rw [ite_eq_left h]
      · rw [ite_eq_right h]
        have h1 : o.val.2 ≠ p.val.1 := fun e => h (Or.inl e)
        have h2 : o.val.2 ≠ p.val.2 := fun e => h (Or.inr e)
        simp [wInc, h1, h2]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun p _ => e2 p), sum_pairs_one o.val.2]
    simp only [wInc_sorted, incident_sorted]
    have hsz2 : o'.val.1 ≠ o.val.2 := by rw [← hs]; exact hsz
    by_cases hz : o.val.2 = o'.val.2
    · trans (∑ r : CoreSite a, if r = o'.val.1 ∨ r = o.val.2 then (0:R) else 1)
      · apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
        intro r _
        by_cases h : o.val.2 = r
        · simp [h]
        · rw [dif_of_not h]
          by_cases h' : o'.val.1 = r
          · simp [h']
          · have n1 : ¬(o'.val.1 = o.val.2 ∨ o'.val.1 = r) := fun e => e.elim hsz2 h'
            have n2 : ¬(r = o'.val.1 ∨ r = o.val.2) := fun e =>
              e.elim (fun e' => h' e'.symm) (fun e' => h e'.symm)
            rw [if_of_not n1, if_of_not n2]
            simp only [← hz, ite_true]
            split_ifs <;> simp [epsR_sq]
      · rw [sum_off_two _ _ hsz2, sum_const_sites]
        simp only [hz, ite_true]
        ring
    · have hz' : o.val.2 ≠ o'.val.2 := hz
      have hs2 : o'.val.1 ≠ o'.val.2 := hsz'
      rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single o'.val.2]
      · rw [dif_of_not hz']
        have n1 : ¬(o'.val.1 = o.val.2 ∨ o'.val.1 = o'.val.2) := fun e => e.elim hsz2 hs2
        rw [if_of_not n1]
        simp only [ite_true, Ne.symm hz', ite_false, hz', mul_zero, add_zero]
        rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hz' with hl | hl
        · simp [hl, not_lt_of_gt hl]
        · simp [hl, not_lt_of_gt hl]
      · intro r hr
        by_cases h : o.val.2 = r
        · simp [h]
        · rw [dif_of_not h]
          simp [Ne.symm hz, Ne.symm hr, Ne.symm h]
  · have hoo : o ≠ o' := fun e => hs (by rw [e])
    simp [hs, hoo]

end BrauerHook
```

## 36. `SiteTriples` (new, 436 lines, sha256 `c8297e6a160fc9419bb95559c2f5eb6ea0e97ced6bb5d8fa246f048644a09566`)

```lean
import SiteMatrices

/-!
Triple-level incidence identities for the native core in site coordinates.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

variable {R : Type*} [Field R]

theorem memT_sort3 {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x)
    (y : CoreSite a) : memT (sort3 p x hx) y ↔ y = p.val.1 ∨ y = p.val.2 ∨ y = x := by
  have hv := sort3_val p x hx
  have hp := cup_lt p
  have hx' := (incident_iff_val p x).not.mp hx
  rw [memT_iff_val]
  simp only [Fin.ext_iff]
  split_ifs at hv <;> omega

/-- The three configurations inside a triple. -/
def triConf₁ {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) : SConf a :=
  ⟨(⟨(t.val.2.1, t.val.2.2), t.property.2⟩, t.val.1), by
    rw [incident_iff_val]; have := tri_lt t; simp only; omega⟩

def triConf₂ {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) : SConf a :=
  ⟨(⟨(t.val.1, t.val.2.2), lt_trans t.property.1 t.property.2⟩, t.val.2.1), by
    rw [incident_iff_val]; have := tri_lt t; simp only; omega⟩

def triConf₃ {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) : SConf a :=
  ⟨(⟨(t.val.1, t.val.2.1), t.property.1⟩, t.val.2.2), by
    rw [incident_iff_val]; have := tri_lt t; simp only; omega⟩

def inTri {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (c : SConf a) : Prop :=
  memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2

instance {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) : DecidablePred (inTri t) :=
  fun c => inferInstanceAs (Decidable (memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧
    memT t c.val.2))

theorem inTri_iff {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (c : SConf a) :
    inTri t c ↔ c = triConf₁ t ∨ c = triConf₂ t ∨ c = triConf₃ t := by
  have ht := tri_lt t
  have hc := cup_lt c.val.1
  have hx := (incident_iff_val c.val.1 c.val.2).not.mp c.property
  simp only [inTri, memT_iff_val, sconf_ext, cup_ext, triConf₁, triConf₂, triConf₃]
  omega

theorem triConf_ne {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) :
    triConf₁ t ≠ triConf₂ t ∧ triConf₁ t ≠ triConf₃ t ∧ triConf₂ t ≠ triConf₃ t := by
  have ht := tri_lt t
  simp only [ne_eq, sconf_ext, cup_ext, triConf₁, triConf₂, triConf₃]
  omega

theorem sum_inTri {M : Type*} [AddCommMonoid M] {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (H : SConf a → M) :
    ∑ c : SConf a, (if inTri t c then H c else 0) =
      H (triConf₁ t) + H (triConf₂ t) + H (triConf₃ t) := by
  obtain ⟨n12, n13, n23⟩ := triConf_ne t
  have e : ∀ c : SConf a, (if inTri t c then H c else 0) =
      (if c = triConf₁ t then H c else 0) + (if c = triConf₂ t then H c else 0) +
        (if c = triConf₃ t then H c else 0) := by
    intro c
    by_cases h : inTri t c
    · rw [ite_eq_left h]
      rcases (inTri_iff t c).mp h with rfl | rfl | rfl
      · simp [n12, n13]
      · simp [Ne.symm n12, n23]
      · simp [Ne.symm n13, Ne.symm n23]
    · rw [ite_eq_right h]
      have h' := (inTri_iff t c).not.mp h
      simp only [not_or] at h'
      simp [h'.1, h'.2.1, h'.2.2]
  simp_rw [e, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true]

theorem sum_inTri' {M : Type*} [AddCommMonoid M] {a : ℕ} (t : Tri a) (H : SConf a → M) :
    ∑ c : SConf a, (if memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2 then
      H c else 0) = H (triConf₁ t) + H (triConf₂ t) + H (triConf₃ t) :=
  sum_inTri t H

theorem inTri_sort3 {a : ℕ} (c : SConf a) (t : Tri a) :
    inTri t c ↔ t = sort3 c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property :=
  memT_sort3_iff c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property t

theorem fSign_sq (b : Bool) {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) :
    (fSign b p x : R) * fSign b p x = 1 := by
  unfold fSign
  split_ifs <;> norm_num

theorem gram_T (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) R)ᵀ * incT a b =
      (3 : R) • (1 : Matrix (Tri a) (Tri a) R) := by
  ext t u
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incT, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  have e : ∀ c : SConf a,
      ((if memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2 then
        (fSign b c.val.1 c.val.2 : R) else 0) *
       (if memT u c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT u c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT u c.val.2 then
        fSign b c.val.1 c.val.2 else 0)) =
      if inTri t c then (if t = u then 1 else 0) else 0 := by
    intro c
    by_cases h : inTri t c
    · have ht := (inTri_sort3 c t).mp h
      by_cases htu : t = u
      · subst htu
        have h' : memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2 := h
        simp [h', h, fSign_sq]
      · have hu : ¬ (memT u c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT u c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT u c.val.2) := by
          intro hu
          exact htu (ht.trans ((inTri_sort3 c u).mp hu).symm)
        simp [hu, htu, h]
    · have h' : ¬ (memT t c.val.1.val.1 ∧ memT t c.val.1.val.2 ∧ memT t c.val.2) := h
      simp [h', h]
  simp_rw [e]
  rw [sum_inTri]
  by_cases htu : t = u <;> simp [htu]
  norm_num

theorem L_Dt (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (c : SConf a) (o : OPair a) :
    (((incL a : Matrix (SConf a) (CoreCup a) R) *
      (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) R)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R) c o) =
      (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) R) o c.val.1 := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Fintype.sum_eq_single c.val.1]
  · simp [incL]
  · intro p hp
    simp [incL, Ne.symm hp]

theorem incT_B (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) R)ᵀ * incB a b =
      (epsR b : R) • ((incK a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Tri a) R)ᵀ * (incD a b)ᵀ) := by
  rw [incK, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  ext t o
  rw [Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, L_Dt, incT, ite_mul, zero_mul]
  rw [sum_inTri', sum_inTri']
  obtain ⟨⟨t1, t2, t3⟩, h12, h23⟩ := t
  obtain ⟨⟨s, z⟩, hsz⟩ := o
  simp only [triConf₁, triConf₂, triConf₃, incB, incD, wInc, fSign]
  simp only at h12 h23 hsz ⊢
  have h13 : t1 < t3 := lt_trans h12 h23
  have n12 : t1 ≠ t2 := ne_of_lt h12
  have n13 : t1 ≠ t3 := ne_of_lt h13
  have n23 : t2 ≠ t3 := ne_of_lt h23
  have l21 : ¬ t2 < t1 := not_lt_of_gt h12
  have l31 : ¬ t3 < t1 := not_lt_of_gt h13
  have l32 : ¬ t3 < t2 := not_lt_of_gt h23
  by_cases hs1 : s = t1
  · subst hs1
    by_cases hz2 : z = t2
    · subst hz2; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
        Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
    · by_cases hz3 : z = t3
      · subst hz3; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
          Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
      · cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
          Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32, hz2, hz3, hsz]
  · by_cases hs2 : s = t2
    · subst hs2
      by_cases hz1 : z = t1
      · subst hz1; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
          Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
      · by_cases hz3 : z = t3
        · subst hz3; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
            Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
        · cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
            Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32, hz1, hz3, hsz]
    · by_cases hs3 : s = t3
      · subst hs3
        by_cases hz1 : z = t1
        · subst hz1; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
            Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
        · by_cases hz2 : z = t2
          · subst hz2; cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
              Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32]
          · cases b <;> simp [epsR, n12, n13, n23, Ne.symm n12, Ne.symm n13,
              Ne.symm n23, h12, h13, h23, l21, l31, l32, hz1, hz2, hsz]
      · cases b <;> simp [epsR, Ne.symm hs1, Ne.symm hs2, Ne.symm hs3, hs1, hs2, hs3]

theorem otherEnd_spec {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) {z : CoreSite a} (hz : incident p z) :
    otherEnd p z ≠ z ∧ incident p (otherEnd p z) := by
  have hp := cup_lt p
  have hz' := (incident_iff_val p z).mp hz
  unfold otherEnd
  split_ifs with h
  · refine ⟨fun e => ?_, Or.inr rfl⟩
    rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h e
    omega
  · refine ⟨fun e => ?_, Or.inl rfl⟩
    rw [Fin.ext_iff] at h e
    omega

/-- Two cups are equal, disjoint, or share exactly one site. -/
theorem pair_cases {a : ℕ} (p q : CoreCup a) :
    p = q ∨ (∀ z, incident p z → ¬ incident q z) ∨
      ∃ (bb u v : CoreSite a) (hbu : bb ≠ u) (hbv : bb ≠ v),
        u ≠ v ∧ p = sortedCorePair a bb v hbv ∧ q = sortedCorePair a bb u hbu := by
  by_cases H : ∃ z, incident p z ∧ incident q z
  · obtain ⟨bb, hp, hq⟩ := H
    obtain ⟨hv, hpv⟩ := otherEnd_spec p hp
    obtain ⟨hu, hqu⟩ := otherEnd_spec q hq
    have ep := core_pair_by_both p (Ne.symm hv) hp hpv
    have eq := core_pair_by_both q (Ne.symm hu) hq hqu
    by_cases huv : otherEnd q bb = otherEnd p bb
    · left
      rw [ep, eq]
      congr 1
      exact huv.symm
    · right; right
      exact ⟨bb, otherEnd q bb, otherEnd p bb, Ne.symm hu, Ne.symm hv, huv, ep, eq⟩
  · right; left
    exact fun z hp hq => H ⟨z, hp, hq⟩

theorem sorted_val {a : ℕ} (z r : CoreSite a) (h : z ≠ r) :
    (sortedCorePair a z r h).val = (min z r, max z r) := rfl

theorem sorted_both {a : ℕ} (z r : CoreSite a) (h : z ≠ r) (P : CoreSite a → Prop) :
    (P (sortedCorePair a z r h).val.1 ∧ P (sortedCorePair a z r h).val.2) ↔ (P z ∧ P r) := by
  rw [sorted_val]
  rcases le_total z r with hl | hl
  · simp [min_eq_left hl, max_eq_right hl]
  · simp [min_eq_right hl, max_eq_left hl, and_comm]

/-- The sign identity relating the triple move and the ordered-pair move. -/
theorem sign_triangle (b : Bool) {a : ℕ} (bb u v : CoreSite a) (hbu : bb ≠ u) (hbv : bb ≠ v)
    (huv : u ≠ v) :
    (fSign b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) u * fSign b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) v : R) =
      epsR b * (wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) bb *
        wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) bb) := by
  rw [wInc_sorted, wInc_sorted]
  simp only [ite_true]
  unfold fSign
  rw [sorted_val, sorted_val]
  simp only [min_def, max_def]
  have hbu' : bb.val ≠ u.val := fun e => hbu (Fin.ext e)
  have hbv' : bb.val ≠ v.val := fun e => hbv (Fin.ext e)
  have huv' : u.val ≠ v.val := fun e => huv (Fin.ext e)
  cases b
  · simp [epsR]
  · simp only [Fin.lt_def, Fin.le_def, epsR, true_and, ite_true]
    split_ifs <;> first | (exfalso; omega) | norm_num

theorem memT_sort3' {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x)
    (y : CoreSite a) : memT (sort3 p x hx) y ↔ incident p y ∨ y = x := by
  rw [memT_sort3]
  simp only [incident, or_assoc]

theorem PT_entry (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (c d : SConf a) :
    ((incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) R) * (incT a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) R) c d =
      if memT (sort3 c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property) d.val.1.val.1 ∧
          memT (sort3 c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property) d.val.1.val.2 ∧
          memT (sort3 c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property) d.val.2 then
        (fSign b c.val.1 c.val.2 * fSign b d.val.1 d.val.2 : R) else 0 := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incT, ite_mul, zero_mul]
  rw [sum_tri_cover c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property]
  simp only [mul_ite, mul_zero]

theorem LtML (a : ℕ) (M : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) R) (p q : CoreCup a) :
    ((incL a : Matrix (SConf a) (CoreCup a) R)ᵀ * M * incL a : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) R)
      p q =
      ∑ y : CoreSite a, if h' : incident q y then 0 else
        ∑ x : CoreSite a, if h : incident p x then 0 else M ⟨(p, x), h⟩ ⟨(q, y), h'⟩ := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_sconf']
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single q]
  · apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro y _
    by_cases h' : incident q y
    · simp [h']
    · simp only [h', dite_false, incL, ite_true, mul_one]
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_sconf', Fintype.sum_eq_single p]
      · apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
        intro x _
        by_cases h : incident p x <;> simp [h, Matrix.transpose_apply, incL]
      · intro r hr
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro x _
        by_cases h : incident r x <;> simp [h, Matrix.transpose_apply, incL, hr]
  · intro r hr
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro y _
    by_cases h' : incident r y <;> simp [h', incL, hr]

theorem wInc_sq_sum (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (∑ x : CoreSite a, (wInc a b p x : R) * wInc a b p x) = 2 := by
  have hp := cup_ne p
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_add p.val.1 p.val.2 hp]
  · simp [wInc, hp, Ne.symm hp, epsR_sq]
    norm_num
  · intro x hx
    simp [wInc, hx.1, hx.2]

theorem gram_K (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incK a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Tri a) R) * (incK a b)ᵀ =
      ((a : R) + 3 - 2 - 2 * epsR b) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) R) +
        (epsR b : R) • ((wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) R) * (wInc a b)ᵀ) := by
  rw [incK, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_assoc,
    ← Matrix.mul_assoc (incT a b), ← Matrix.mul_assoc]
  ext p q
  rw [LtML]
  simp only [PT_entry]
  simp only [Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul,
    Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, inTri, memT_sort3']
  rcases pair_cases p q with rfl | hd | ⟨bb, u, v, hbu, hbv, huv, rfl, rfl⟩
  · -- equal cups
    have e : ∀ y : CoreSite a, (if h' : incident p y then (0 : R) else
        ∑ x : CoreSite a, if h : incident p x then 0 else
          if (incident p p.val.1 ∨ p.val.1 = x) ∧ (incident p p.val.2 ∨ p.val.2 = x) ∧
            (incident p y ∨ y = x) then fSign b p x * fSign b p y else 0) =
        if y = p.val.1 ∨ y = p.val.2 then 0 else 1 := by
      intro y
      by_cases h' : incident p y
      · have : y = p.val.1 ∨ y = p.val.2 := h'
        simp [h', this]
      · have : ¬ (y = p.val.1 ∨ y = p.val.2) := h'
        rw [dif_of_not h', if_of_not this, Fintype.sum_eq_single y]
        · have h1 : incident p p.val.1 := Or.inl rfl
          have h2 : incident p p.val.2 := Or.inr rfl
          simp [h', h1, h2, fSign_sq]
        · intro x hx
          by_cases h : incident p x
          · simp [h]
          · simp [h, h', Ne.symm hx]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun y _ => e y), sum_off_two _ _ (cup_ne p), sum_const_sites,
      wInc_sq_sum]
    simp only [ite_true, Finset.sum_const_zero, mul_one]
    ring
  · -- disjoint cups
    have hpq : p ≠ q := by
      rintro rfl
      exact hd p.val.1 (Or.inl rfl) (Or.inl rfl)
    have e1 : ∀ y : CoreSite a, (if h' : incident q y then (0 : R) else
        ∑ x : CoreSite a, if h : incident p x then 0 else
          if (incident p q.val.1 ∨ q.val.1 = x) ∧ (incident p q.val.2 ∨ q.val.2 = x) ∧
            (incident p y ∨ y = x) then fSign b p x * fSign b q y else 0) = 0 := by
      intro y
      by_cases h' : incident q y
      · simp [h']
      · rw [dif_of_not h']
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro x _
        by_cases h : incident p x
        · simp [h]
        · rw [dif_of_not h]
          apply if_of_not
          rintro ⟨c1, c2, -⟩
          have n1 : ¬ incident p q.val.1 := fun hh => hd _ hh (Or.inl rfl)
          have n2 : ¬ incident p q.val.2 := fun hh => hd _ hh (Or.inr rfl)
          have e1 : q.val.1 = x := c1.resolve_left n1
          have e2 : q.val.2 = x := c2.resolve_left n2
          exact cup_ne q (e1.trans e2.symm)
    have e2 : ∀ x : CoreSite a, (wInc a b p x : R) * wInc a b q x = 0 := by
      intro x
      by_cases h : incident p x
      · have hq : ¬ incident q x := hd x h
        have h1 : x ≠ q.val.1 := fun e => hq (Or.inl e)
        have h2 : x ≠ q.val.2 := fun e => hq (Or.inr e)
        simp [wInc, h1, h2]
      · have h1 : x ≠ p.val.1 := fun e => h (Or.inl e)
        have h2 : x ≠ p.val.2 := fun e => h (Or.inr e)
        simp [wInc, h1, h2]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun y _ => e1 y), Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun x _ => e2 x)]
    simp [hpq]
  · -- cups sharing one site
    set p := sortedCorePair a bb v hbv with hp
    set q := sortedCorePair a bb u hbu with hq
    have ip : ∀ z, incident p z ↔ z = bb ∨ z = v := incident_sorted hbv
    have iq : ∀ z, incident q z ↔ z = bb ∨ z = u := incident_sorted hbu
    have hpq : p ≠ q := by
      intro e
      have h := (ip u).mp (e ▸ (iq u).mpr (Or.inr rfl))
      exact h.elim (fun e' => hbu e'.symm) (fun e' => huv e')
    have cq : ∀ P : CoreSite a → Prop, (P q.val.1 ∧ P q.val.2) ↔ (P bb ∧ P u) :=

    have nv : ¬ incident q v := fun h => ((iq v).mp h).elim (fun e => hbv e.symm) (Ne.symm huv)
    have e1 : ∀ y x : CoreSite a, ((incident p q.val.1 ∨ q.val.1 = x) ∧
        (incident p q.val.2 ∨ q.val.2 = x) ∧ (incident p y ∨ y = x)) ↔
        ((incident p bb ∨ bb = x) ∧ (incident p u ∨ u = x) ∧ (incident p y ∨ y = x)) := by
      intro y x
      rw [← and_assoc, ← and_assoc, cq (fun z => incident p z ∨ z = x)]
    simp only [e1]
    have hb : incident p bb := (ip bb).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
    rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single v]
    · rw [dif_of_not nv, Fintype.sum_eq_single u]
      · rw [dif_of_not nu]
        have hv : incident p v := (ip v).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
        rw [ite_eq_left ⟨Or.inl hb, Or.inr rfl, Or.inl hv⟩]
        rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single bb]
        · rw [sign_triangle b bb u v hbu hbv huv]

          exact Or.inl rfl
        · intro j hj
          by_cases hjp : incident p j
          · have hjq : ¬ incident q j := by
              intro h
              rcases (ip j).mp hjp with e | e
              · exact hj e
              · rcases (iq j).mp h with e' | e'

            have h2 : j ≠ q.val.2 := fun e => hjq (Or.inr e)

            have h2 : j ≠ p.val.2 := fun e => hjp (Or.inr e)
            simp [wInc, h1, h2]
      · intro x hx
        by_cases h : incident p x
        · simp [h]
        · rw [dif_of_not h]
          apply if_of_not
          rintro ⟨-, c2, -⟩
          exact c2.elim nu (fun e => hx e.symm)
    · intro y hy
      by_cases h' : incident q y
      · simp [h']
      · rw [dif_of_not h']
        apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
        intro x _
        by_cases h : incident p x
        · simp [h]
        · rw [dif_of_not h]
          apply if_of_not
          rintro ⟨-, c2, c3⟩
          have hxu : x = u := (c2.resolve_left nu).symm
          rcases c3 with c3 | c3
          · rcases (ip y).mp c3 with e | e

            · exact hy e
          · exact h' ((iq y).mpr (Or.inr (c3.trans hxu)))
```

```lean
import SiteTriples
import BoundarySplitting

/-!
The physical site of each through-strand coordinate of a core cup, the
resulting identification of the native core index with site configurations, and
the site description of the native through transport.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem slotValue_cases {a n : ℕ} (c : Cup a n) (i : ℕ) :
    (i < (left c).val ∧ slotValue c i = i) ∨
    ((left c).val ≤ i ∧ i + 1 < (right c).val ∧ slotValue c i = i + 1) ∨
    ((left c).val ≤ i ∧ (right c).val ≤ i + 1 ∧ slotValue c i = i + 2) := by
  unfold slotValue
  by_cases h₁ : i < (left c).val
  · left; exact ⟨h₁, by simp [h₁]⟩
  · by_cases h₂ : i + 1 < (right c).val
    · right; left; exact ⟨by omega, h₂, by simp [h₁, h₂]⟩
    · right; right; exact ⟨by omega, by omega, by simp [h₁, h₂]⟩

/-- The physical core site of through coordinate `i` of the core cup `p`. -/
def slotSite (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) : CoreSite a :=
  ⟨slotValue (embedCore a 0 p) i.val, by
    have hl := cup_lt p
    have hr := p.val.2.isLt
    have hi := i.isLt
    have hc := slotValue_cases (embedCore a 0 p) i.val
    simp only [left_core_value, right_core_value] at hc
    omega⟩

theorem slotSite_val (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (slotSite a p i).val =
      if i.val < p.val.1.val then i.val else
        if i.val + 1 < p.val.2.val then i.val + 1 else i.val + 2 := rfl

theorem slotSite_not_incident (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    ¬ incident p (slotSite a p i) := by
  rw [incident_iff_val, slotSite_val]
  have hl := cup_lt p
  split_ifs <;> omega

theorem slotSite_injective (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) : Function.Injective (slotSite a p) := by
  intro i j h
  have h' := congrArg Fin.val h
  rw [slotSite_val, slotSite_val] at h'
  have hl := cup_lt p
  apply Fin.ext
  split_ifs at h' <;> omega

theorem slotSite_surjective (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident p x) :
    ∃ i, slotSite a p i = x := by
  have hl := cup_lt p
  have hx' := (incident_iff_val p x).not.mp hx
  have hxl := x.isLt
  have hr := p.val.2.isLt
  refine ⟨⟨if x.val < p.val.1.val then x.val else
      if x.val < p.val.2.val then x.val - 1 else x.val - 2, by split_ifs <;> omega⟩, ?_⟩
  apply Fin.ext
  rw [slotSite_val]
  simp only
  split_ifs <;> omega

/-- Native core coordinates as site configurations. -/
def toConf (a : ℕ) (c : CoreIndex a) : SConf a :=
  ⟨(c.1, slotSite a c.1 c.2), slotSite_not_incident a c.1 c.2⟩

theorem toConf_bijective (a : ℕ) : Function.Bijective (toConf a) := by
  constructor
  · intro c d h
    have h1 : c.1 = d.1 := congrArg (fun e : SConf a => e.val.1) h
    have h2 : slotSite a c.1 c.2 = slotSite a d.1 d.2 := congrArg (fun e : SConf a => e.val.2) h
    rw [h1] at h2
    exact Prod.ext h1 (slotSite_injective a d.1 h2)
  · intro c
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := slotSite_surjective a c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property
    exact ⟨(c.val.1, i), Subtype.ext (Prod.ext rfl hi)⟩

noncomputable def confEquiv (a : ℕ) : CoreIndex a ≃ SConf a :=
  Equiv.ofBijective (toConf a) (toConf_bijective a)

@[simp] theorem confEquiv_apply (a : ℕ) (c : CoreIndex a) : confEquiv a c = toConf a c := rfl

/-- The hook action on a basis coordinate, for a relabeling fixing the last letter. -/
theorem hookAct_delta_fix {d : ℕ} (b : Bool) (π : Equiv.Perm (Letters d))
    (hπ : π (Fin.last d) = Fin.last d) (j i : Fin d) :
    hookAct b π (delta j) i =
      (if b then permSign π else 1) * (if π j.castSucc = i.castSucc then 1 else 0) := by
  unfold hookAct permute
  congr 1
  by_cases h : π j.castSucc = i.castSucc
  · rw [ite_eq_left h, ← h, Equiv.symm_apply_apply, expand_castSucc]
    simp [delta]
  · rw [ite_eq_right h]
    have hn : π.symm i.castSucc ≠ Fin.last d := by
      intro e
      have := congrArg π e
      rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply, hπ] at this
      exact Fin.castSucc_ne_last i this
    have H : ∀ x : Letters d, x ≠ Fin.last d → ∃ k : Fin d, k.castSucc = x :=
      fun x => Fin.lastCases (fun h => (h rfl).elim) (fun k _ => ⟨k, rfl⟩) x
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := H _ hn
    rw [← hk, expand_castSucc]
    have hkj : k ≠ j := by
      rintro rfl
      apply h
      rw [hk, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
    simp [delta, hkj]

theorem slotValue_core_injective (a : ℕ) (q : CoreCup a) {m n : ℕ} (hm : m < a + 2)
    (hn : n < a + 2) (h : slotValue (embedCore a 0 q) m = slotValue (embedCore a 0 q) n) :
    m = n := by
  have hl := cup_lt q
  have hcm := slotValue_cases (embedCore a 0 q) m
  have hcn := slotValue_cases (embedCore a 0 q) n
  simp only [left_core_value, right_core_value] at hcm hcn
  omega

/-- The physical through transport in site coordinates. -/
theorem transport_slot (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) {bb u v : SeedSite a}
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) bb u v) (j i : Fin (a+1)) :
    activeTransport s j.castSucc = i.castSucc ↔
      (slotSite a q i).val =
        if (slotSite a p j).val = u.val then v.val else (slotSite a p j).val := by
  have h := active_swap_site s j.castSucc
  rw [Fin.val_castSucc] at h
  change slotValue (embedCore a 0 q) _ = if (slotSite a p j).val = u.val then v.val
    else (slotSite a p j).val at h
  rw [← h]
  constructor
  · intro e
    rw [e]
    rfl
  · intro e
    apply Fin.ext
    rw [Fin.val_castSucc]
    exact slotValue_core_injective a q (activeTransport s j.castSucc).isLt
      (by have := i.isLt; omega) e.symm

end BrauerHook
```
