```lean
import SiteSlots

/-!
Bridge from the native core to the incidence form: after the orientation gauge,
every entry of the actual native core equals the corresponding entry of
`ε • (B * Bᵀ) + T * Tᵀ - (1 + 2ε) • 1` in site coordinates.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem wInc_true_eq (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) :
    (wInc a true p z : ℂ) = orientedIncidence a p z := by
  simp [wInc, orientedIncidence, epsR]

theorem gauge_sq' (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) : gauge a b p * gauge a b p = 1 :=
  gauge_sq a b p

/-- The gauged sign of a shared-site move. -/
theorem gauge_sign (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p q : CoreCup a) (bb : CoreSite a) {u v : SeedSite a}
    (hp : incident p bb) (hq : incident q bb)
    (s : Shared (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) (injectCoreSite a bb) u v) :
    gauge a b q * (if b then permSign (activeTransport s) else 1) * gauge a b p =
      epsR b * ((wInc a b p bb : ℂ) * wInc a b q bb) := by
  cases b
  · have hp' : bb = p.val.1 ∨ bb = p.val.2 := hp
    have hq' : bb = q.val.1 ∨ bb = q.val.2 := hq
    have e1 : (wInc a false p bb : ℂ) = 1 := by
      rcases hp' with h | h <;> simp [wInc, h, epsR]
    have e2 : (wInc a false q bb : ℂ) = 1 := by
      rcases hq' with h | h <;> simp [wInc, h, epsR]
    simp [gauge, e1, e2, epsR]
  · simp only [gauge, ite_true, wInc_true_eq]
    rw [through_sign_orientation a _ _ s, sign_endpointPerm, ← orientation_at_site a p bb hp,
      ← orientation_at_site a q bb hq]
    have h1 := coreOrientation_sq a p
    have h2 := coreOrientation_sq a q
    simp only [coreOrientation] at h1 h2 ⊢
    simp only [epsR, ite_true]
    linear_combination (-(orientedIncidence a p bb * orientedIncidence a q bb) *
      cupOrientation a (embedCore a 0 p) * cupOrientation a (embedCore a 0 p)) * h2 +
      (-(orientedIncidence a p bb * orientedIncidence a q bb)) * h1

variable {R : Type*} [Field R]

theorem BBt_entry (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (c d : SConf a) :
    ((incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R) * (incB a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) R)
      c d = if c.val.2 = d.val.2 then
        ∑ z : CoreSite a, (wInc a b c.val.1 z : R) * wInc a b d.val.1 z else 0 := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, sum_opair']
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, incB]
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single c.val.2]
  · by_cases h : c.val.2 = d.val.2
    · rw [ite_eq_left h]
      apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
      intro z _
      by_cases hz : c.val.2 = z
      · subst hz
        have hc := c.property
        have h1 : c.val.2 ≠ c.val.1.val.1 := fun e => hc (Or.inl e)
        have h2 : c.val.2 ≠ c.val.1.val.2 := fun e => hc (Or.inr e)
        simp [wInc, h1, h2]
      · rw [dif_of_not hz]
        simp [h]
    · rw [ite_eq_right h]
      apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
      intro z _
      by_cases hz : c.val.2 = z
      · simp [hz]
      · rw [dif_of_not hz]
        simp [Ne.symm h]
  · intro s hs
    apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
    intro z _
    by_cases hz : s = z
    · simp [hz]
    · rw [dif_of_not hz]
      simp [Ne.symm hs]

theorem toConf_val (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (toConf a (p, i)).val = (p, slotSite a p i) := rfl

theorem wInc_off (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) (h : ¬ incident p z) :
    (wInc a b p z : R) = 0 := by
  have h1 : z ≠ p.val.1 := fun e => h (Or.inl e)
  have h2 : z ≠ p.val.2 := fun e => h (Or.inr e)
  simp [wInc, h1, h2]
```

```lean
import SiteBridge

/-!
Entrywise bridge between the actual native core and its incidence form.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem embed_left (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) : left (embedCore a 0 p) = injectCoreSite a p.val.1 :=
  rfl

theorem embed_right (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) :
    right (embedCore a 0 p) = injectCoreSite a p.val.2 := rfl

theorem seed_disjoint (a : ℕ) (p q : CoreCup a) (hd : ∀ z, incident p z → ¬ incident q z) :
    ∀ z, touches (embedCore a 0 q) z → ¬ touches (embedCore a 0 p) z := by
  intro z hz hp
  rcases hz with rfl | rfl
  · rw [embed_left, incident_embed] at hp
    exact hd _ hp (Or.inl rfl)
  · rw [embed_right, incident_embed] at hp
    exact hd _ hp (Or.inr rfl)

/-- The seed-level shared structure of two cups through a common core site. -/
theorem sharedOf (a : ℕ) (bb u v : CoreSite a) (hbu : bb ≠ u) (hbv : bb ≠ v) (huv : u ≠ v) :
    Shared (embedCore a 0 (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv)) (embedCore a 0 (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu))
      (injectCoreSite a bb) (injectCoreSite a u) (injectCoreSite a v) :=
  ⟨fun e => hbu (injectCoreSite_inj a e), fun e => hbv (injectCoreSite_inj a e),
    fun e => huv (injectCoreSite_inj a e), sortedCorePair_touches a bb v hbv,
    sortedCorePair_touches a bb u hbu⟩

theorem shared_final (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (bb u v : CoreSite a) (hbu : bb ≠ u) (hbv : bb ≠ v)
    (huv : u ≠ v) (x y : CoreSite a) (hx : ¬ incident (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) x)
    (hy : ¬ incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) y) :
    epsR b * ((wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) bb : ℂ) *
        wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) bb) *
      (if y.val = (if x.val = u.val then v.val else x.val) then (1 : ℂ) else 0) =
    epsR b * (if y = x then (wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) bb : ℂ) *
        wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) bb else 0) +
      (if (incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.1 ∨
            (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.1 = y) ∧
          (incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.2 ∨
            (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.2 = y) ∧
          (incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) x ∨ x = y) then
        fSign b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) y * fSign b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) x else 0) := by
  have ip := incident_sorted hbv
  have iq := incident_sorted hbu
  have st := sign_triangle (R := ℂ) b bb u v hbu hbv huv
  have hxb : x ≠ bb := fun e => hx ((ip x).mpr (Or.inl e))
  have hxv : x ≠ v := fun e => hx ((ip x).mpr (Or.inr e))
  have hyb : y ≠ bb := fun e => hy ((iq y).mpr (Or.inl e))
  have hyu : y ≠ u := fun e => hy ((iq y).mpr (Or.inr e))
  have hcond : ((incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.1 ∨
        (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.1 = y) ∧
      (incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.2 ∨
        (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv).val.2 = y) ∧
      (incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) x ∨ x = y)) ↔ (y = v ∧ x = u) := by
    rw [← and_assoc, sorted_both bb v hbv
      (fun z => incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) z ∨ z = y)]
    simp only [iq]
    constructor
    · rintro ⟨⟨-, h2⟩, h3⟩
      have hvy : v = y := by
        rcases h2 with (e | e) | e
        · exact (hbv e.symm).elim
        · exact (huv e.symm).elim
        · exact e
      refine ⟨hvy.symm, ?_⟩
      rcases h3 with (e | e) | e
      · exact (hxb e).elim
      · exact e
      · exact (hxv (e.trans hvy.symm)).elim
    · rintro ⟨hyv, hxu⟩
      exact ⟨⟨by simp, Or.inr hyv.symm⟩, Or.inl (Or.inr hxu)⟩
  rw [if_congr hcond rfl rfl]
  by_cases hxu : x = u
  · rw [ite_eq_left (show x.val = u.val from congrArg Fin.val hxu)]
    by_cases hyv : y = v
    · have hyx : y ≠ x := by rw [hyv, hxu]; exact Ne.symm huv
      rw [ite_eq_left (show y.val = v.val from congrArg Fin.val hyv),
        ite_eq_right (show ¬ y = x from hyx), ite_eq_left (show y = v ∧ x = u from ⟨hyv, hxu⟩),
        mul_one, mul_zero, zero_add, hyv, hxu]
      linear_combination (-1 : ℂ) * st
    · have hyv' : y.val ≠ v.val := fun e => hyv (Fin.ext e)
      have hyx : y ≠ x := by rw [hxu]; exact hyu
      rw [ite_eq_right (show ¬ y.val = v.val from hyv'), ite_eq_right (show ¬ y = x from hyx),
        ite_eq_right (show ¬ (y = v ∧ x = u) from fun e => hyv e.1)]
      ring
  · have hxu' : x.val ≠ u.val := fun e => hxu (Fin.ext e)
    rw [ite_eq_right (show ¬ x.val = u.val from hxu'),
      ite_eq_right (show ¬ (y = v ∧ x = u) from fun e => hxu e.2)]
    by_cases hyx : y = x
    · rw [ite_eq_left (show y.val = x.val from congrArg Fin.val hyx),
        ite_eq_left (show y = x from hyx)]
      ring
    · have hyx' : y.val ≠ x.val := fun e => hyx (Fin.ext e)
      rw [ite_eq_right (show ¬ y.val = x.val from hyx'), ite_eq_right (show ¬ y = x from hyx)]
      ring

theorem bridge_entry (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (q : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) (p : CoreCup a)
    (j : Fin (a+1)) :
    gauge a b q * actualCore a b (q, i) (p, j) * gauge a b p =
      epsR b * (((incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) ℂ) * (incB a b)ᵀ :
          Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ) (toConf a (q, i)) (toConf a (p, j))) +
        ((incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) ℂ) * (incT a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ)
          (toConf a (q, i)) (toConf a (p, j)) -
        (1 + 2 * epsR b) * (if (q, i) = (p, j) then 1 else 0) := by
  have hA : actualCore a b (q, i) (p, j) =
      nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i := rfl
  rw [hA, BBt_entry, PT_entry]
  simp only [toConf_val]
  have hx := slotSite_not_incident a p j
  have hy := slotSite_not_incident a q i
  rcases pair_cases p q with rfl | hd | ⟨bb, u, v, hbu, hbv, huv, rfl, rfl⟩
  · -- equal cups
    have h0 : nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 p) j i = 0 := by
      simp [nativeMove]
    rw [h0, mul_zero, zero_mul]
    by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij
      have h1 : incident p p.val.1 := Or.inl rfl
      have h2 : incident p p.val.2 := Or.inr rfl
      simp only [memT_sort3', h1, h2, true_or, or_true, true_and, ite_true, wInc_sq_sum,
        fSign_sq]
      ring
    · have hxy : slotSite a p i ≠ slotSite a p j := fun e => hij (slotSite_injective a p e)
      have hpij : (p, i) ≠ (p, j) := fun e => hij (congrArg Prod.snd e)
      have hm : ¬ (memT (sort3 p (slotSite a p i) hy) p.val.1 ∧
          memT (sort3 p (slotSite a p i) hy) p.val.2 ∧
          memT (sort3 p (slotSite a p i) hy) (slotSite a p j)) := by
        rintro ⟨-, -, h3⟩
        rw [memT_sort3'] at h3
        exact h3.elim hx (fun e => hxy e.symm)
      simp [hxy, hm, hpij]
  · -- disjoint cups
    have hne : embedCore a 0 p ≠ embedCore a 0 q := by
      intro e
      exact hd p.val.1 (Or.inl rfl) ((incident_embed q _).mp (e ▸
        (incident_embed p p.val.1).mpr (Or.inl rfl)))
    have h0 : nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 p) (embedCore a 0 q) j i = 0 := by
      simp [nativeMove, hne, mixedTransport_disjoint (k := 0) (seed_disjoint a p q hd)]
    rw [h0, mul_zero, zero_mul]
    have hpq : (q, i) ≠ (p, j) := by
      intro e
      exact hne (congrArg (embedCore a 0) (congrArg Prod.fst e).symm)
    have hs : ∑ z : CoreSite a, (wInc a b q z : ℂ) * wInc a b p z = 0 := by
      apply Finset.sum_eq_zero
      intro z _
      by_cases hz : incident p z
      · rw [wInc_off a b q z (hd z hz), zero_mul]
      · rw [wInc_off a b p z hz, mul_zero]
    have hm : ¬ (memT (sort3 q (slotSite a q i) hy) p.val.1 ∧
        memT (sort3 q (slotSite a q i) hy) p.val.2 ∧
        memT (sort3 q (slotSite a q i) hy) (slotSite a p j)) := by
      rintro ⟨h1, h2, -⟩
      rw [memT_sort3'] at h1 h2
      have e1 := h1.resolve_left (hd _ (Or.inl rfl))
      have e2 := h2.resolve_left (hd _ (Or.inr rfl))
      exact cup_ne p (e1.trans e2.symm)
    simp [hs, hm, hpq]
  · -- cups sharing one site
    have s := sharedOf a bb u v hbu hbv huv
    have ip := incident_sorted hbv
    have iq := incident_sorted hbu
    have hne := shared_ne s
    have hπ := shared_core_fixes_last a _ _ s
    have hmove : nativeMove a 0 b (embedCore a 0 (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv))
        (embedCore a 0 (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu)) j i =
        (if b then permSign (activeTransport s) else 1) *
          (if activeTransport s j.castSucc = i.castSucc then 1 else 0) := by
      simp only [nativeMove, hne, ite_false, mixedTransport_shared s]
      exact hookAct_delta_fix b _ hπ j i
    rw [hmove]
    have hbp : incident (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) bb := (ip bb).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
    have hbq : incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) bb := (iq bb).mpr (Or.inl rfl)
    have hg := gauge_sign a b _ _ bb hbp hbq s
    have ht := transport_slot a _ _ s j i
    have hsum : ∑ z : CoreSite a, (wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) z : ℂ) *
        wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) z =
        wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) bb * wInc a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) bb := by
      rw [Fintype.sum_eq_single bb]
      intro z hz
      by_cases hzp : incident (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) z
      · have hzv : z = v := ((ip z).mp hzp).resolve_left hz
        have hzq : ¬ incident (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) z := by
          intro h
          rcases (iq z).mp h with e | e
          · exact hz e
          · exact huv (e.symm.trans hzv)
        rw [wInc_off a b _ z hzq, zero_mul]
      · rw [wInc_off a b _ z hzp, mul_zero]
    have hpq : (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu, i) ≠ (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv, j) := fun e =>
      hne (congrArg (embedCore a 0) (congrArg Prod.fst e).symm)
    rw [hsum]
    simp only [hpq, ite_false, mul_zero, sub_zero]
    have hcomm : gauge a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) *
        ((if b then permSign (activeTransport s) else 1) *
          (if activeTransport s j.castSucc = i.castSucc then (1 : ℂ) else 0)) *
        gauge a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv) =
        (gauge a b (sortedCorePair a bb u hbu) * (if b then permSign (activeTransport s) else 1) *
          gauge a b (sortedCorePair a bb v hbv)) *
          (if activeTransport s j.castSucc = i.castSucc then 1 else 0) := by ring
    rw [hcomm, hg]
    simp only [memT_sort3']
    have hval : (injectCoreSite a u).val = u.val := rfl
    have hval' : (injectCoreSite a v).val = v.val := rfl
    rw [hval, hval'] at ht
    simp only [ht]
    exact shared_final a b bb u v hbu hbv huv _ _ hx hy

end BrauerHook
```

## 40. `CoreSiteForm` (new, 131 lines, sha256 `647ada5d6596399833c71515aa2df0fe65bad60ac5ca77ad0ca02bde737e5f27`)

```lean
import SiteBridgeEntry

/-!
The actual native core over the loop field, reindexed by site configurations,
equals the incidence form conjugated by the orientation gauge.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

section Maps
variable {R : Type*} [Field R] (φ : ℂ →+* R)

theorem map_epsR (b : Bool) : φ (epsR b) = epsR b := by
  cases b <;> simp [epsR]

theorem map_wInc (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (z : CoreSite a) :
    φ (wInc a b p z) = wInc a b p z := by
  unfold wInc
  split_ifs <;> simp [map_epsR]

theorem map_fSign (b : Bool) {a : ℕ} (p : CoreCup a) (x : CoreSite a) :
    φ (fSign b p x) = fSign b p x := by
  unfold fSign
  split_ifs <;> simp

theorem map_BBt (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (c d : SConf a) :
    φ (((incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) ℂ) * (incB a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ)
      c d) =
      ((incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R) * (incB a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) R)
        c d := by
  rw [BBt_entry, BBt_entry]
  split_ifs
  · simp [map_sum, map_mul, map_wInc]
  · simp

theorem map_TTt (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (c d : SConf a) :
    φ (((incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) ℂ) * (incT a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ)
      c d) =
      ((incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) R) * (incT a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) R)
        c d := by
  rw [PT_entry, PT_entry]
  split_ifs
  · simp [map_mul, map_fSign]
  · simp

end Maps

theorem confEquiv_symm_toConf (a : ℕ) (c : CoreIndex a) : (confEquiv a).symm (toConf a c) = c :=
  (confEquiv a).symm_apply_apply c

theorem coreMatrix_entry (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (i j : CoreIndex a) :
    coreMatrix a 0 b i j = (if i = j then X else 0) + C (actualCore a b i j) := by
  rw [coreMatrix_is_charmatrix, Matrix.charmatrix_apply]
  simp [actualCore, Matrix.diagonal_apply]

/-- Spectral parameter of the incidence form. -/
noncomputable def yParam (b : Bool) : LoopField := lp X - (1 + 2 * epsR b)

/-- The orientation gauge on site configurations. -/
noncomputable def gaugeMat (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) LoopField :=
  Matrix.diagonal (fun c => lp (C (gauge a b c.val.1)))

theorem lp_gauge_sq (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) :
    lp (C (gauge a b p)) * lp (C (gauge a b p)) = 1 := by
  rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul, gauge_sq, map_one, map_one]

theorem core_site_form (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b)).submatrix (confEquiv a).symm (confEquiv a).symm =
      gaugeMat a b * Chain.coreForm (incB a b) (incT a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) * gaugeMat a b := by
  ext c d
  obtain ⟨⟨q, k⟩, rfl⟩ := (confEquiv a).surjective c
  obtain ⟨⟨p, l⟩, rfl⟩ := (confEquiv a).surjective d
  simp only [Matrix.submatrix_apply, Equiv.symm_apply_apply, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply,
    Matrix.map_apply, gaugeMat, Matrix.diagonal_mul, Matrix.mul_diagonal, Chain.coreForm,
    Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul, coreMatrix_entry,
    confEquiv_apply, confEquiv_symm_toConf, toConf_val]
  have hb := bridge_entry a b q k p l
  have hA : actualCore a b (q, k) (p, l) = gauge a b q * gauge a b p *
      (epsR b * (((incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) ℂ) * (incB a b)ᵀ :
          Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ) (toConf a (q, k)) (toConf a (p, l))) +
        ((incT a b : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) ℂ) * (incT a b)ᵀ : Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ)
          (toConf a (q, k)) (toConf a (p, l)) -
        (1 + 2 * epsR b) * (if (q, k) = (p, l) then 1 else 0)) := by
    rw [← hb]
    have h1 := gauge_sq a b q
    have h2 := gauge_sq a b p
    linear_combination (-(actualCore a b (q, k) (p, l)) * gauge a b p * gauge a b p) * h1 +
      (-(actualCore a b (q, k) (p, l))) * h2
  have heq : (toConf a (q, k) = toConf a (p, l)) ↔ ((q, k) = (p, l)) :=
    (toConf_bijective a).1.eq_iff
  rw [hA]
  simp only [map_add, map_mul, map_sub, map_one, map_ofNat, heq]
  have e1 := map_BBt (lp.comp C) a b (toConf a (q, k)) (toConf a (p, l))
  have e2 := map_TTt (lp.comp C) a b (toConf a (q, k)) (toConf a (p, l))
  have e3 := map_epsR (lp.comp C) b
  simp only [RingHom.comp_apply] at e1 e2 e3
  rw [e1, e2, e3]
  by_cases hqp : (q, k) = (p, l)
  · have hq : q = p := congrArg Prod.fst hqp
    have hk : k = l := congrArg Prod.snd hqp
    subst hq
    subst hk
    simp only [ite_true, map_one, mul_one]
    have hg := lp_gauge_sq a b q
    unfold yParam
    linear_combination (-(lp X)) * hg
  · simp only [hqp, ite_false, map_zero, mul_zero, sub_zero, zero_add, add_zero]
    ring

theorem gauge_det (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : (gaugeMat a b).det * (gaugeMat a b).det = 1 := by
  rw [gaugeMat, Matrix.det_diagonal, ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  apply Finset.prod_eq_one
  intro c _
  exact lp_gauge_sq a b c.val.1

theorem core_det_form (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lp (coreMatrix a 0 b).det =
      (Chain.coreForm (incB a b) (incT a b) (epsR b) (yParam b)).det := by
  rw [RingHom.map_det, ← Matrix.det_submatrix_equiv_self (confEquiv a).symm, core_site_form,
    Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul]
  have h := gauge_det a b
  linear_combination ((Chain.coreForm (incB a b) (incT a b) (epsR b) (yParam b)).det) * h

end BrauerHook
```

## 41. `SiteChain` (new, 77 lines, sha256 `a514163a6f0b96c847744b936ac60415908c41024016e99660f5724ccad1f22d`)

```lean
import CoreSiteForm

/-!
The site incidence matrices satisfy the identities of the abstract reduction,
and the index sets have the expected sizes.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem site_identities {R : Type*} [Field R] (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Chain.Identities (incB a b : Matrix (SConf a) (OPair a) R) (incT a b) (incH a) (incD a b)
      (wInc a b) (incK a b) (epsR b) ((a : R) + 3) where
  hε := epsR_sq b
  hm := by simp [Fintype.card_fin]
  h1 := gram_B a b
  h2 := gram_T a b
  h3 := incT_B a b
  h4 := gram_K a b
  h5 := gram_H a
  h6 := incD_H a b
  h7 := gram_D a b
  h8 := gram_N a b

theorem card_sconf (a : ℕ) : Fintype.card (SConf a) = (a+3).choose 2 * (a+1) := by
  rw [← Fintype.card_congr (confEquiv a), Fintype.card_prod, card_core, Fintype.card_fin]

theorem card_opair (a : ℕ) : Fintype.card (OPair a) = (a+3) * (a+2) := by
  have h := congrArg Matrix.trace (gram_H (R := ℂ) a)
  rw [Matrix.trace_mul_comm, Matrix.trace_smul, Matrix.trace_one, Fintype.card_fin] at h
  have e : Matrix.trace ((incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) ℂ) * (incH a)ᵀ) =
      (Fintype.card (OPair a) : ℂ) := by
    rw [Matrix.trace]
    have : ∀ o : OPair a, (((incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) ℂ) * (incH a)ᵀ :
        Matrix (OPair a) (OPair a) ℂ) o o) = 1 := by
      intro o
      rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
      simp [incH]
    simp only [Matrix.diag]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun o _ => this o)]
    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul, mul_one]
  rw [e, smul_eq_mul] at h
  have h' : ((Fintype.card (OPair a) : ℕ) : ℂ) = (((a+3) * (a+2) : ℕ) : ℂ) := by
    push_cast at h ⊢
    linear_combination h
  exact_mod_cast h'

theorem card_tri (a : ℕ) : 3 * Fintype.card (Tri a) = Fintype.card (SConf a) := by
  have h := congrArg Matrix.trace (gram_T (R := ℂ) a false)
  rw [Matrix.trace_mul_comm, Matrix.trace_smul, Matrix.trace_one] at h
  have e : Matrix.trace ((incT a false : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) ℂ) * (incT a false)ᵀ) =
      (Fintype.card (SConf a) : ℂ) := by
    rw [Matrix.trace]
    have : ∀ c : SConf a,
        (((incT a false : Matrix (SConf a) (Tri a) ℂ) * (incT a false)ᵀ :
          Matrix (SConf a) (SConf a) ℂ) c c) = 1 := by
      intro c
      rw [PT_entry]
      have h1 := (memT_sort3' c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property c.val.1.val.1).mpr (Or.inl (Or.inl rfl))
      have h2 := (memT_sort3' c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property c.val.1.val.2).mpr (Or.inl (Or.inr rfl))
      have h3 := (memT_sort3' c.val.1 c.val.2 c.property c.val.2).mpr (Or.inr rfl)
      rw [ite_eq_left ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩, fSign_sq]
    simp only [Matrix.diag]
    rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl (fun c _ => this c)]
    simp only [Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, nsmul_eq_mul, mul_one]
  rw [e, smul_eq_mul] at h
  have h' : ((3 * Fintype.card (Tri a) : ℕ) : ℂ) = ((Fintype.card (SConf a) : ℕ) : ℂ) := by
    push_cast
    linear_combination -h
  exact_mod_cast h'

end BrauerHook
```

## 42. `LoopScalars` (new, 52 lines, sha256 `abe11dc50bc3c5329d7e1f32f31862f506a92cb297bfd398fe6d57c729bd013a`)

```lean
import SiteChain

/-!
Elementary facts about the loop variable in the fraction field: linear and
monic polynomial expressions in it are nonzero.
-/

open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

/-- The loop variable in the fraction field. -/
noncomputable abbrev xL : LoopField := lp X

theorem lp_ne_of_monic {p : ℂ[X]} (hp : p.Monic) : lp p ≠ 0 := by
  intro h
  exact hp.ne_zero (lp_injective (h.trans lp.map_zero.symm))

theorem lp_C (c : ℂ) : lp (C c) = (lp.comp C) c := rfl

theorem xL_add_ne (c : ℂ) : xL + lp (C c) ≠ 0 := by
  rw [← map_add]
  exact lp_ne_of_monic (monic_X_add_C c)

theorem lpC_natCast (n : ℕ) : lp (C (n : ℂ)) = (n : LoopField) := by
  simp

/-- `x + c ≠ 0` for every constant `c` coming from `ℂ`. -/
theorem xL_add_const_ne (c : ℂ) (d : LoopField) (h : lp (C c) = d) : xL + d ≠ 0 := by
  rw [← h]
  exact xL_add_ne c

theorem xL_ne : xL ≠ 0 := by
  have h := xL_add_ne 0
  simp only [map_zero, add_zero] at h
  exact h

theorem cubic_ne (c2 c1 c0 : ℂ) :
    xL ^ 3 + lp (C c2) * xL ^ 2 + lp (C c1) * xL + lp (C c0) ≠ 0 := by
  have hm : (X ^ 3 + C c2 * X ^ 2 + C c1 * X + C c0 : ℂ[X]).Monic := by monicity!
  have h := lp_ne_of_monic hm
  simpa [map_add, map_mul, map_pow] using h

theorem quad_ne (c1 c0 : ℂ) : xL ^ 2 + lp (C c1) * xL + lp (C c0) ≠ 0 := by
  have hm : (X ^ 2 + C c1 * X + C c0 : ℂ[X]).Monic := by monicity!
  have h := lp_ne_of_monic hm
  simpa [map_add, map_mul, map_pow] using h

end BrauerHook
```

## 43. `OrdScalars` (new, 162 lines, sha256 `d62fc6adf5ea57b24839ca0ceb1173d977737fb9151947b0a55414e412503017`)

```lean
import LoopScalars

/-!
Closed forms of the scalars of the reduction for the ordinary hook (`ε = 1`).
-/

open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Ord

variable (a : ℕ)

/-- The site count as an element of the loop field. -/
noncomputable abbrev mL : LoopField := (a : LoopField) + 3

theorem eps : (epsR false : LoopField) = 1 := by simp [epsR]

theorem Y : yParam false = xL - 3 := by
  simp only [yParam, eps]; ring

theorem alpha : Chain.alpha (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) = xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3) := by
  simp only [Chain.alpha, Y, eps, mL]; ring

theorem gam : Chain.gam (yParam false) = xL⁻¹ := by
  simp only [Chain.gam, Y]; ring_nf

theorem beta : Chain.beta (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) = ((a : LoopField) - 1) / xL := by
  simp only [Chain.beta, Y, eps, mL]; ring_nf

theorem gee : Chain.gee (epsR false) (mL a) = (a : LoopField) + 1 := by
  simp only [Chain.gee, eps, mL]; ring

theorem hx : xL ≠ 0 := xL_ne

theorem h2 : xL - 2 ≠ 0 := by
  rw [sub_eq_add_neg]
  exact xL_add_const_ne (-2) _ (by rw [map_neg, map_neg, map_ofNat C 2, map_ofNat lp 2])

theorem h3 : xL - 3 ≠ 0 := by
  rw [sub_eq_add_neg]
  exact xL_add_const_ne (-3) _ (by rw [map_neg, map_neg, map_ofNat C 3, map_ofNat lp 3])

theorem ha1 : xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1) ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne ((a : ℂ) - 1) _ (by rw [map_sub, map_sub, map_natCast C, map_natCast lp,
    map_one, map_one])

theorem ha3 : xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3) ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne ((a : ℂ) - 3) _ (by rw [map_sub, map_sub, map_natCast C, map_natCast lp,
    map_ofNat C 3, map_ofNat lp 3])

theorem h2a : xL + 2 * (a : LoopField) ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne (2 * (a : ℂ)) _ (by rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_natCast C, map_natCast lp,
    map_ofNat C 2, map_ofNat lp 2])

theorem h2a2 : xL + 2 * ((a : LoopField) + 1) ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne (2 * ((a : ℂ) + 1)) _ (by rw [map_mul, map_mul, map_add, map_add,
    map_natCast C, map_natCast lp, map_ofNat C 2, map_ofNat lp 2, map_one, map_one])

theorem alpha2beta : Chain.alpha (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) -
    2 * Chain.beta (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
      (xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)) / xL := by
  rw [alpha, beta, eq_div_iff hx]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem delt : Chain.delt (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    ((a : LoopField) - 1) / ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have e := alpha2beta a
  rw [Chain.delt, e, beta]
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  field_simp

theorem gamma' : Chain.gam (yParam false) = 1 / xL := by rw [gam]; ring

theorem one2delt : 1 + 2 * Chain.delt (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    xL * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) / ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  rw [delt, eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem sZero : Chain.sZero (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    (xL - 3) * (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) / (xL - 2) := by
  have h₀ := hx
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  simp only [Chain.sZero, Chain.bx1, Chain.bx2, Chain.bx3, Chain.bx4]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee, gam]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff h₁]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem sOne : Chain.sOne (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    (xL - 3) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) ^ 2 * (xL + 2 * ((a : LoopField) + 1)) /
      ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₀ := hx
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  simp only [Chain.sOne, Chain.bx1, Chain.bx2, Chain.bx3, Chain.bx4, Chain.by1, Chain.by2,
    Chain.by3, Chain.by4]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee, gam]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem bx1 : Chain.bx1 (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    (xL ^ 3 + (3 * (a : LoopField) - 4) * xL ^ 2 + (2 * (a : LoopField) ^ 2 - 9 * a + 5) * xL +
      (-3 * (a : LoopField) ^ 2 + 6 * a - 3)) / ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₀ := hx
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  simp only [Chain.bx1]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem bx1m : Chain.bx1 (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) +
    mL a * Chain.by1 (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) =
    (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) * (xL ^ 2 + (2 * (a : LoopField) - 1) * xL + (-2 * a + 2)) /
      ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₀ := hx
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ha1 a
  simp only [Chain.bx1, Chain.by1]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem cubic_ne_a : xL ^ 3 + (3 * (a : LoopField) - 4) * xL ^ 2 +
    (2 * (a : LoopField) ^ 2 - 9 * a + 5) * xL + (-3 * (a : LoopField) ^ 2 + 6 * a - 3) ≠ 0 := by
  have h := cubic_ne (3 * (a : ℂ) - 4) (2 * (a : ℂ) ^ 2 - 9 * a + 5) (-3 * (a : ℂ) ^ 2 + 6 * a - 3)
  simp only [lp_C, map_sub, map_add, map_mul, map_neg, map_pow, map_natCast, map_ofNat] at h
  exact h

theorem quad_ne_a : xL ^ 2 + (2 * (a : LoopField) - 1) * xL + (-2 * (a : LoopField) + 2) ≠ 0 := by
  have h := quad_ne (2 * (a : ℂ) - 1) (-2 * (a : ℂ) + 2)
  simp only [lp_C, map_sub, map_add, map_mul, map_neg, map_one, map_natCast, map_ofNat] at h
  exact h

theorem generic : Chain.Generic (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) where
  hY := by rw [Y]; exact h3
  hY3 := by rw [Y, sub_add_cancel]; exact hx
  ha := by rw [alpha]; exact ha3 a
  hab := by rw [alpha2beta]; exact div_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h2 (ha1 a)) hx
  hx1 := by rw [bx1]; exact div_ne_zero (cubic_ne_a a) (mul_ne_zero h2 (ha1 a))
  hx1m := by rw [bx1m]; exact div_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (ha3 a) (quad_ne_a a)) (mul_ne_zero h2 (ha1 a))
  hs0 := by rw [sZero]; exact div_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h3 (h2a a)) (ha3 a)) h2

end Ord
end BrauerHook
```

## 44. `OrdDet` (new, 131 lines, sha256 `0fb990ef80e76f69bae974aa4652619f1a09613c7458c0ae2bdd1c6d5a1fb106`)

```lean
import OrdScalars

/-!
The determinant of the actual native core for the ordinary hook.
-/

open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem two_choose_two (a : ℕ) : 2 * (a+3).choose 2 = (a+3) * (a+2) := by
  rw [Nat.choose_two_right, show a + 3 - 1 = a + 2 by omega]
  have h := Nat.even_mul_succ_self (a+2)
  have e : (a+2)*(a+2+1) = (a+3)*(a+2) := by ring
  rw [e] at h
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := h
  omega

theorem card_sconf_two (a : ℕ) : 2 * Fintype.card (SConf a) = (a+3) * (a+2) * (a+1) := by
  rw [card_sconf, ← mul_assoc, two_choose_two]

section Monomial
variable {F : Type*} [Field F]

theorem cancel_x (x w : F) (e q : ℕ) (hx : x ≠ 0) :
    x ^ (e + q) * (w / x) ^ q = x ^ e * w ^ q := by
  calc x ^ (e + q) * (w / x) ^ q = x ^ e * (w ^ q * (x ^ q / x ^ q)) := by
        rw [div_pow, pow_add]; ring
    _ = x ^ e * w ^ q := by rw [div_self (pow_ne_zero _ hx), mul_one]

theorem cancel_u (u3 u2 y w : F) (e3 e4 : ℕ) (h3 : u3 ≠ 0) (h2 : u2 ≠ 0) :
    (u3 * u2) ^ (e3 + e4 + 1) * ((y / u3) ^ e4 * (w / (u3 * u2))) =
      u3 ^ e3 * u2 ^ (e3 + e4) * (y ^ e4 * w) := by
  calc (u3 * u2) ^ (e3 + e4 + 1) * ((y / u3) ^ e4 * (w / (u3 * u2))) =
        u3 ^ e3 * u2 ^ (e3 + e4) * (y ^ e4 * w) *
          ((u3 ^ e4 / u3 ^ e4) * ((u3 * u2) / (u3 * u2))) := by
        rw [div_pow]; ring
    _ = u3 ^ e3 * u2 ^ (e3 + e4) * (y ^ e4 * w) := by
        rw [div_self (pow_ne_zero _ h3), div_self (mul_ne_zero h3 h2)]; ring

/-- The reduced right side of the ordinary determinant identity. -/
theorem ord_rhs (x u1 u2 u3 u4 u5 u7 : F) (e3 e4 e6 S O : ℕ)
    (hx : x ≠ 0) (h3 : u3 ≠ 0) (h2 : u2 ≠ 0) :
    x ^ (e6 + (e3 + e4 + 1)) * u7 ^ S * (u3 * u2 / x) ^ (e3 + e4 + 1) * u5 ^ O *
      ((u7 * u4 * u5 / u3) ^ (e4 + 1 - 1) * (u7 * u5 ^ 2 * u1 / (u3 * u2))) =
    x ^ e6 * u7 ^ S * u5 ^ O * (u3 ^ e3 * u2 ^ (e3 + e4) * ((u7 * u4 * u5) ^ e4 *
      (u7 * u5 ^ 2 * u1))) := by
  rw [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
  calc _ = (x ^ (e6 + (e3 + e4 + 1)) * (u3 * u2 / x) ^ (e3 + e4 + 1)) * u7 ^ S * u5 ^ O *
        ((u7 * u4 * u5 / u3) ^ e4 * (u7 * u5 ^ 2 * u1 / (u3 * u2))) := by ring
    _ = x ^ e6 * u7 ^ S * u5 ^ O * ((u3 * u2) ^ (e3 + e4 + 1) *
        ((u7 * u4 * u5 / u3) ^ e4 * (u7 * u5 ^ 2 * u1 / (u3 * u2)))) := by
        rw [cancel_x x (u3 * u2) e6 (e3 + e4 + 1) hx]; ring
    _ = _ := by rw [cancel_u u3 u2 _ _ e3 e4 h3 h2]

/-- The reduced left side of the ordinary determinant identity. -/
theorem ord_lhs (x u1 u2 u3 u4 u5 u7 : F) (e3 e4 e5 e6 e7 : ℕ) :
    u1 * u2 ^ (e4 + e3) * u3 ^ e3 * u4 ^ e4 * u5 ^ e5 * x ^ e6 * u7 ^ e7 *
      u7 ^ (e6 + (e3 + e4 + 1)) * u7 ^ (e5 + e3 + 2 * e4 + 1) *
      u5 ^ (e3 + e4 + 1) * u5 ^ ((e4 + 1) + (e4 + 1)) =
    x ^ e6 * u7 ^ (e7 + e6 + e5 + 2 * e3 + 2 * e4 + 1) * u5 ^ (e5 + e3 + 2 * e4 + 1) *
      (u3 ^ e3 * u2 ^ (e3 + e4) * ((u7 * u4 * u5) ^ e4 * (u7 * u5 ^ 2 * u1))) := by
  ring

end Monomial

theorem ord_cards (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    Fintype.card (CoreSite a) = (a + 2) + 1 ∧
    Fintype.card (CoreCup a) = (a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) + 1 ∧
    Fintype.card (Tri a) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) / 6 + ((a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) + 1) ∧
    Fintype.card (OPair a) = (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 3) * a / 2 + 2 * (a + 2) + 1 ∧
    Fintype.card (SConf a) = (a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3 + (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) / 6 +
      (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + 2 * ((a + 3) * a / 2) + 2 * (a + 2) + 1 := by
  have hQ2 := two_choose_two a
  have hS2 := card_sconf_two a
  have hT3 := card_tri a
  have hO := card_opair a
  have hQc := card_core a
  have r1 : (a + 3) * (a + 2) = (a + 3) * a + 2 * (a + 3) := by ring
  have r2 : (a + 3) * (a + 2) = (a + 2) * (a + 1) + 2 * (a + 2) := by ring
  have r3 : (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) + 3 * ((a + 3) * (a + 2)) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a + 1) := by
    obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, a = n + 2 := ⟨a - 2, by omega⟩
    rw [show n + 2 - 2 = n by omega]
    ring
  have r4 : (a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) + 3 * ((a + 3) * (a + 1)) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a + 1) := by
    obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, a = n + 2 := ⟨a - 2, by omega⟩
    rw [show n + 2 - 1 = n + 1 by omega]
    ring
  have r5 : (a + 3) * (a + 1) + (a + 3) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) := by ring
  refine ⟨by simp [Fintype.card_fin], by omega, by omega, by omega, by omega⟩

theorem lp_ordinaryCoreProduct (a : ℕ) :
    lp (ordinaryCoreProduct a) =
      (xL + 2 * ((a : LoopField) + 1)) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)) ^ ((a + 2) + ((a + 3) * a / 2)) *
        (xL - 2) ^ ((a + 3) * a / 2) * (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) ^ (a + 2) *
        (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) ^ ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) *
        xL ^ ((a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) / 6) * (xL - 3) ^ ((a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3) := by
  simp only [ordinaryCoreProduct, map_mul, map_pow, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_ofNat,
    map_one]

theorem ordinary_core_det (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    (coreMatrix a 0 false).det = ordinaryCoreProduct a := by
  apply lp_injective
  rw [core_det_form]
  have hc := Chain.chain_det (P := CoreSite a) (site_identities (R := LoopField) a false)
    (Ord.generic a)
  set K := (Chain.coreForm (incB a false) (incT a false) (epsR false) (yParam false)).det with hK
  rw [Ord.alpha2beta, Ord.sZero, Ord.sOne, Ord.alpha, Ord.Y, sub_add_cancel] at hc
  obtain ⟨hP, hQ, hT, hO, hS⟩ := ord_cards a ha
  rw [hP, hQ, hT, hO, hS] at hc
  rw [ord_rhs xL _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ Ord.hx Ord.h2 (Ord.ha1 a)] at hc
  rw [lp_ordinaryCoreProduct]
  have hL := ord_lhs xL (xL + 2 * ((a : LoopField) + 1)) (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)) (xL - 2)
    (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) (xL - 3)
    ((a + 3) * a / 2) (a + 2) ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) ((a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) / 6)
    ((a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3)
  have hD : (xL - 3) ^ ((a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a - 2) / 6 + ((a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) + 1)) *
      (xL - 3) ^ ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 3) * a / 2 + 2 * (a + 2) + 1) *
      (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) ^ ((a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) + 1) *
      (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) ^ ((a + 2 + 1) + (a + 2 + 1)) ≠ 0 := by
    have h3 := Ord.h3
    have h5 := Ord.ha3 a
    apply mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero ?_ ?_) ?_) ?_ <;> exact pow_ne_zero _ ‹_›
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ hD
  simp only [← mul_assoc]
  rw [hc, hL]

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import OrdDet

/-!
Closed forms of the scalars of the reduction for the transposed hook (`ε = -1`).
-/

open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Tr

variable (a : ℕ)

noncomputable abbrev mL : LoopField := (a : LoopField) + 3

theorem eps : (epsR true : LoopField) = -1 := by simp [epsR]

theorem Y : yParam true = xL + 1 := by
  simp only [yParam, eps]; ring

theorem alpha : Chain.alpha (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) = xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1) := by
  simp only [Chain.alpha, Y, eps, mL]; ring

theorem gam : Chain.gam (yParam true) = (xL + 4)⁻¹ := by
  simp only [Chain.gam, Y]; ring_nf

theorem beta : Chain.beta (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) = -((a : LoopField) + 3) / (xL + 4) := by
  simp only [Chain.beta, Y, eps, mL]; ring_nf

theorem gee : Chain.gee (epsR true) (mL a) = (a : LoopField) + 3 := by
  simp only [Chain.gee, eps, mL]; ring

theorem h1 : xL + 1 ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne 1 _ (by rw [map_one, map_one])

theorem h2 : xL + 2 ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne 2 _ (by rw [map_ofNat C 2, map_ofNat lp 2])

theorem h4 : xL + 4 ≠ 0 :=
  xL_add_const_ne 4 _ (by rw [map_ofNat C 4, map_ofNat lp 4])

theorem hap : xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1) ≠ 0 := by
  rw [sub_eq_add_neg]
  exact xL_add_const_ne (-((a : ℂ) + 1)) _ (by rw [map_neg, map_neg, map_add, map_add,
    map_natCast C, map_natCast lp, map_one, map_one])

theorem ham : xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1) ≠ 0 := by
  rw [sub_eq_add_neg]
  exact xL_add_const_ne (-((a : ℂ) - 1)) _ (by rw [map_neg, map_neg, map_sub, map_sub,
    map_natCast C, map_natCast lp, map_one, map_one])

theorem alpha2beta : Chain.alpha (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) -
    2 * Chain.beta (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) =
      (xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)) / (xL + 4) := by
  have h := h4
  rw [alpha, beta, eq_div_iff h]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem delt : Chain.delt (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) =
    -((a : LoopField) + 3) / ((xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have e := alpha2beta a
  rw [Chain.delt, e, beta]
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ham a
  have h₄ := h4
  field_simp

theorem sZero : Chain.sZero (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) =
    (xL + 1) * (xL + 4) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) ^ 2 /
      ((xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ham a
  have h₄ := h4
  simp only [Chain.sZero, Chain.bx1, Chain.bx2, Chain.bx3, Chain.bx4]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee, gam]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem sOne : Chain.sOne (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) =
    (xL + 1) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) := by
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ham a
  have h₄ := h4
  simp only [Chain.sOne, Chain.bx1, Chain.bx2, Chain.bx3, Chain.bx4, Chain.by1, Chain.by2,
    Chain.by3, Chain.by4]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee, gam]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem bx1 : Chain.bx1 (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) =
    (xL ^ 3 + (-(a : LoopField) + 4) * xL ^ 2 + (-3 * (a : LoopField) + 5) * xL +
      (-(a : LoopField) ^ 2 - 8 * a - 7)) / ((xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1))) := by
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ham a
  simp only [Chain.bx1]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero h₁ h₂)]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem bx1m : Chain.bx1 (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) +
    mL a * Chain.by1 (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) = xL + 1 := by
  have h₁ := h2
  have h₂ := ham a
  simp only [Chain.bx1, Chain.by1]
  rw [alpha, delt, gee]
  simp only [eps, mL]
  field_simp
  ring

theorem cubic_ne_a : xL ^ 3 + (-(a : LoopField) + 4) * xL ^ 2 + (-3 * (a : LoopField) + 5) * xL +
    (-(a : LoopField) ^ 2 - 8 * a - 7) ≠ 0 := by
  have h := cubic_ne (-(a : ℂ) + 4) (-3 * (a : ℂ) + 5) (-(a : ℂ) ^ 2 - 8 * a - 7)
  simp only [lp_C, map_sub, map_add, map_mul, map_neg, map_pow, map_natCast, map_ofNat] at h
  exact h

theorem generic : Chain.Generic (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) where
  hY := by rw [Y]; exact h1
  hY3 := by rw [Y, show xL + 1 + 3 = xL + 4 by ring]; exact h4
  ha := by rw [alpha]; exact hap a
  hab := by rw [alpha2beta]; exact div_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h2 (ham a)) h4
  hx1 := by rw [bx1]; exact div_ne_zero (cubic_ne_a a) (mul_ne_zero h2 (ham a))
  hx1m := by rw [bx1m]; exact h1
  hs0 := by
    rw [sZero]
    exact div_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h1 h4) (pow_ne_zero _ (hap a)))
      (mul_ne_zero h2 (ham a))

end Tr
end BrauerHook
```

## 46. `TrDet` (new, 102 lines, sha256 `c8c149638b1cafa983d9a61c9670c557da9aeec42135b9504046d820bed0e2a5`)

```lean
import TrScalars

/-!
The determinant of the actual native core for the transposed hook.
-/

open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

section Monomial
variable {F : Type*} [Field F]

theorem tr_bracket (v5 w y : F) (f5 g p : ℕ) (h5 : v5 ≠ 0) (hw : w ≠ 0) :
    v5 ^ (f5 + g) * (w / v5) ^ (g + p) * ((y * v5) / w) ^ p = v5 ^ f5 * w ^ g * y ^ p := by
  calc v5 ^ (f5 + g) * (w / v5) ^ (g + p) * ((y * v5) / w) ^ p =
        v5 ^ f5 * w ^ g * y ^ p * ((v5 ^ (g + p) / v5 ^ (g + p)) * (w ^ p / w ^ p)) := by
        rw [div_pow, div_pow]; ring
    _ = v5 ^ f5 * w ^ g * y ^ p := by
        rw [div_self (pow_ne_zero _ h5), div_self (pow_ne_zero _ hw)]; ring

/-- The reduced right side of the transposed determinant identity. -/
theorem tr_rhs (v1 v2 v3 v4 v5 : F) (f5 g p S O : ℕ) (h5 : v5 ≠ 0) (h2 : v2 ≠ 0)
    (h3 : v3 ≠ 0) :
    v5 ^ (f5 + g) * v4 ^ S * (v2 * v3 / v5) ^ (g + p) * v1 ^ O *
      ((v4 * v5 * v1 ^ 2 / (v2 * v3)) ^ (p + 1 - 1) * (v4 * v1)) =
    v5 ^ f5 * v4 ^ S * v1 ^ O * ((v2 * v3) ^ g * (v4 * v1 ^ 2) ^ p * (v4 * v1)) := by
  rw [Nat.add_sub_cancel]
  calc _ = (v5 ^ (f5 + g) * (v2 * v3 / v5) ^ (g + p) * ((v4 * v1 ^ 2) * v5 / (v2 * v3)) ^ p) *
        v4 ^ S * v1 ^ O * (v4 * v1) := by ring
    _ = _ := by rw [tr_bracket v5 (v2 * v3) _ f5 g p h5 (mul_ne_zero h2 h3)]; ring

/-- The reduced left side of the transposed determinant identity. -/
theorem tr_lhs (v1 v2 v3 v4 v5 : F) (f1' f4 f5 g p : ℕ) :
    v1 ^ (p + f1') * v2 ^ g * v3 ^ g * v4 ^ f4 * v5 ^ f5 *
      v4 ^ (f5 + g) * v4 ^ (p + f1' + g + p + 1) * v1 ^ (g + p) * v1 ^ ((p + 1) + (p + 1)) =
    v5 ^ f5 * v4 ^ (f4 + f5 + 2 * g + (p + f1')) * v1 ^ (p + f1' + g + p + 1) *
      ((v2 * v3) ^ g * (v4 * v1 ^ 2) ^ p * (v4 * v1)) := by
  ring

end Monomial

theorem tr_cards (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    Fintype.card (CoreSite a) = (a + 2) + 1 ∧
    Fintype.card (CoreCup a) = (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 2) ∧
    Fintype.card (Tri a) = a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) / 6 + (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 ∧
    Fintype.card (OPair a) = (a + 2) + (a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 2) + 1 ∧
    Fintype.card (SConf a) = (a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3 + a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) / 6 +
      2 * ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) + ((a + 2) + (a + 3) * a / 2) := by
  have hQ2 := two_choose_two a
  have hS2 := card_sconf_two a
  have hT3 := card_tri a
  have hO := card_opair a
  have hQc := card_core a
  have r1 : (a + 3) * (a + 2) = (a + 3) * a + 2 * (a + 3) := by ring
  have r2 : (a + 3) * (a + 2) = (a + 2) * (a + 1) + 2 * (a + 2) := by ring
  have r4 : (a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) + 3 * ((a + 3) * (a + 1)) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a + 1) := by
    obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, a = n + 1 := ⟨a - 1, by omega⟩
    rw [show n + 1 - 1 = n by omega]
    ring
  have r5 : (a + 3) * (a + 1) + (a + 3) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) := by ring
  have r6 : a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) + 3 * ((a + 2) * (a + 1)) = (a + 3) * (a + 2) * (a + 1) := by ring
  refine ⟨by simp [Fintype.card_fin], by omega, by omega, by omega, by omega⟩

theorem lp_transposeCoreProduct (a : ℕ) :
    lp (transposeCoreProduct a) =
      (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) ^ ((a + 2) + ((a + 3) * a / 2)) *
        (xL + 2) ^ ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)) ^ ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) *
        (xL + 1) ^ ((a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3) * (xL + 4) ^ (a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) / 6) := by
  simp only [transposeCoreProduct, map_mul, map_pow, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_ofNat,
    map_one]

theorem transpose_core_det (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    (coreMatrix a 0 true).det = transposeCoreProduct a := by
  apply lp_injective
  rw [core_det_form]
  have hc := Chain.chain_det (P := CoreSite a) (site_identities (R := LoopField) a true)
    (Tr.generic a)
  set K := (Chain.coreForm (incB a true) (incT a true) (epsR true) (yParam true)).det with hK
  rw [Tr.alpha2beta, Tr.sZero, Tr.sOne, Tr.alpha, Tr.Y, show xL + 1 + 3 = xL + 4 by ring] at hc
  obtain ⟨hP, hQ, hT, hO, hS⟩ := tr_cards a ha
  rw [hP, hQ, hT, hO, hS] at hc
  rw [tr_rhs _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ Tr.h4 Tr.h2 (Tr.ham a)] at hc
  rw [lp_transposeCoreProduct]
  have hL := tr_lhs (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) (xL + 2) (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)) (xL + 1)
    (xL + 4) ((a + 3) * a / 2) ((a + 3) * (a - 1) * (a + 1) / 3) (a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) / 6)
    ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) (a + 2)
  have hD : (xL + 1) ^ (a * (a + 1) * (a + 2) / 6 + (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2) *
      (xL + 1) ^ ((a + 2) + (a + 3) * a / 2 + (a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 2) + 1) *
      (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) ^ ((a + 2) * (a + 1) / 2 + (a + 2)) *
      (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) ^ ((a + 2 + 1) + (a + 2 + 1)) ≠ 0 := by
    have h1 := Tr.h1
    have h5 := Tr.hap a
    apply mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero ?_ ?_) ?_) ?_ <;> exact pow_ne_zero _ ‹_›
  apply mul_right_cancel₀ hD
  simp only [← mul_assoc]
  rw [hc, hL]

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import TrDet

/-!
The incidence form pulled back to the native core index, its symmetry, and the
gauge.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

noncomputable def BCI (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (OPair a) LoopField :=
  (incB a b).submatrix (toConf a) id

noncomputable def TCI (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (Tri a) LoopField :=
  (incT a b).submatrix (toConf a) id

noncomputable def GCI (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreIndex a) LoopField :=
  Matrix.diagonal (fun c => lp (C (gauge a b c.1)))

theorem toConf_coe (a : ℕ) : (toConf a) = ⇑(confEquiv a) := rfl

theorem gram_pull {X Y : Type*} [Fintype X] (a : ℕ) (M : Matrix (SConf a) X LoopField)
    (N : Matrix (SConf a) Y LoopField) :
    (M.submatrix (toConf a) id)ᵀ * N.submatrix (toConf a) id = Mᵀ * N := by
  rw [Matrix.transpose_submatrix, toConf_coe, Matrix.submatrix_mul_equiv, Matrix.submatrix_id_id]

theorem identities_CI (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Chain.Identities (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (incH a) (incD a b) (wInc a b) (incK a b) (epsR b)
      ((a : LoopField) + 3) := by
  have I := site_identities (R := LoopField) a b
  exact ⟨I.hε, I.hm, by rw [BCI, gram_pull]; exact I.h1, by rw [TCI, gram_pull]; exact I.h2,
    by rw [TCI, BCI, gram_pull]; exact I.h3, I.h4, I.h5, I.h6, I.h7, I.h8⟩

theorem toConf_fst (a : ℕ) (i : CoreIndex a) : (toConf a i).val.1 = i.1 := rfl

theorem form_pull (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (i j : CoreIndex a) :
    Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) i j =
      Chain.coreForm (incB a b) (incT a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) (toConf a i) (toConf a j) := by
  simp only [Chain.coreForm, Matrix.add_apply, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, BCI, TCI,
    Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, Matrix.submatrix_apply, id]
  congr 2
  by_cases h : i = j
  · simp [h]
  · have h' : toConf a i ≠ toConf a j := fun e => h ((toConf_bijective a).1 e)
    simp [h, h']

theorem core_form_CI (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b) =
      GCI a b * Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) * GCI a b := by
  ext i j
  have h := congrFun (congrFun (core_site_form a b) (toConf a i)) (toConf a j)
  simp only [Matrix.submatrix_apply, confEquiv_symm_toConf, gaugeMat, Matrix.diagonal_mul,
    Matrix.mul_diagonal, toConf_fst] at h
  rw [h]
  simp only [GCI, Matrix.diagonal_mul, Matrix.mul_diagonal, form_pull]

theorem GCI_sq (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : GCI a b * GCI a b = 1 := by
  rw [GCI, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, ← Matrix.diagonal_one]
  congr 1
  funext c
  exact lp_gauge_sq a b c.1

theorem GCI_transpose (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : (GCI a b)ᵀ = GCI a b := Matrix.diagonal_transpose _

theorem coreForm_symm (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b))ᵀ =
      Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) := by
  simp only [Chain.coreForm, Matrix.transpose_add, Matrix.transpose_smul, Matrix.transpose_mul,
    Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.transpose_one]

theorem core_symm (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b))ᵀ = lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b) := by
  rw [core_form_CI, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_mul, GCI_transpose, coreForm_symm,
    Matrix.mul_assoc]

theorem lineAdj_symm (a : ℕ) : (lineAdj a)ᵀ = lineAdj a := by
  ext p q
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, lineAdj]
  by_cases h : p = q
  · simp [h]
  · have h' : q ≠ p := Ne.symm h
    have e : (∃ s, incident q s ∧ incident p s) ↔ (∃ s, incident p s ∧ incident q s) :=
      ⟨fun ⟨s, h1, h2⟩ => ⟨s, h2, h1⟩, fun ⟨s, h1, h2⟩ => ⟨s, h2, h1⟩⟩
    simp only [h, h', ite_false, e]

theorem orientedLine_symm (a : ℕ) : (orientedLine a)ᵀ = orientedLine a := by
  simp only [orientedLine, Matrix.transpose_sub, Matrix.transpose_smul, Matrix.transpose_one,
    Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_transpose]

theorem line_symm (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b))ᵀ = lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b) := by
  have hl : (lineAdjacency a b)ᵀ = lineAdjacency a b := by
    cases b
    · exact lineAdj_symm a
    · exact orientedLine_symm a
  have e : linePolynomial a b =
      (X : ℂ[X]) • (1 : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreCup a) ℂ[X]) + C.mapMatrix (lineAdjacency a b) := by
    cases b <;> rfl
  rw [e]
  ext p q
  have hpq := congrFun (congrFun hl p) q
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply] at hpq
  by_cases h : p = q
  · subst h; simp [Matrix.transpose_apply]
  · simp [Matrix.transpose_apply, RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, h, Ne.symm h, hpq]

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import Compress1

/-!
The actual native boundary rows and columns in site coordinates.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem dif_of_yes {c : Prop} [Decidable c] {α : Sort*} {t : c → α} {e : ¬ c → α}
    (h : c) : dite c t e = t h := by simp [h]

def csite (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : CoreSite a := ⟨j.val, by have := j.isLt; omega⟩
def mm2 (a : ℕ) : CoreSite a := ⟨a+1, by omega⟩

theorem expand_val {d : ℕ} (f : Fin d → ℂ) (y : Letters d) :
    expand f y = if h : y.val < d then f ⟨y.val, h⟩ else -∑ k, f k := by
  refine Fin.lastCases ?_ (fun k => ?_) y
  · simp [expand_last]
  · have h : (k.castSucc : Letters d).val < d := by simp
    rw [dif_of_yes h, expand_castSucc]
    rfl

theorem slot_marked_val (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (slotSite a (markedPair a t) i).val = if i.val < t.val then i.val else i.val + 1 := by
  rw [slotSite_val]
  have hi := i.isLt
  change (if i.val < t.val then i.val else if i.val + 1 < a + 2 then i.val + 1 else i.val + 2) = _
  split_ifs <;> omega

theorem transport_marked_val (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    (activeTransport (core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t)
      (markedPair_right_value a t)) i.castSucc).val =
        (slotSite a (markedPair a t) i).val := by
  rw [marked_to_first_tail a 0 _ (markedPair_right_value a t), slot_marked_val]
  have hi := i.isLt
  change (if i.val < t.val then i.val else if i.val < a+1 then i.val + 1 else t.val) = _
  split_ifs <;> omega

theorem bIn_marked (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t, i) j =
      C ((if slotSite a (markedPair a t) i = csite a j then 1 else 0) -
        (if slotSite a (markedPair a t) i = mm2 a then 1 else 0)) := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  let sr := Shared.mk s.distinctBV s.distinctBU s.distinctUV.symm s.target s.source
  have hn : coreInc a 0 (markedPair a t, i) ≠ tailInc a 0 (0, j) :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h)
  simp only [boundaryIn, Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, hn, ite_false, zero_add]
  simp only [coreInc, tailInc]
  rw [nativeMove, ite_eq_right (Ne.symm (core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0)),
    mixedTransport_shared sr, activeTransport_inverse s sr]
  congr 1
  simp only [hookAct, Bool.false_eq_true, ite_false, one_mul, permute, Equiv.symm_symm]
  rw [expand_val]
  have hv := transport_marked_val a t i
  change (activeTransport s i.castSucc).val = _ at hv
  have hx := slot_marked_val a t i
  have hi := i.isLt
  have hj := j.isLt
  by_cases h : (activeTransport s i.castSucc).val < a + 1
  · rw [dif_of_yes h]
    have h1 : slotSite a (markedPair a t) i ≠ mm2 a := by
      intro e; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; simp only [mm2] at e; omega
    rw [ite_eq_right h1, sub_zero]
    simp only [delta, Fin.ext_iff, csite]
    rw [hv]
  · rw [dif_of_not h]
    have h1 : slotSite a (markedPair a t) i = mm2 a := by
      apply Fin.ext; simp only [mm2]; omega
    have h2 : slotSite a (markedPair a t) i ≠ csite a j := by
      rw [h1]; intro e; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; simp only [mm2, csite] at e; omega
    rw [ite_eq_left h1, ite_eq_right h2]
    simp [delta]

theorem bOut_marked (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t, j) =
      C ((if slotSite a (markedPair a t) j = csite a i then 1 else 0) -
        (if t = i.castSucc then 1 else 0)) := by
  let s := core_first_tail_shared_marked a 0 (markedPair a t) (markedPair_right_value a t)
  have hn : tailInc a 0 (0, i) ≠ coreInc a 0 (markedPair a t, j) :=
    fun h => core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0 (congrArg Prod.fst h.symm)
  simp only [boundaryOut, Matrix.submatrix_apply, normalMatrix_entry, hn, ite_false, zero_add]
  simp only [coreInc, tailInc]
  rw [nativeMove, ite_eq_right (core_tail_ne a 0 (markedPair a t) 0), mixedTransport_shared s]
  congr 1
  simp only [hookAct, Bool.false_eq_true, ite_false, one_mul, permute]
  rw [expand_val]
  set y := (activeTransport s).symm i.castSucc with hy
  have hπy : activeTransport s y = i.castSucc := by rw [hy, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
  have hg := marked_ghost_image a 0 t
  change activeTransport s (Fin.last (a+1)) = t at hg
  by_cases h : y.val < a + 1
  · rw [dif_of_yes h]
    have e1 : activeTransport s (Fin.castSucc ⟨y.val, h⟩) = i.castSucc := by
      rw [show (Fin.castSucc ⟨y.val, h⟩ : Fin (a+2)) = y from Fin.ext rfl]
      exact hπy
    have hv : i.val = (slotSite a (markedPair a t) ⟨y.val, h⟩).val := by
      have := transport_marked_val a t ⟨y.val, h⟩
      change (activeTransport s (Fin.castSucc ⟨y.val, h⟩)).val = _ at this
      rw [e1, Fin.val_castSucc] at this
      exact this
    have ht : t ≠ i.castSucc := by
      intro e
      have h2 : activeTransport s y = activeTransport s (Fin.last (a+1)) := by
        rw [hπy, hg, e]
      have := (activeTransport s).injective h2
      rw [Fin.ext_iff] at this
      simp only [Fin.val_last] at this
      omega
    rw [ite_eq_right ht, sub_zero]
    simp only [delta]
    by_cases hk : (⟨y.val, h⟩ : Fin (a+1)) = j
    · have hc : slotSite a (markedPair a t) j = csite a i := by
        rw [← hk]; apply Fin.ext; simp only [csite]; exact hv.symm
      rw [ite_eq_left hk, ite_eq_left hc]
    · have hc : slotSite a (markedPair a t) j ≠ csite a i := by
        intro e
        apply hk
        apply slotSite_injective a (markedPair a t)
        apply Fin.ext
        rw [e]
        simp only [csite]
        exact hv.symm
      rw [ite_eq_right hk, ite_eq_right hc]
  · rw [dif_of_not h]
    have hyl : y = Fin.last (a+1) := by
      apply Fin.ext; simp only [Fin.val_last]; have := y.isLt; omega
    have ht : t = i.castSucc := by rw [← hg, ← hyl, hπy]
    have h2 : slotSite a (markedPair a t) j ≠ csite a i := by
      intro e
      have hs := slotSite_not_incident a (markedPair a t) j
      apply hs
      left
      rw [e]
      apply Fin.ext
      simp only [csite, markedPair, ht, Fin.val_castSucc]
    rw [ite_eq_right h2, ite_eq_left ht]
    simp [delta]

end BrauerHook
```

## 49. `Compress3` (new, 99 lines, sha256 `66597b9a8ae294e5e7d619b48d8243fa6e1171509ab8a5e30a8d2818bdb19c5c`)

```lean
import Compress2

/-!
Test vectors for the boundary compression and the matrix forms of the native
boundary rows and columns.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem csite_ne_last (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : csite a j ≠ lastCore a := by
  intro e; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; simp only [csite, lastCore, Fin.val_last] at e; omega

theorem mm2_ne_last (a : ℕ) : mm2 a ≠ lastCore a := by
  intro e; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; simp only [mm2, lastCore, Fin.val_last] at e; omega

theorem csite_ne_mm2 (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : csite a j ≠ mm2 a := by
  intro e; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; simp only [csite, mm2] at e; omega

theorem csite_inj (a : ℕ) {i j : Fin (a+1)} : csite a i = csite a j ↔ i = j := by
  constructor
  · intro e; apply Fin.ext; rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e; exact e
  · rintro rfl; rfl

def opair (a : ℕ) (s z : CoreSite a) (h : s ≠ z) : OPair a := ⟨(s, z), h⟩

noncomputable def wV (a : ℕ) : Matrix (OPair a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField := fun o j =>
  (if o = opair a (csite a j) (lastCore a) (csite_ne_last a j) then 1 else 0) -
    (if o = opair a (mm2 a) (lastCore a) (mm2_ne_last a) then 1 else 0)

noncomputable def OmV (a : ℕ) : Matrix (OPair a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField := fun o i =>
  if o = opair a (csite a i) (lastCore a) (csite_ne_last a i) then 1 else 0

noncomputable def UV (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreSite a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField := fun x j =>
  (if x = csite a j then 1 else 0) - (if x = mm2 a then 1 else 0)

noncomputable def RV (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField := fun p j =>
  (if p = markedPair a j.castSucc then 1 else 0) - (if p = markedPair a (Fin.last (a+1)) then 1 else 0)

noncomputable def LamCI (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreIndex a) (CoreCup a) LoopField :=
  fun c p => if c.1 = p then 1 else 0

noncomputable def SelV (a : ℕ) : Matrix (CoreCup a) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField :=
  fun p i => if p = markedPair a i.castSucc then 1 else 0

theorem markedPair_fst (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) : (markedPair a t).val.1 = t.castSucc := rfl
theorem markedPair_snd (a : ℕ) (t : Fin (a+2)) : (markedPair a t).val.2 = lastCore a := rfl

theorem markedPair_iff (a : ℕ) (p : CoreCup a) (t : Fin (a+2)) :
    p = markedPair a t ↔ (p.val.1 = t.castSucc ∧ p.val.2 = lastCore a) := by
  constructor
  · rintro rfl; exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩; exact Subtype.ext (Prod.ext h1 h2)

theorem csite_cast (a : ℕ) (j : Fin (a+1)) : csite a j = j.castSucc.castSucc := rfl
theorem mm2_cast (a : ℕ) : mm2 a = (Fin.last (a+1)).castSucc := rfl

theorem markedPair_inj (a : ℕ) {s t : Fin (a+2)} : markedPair a s = markedPair a t ↔ s = t :=
  (markedEmbedding a).injective.eq_iff

theorem wInc_last (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (wInc a b p (lastCore a) : LoopField) = if isMarked p then 1 else 0 := by
  have hl : lastCore a ≠ p.val.1 := by
    intro e
    have h1 := cup_lt p
    have h2 := p.val.2.isLt
    rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e
    simp only [lastCore, Fin.val_last] at e
    omega
  unfold wInc isMarked
  rw [if_of_not hl]
  by_cases h : p.val.2 = lastCore a
  · simp [h]
  · simp [h, Ne.symm h]

theorem incD_last (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (s : CoreSite a) (hs : s ≠ lastCore a) (p : CoreCup a) :
    (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField) (opair a s (lastCore a) hs) p =
      if p.val.1 = s ∧ p.val.2 = lastCore a then 1 else 0 := by
  have hl : ¬ (s = p.val.2 ∧ lastCore a = p.val.1) := by
    rintro ⟨-, e⟩
    have h1 := cup_lt p
    have h2 := p.val.2.isLt
    rw [Fin.ext_iff] at e
    simp only [lastCore, Fin.val_last] at e
    omega
  change (if s = p.val.1 ∧ lastCore a = p.val.2 then (1 : LoopField) else
    if s = p.val.2 ∧ lastCore a = p.val.1 then epsR b else 0) = _
  rw [if_of_not hl]
  by_cases h : p.val.1 = s ∧ p.val.2 = lastCore a
  · rw [ite_eq_left ⟨h.1.symm, h.2.symm⟩, ite_eq_left h]
  · rw [ite_eq_right (fun e => h ⟨e.1.symm, e.2.symm⟩), ite_eq_right h]

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
/-!
The boundary test data satisfy the hypotheses of the abstract compression, and
the gauged native boundary rows and columns factor through the incidence form.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem opair_eq_iff (a : ℕ) (s z s' z' : CoreSite a) (h : s ≠ z) (h' : s' ≠ z') :
    opair a s z h = opair a s' z' h' ↔ (s = s' ∧ z = z') := by
  constructor
  · intro e
    have := congrArg Subtype.val e
    simp only [opair, Prod.mk.injEq] at this
    exact this
  · rintro ⟨rfl, rfl⟩; rfl

@[simp] theorem opair_fst (a : ℕ) (s z : CoreSite a) (h : s ≠ z) : (opair a s z h).val.1 = s := rfl
@[simp] theorem opair_snd (a : ℕ) (s z : CoreSite a) (h : s ≠ z) : (opair a s z h).val.2 = z := rfl

theorem incH_opair (a : ℕ) (s z : CoreSite a) (h : s ≠ z) (x : CoreSite a) :
    (incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) (opair a s z h) x =
      if s = x then 1 else 0 := rfl

theorem sum_opair_ind {M : Type*} [AddCommMonoid M] (a : ℕ) (o₀ : OPair a) (F : OPair a → M) :
    ∑ o : OPair a, (if o = o₀ then F o else 0) = F o₀ := by
  rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq']
  simp

theorem tf1 (a : ℕ) : (incH a : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreSite a) LoopField)ᵀ * wV a = UV a := by
  ext x j
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, wV, mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib,
    mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, sum_opair_ind, incH_opair, UV]
  have h3 := csite_ne_mm2 a j
  by_cases hx : x = csite a j
  · subst hx; simp [h3, Ne.symm h3]
  · by_cases hm : x = mm2 a
    · subst hm; simp [h3, Ne.symm h3]
    · simp [hx, hm, Ne.symm hx, Ne.symm hm]

theorem tf2 (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField)ᵀ * wV a = RV a := by
  ext p j
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, wV, mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib,
    mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, sum_opair_ind]
  simp only [incD_last, RV, markedPair_iff, csite_cast, mm2_cast]

theorem wInc_marked (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (t : Fin (a+2)) (x : CoreSite a) :
    (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) (markedPair a t) x =
      if x = t.castSucc then (epsR b : LoopField) else if x = lastCore a then 1 else 0 := rfl

theorem tf3 (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField)ᵀ * RV a =
      (epsR b : LoopField) • UV a := by
  ext x j
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, RV, mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib,
    mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true,
    Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, UV, wInc_marked, ← csite_cast, ← mm2_cast]
  have h1 := csite_ne_last a j
  have h2 := mm2_ne_last a
  have h3 := csite_ne_mm2 a j
  by_cases hc : x = csite a j
  · subst hc; simp [h1, h3]
  · by_cases hm : x = mm2 a
    · subst hm; simp [h2, Ne.symm h3]
    · simp [hc, hm]

  ext x j
  simp [Chain.J, Matrix.mul_apply, UV, Finset.sum_sub_distrib]

  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, OmV, ite_mul, one_mul, zero_mul,
    sum_opair_ind, wV, opair_eq_iff, csite_inj, and_true, Matrix.one_apply]

  simp [h3]

  ext i j

  by_cases hij : i = j
  · subst hij; simp [h3]
  · have : csite a i ≠ csite a j := fun e => hij ((csite_inj a).mp e)
    simp [hij, this, h3]

theorem dt_om (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) :
    ((incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField)ᵀ * OmV a) p i =
      if p = markedPair a i.castSucc then 1 else 0 := by
  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, OmV, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero,
    sum_opair_ind]
  simp only [incD_last, markedPair_iff, csite_cast]

theorem tp2 (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    ((wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField)ᵀ *
      ((incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField)ᵀ * OmV a))ᵀ * UV a =
      (epsR b : LoopField) • (1 : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField) := by
  ext i j
  have e : ∀ x : CoreSite a, ((wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField)ᵀ *
      ((incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField)ᵀ * OmV a)) x i =
      (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) (markedPair a i.castSucc) x := by
    intro x
    rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
    simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, dt_om, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, Finset.sum_ite_eq',
      Finset.mem_univ, ite_true]
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, e, wInc_marked, UV, ← csite_cast, Matrix.smul_apply,
    Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  have h1 := csite_ne_last a j
  have h2 := mm2_ne_last a
  have h3 := csite_ne_mm2 a i
  rw [Fintype.sum_eq_add (csite a i) (lastCore a) (csite_ne_last a i)]
  · by_cases hij : i = j
    · subst hij; simp [h1, h2, h3, Ne.symm h1, Ne.symm h2, Ne.symm h3]
    · have hne : csite a i ≠ csite a j := fun e => hij ((csite_inj a).mp e)
      simp [hij, hne, h1, h2, h3, Ne.symm h1, Ne.symm h2]
  · intro x hx
    simp [hx.1, hx.2]

theorem tp3 (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    ((incD a b : Matrix (OPair a) (CoreCup a) LoopField)ᵀ * OmV a)ᵀ * RV a = 1 := by
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, dt_om, ite_mul, one_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq',
    Finset.mem_univ, ite_true, RV, markedPair_inj, Matrix.one_apply]
  have hl : i.castSucc ≠ Fin.last (a+1) := Fin.castSucc_ne_last i
  by_cases hij : i = j
  · subst hij; simp [hl]
  · have : i.castSucc ≠ j.castSucc := fun e => hij (Fin.castSucc_injective _ e)
    simp [hij, this, hl]

theorem test_data (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    Chain.TestData (incH a) (incD a b) (wInc a b) (epsR b) (wV a) (OmV a) (UV a) (RV a) :=
  ⟨tf1 a, tf2 a b, tf3 a b, tf4 a, tp0 a, tp1 a, tp2 a b, tp3 a b⟩

end BrauerHook
```

```lean
import Compress4

/-!
The gauged native boundary rows and columns factor through the incidence form,
and the full boundary compression splits into the incidence term and the
constant-line term.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false
set_option linter.unusedVariables false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem BCI_opair (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (i : Fin (a+1)) (s z : CoreSite a)
    (h : s ≠ z) :
    BCI a b (p, i) (opair a s z h) =
      if slotSite a p i = s then (wInc a b : Matrix (CoreCup a) (CoreSite a) LoopField) p z
        else 0 := rfl

theorem lp_ind (P : Prop) [Decidable P] :
    lp (C (if P then (1 : ℂ) else 0)) = if P then (1 : LoopField) else 0 := by
  split_ifs <;> simp

theorem gauge_mark (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (t : Fin (a+2)) (f : ℂ[X]) :
    lp (C (gauge a b (markedPair a t))) *
      lp (if b then C (coreOrientation a (markedPair a t)) * f else f) = lp f := by
  cases b
  · simp [gauge]
  · simp only [gauge, ite_true]
    rw [← map_mul, ← mul_assoc, ← C_mul, coreOrientation_sq, map_one, one_mul]

theorem bIn_b (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryIn a 0 b (markedPair a t, i) j =
      if b then C (coreOrientation a (markedPair a t)) * boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t, i) j
        else boundaryIn a 0 false (markedPair a t, i) j := by
  cases b
  · rfl
  · exact transposed_marked_input_action a t i j

theorem bOut_b (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (t : Fin (a+2)) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryOut a 0 b i (markedPair a t, j) =
      if b then C (coreOrientation a (markedPair a t)) * boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t, j)
        else boundaryOut a 0 false i (markedPair a t, j) := by
  cases b
  · rfl
  · exact transposed_marked_output_action a t i j

theorem bIn_unmarked (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryIn a 0 b (p, i) j = 0 := by
  cases b
  · exact ordinary_marked_in_zero a p hp i j
  · exact transposed_input_unmarked a p hp i j

theorem bOut_unmarked (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (p : CoreCup a) (hp : ¬ isMarked p) (i j : Fin (a+1)) :
    boundaryOut a 0 b i (p, j) = 0 := by
  cases b
  · exact ordinary_marked_out_zero a p hp i j
  · exact transposed_output_unmarked a p hp i j

theorem gauge_in (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    GCI a b * (boundaryIn a 0 b).map lp = BCI a b * wV a := by
  ext ⟨p, i⟩ j
  rw [GCI, Matrix.diagonal_mul, Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [wV, mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, sum_opair_ind,
    BCI_opair, wInc_last, Matrix.map_apply]
  by_cases hp : isMarked p
  · obtain ⟨t, rfl⟩ := (image_marked_pair a p).mp hp
    rw [bIn_b, gauge_mark, bIn_marked, map_sub, map_sub, lp_ind, lp_ind]
    simp [hp]
  · rw [bIn_unmarked a b p hp]
    simp [hp]

theorem gauge_out (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    (boundaryOut a 0 b).map lp * GCI a b = (BCI a b * OmV a)ᵀ - (LamCI a * SelV a)ᵀ := by
  ext i ⟨p, j⟩
  rw [GCI, Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply,
    Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.mul_apply]
  simp only [OmV, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, sum_opair_ind, BCI_opair, wInc_last, LamCI,
    ite_mul, one_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq, Finset.mem_univ, ite_true, SelV,
    Matrix.map_apply]
  by_cases hp : isMarked p
  · obtain ⟨t, rfl⟩ := (image_marked_pair a p).mp hp
    rw [bOut_b, mul_comm, gauge_mark, bOut_marked, map_sub, map_sub, lp_ind, lp_ind]
    simp [hp, markedPair_inj]
  · rw [bOut_unmarked a b p hp]
    have hne : ∀ t : Fin (a+2), p ≠ markedPair a t := fun t e => hp (e ▸ rfl)
    simp [hp, hne]

theorem lineJ_eq (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : lineJFrac a b = GCI a b * LamCI a := by
  ext c q
  simp only [lineJFrac, lineJPoly, Matrix.map_apply, lineJ, GCI, Matrix.diagonal_mul, LamCI]
  by_cases h : c.1 = q
  · subst h; simp
  · simp [h]

theorem lineJ_transpose (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : (lineJFrac a b)ᵀ = dLoop a • lineLFrac a b := by
  ext q c
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, lineJFrac, lineJPoly, lineLFrac, lineLPoly, Matrix.map_apply,
    lineJ, lineL, Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, dLoop]
  have hn : ((a : ℂ) + 1) ≠ 0 := by
    have h : (((a + 1 : ℕ) : ℂ)) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.succ_ne_zero _)
    simpa using h
  by_cases h : c.1 = q
  · subst h
    simp only [ite_true]
    rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul, ← mul_assoc, mul_inv_cancel₀ hn, one_mul]
  · simp [h, Ne.symm h]

theorem GCI_inv (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : (GCI a b)⁻¹ = GCI a b :=
  Matrix.inv_eq_right_inv (GCI_sq a b)

end BrauerHook
```

## 52. `Compress6` (new, 57 lines, sha256 `83af10679ce45ef706f4308bff6254447878e4cc7a69ee561b13b51e6338d4c7`)

```lean
import Compress5

/-!
The full native boundary compression splits into the incidence compression and
the constant-line resolvent applied to the line input.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem compression_split (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    boundaryCompression a 0 b =
      (BCI a b * OmV a)ᵀ * (Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b))⁻¹ *
          (BCI a b * wV a) -
        (SelV a)ᵀ * (dLoop a • ((lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b))⁻¹ * lineInput a)) := by
  set K := lp.mapMatrix (coreMatrix a 0 b) with hKd
  set F := Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b) with hFd
  set G := GCI a b with hGd
  set J := lineJFrac a b with hJd
  set D := lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a b) with hDd
  set Bm := (boundaryIn a 0 b).map lp with hBm
  set Om := (boundaryOut a 0 b).map lp with hOm
  have hKdef : K = G * F * G := core_form_CI a b
  have hKinv : K⁻¹ = G * F⁻¹ * G := by
    rw [hKdef, Matrix.mul_inv_rev, Matrix.mul_inv_rev, hGd, GCI_inv, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  have hJ : J = G * LamCI a := lineJ_eq a b
  have hΛ : LamCI a = G * J := by rw [hJ, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, hGd, GCI_sq, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hKJ : K⁻¹ * J = J * D⁻¹ := inverse_summand K D J (coreMatrix_line_frac a b)
    (core_field_det_ne a 0 b).isUnit (line_polynomial_field_det_ne a b).isUnit
  have hJtK : Jᵀ * K⁻¹ = D⁻¹ * Jᵀ := by
    have h := congrArg Matrix.transpose hKJ
    rw [Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_mul, Matrix.transpose_nonsing_inv,
      Matrix.transpose_nonsing_inv, hKd, core_symm, hDd, line_symm] at h
    exact h
  have hLam : (LamCI a)ᵀ * F⁻¹ * (BCI a b * wV a) = dLoop a • (D⁻¹ * lineInput a) := by
    rw [← gauge_in, hΛ, Matrix.transpose_mul, hGd, GCI_transpose]
    calc Jᵀ * GCI a b * F⁻¹ * (GCI a b * Bm) = Jᵀ * (GCI a b * F⁻¹ * GCI a b) * Bm := by
          simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
      _ = Jᵀ * K⁻¹ * Bm := by rw [hKinv]
      _ = D⁻¹ * (Jᵀ * Bm) := by rw [hJtK, Matrix.mul_assoc]
      _ = dLoop a • (D⁻¹ * lineInput a) := by
          rw [hJd, lineJ_transpose, Matrix.smul_mul, hBm, boundary_in_line, Matrix.mul_smul]
  unfold boundaryCompression
  rw [← hKd, ← hBm, ← hOm, hKinv]
  calc Om * (G * F⁻¹ * G) * Bm = (Om * G) * F⁻¹ * (G * Bm) := by simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    _ = ((BCI a b * OmV a)ᵀ - (LamCI a * SelV a)ᵀ) * F⁻¹ * (BCI a b * wV a) := by
        rw [hOm, hGd, gauge_out, hBm, gauge_in]
    _ = _ := by
        rw [Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.transpose_mul (LamCI a) (SelV a),
          Matrix.mul_assoc (SelV a)ᵀ, Matrix.mul_assoc (SelV a)ᵀ, hLam]

end BrauerHook
```

## 53. `ResidualTargets` (inherited, 80 lines, sha256 `e96b365e096ccb62553a77fb84906a277e250a004c7b0e51e1506270f9d5585a`)

```lean
import BoundarySplitting

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

/-- Expected determinant on the ordinary a-dimensional through component.
The missing uniform assertion is its equality with the actual residualCore determinant. -/
noncomputable def ordinaryResidualProduct (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X + C (2*(a:ℂ)))^(a+2) *
  (X + C ((a:ℂ)-1))^((a+3)*a/2) *
  (X + C ((a:ℂ)-3))^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) *
  X^((a+3)*(a+2)*(a-2)/6) *
  (X - 3)^((a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1)/3)

/-- Expected determinant on the transposed a-dimensional through component. -/
noncomputable def transposeResidualProduct (a : ℕ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (X - C ((a:ℂ)+1))^((a+3)*a/2) *
  (X - C ((a:ℂ)-1))^((a+2)*(a+1)/2) *
  (X + 1)^((a+3)*(a-1)*(a+1)/3) *
  (X + 4)^(a*(a+1)*(a+2)/6)

private theorem combine_ordinary {M : Type*} [CommMonoid M] (A B C D E F G : M)
    (r s t u v : ℕ) :
    A * B^(r+s) * C^s * D^r * E^t * F^u * G^v =
      (A * B^r * C^s) * (D^r * B^s * E^t * F^u * G^v) := by
  rw [pow_add]
  ac_rfl

private theorem combine_transpose {M : Type*} [CommMonoid M] (A B C D E : M)
    (r s t u v : ℕ) :
    A^(r+s) * B^t * C^t * D^u * E^v =
      (A^r * B^t) * (A^s * C^t * D^u * E^v) := by
  rw [pow_add]
  ac_rfl

theorem ordinary_core_product_split (a : ℕ) :
    ordinaryCoreProduct a = (linePolynomial a false).det * ordinaryResidualProduct a := by
  change ordinaryCoreProduct a = (lineChar a).det * ordinaryResidualProduct a
  rw [ordinary_line_charpoly,ordinaryCoreProduct,ordinaryResidualProduct]
  exact combine_ordinary _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

theorem transpose_core_product_split (a : ℕ) :
    transposeCoreProduct a = (linePolynomial a true).det * transposeResidualProduct a := by
  change transposeCoreProduct a = (transLineChar a).det * transposeResidualProduct a
  rw [transposed_line_charpoly,transposeCoreProduct,transposeResidualProduct]
  exact combine_transpose _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _

noncomputable def missingBoundary (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : LoopField :=
  (if b then lp (transposeR a) / lp (transposeQ a)
   else lp (ordinaryR a) / lp (ordinaryQ a)) - firstResolvent a b

/-- Exact remaining uniform inputs for ordinary (a+1,1). -/
theorem ordinary_normalized_of_residual (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a)
    (hdet : (residualCore a false).det = ordinaryResidualProduct a)
    (hboundary : residualCompression a false =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (missingBoundary a false)) :
    NativeNormalized a false (ordinaryCCorrected a) (printedOrdinaryP a) := by
  apply ordinary_normalized_of_seed a ha
  · rw [native_core_split_det,hdet,ordinary_core_product_split]
  · rw [native_boundary_split,hboundary,← map_add]
    simp [missingBoundary]

/-- Exact remaining uniform inputs for the native transposed range. -/
theorem transpose_normalized_of_residual (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a)
    (hdet : (residualCore a true).det = transposeResidualProduct a)
    (hboundary : residualCompression a true =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (missingBoundary a true)) :
    NativeNormalized a true (transposeCPrinted a) (correctedTransposeP a) := by
  apply transpose_normalized_of_seed a ha
  · rw [native_core_split_det,hdet,transpose_core_product_split]
  · rw [native_boundary_split,hboundary,← map_add]
    simp [missingBoundary]

#print axioms ordinary_core_product_split
#print axioms transpose_core_product_split
#print axioms ordinary_normalized_of_residual
#print axioms transpose_normalized_of_residual

end BrauerHook
```
