## 55. `OrdBdry` (new, 129 lines, sha256 `defd78688bbf83bd20cb629a0a7d260c76aff54d98627e1f973b3531dba20195`)

```lean
import Compress6

/-!
The full native boundary compression for the ordinary hook.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook

theorem coreForm_det_ne (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
    IsUnit (Chain.coreForm (BCI a b) (TCI a b) (epsR b) (yParam b)).det := by
  apply isUnit_iff_ne_zero.mpr
  intro h
  have hK := core_field_det_ne a 0 b
  rw [core_form_CI, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul, h, mul_zero, zero_mul] at hK
  exact hK rfl

namespace Ord

variable (a : ℕ)

noncomputable def d1 : LoopField :=
  (xL ^ 2 + ((a : LoopField) - 3) * xL - 2 * a) /
    ((xL - 3) * (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)))
noncomputable def e2 : LoopField :=
  -(xL ^ 2 - 3 * xL + 2 * a) /
    ((xL - 3) * (xL - 2) * (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)))
noncomputable def c1 : LoopField := -((a : LoopField) - 1) / ((xL - 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)))

theorem solve : Chain.SolveScalars (epsR false) (mL a) (yParam false) (d1 a) (e2 a) (c1 a) := by
  have h0 := hx
  have h2' := h2
  have h3' := h3
  have h1 := ha1 a
  have h4 := h2a a
  have n2 := h2; ring_nf at n2
  have n3 := h3; ring_nf at n3
  have n1 := ha1 a; ring_nf at n1
  have n4 := h2a a; ring_nf at n4
  constructor
  · rw [alpha, gee, eps]
    simp only [d1, e2, c1, mL]
    field_simp
    ring
  · rw [alpha2beta, beta, c1]
    field_simp
  · rw [alpha2beta, beta, gam, gee, eps]
    simp only [d1, e2, c1]
    field_simp
    ring

theorem chain_term : (BCI a false * OmV a)ᵀ *
    (Chain.coreForm (BCI a false) (TCI a false) (epsR false) (yParam false))⁻¹ *
      (BCI a false * wV a) =
    (1 * (1 - (xL - 3) / (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) *
      (1 - d1 a - 1 * e2 a - c1 a))) • (1 : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField) := by
  have h := Chain.chain_compress (identities_CI a false) (test_data a false) (solve a)
    (by rw [Y, sub_add_cancel]; exact hx) (by rw [alpha]; exact ha3 a) (coreForm_det_ne a false)
  rw [h, alpha, Y, eps]

theorem line_term : (SelV a)ᵀ * (dLoop a • ((lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a false))⁻¹ *
    lineInput a)) =
    (-((wLoop a - 1) / (xLoop * wLoop a))) • (1 : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField) := by
  have hinv : (lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a false))⁻¹ * lineInput a = lineResponse a :=
    lineResponse_inverse a
  rw [hinv]
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, SelV, Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, ite_mul, one_mul,
    zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true, lineResponse,
    markedSolution_value, expand_castSucc, delta]
  have hd := dLoop_ne a
  by_cases hij : i = j
  · subst hij
    simp only [ite_true, map_one, mul_one]
    field_simp
  · simp [hij, Ne.symm hij]

theorem lp_R : lp (ordinaryR (a : ℂ)) =
    ((a : LoopField) + 2) * (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 2)) *
      (xL ^ 2 + (2 * (a : LoopField) - 3) * xL - 2 * a) := by
  simp only [ordinaryR, map_mul, map_add, map_sub, map_pow, map_natCast, map_ofNat]

theorem lp_Q : lp (ordinaryQ (a : ℂ)) =
    (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1)) * (xL - 2) * (xL + 2 * (a : LoopField)) *
      (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) := by
  simp only [ordinaryQ, map_mul, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_ofNat, map_one]

theorem wLoop_eq : wLoop a = xL + ((a : LoopField) - 1) := by
  simp only [wLoop, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_one]

theorem xLoop_eq : xLoop = xL - 2 := by
  simp only [xLoop, map_sub, map_ofNat]

theorem scalar_identity :
    1 * (1 - (xL - 3) / (xL + ((a : LoopField) - 3)) * (1 - d1 a - 1 * e2 a - c1 a)) -
      (-((wLoop a - 1) / (xLoop * wLoop a))) = lp (ordinaryR (a : ℂ)) / lp (ordinaryQ (a : ℂ)) := by
  rw [lp_R, lp_Q, wLoop_eq, xLoop_eq]
  have h0 := hx
  have h2' := h2
  have h3' := h3
  have h1 := ha1 a
  have h4 := h2a a
  have h5 := ha3 a
  have n2 := h2; ring_nf at n2
  have n3 := h3; ring_nf at n3
  have n1 := ha1 a; ring_nf at n1
  have n4 := h2a a; ring_nf at n4
  have n5 := ha3 a; ring_nf at n5
  simp only [d1, e2, c1]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero h1 h2') h4) h5)]
  field_simp
  ring

end Ord

theorem ordinary_boundary (a : ℕ) :
    boundaryCompression a 0 false =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (lp (ordinaryR (a : ℂ)) / lp (ordinaryQ (a : ℂ))) := by
  rw [compression_split, Ord.chain_term, Ord.line_term, ← sub_smul, Ord.scalar_identity]
  ext i j
  simp [Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply, Matrix.one_apply]

end BrauerHook
```

## 57. `TrBdry` (new, 115 lines, sha256 `3866f1778a9ae20b6f3f19070f8e63ffe0ccfae0df45c866af0bfa2c779d6a4b`)

```lean
import OrdBdry

/-!
The full native boundary compression for the transposed hook.
-/

open scoped BigOperators Matrix
open Polynomial

set_option linter.unusedSectionVars false
set_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false

namespace BrauerHook
namespace Tr

variable (a : ℕ)

noncomputable def d1 : LoopField := 1 / (xL + 1)
noncomputable def e2 : LoopField := 1 / ((xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)))
noncomputable def c1 : LoopField := ((a : LoopField) + 3) / ((xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)))

theorem solve : Chain.SolveScalars (epsR true) (mL a) (yParam true) d1 (e2 a) (c1 a) := by
  have g1 := h1
  have g2 := h2
  have g4 := h4
  have gm := ham a
  have gp := hap a
  have n1 := h1; ring_nf at n1
  have n2 := h2; ring_nf at n2
  have n4 := h4; ring_nf at n4
  have nm := ham a; ring_nf at nm
  have np := hap a; ring_nf at np
  constructor
  · rw [alpha, gee, eps]
    simp only [d1, e2, c1, mL]
    field_simp
    ring
  · rw [alpha2beta, beta, c1]
    field_simp
  · rw [alpha2beta, beta, gam, gee, eps]
    simp only [d1, e2, c1]
    field_simp
    ring

theorem chain_term : (BCI a true * OmV a)ᵀ *
    (Chain.coreForm (BCI a true) (TCI a true) (epsR true) (yParam true))⁻¹ *
      (BCI a true * wV a) =
    (-1 * (1 - (xL + 1) / (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) *
      (1 - d1 - -1 * e2 a - c1 a))) • (1 : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField) := by
  have h := Chain.chain_compress (identities_CI a true) (test_data a true) (solve a)
    (by rw [Y, show xL + 1 + 3 = xL + 4 by ring]; exact h4) (by rw [alpha]; exact hap a)
    (coreForm_det_ne a true)
  rw [h, alpha, Y, eps]

theorem line_term : (SelV a)ᵀ * (dLoop a • ((lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a true))⁻¹ *
    lineInput a)) =
    (-((wTrans a + 1) / (xTrans * wTrans a))) •
      (1 : Matrix (Fin (a+1)) (Fin (a+1)) LoopField) := by
  have hinv : (lp.mapMatrix (linePolynomial a true))⁻¹ * lineInput a = transResponse a :=
    transResponse_inverse a
  rw [hinv]
  ext i j
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.smul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply, SelV, Matrix.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul, ite_mul, one_mul,
    zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, ite_true, transResponse,
    transSolution_value, expand_castSucc, delta]
  have hd := dLoop_ne a
  by_cases hij : i = j
  · subst hij
    simp only [ite_true, map_one, mul_one]
    field_simp
  · simp [hij, Ne.symm hij]

theorem lp_R : lp (transposeR (a : ℂ)) =
    ((a : LoopField) + 2) * (xL + 1) * (xL - (a : LoopField)) := by
  simp only [transposeR, map_mul, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_ofNat, map_one]

theorem lp_Q : lp (transposeQ (a : ℂ)) =
    (xL + 2) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) - 1)) * (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) := by
  simp only [transposeQ, map_mul, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_ofNat, map_one]

theorem wTrans_eq : wTrans a = xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1) := by
  simp only [wTrans, map_add, map_sub, map_natCast, map_one]

theorem xTrans_eq : xTrans = xL + 2 := by
  simp only [xTrans, map_add, map_ofNat]

theorem scalar_identity :
    -1 * (1 - (xL + 1) / (xL - ((a : LoopField) + 1)) * (1 - d1 - -1 * e2 a - c1 a)) -
      (-((wTrans a + 1) / (xTrans * wTrans a))) =
        lp (transposeR (a : ℂ)) / lp (transposeQ (a : ℂ)) := by
  rw [lp_R, lp_Q, wTrans_eq, xTrans_eq]
  have g1 := h1
  have g2 := h2
  have gm := ham a
  have gp := hap a
  have n1 := h1; ring_nf at n1
  have n2 := h2; ring_nf at n2
  have nm := ham a; ring_nf at nm
  have np := hap a; ring_nf at np
  simp only [d1, e2, c1]
  rw [eq_div_iff (mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero g2 gm) gp)]
  field_simp
  ring

end Tr

theorem transpose_boundary (a : ℕ) :
    boundaryCompression a 0 true =
      Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (lp (transposeR (a : ℂ)) / lp (transposeQ (a : ℂ))) := by
  rw [compression_split, Tr.chain_term, Tr.line_term, ← sub_smul, Tr.scalar_identity]
  ext i j
  simp [Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_apply, Matrix.one_apply]

end BrauerHook
```

## 58. `ResidualFinal` (new, 92 lines, sha256 `5abd31eaebb3c5aa9e7515ff0885ffd337bfed67b0955e6953d4e559005f4139`)

```lean
import TrBdry
import ResidualTargets

/-!
The four residual inputs of `ResidualTargets` and the unconditional normalized
endpoints for both hook families, together with the literal negation of the
printed ordinary clauses.
-/

open Polynomial

namespace BrauerHook

theorem line_det_ne (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) : (linePolynomial a b).det ≠ 0 := by
  intro h
  apply line_polynomial_field_det_ne a b
  rw [← RingHom.map_det, h, map_zero]

/-- Residual input 1: the ordinary sum-zero core determinant. -/
theorem residual_det_ordinary (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    (residualCore a false).det = ordinaryResidualProduct a := by
  have h := native_core_split_det a false
  rw [ordinary_core_det a ha, ordinary_core_product_split] at h
  exact (mul_left_cancel₀ (line_det_ne a false) h).symm

/-- Residual input 3: the transposed sum-zero core determinant. -/
theorem residual_det_transpose (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    (residualCore a true).det = transposeResidualProduct a := by
  have h := native_core_split_det a true
  rw [transpose_core_det a ha, transpose_core_product_split] at h
  exact (mul_left_cancel₀ (line_det_ne a true) h).symm

theorem residual_from_full (a : ℕ) (b : Bool) (r : LoopField)
    (h : boundaryCompression a 0 b = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) r) :
    residualCompression a b = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (r - firstResolvent a b) := by
  have hs := native_boundary_split a b
  rw [h] at hs
  rw [map_sub, hs, add_sub_cancel_right]

/-- Residual input 2: the ordinary sum-zero boundary compression. -/
theorem residual_boundary_ordinary (a : ℕ) :
    residualCompression a false = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (missingBoundary a false) := by
  rw [residual_from_full a false _ (ordinary_boundary a)]
  simp [missingBoundary]

/-- Residual input 4: the transposed sum-zero boundary compression. -/
theorem residual_boundary_transpose (a : ℕ) :
    residualCompression a true = Matrix.scalar (Fin (a+1)) (missingBoundary a true) := by
  rw [residual_from_full a true _ (transpose_boundary a)]
  simp [missingBoundary]

/-- Unconditional corrected ordinary hook endpoint, `a ≥ 2`, `k ≥ 0`. -/
theorem ordinary_normalized (a : ℕ) (ha : 2 ≤ a) :
    NativeNormalized a false (ordinaryCCorrected a) (printedOrdinaryP a) :=
  ordinary_normalized_of_residual a ha (residual_det_ordinary a ha) (residual_boundary_ordinary a)

/-- Unconditional corrected transposed hook endpoint, `a ≥ 1`, `k ≥ 0`. -/
theorem transpose_normalized (a : ℕ) (ha : 1 ≤ a) :
    NativeNormalized a true (transposeCPrinted a) (correctedTransposeP a) :=
  transpose_normalized_of_residual a ha (residual_det_transpose a ha)
    (residual_boundary_transpose a)

/-- The full actual native core determinants. -/
theorem native_core_dets :
    (∀ a : ℕ, 2 ≤ a → (coreMatrix a 0 false).det = ordinaryCoreProduct a) ∧
    (∀ a : ℕ, 1 ≤ a → (coreMatrix a 0 true).det = transposeCoreProduct a) :=
  ⟨ordinary_core_det, transpose_core_det⟩

#check @residual_det_ordinary
#check @residual_boundary_ordinary
#check @residual_det_transpose
#check @residual_boundary_transpose
#check @ordinary_normalized
#check @transpose_normalized
#check @printed_ordinary_clauses_negation
#check @height_five_printed_negation

#print axioms residual_det_ordinary
#print axioms residual_boundary_ordinary
#print axioms residual_det_transpose
#print axioms residual_boundary_transpose
#print axioms ordinary_normalized
#print axioms transpose_normalized
#print axioms native_core_dets
#print axioms ordinary_core_det
#print axioms transpose_core_det
#print axioms ordinary_boundary
#print axioms transpose_boundary
#print axioms printed_ordinary_clauses_negation
#print axioms height_five_printed_negation

end BrauerHook
```
